## 2.3 具有 Holling II 功能性反应的随机生态流行病模型

## 2.3.1 系统 (2.4) 的正解存在唯一性

本节, 先通过变量代换给出系统 (2.4) 存在唯一的局部解, 再给出此解是全局存在的.

定理2.7 对任何初值 $(S(0), I(0), P(0)) \in \mathbb{R}_+^3$ 且 $S(0) + I(0) \leqslant K$ , 系统 (2.4) 几乎必然存在唯一的正解 $(S(t), I(t), P(t))$ .

证明 对 $t \geqslant 0$ , 考虑初值为 $(u(0) = \log S(0), I(0), P(0))$ 的方程

$$
\left\{ \begin{array}{l} \mathrm{d} u (t) = \left[ r - \frac {r}{K} \mathrm{e} ^ {u (t)} - \left(\frac {r}{K} + \lambda\right) I (t) - \frac {\alpha P (t)}{a + \mathrm{e} ^ {u (t)}} - \frac {\sigma^ {2} I ^ {2}}{2} \right] \mathrm{d} t - \sigma I (t) \mathrm{d} B (t), \\ \mathrm{d} I (t) = (\lambda \mathrm{e} ^ {u (t)} I (t) - \beta I (t) P (t) - \mu I (t)) \mathrm{d} t + \sigma \mathrm{e} ^ {u (t)} I (t) \mathrm{d} B (t), \\ \mathrm{d} P (t) = \left(- \theta \beta I (t) P (t) - \delta P (t) + \frac {\theta \alpha \mathrm{e} ^ {u (t)} P (t)}{a + \mathrm{e} ^ {u (t)}}\right) \mathrm{d} t. \end{array} \right. \tag {2.15}
$$

显然, 系统 (2.15) 的系数是局部 Lipschitz 连续的, 则系统存在唯一的局部解 $(u(t), I(t), P(t))$ ( $t \in [0, \tau_e)$ ), 其中 $\tau_e$ 是爆破时间. 于是, 由 Itô 公式, 显然可得 $(S(t) = \mathrm{e}^{u(t)}, I(t), P(t))$ 是系统 (2.4) 满足初值 $(S(0), I(0), P(0)) \in \mathbb{R}_+^3$ 的唯一局部正解.

并且, 若 $S(0) + I(0) \leqslant K$ , 则对 $t \in [0, \tau_e)$ , $S(t) + I(t) \leqslant K$ 几乎必然成立. 事实上,

$$
\begin{array}{l} \frac {\mathrm{d} (S + I)}{\mathrm{d} t} = r S \left(1 - \frac {S + I}{K}\right) - \frac {\alpha S P}{a + S} - \beta I P - \mu I \\ \leqslant \max \left\{0, r (S + I) \left(1 - \frac {S + I}{K}\right) \right\}, \\ \end{array}
$$

则

$$
S (t) + I (t) \leqslant \max \left\{S (0) + I (0), \left[ \frac {1}{K} + \left(\frac {1}{S (0) + I (0)} - \frac {1}{K}\right) \mathrm{e} ^ {- r t} \right] ^ {- 1} \right\} \leqslant K.
$$

设 $W(t) = S(t) + I(t) + \frac{1}{\theta} P(t)$ , 那么

$$
\begin{array}{l} \frac {\mathrm{d} W (t)}{\mathrm{d} t} + \eta W (t) = r S \left(1 - \frac {S + I}{K}\right) - 2 \beta I P - \mu I - \frac {\delta}{\theta} P + \eta \left(S + I + \frac {1}{\theta} P\right) \\ \leqslant S \left[ \eta + r \left(1 - \frac {S}{K}\right) \right] - (\mu - \eta) I - \frac {\delta - \eta}{\theta} P \\ \end{array}
$$

$$
\begin{array}{l} \leqslant \frac {K (r + \eta) ^ {2}}{4 r} - (\mu - \eta) I - \frac {\delta - \eta}{\theta} P \\ \leqslant \frac {K (r + \eta) ^ {2}}{4 r}, \\ \end{array}
$$

这里选择 $\eta = \min \{\mu, \delta\}$ 使得 $\mu - \eta > 0, \delta - \eta > 0$ . 于是, 由比较定理可得

$$
\begin{array}{l} W (t) \leqslant \mathrm{e} ^ {- \eta t} \left[ W (0) + \frac {K}{4 r \eta} (\eta + r) ^ {2} \left(\mathrm{e} ^ {\eta t} - 1\right) \right] \\ \leqslant \max \left\{W (0), \frac {K}{4 r \eta} (\eta + r) ^ {2} \right\} \\ \end{array}
$$

和

$$
\limsup _ {t \to \infty} W (t) \leqslant \frac {K}{4 r \eta} (\eta + r) ^ {2} := \frac {B}{\eta},
$$

这是不依赖于初值的.

下面证明该正解是全局存在的. 显然只需证明 $\tau_{e} = \infty$ a.s.. 令 $k_{0} > 0$ 足够大, 使得 $S(0), I(0)$ 和 $P(0)$ 均位于区间 $\left[\frac{1}{k_0}, k_0\right]$ 中. 对任意整数 $k \geqslant k_0$ , 定义停时

$$
\tau_ {k} = \inf \left\{t \in [ 0, \tau_ {e}): \min \{S (t), I (t), P (t) \} \leqslant {\frac {1}{k}}   \text {或}   \max \{S (t), I (t), P (t) \} \geqslant k \right\},
$$

其中，约定 $\inf \varnothing = \infty$ （通常， $\varnothing$ 表示空集）。显然， $\tau_{k}$ 关于 $k$ 是单调增加的。令 $\tau_{\infty} = \lim_{k \to \infty} \tau_{k}$ ，从而 $\tau_{\infty} \leqslant \tau_{e}$ a.s.。若能证明 $\tau_{\infty} = \infty$ a.s.，则显然有 $\tau_{e} = \infty$ ，即 $(S(0), I(0), P(0)) \in \mathbb{R}_{+}^{3}(t \geqslant 0)$ a.s.。换言之，完成定理的证明只需证明 $\tau_{\infty} = \infty$ a.s.。若不然，存在 $T > 0$ 和 $\varepsilon \in (0,1)$ 使得

$$
P \{\tau_ {\infty} \leqslant T \} > \varepsilon .
$$

于是存在 $k_{1} \geqslant k_{0}$ 使得

$$
P \{\tau_ {k} \leqslant T \} \geqslant \varepsilon , \quad \text {对所有} k \geqslant k _ {1}.
$$

定义 $C^{2}$ -函数 $V: R_{+}^{3} \rightarrow \overline{R}_{+}$ 

$$
V (S, I, P) = S - 1 - \log S + I - 1 - \log I + \frac {1}{\theta} (P - 1 - \log P).
$$

由于 $a - b - b\log \frac{a}{b}\geqslant 0(b > 0),\forall a > 0,$ 所以此函数是非负定的.由Itô公式可得

$$
\begin{array}{l} \mathrm{d} V = \left[ (S - 1) \left(r \left(1 - \frac {S + I}{K}\right) - \lambda I - \frac {\alpha P}{a + S}\right) + \frac {\sigma^ {2} I ^ {2}}{2} \right] \mathrm{d} t - \sigma I (S - 1) \mathrm{d} B (t) \\ + \left[ (I - 1) (\lambda S - \beta P - \mu) + \frac {\sigma^ {2} S ^ {2}}{2} \right] \mathrm{d} t + \sigma S (I - 1) \mathrm{d} B (t) \\ \end{array}
$$

$$
+ \frac {1}{\theta} (P - 1) \left(- \theta \beta I - \delta + \frac {\theta \alpha S}{a + S}\right) d t
$$

$$
:= L V \mathrm{d} t + \sigma (I - S) \mathrm{d} B (t),
$$

其中

$$
\begin{array}{l} L V = \mu + \frac {\delta}{\theta} - r + \left(r + \frac {r}{K} - \lambda\right) S + \left(\frac {r}{K} + \lambda + \beta - \mu\right) I + \left(\beta + \frac {\alpha}{a + S} - \frac {\delta}{\theta}\right) P \\ - \frac {r}{K} S ^ {2} - \frac {r}{K} I S - 2 \beta I P - \frac {\alpha S}{a + S} + \frac {\sigma^ {2}}{2} (S ^ {2} + I ^ {2}) \\ \leqslant \mu + \frac {\delta}{\theta} + \left(r + \frac {r}{K}\right) S + \left(\frac {r}{K} + \lambda + \beta\right) I + \left(\beta + \frac {\alpha}{a}\right) P + \frac {\sigma^ {2}}{2} \left(S ^ {2} + I ^ {2}\right) \\ \leqslant \mu + \frac {\delta}{\theta} + \max \left\{r + \frac {r}{K}, \frac {r}{K} + \lambda + \beta , \theta \left(\beta + \frac {\alpha}{a}\right) \right\} \frac {B}{\eta} + K ^ {2} \sigma^ {2}. \\ \end{array}
$$

用 Li 和 Mao 在文献 [34] 中的定理 2.1 类似的证明方法, 可以证明结果成立. □ 注 2.2 由定理 2.7 可知

$$
\Gamma = \left\{(S, I, P) \in \mathbb {R} _ {+} ^ {3}: S + I \leqslant K, S + I + \frac {1}{\theta} P \leqslant \frac {B}{\eta} \right\}
$$

是系统 (2.4) 的一个正不变集, 其中 $B$ 和 $\eta$ 如定理 2.7 的证明中所定义. 从现在开始, 我们总是假定初值在 $(S(0), I(0), P(0)) \in \Gamma$ 中.

## 2.3.2 平衡点 $E_0 = (0,0,0)$ 的随机不稳定性

对于系统 (2.2), 平衡点 $E_0$ 不稳定. 显然 $E_0$ 仍然是系统 (2.4) 的平衡点. 下面我们证明系统 (2.4) 的平衡点 $E_0$ 是随机不稳定的.

定理 2.8 设 $(S(t), I(t), P(t))$ 是系统 (2.4) 满足初值 $(S(0), I(0), P(0)) \in \Gamma$ 的解，则系统 (2.4) 的平衡点 $E_{0} = (0, 0, 0)$ 是随机不稳定的.

证明 若不然, 则一定存在 $\Omega_0$ 和 $T_0 > 0$ , 使得对 $t \geqslant T_0, \omega \in \Omega_0$ , 有 $P\{\Omega_0\} > 0$ 和 $S(t) \leqslant \frac{K}{4}, I(t) \leqslant \frac{Kr}{2(2r + 2K\lambda + K^2\sigma^2)}, P(t) \leqslant \frac{ar}{4\alpha}$ . 因此

$$
\begin{array}{l} \mathrm{d} \log S = \left[ r - \frac {r}{K} S - \left(\frac {r}{K} + \lambda\right) I - \frac {\alpha P}{a + S} - \frac {\sigma^ {2}}{2} I ^ {2} \right] \mathrm{d} t - \sigma I \mathrm{d} B (t) \\ \geqslant \left[ r - \frac {r}{K} S - \left(\frac {r}{K} + \lambda + \frac {K \sigma^ {2}}{2}\right) I - \frac {\alpha P}{a} \right] d t - \sigma I d B (t) \\ \geqslant \frac {r}{4} \mathrm{d} t - \sigma I \mathrm{d} B (t), \\ \end{array}
$$

于是有

$$
\log S (t) - \log S (T _ {0}) \geqslant \frac {r}{4} (t - T _ {0}) - \sigma \int_ {T _ {0}} ^ {t} I (s) \mathrm{d} B (s).
$$

设 $M(t) = \int_{T_0}^{t}I(s)\mathrm{d}B(s)$ 是一个实值连续局部鞅， $M(T_0) = 0$ ，且有

$$
\limsup _ {t \to \infty} \frac {\langle M , M \rangle_ {t}}{t} = \limsup _ {t \to \infty} \frac {\int_ {T _ {0}} ^ {t} I ^ {2} (s) \mathrm{d} s}{t} \leqslant K ^ {2} <   \infty \text { a.s., }
$$

由强大数定律得

$$
\lim _ {t \to \infty} \frac {M (t)}{t} = \lim _ {t \to \infty} \frac {\int_ {T _ {0}} ^ {t} I (s) \mathrm{d} B (s)}{t} = 0 \text { a.s.},
$$

因此

$$
\liminf _ {t \to \infty} \frac {\log S (t)}{t} \geqslant \frac {r}{4},
$$

从而得到矛盾.

![image](https://cdn-mineru.openxlab.org.cn/result/2026-06-15/d22a62fd-b150-48dd-92fc-0b0e864b3dd2/470546cc8737daa51a5ab6f1bb6f8b715ff46feca028003078032ce47ae21d4c.jpg)


## 2.3.3 平衡点 $E_{1} = (K,0,0)$ 的全局渐近稳定性

对于系统 (2.2), 当 $\lambda < \frac{\mu}{K}$ 且 $\alpha < \frac{\delta(a + K)}{K\theta}$ 时, 平衡点 $E_{1}$ 全局渐近稳定. 显然 $E_{1}$ 仍然是系统 (2.4) 的平衡点. 下面我们首先证明在一定条件下系统 (2.4) 是大范围随机渐近稳定的, 接着给出解的收敛率.

定理2.9 设 $(S(t), I(t), P(t))$ 是系统(2.4)满足初值 $(S(0), I(0), P(0)) \in \Gamma$ 的解, 当 $K\lambda < \mu - \frac{\lambda K^3\sigma^2}{2(r + K\lambda)}$ 且 $\alpha < \frac{\delta(a + K)}{K\theta}$ 时, 系统(2.4)的平衡点 $E_1 = (K, 0, 0)$ 是大范围随机渐近稳定的.

证明 定义 $C^2$ 函数 $V: \mathbb{R}_+^3 \to \overline{\mathbb{R}}_+$ ,

$$
V (S, I, P) = S - K - K \log \frac {S}{K} + \frac {\frac {r}{K} + \lambda}{\lambda} I + \frac {1}{\theta} P.
$$

设 $L$ 是系统(2.4)的生成算子，则

$$
\begin{array}{l} L V = (S - K) \left[ r \left(1 - \frac {S + I}{K}\right) - \lambda I - \frac {\alpha P}{a + S} \right] + \frac {K \sigma^ {2}}{2} I ^ {2} + \frac {\frac {r}{K} + \lambda}{\lambda} (\lambda S I - \beta I P - \mu I) \\ + \frac {1}{\theta} \left(- \theta \beta I P - \delta P + \frac {\theta \alpha S P}{a + S}\right) \\ = - \frac {r}{K} (S - K) ^ {2} + \left[ K \left(\frac {r}{K} + \lambda\right) - \frac {\frac {r}{K} + \lambda}{\lambda} \mu \right] I - \left(\frac {\frac {r}{K} + \lambda}{\lambda} + 1\right) \beta I P - \frac {\delta}{\theta} P \\ + \frac {K \sigma^ {2}}{2} I ^ {2} + \frac {\alpha S P}{a + S} \\ \end{array}
$$

$$
\begin{array}{l} = - \frac {r}{K} (S - K) ^ {2} + \left[ K \left(\frac {r}{K} + \lambda\right) - \frac {\frac {r}{K} + \lambda}{\lambda} \mu + \frac {K \sigma^ {2}}{2} I \right] I \\ + \left[ \frac {\alpha S}{a + S} - \frac {\delta}{\theta} - \left(\frac {\frac {r}{K} + \lambda}{\lambda} + 1\right) \beta I \right] P \\ \leqslant - \frac {r}{K} (S - K) ^ {2} + \left[ K \left(\frac {r}{K} + \lambda\right) - \frac {\frac {r}{K} + \lambda}{\lambda} \mu + \frac {K ^ {2} \sigma^ {2}}{2} \right] I + \left(\frac {\alpha K}{a + K} - \frac {\delta}{\theta}\right) P, \\ \end{array}
$$

由此函数的非负定性有 $K\left(\frac{r}{K} + \lambda\right) - \frac{\frac{r}{K} + \lambda}{\lambda}\mu + \frac{K^2\sigma^2}{2} < 0$ 且 $\frac{\alpha K}{a + K} - \frac{\delta}{\theta} < 0$ ，也就是说， $K\lambda - \mu + \frac{\lambda K^3\sigma^2}{2(r + K\lambda)} < 0$ 且 $\alpha < \frac{\delta(a + K)}{K\theta}$ . 因此，由定理1.6 (Mao[11])，系统(2.4)的平衡点 $E_1 = (K, 0, 0)$ 是大范围随机渐近稳定的。

下面我们计算 $I(t)$ , $P(t)$ 和 $S(t)$ 的收敛率.

定理2.10 设 $(S(t), I(t), P(t))$ 是系统(2.4)满足初值 $(S(0), I(0), P(0)) \in \Gamma$ 的解. 假设

(a) $\sigma^{2} > \max\left\{\frac{\lambda}{K}, \frac{\lambda^{2}}{2\mu}\right\}$ , 

或 (b) $\max \left\{0, \frac{2(\lambda K - \mu)}{K^2}\right\} < \sigma^2 \leqslant \frac{\lambda}{K},$ 

(c) $\alpha < \frac{\delta(a + K)}{K\theta}$ , 

则

$\limsup_{t\to \infty}\frac{\log I(t)}{t}\leqslant \frac{\lambda^2}{2\sigma^2} -\mu < 0$ a.s., 若 (a) 成立;

$\limsup_{t \to \infty} \frac{\log I(t)}{t} \leqslant \lambda K - \mu - \frac{\sigma^2 K^2}{2} < 0$ a.s., 若 (b) 成立;

$\limsup_{t\to \infty}\frac{\log P(t)}{t}\leqslant -\left(\delta -\frac{\theta\alpha K}{a + K}\right) <   0$ a.s., 若 (c) 成立,

并且

$$
\lim _ {t \rightarrow \infty} \frac {1}{t} \int_ {0} ^ {t} S (s) \mathrm{d} s = K \text { a   .   s   . }.
$$

证明 由 Itô 公式, 可得

$$
\mathrm{d} \log I = \left(\lambda S - \beta P - \mu - \frac {\sigma^ {2}}{2} S ^ {2}\right) \mathrm{d} t + \sigma S \mathrm{d} B (t) \leqslant \left(\lambda S - \mu - \frac {\sigma^ {2}}{2} S ^ {2}\right) \mathrm{d} t + \sigma S \mathrm{d} B (t).
$$

令

$$
f (S) := \lambda S - \mu - \frac {\sigma^ {2}}{2} S ^ {2}, \quad s \in (0, K ].
$$

下面分两种情形讨论.

(i) 当 $\frac{\lambda}{\sigma^2} < K$ 时, 有

$$
f (S) \leqslant f \left(\frac {\lambda}{\sigma^ {2}}\right) = \frac {\lambda^ {2}}{2 \sigma^ {2}} - \mu ,
$$

则

$$
\mathrm{d} \log I \leqslant \left(\frac {\lambda^ {2}}{2 \sigma^ {2}} - \mu\right) \mathrm{d} t + \sigma S \mathrm{d} B (t)
$$

和

$$
\frac {\log I (t)}{t} \leqslant \frac {\log I (0)}{t} + \left(\frac {\lambda^ {2}}{2 \sigma^ {2}} - \mu\right) + \frac {\sigma}{t} \int_ {0} ^ {t} S (x) \mathrm{d} B (x). \tag {2.16}
$$

设 $M_{1}(t) = \int_{0}^{t}S(x)\mathrm{d}B(x)$ 是一个实值连续局部鞅， $M_{1}(0) = 0$ ，且有

$$
\limsup _ {t \to \infty} \frac {\langle M _ {1} , M _ {1} \rangle_ {t}}{t} = \limsup _ {t \to \infty} \frac {\int_ {0} ^ {t} S ^ {2} (x) \mathrm{d} x}{t} \leqslant K ^ {2} <   \infty \text {   a.s. },
$$

由强大数定律得

$$
\lim _ {t \to \infty} \frac {M _ {1} (t)}{t} = \lim _ {t \to \infty} \frac {\int_ {0} ^ {t} S (x) \mathrm{d} B (x)}{t} = 0 \text { a.s. }.
$$

再由式 (2.16) 得到

$$
\lim _ {t \to \infty} \sup \frac {\log I (t)}{t} \leqslant \frac {\lambda^ {2}}{2 \sigma^ {2}} - \mu \text { a.s. }
$$

由条件 (a), 易知

$$
\lim _ {t \to \infty} \sup \frac {\log I (t)}{t} \leqslant \frac {\lambda^ {2}}{2 \sigma^ {2}} - \mu <   0 \quad \text { a.s. } \tag {2.17}
$$

也就是说, $I(t)$ 几乎必然以指数收敛于0.换言之,已感染者依概率1死亡.

(ii) 当 $\frac{\lambda}{\sigma^{2}} \geqslant K$ 时, 有

$$
f (S) \leqslant f (K) = \lambda K - \mu - \frac {\sigma^ {2} K ^ {2}}{2},
$$

则

$$
\mathrm{d} \log I \leqslant \left(\lambda K - \mu - \frac {\sigma^ {2} K ^ {2}}{2}\right) \mathrm{d} t + \sigma S \mathrm{d} B (t).
$$

类似于 (i) 的讨论, 可得

$$
\limsup _ {t \to \infty} \frac {\log I (t)}{t} \leqslant \lambda K - \mu - \frac {\sigma^ {2} K ^ {2}}{2} \quad \text { a.s. }
$$

利用条件 (b), 可以得到

$$
\limsup _ {t \to \infty} \frac {\log I (t)}{t} \leqslant \lambda K - \mu - \frac {\sigma^ {2} K ^ {2}}{2} <   0 \quad \text { a.s. } \tag {2.18}
$$

也就是说, $I(t)$ 几乎必然以指数收敛于0.换言之,已感染者依概率1死亡.

用相同的方法, 由 Itô 公式, 可得

$$
\begin{array}{l} \mathrm{d} P = \left(- \theta \beta I P - \delta P + \frac {\theta \alpha S P}{a + S}\right) \mathrm{d} t = P \left(- \theta \beta I - \delta + \frac {\theta \alpha S}{a + S}\right) \mathrm{d} t \\ \leqslant P \left(- \theta \beta I - \delta + \frac {\theta \alpha K}{a + K}\right) d t \leqslant - P \left(\delta - \frac {\theta \alpha K}{a + K}\right) d t, \\ \end{array}
$$

则

$$
\limsup _ {t \to \infty} \frac {\log P (t)}{t} \leqslant - \left(\delta - \frac {\theta \alpha K}{a + K}\right) \quad a. s..
$$

由条件 (c) 可得

$$
\limsup _ {t \to \infty} \frac {\log P (t)}{t} \leqslant - \left(\delta - \frac {\theta \alpha K}{a + K}\right) <   0 \quad \text { a.s. } \tag {2.19}
$$

也就是说, $P(t)$ 几乎必然以指数收敛于0.换言之,捕食者依概率1死亡.

由 Itô 公式有

$$
\mathrm{d} \log S = \left[ r - \frac {r}{K} S - \left(\frac {r}{K} + \lambda\right) I - \frac {\alpha P}{a + S} - \frac {\sigma^ {2}}{2} I ^ {2} \right] \mathrm{d} t - \sigma I \mathrm{d} B (t),
$$

则

$$
\begin{array}{l} \log S (t) - \log S (0) = r t - \frac {r}{K} \int_ {0} ^ {t} S (s) \mathrm{d} s - \left(\frac {r}{K} + \lambda\right) \int_ {0} ^ {t} I (s) \mathrm{d} s - \alpha \int_ {0} ^ {t} \frac {P (s)}{a + S (s)} \mathrm{d} s \\ - \frac {\sigma^ {2}}{2} \int_ {0} ^ {t} I ^ {2} (s) \mathrm{d} s - \sigma \int_ {0} ^ {t} I (s) \mathrm{d} B (s) \\ \end{array}
$$

和

$$
\begin{array}{l} \frac {r}{K} \frac {1}{t} \int_ {0} ^ {t} S (s) \mathrm{d} s = r - \frac {\log S (t) - \log S (0)}{t} - \left(\frac {r}{K} + \lambda\right) \frac {1}{t} \int_ {0} ^ {t} I (s) \mathrm{d} s - \frac {\alpha}{t} \int_ {0} ^ {t} \frac {P (s)}{a + S (s)} \mathrm{d} s \\ - \frac {\sigma^ {2}}{2} \frac {1}{t} \int_ {0} ^ {t} I ^ {2} (s) \mathrm{d} s - \sigma \frac {1}{t} \int_ {0} ^ {t} I (s) \mathrm{d} B (s) \\ \geqslant r - \frac {\log K - \log S (0)}{t} - \left(\frac {r}{K} + \lambda\right) \frac {1}{t} \int_ {0} ^ {t} I (s) d s - \frac {\alpha}{a t} \int_ {0} ^ {t} P (s) d s \\ - \frac {\sigma^ {2}}{2} \frac {1}{t} \int_ {0} ^ {t} I ^ {2} (s) \mathrm{d} s - \sigma \frac {1}{t} \int_ {0} ^ {t} I (s) \mathrm{d} B (s). \\ \end{array}
$$

再由式 (2.17)~(2.19)，可得

$$
\liminf _ {t \to \infty} \frac {1}{t} \int_ {0} ^ {t} S (s) \mathrm{d} s \geqslant K \quad \text { a.s. }
$$

由于

$$
K \leqslant \liminf _ {t \to \infty} \frac {1}{t} \int_ {0} ^ {t} S (s) \mathrm{d} s \leqslant \limsup _ {t \to \infty} \frac {1}{t} \int_ {0} ^ {t} S (s) \mathrm{d} s \leqslant K \text { a.s. },
$$

得到

$$
\lim _ {t \rightarrow \infty} \frac {1}{t} \int_ {0} ^ {t} S (s) \mathrm{d} s = K \text { a   .   s   . }.
$$

## 2.3.4 系统 (2.2) 的平衡点 $E_{2} = (\overline{S},\overline{I},0)$ 附近的渐近性行为

对于系统 (2.2), 当 $\lambda > \frac{\mu}{K}$ 时, 平衡点 $E_{2}$ 存在, 但是它不是系统 (2.4) 的平衡点. 下面我们首先计算 $P(t)$ 的收敛率, 接着证明在一定条件下系统 (2.4) 的解在 $E_{2}$ 附近.

定理2.11 设 $(S(t), I(t), P(t))$ 是系统(2.4)初值为 $(S(0), I(0), P(0)) \in \Gamma$ 的解，当 $\lambda K > \mu$ 且 $\alpha < \frac{\delta(a + K)}{K\theta}$ ，则

$$
\lim _ {t \to \infty} \sup \frac {\log P (t)}{t} \leqslant - \left(\delta - \frac {\theta \alpha K}{a + K}\right) <   0 \quad \mathrm{a.s.} \tag {2.20}
$$

和

$$
\limsup _ {t \to \infty} \frac {1}{t} \int_ {0} ^ {t} [ (S (s) - \overline {{S}}) ^ {2} + (I (s) - \overline {{I}}) ^ {2} ] \mathrm{d} s \leqslant \frac {\sigma^ {2} K ^ {2} \left(\overline {{S}} + \frac {\frac {r}{K} + \lambda}{\lambda} \overline {{I}} + \frac {\eta_ {1} r ^ {2}}{\lambda^ {2}}\right)}{2 m} \quad \text { a.s., }
$$

其中 $E_{2} = (\overline{S},\overline{I},0)$ 是系统(2.2）的边界平衡点， $m = \min \left\{\frac{\mu\eta_1}{2}\left(\frac{\frac{r}{K} + \lambda}{\lambda}\right)^2,\frac{r}{2K}\right\}$ 

和 $\eta_{1} = \frac{r}{2K}\left[r - \frac{r}{K}\overline{S} +\frac{r}{K}\frac{\frac{r}{K} + \lambda}{\lambda}\overline{I} +\frac{\left(r - \frac{2r}{K}\overline{S} -\mu\right)^{2}}{2\mu}\right]^{-1}.$ 

证明 由 Itô 公式得到对 t > 0,

$$
\begin{array}{l} \mathrm{d} P = \left(- \theta \beta I P - \delta P + \frac {\theta \alpha S P}{a + S}\right) \mathrm{d} t = P \left(- \theta \beta I - \delta + \frac {\theta \alpha S}{a + S}\right) \mathrm{d} t \\ \leqslant P \left(- \theta \beta I - \delta + \frac {\theta \alpha K}{a + K}\right) \mathrm{d} t \leqslant - P \left(\delta - \frac {\theta \alpha K}{a + K}\right) \mathrm{d} t, \\ \end{array}
$$

则

$$
\limsup _ {t \to \infty} \frac {\log P (t)}{t} \leqslant - \left(\delta - \frac {\theta \alpha K}{a + K}\right) \quad \text { a.s. }
$$

从条件 $\alpha < \frac{\delta(a + K)}{K\theta}$ 可以得到

$$
\lim _ {t \to \infty} \sup \frac {\log P (t)}{t} \leqslant - \left(\delta - \frac {\theta \alpha K}{a + K}\right) <   0 \text {   a.s..   }
$$

也就是说, $P(t)$ 几乎必然以指数收敛于0.换言之, 捕食者依概率1死亡. 我们可以推出 $\lim_{t\to \infty}P(t) = 0.$ 

因为 $(\overline{S},\overline{I},0)$ 是系统(2.4)的边界平衡点，所以

$$
r \left(1 - \frac {\overline {{{S}}} + \overline {{{I}}}}{K}\right) = \lambda \overline {{{I}}}, \quad \mu = \lambda \overline {{{S}}}.
$$

定义

$$
\begin{array}{l} V (S, I) = S - \overline {{{S}}} - \overline {{{S}}} \log \frac {S}{\overline {{{S}}}} + \frac {\frac {r}{K} + \lambda}{\lambda} \left(I - \overline {{{I}}} - \overline {{{I}}} \log \frac {I}{\overline {{{I}}}}\right) + \frac {\eta_ {1}}{2} \left[ S - \overline {{{S}}} + \frac {\frac {r}{K} + \lambda}{\lambda} (I - \overline {{{I}}}) \right] ^ {2} \\ := V _ {1} + \eta_ {1} V _ {2}, \\ \end{array}
$$

其中 $\eta_{1}$ 是在定理2.11中所定义的正常数.由Itô公式和式(2.4)，可以计算

$$
\begin{array}{l} \mathrm{d} V _ {1} = \left\{(S - \overline {{{S}}}) \left[ r \left(1 - \frac {S + I}{K}\right) - \lambda I - \frac {\alpha P}{a + S} \right] + \frac {\sigma^ {2} I ^ {2}}{2} \overline {{{S}}} \right\} \mathrm{d} t - \sigma (S - \overline {{{S}}}) I \mathrm{d} B (t) \\ + \frac {\frac {r}{K} + \lambda}{\lambda} \left\{\left[ (I - \overline {{I}}) (\lambda S - \beta P - \mu) + \frac {\sigma^ {2} S ^ {2}}{2} \overline {{I}} \right] \mathrm{d} t + \sigma (I - \overline {{I}}) S \mathrm{d} B (t) \right\} \\ := L V _ {1} \mathrm{d} t + \left[ \frac {\frac {r}{K} + \lambda}{\lambda} \sigma (I - \overline {{{I}}}) S - \sigma (S - \overline {{{S}}}) I \right] \mathrm{d} B (t), \\ \end{array}
$$

其中

$$
\begin{array}{l} L V _ {1} = (S - \overline {{{{S}}}}) \left[ r \left(1 - \frac {S + I}{K}\right) - \lambda I - \frac {\alpha P}{a + S} \right] + \frac {\sigma^ {2} I ^ {2}}{2} \overline {{{{S}}}} \\ + \frac {\frac {r}{K} + \lambda}{\lambda} \left[ (I - \bar {I}) (\lambda S - \beta P - \mu) + \frac {\sigma^ {2} S ^ {2}}{2} \bar {I} \right] \\ \end{array}
$$

$$
\begin{array}{l} = - \frac {r}{K} (S - \bar {S}) ^ {2} - \left(\frac {r}{K} + \lambda\right) (S - \bar {S}) (I - \bar {I}) - \frac {\alpha P}{a + S} (S - \bar {S}) + \frac {\sigma^ {2} I ^ {2}}{2} \bar {S} \\ + \frac {\frac {r}{K} + \lambda}{\lambda} \left[ \lambda (S - \bar {S}) (I - \bar {I}) - \beta P (I - \bar {I}) + \frac {\sigma^ {2} S ^ {2}}{2} \bar {I} \right] \\ = - \frac {r}{K} (S - \overline {{S}}) ^ {2} - \frac {\alpha P}{a + S} (S - \overline {{S}}) - \frac {\frac {r}{K} + \lambda}{\lambda} \beta P (I - \overline {{I}}) + \frac {\sigma^ {2} I ^ {2}}{2} \overline {{S}} + \frac {\frac {r}{K} + \lambda}{\lambda} \frac {\sigma^ {2} S ^ {2}}{2} \overline {{I}}. \\ \end{array}
$$

令 $Y = S - \overline{S} +\frac{\frac{r}{K} + \lambda}{\lambda}(I - \overline{I})$ ，则

$$
\mathrm{d} Y = \left(r S - \frac {r}{K} S ^ {2} - \frac {\alpha S P}{a + S} - \frac {\frac {r}{K} + \lambda}{\lambda} \beta I P - \frac {\frac {r}{K} + \lambda}{\lambda} \mu I\right) \mathrm{d} t + \frac {r \sigma}{K \lambda} S I \mathrm{d} B (t),
$$

$$
\mathrm{d} V _ {2} = \left[ S - \bar {S} + \frac {\frac {r}{K} + \lambda}{\lambda} (I - \bar {I}) \right] \left[ \left(r S - \frac {r}{K} S ^ {2} - \frac {\alpha S P}{a + S} - \frac {\frac {r}{K} + \lambda}{\lambda} \beta I P - \frac {\frac {r}{K} + \lambda}{\lambda} \mu I\right) \mathrm{d} t \right.
$$

$$
\left. + \frac {r \sigma}{K \lambda} S I \mathrm{d} B (t) \right] + \frac {r ^ {2} \sigma^ {2}}{2 K ^ {2} \lambda^ {2}} S ^ {2} I ^ {2} \mathrm{d} t
$$

$$
:= L V _ {2} \mathrm{d} t + \left[ S - \bar {S} + \frac {\frac {r}{K} + \lambda}{\lambda} (I - \bar {I}) \right] \frac {r \sigma}{K \lambda} S I \mathrm{d} B (t),
$$

其中

$$
L V _ {2} = \left[ S - \bar {S} + \frac {\frac {r}{K} + \lambda}{\lambda} (I - \bar {I}) \right] \left(r S - \frac {r}{K} S ^ {2} - \frac {\alpha S P}{a + S} - \frac {\frac {r}{K} + \lambda}{\lambda} \beta I P - \frac {\frac {r}{K} + \lambda}{\lambda} \mu I\right)
$$

$$
+ \frac {r ^ {2} \sigma^ {2}}{2 K ^ {2} \lambda^ {2}} S ^ {2} I ^ {2}
$$

$$
= \left[ S - \bar {S} + \frac {\frac {r}{K} + \lambda}{\lambda} (I - \bar {I}) \right] \left[ - \frac {r}{K} (S - \bar {S}) ^ {2} + \left(r - \frac {2 r}{K} \bar {S}\right) (S - \bar {S}) - \frac {\alpha S P}{a + S} \right.
$$

$$
\left. - \frac {\frac {r}{K} + \lambda}{\lambda} \beta P (I - \bar {I}) - \frac {\frac {r}{K} + \lambda}{\lambda} \mu (I - \bar {I}) - \frac {\frac {r}{K} + \lambda}{\lambda} \beta \bar {I} P \right] + \frac {r ^ {2} \sigma^ {2}}{2 K ^ {2} \lambda^ {2}} S ^ {2} I ^ {2}
$$

$$
= - \frac {r}{K} S (S - \bar {S}) ^ {2} + \left(r - \frac {r}{K} \bar {S} + \frac {r}{K} \frac {\frac {r}{K} + \lambda}{\lambda} \bar {I}\right) (S - \bar {S}) ^ {2} - \frac {\frac {r}{K} + \lambda}{\lambda} \beta P (S - \bar {S}) (I - \bar {I})
$$

$$
- \frac {\alpha S P}{a + S} (S - \bar {S}) - \frac {\frac {r}{K} + \lambda}{\lambda} \beta \bar {I} P (S - \bar {S}) + \frac {\frac {r}{K} + \lambda}{\lambda} \left(r - \frac {2 r}{K} \bar {S} - \mu\right) (S - \bar {S}) (I - \bar {I})
$$

$$
\begin{array}{l} - \frac {r}{K} \frac {\frac {r}{K} + \lambda}{\lambda} I (S - \bar {S}) ^ {2} - \frac {\frac {r}{K} + \lambda}{\lambda} \frac {\alpha S}{a + S} P (I - \bar {I}) - \left(\frac {\frac {r}{K} + \lambda}{\lambda}\right) ^ {2} \beta P (I - \bar {I}) ^ {2} \\ - \left(\frac {\frac {r}{K} + \lambda}{\lambda}\right) ^ {2} \beta \bar {I} P (I - \bar {I}) - \mu \left(\frac {\frac {r}{K} + \lambda}{\lambda}\right) ^ {2} (I - \bar {I}) ^ {2} + \frac {r ^ {2} \sigma^ {2}}{2 K ^ {2} \lambda^ {2}} S ^ {2} I ^ {2} \\ \leqslant \left(r - \frac {r}{K} \overline {{S}} + \frac {r}{K} \frac {\frac {r}{K} + \lambda}{\lambda} \overline {{I}}\right) (S - \overline {{S}}) ^ {2} + \frac {\frac {r}{K} + \lambda}{\lambda} \left(r - \frac {2 r}{K} \overline {{S}} - \mu\right) (S - \overline {{S}}) (I - \overline {{I}}) \\ - \mu \left(\frac {\frac {r}{K} + \lambda}{\lambda}\right) ^ {2} (I - \bar {I}) ^ {2} - \frac {\frac {r}{K} + \lambda}{\lambda} \beta P (S - \overline {{S}}) (I - \bar {I}) - \frac {\alpha S P}{a + S} (S - \overline {{S}}) \\ - \frac {\frac {r}{K} + \lambda}{\lambda} \beta \bar {I} P (S - \bar {S}) - \frac {\frac {r}{K} + \lambda}{\lambda} \frac {\alpha S}{a + S} P (I - \bar {I}) \\ - \left(\frac {\frac {r}{K} + \lambda}{\lambda}\right) ^ {2} \beta \overline {{I}} P (I - \overline {{I}}) + \frac {r ^ {2} \sigma^ {2}}{2 K ^ {2} \lambda^ {2}} S ^ {2} I ^ {2}. \\ \end{array}
$$

利用柯西不等式, 可以得到

$$
\begin{array}{l} L V _ {2} \leqslant \left[ r - \frac {r}{K} \overline {{S}} + \frac {r}{K} \frac {\frac {r}{K} + \lambda}{\lambda} \overline {{I}} + \frac {\left(r - \frac {2 r}{K} \overline {{S}} - \mu\right) ^ {2}}{2 \mu} \right] (S - \overline {{S}}) ^ {2} - \frac {\mu}{2} \left(\frac {\frac {r}{K} + \lambda}{\lambda}\right) ^ {2} (I - \overline {{I}}) ^ {2} \\ - \frac {\frac {r}{K} + \lambda}{\lambda} \beta P (S - \overline {{S}}) (I - \overline {{I}}) - \frac {\alpha S P}{a + S} (S - \overline {{S}}) - \frac {\frac {r}{K} + \lambda}{\lambda} \beta \overline {{I}} P (S - \overline {{S}}) \\ - \frac {\frac {r}{K} + \lambda}{\lambda} \frac {\alpha S}{a + S} P (I - \overline {{I}}) - \left(\frac {\frac {r}{K} + \lambda}{\lambda}\right) ^ {2} \beta \overline {{I}} P (I - \overline {{I}}) + \frac {r ^ {2} \sigma^ {2}}{2 K ^ {2} \lambda^ {2}} S ^ {2} I ^ {2}. \\ \end{array}
$$

选择 $\eta_{1} = \frac{r}{2K}\left[r - \frac{r}{K}\overline{S} +\frac{r}{K}\frac{\frac{r}{K} + \lambda}{\lambda}\overline{I} +\frac{\left(r - \frac{2r}{K}\overline{S} -\mu\right)^{2}}{2\mu}\right]^{-1}$ ，可以得到

$$
L V = L V _ {1} + \eta_ {1} L V _ {2}
$$

$$
\leqslant - \frac {r}{2 K} (S - \overline {{S}}) ^ {2} - \frac {\mu \eta_ {1}}{2} \left(\frac {\frac {r}{K} + \lambda}{\lambda}\right) ^ {2} (I - \overline {{I}}) ^ {2} - \frac {\alpha P}{a + S} (S - \overline {{S}}) - \frac {\frac {r}{K} + \lambda}{\lambda} \beta P (I - \overline {{I}})
$$

$$
\begin{array}{l} + \eta_ {1} \left[ - \frac {\frac {r}{K} + \lambda}{\lambda} \beta P (S - \bar {S}) (I - \bar {I}) - \frac {\alpha S P}{a + S} (S - \bar {S}) - \frac {\frac {r}{K} + \lambda}{\lambda} \beta \bar {I} P (S - \bar {S}) \right. \\ \left. - \frac {\frac {r}{K} + \lambda}{\lambda} \frac {\alpha S}{a + S} P (I - \bar {I}) - \left(\frac {\frac {r}{K} + \lambda}{\lambda}\right) ^ {2} \beta \bar {I} P (I - \bar {I}) \right] + \frac {\sigma^ {2} I ^ {2}}{2} \bar {S} + \frac {\frac {r}{K} + \lambda}{\lambda} \frac {\sigma^ {2} S ^ {2}}{2} \bar {I} \\ + \frac {\eta_ {1} r ^ {2} \sigma^ {2}}{2 K ^ {2} \lambda^ {2}} S ^ {2} I ^ {2}. \\ \end{array}
$$

注意到

$$
\begin{array}{l} \mathrm{d} V = L V \mathrm{d} t + \frac {\frac {r}{K} + \lambda}{\lambda} \sigma (I - \bar {I}) S \mathrm{d} B (t) - \sigma (S - \bar {S}) I \mathrm{d} B (t) \\ + \frac {\eta_ {1} r}{K \lambda} \sigma S I \left[ S - \bar {S} + \frac {\frac {r}{K} + \lambda}{\lambda} (I - \bar {I}) \right] d B (t) \\ = L V \mathrm{d} t + \left[ \frac {\frac {r}{K} + \lambda}{\lambda} \sigma S (I - \bar {I}) - \sigma I (S - \bar {S}) + \frac {\eta_ {1} r}{K \lambda} \sigma S I (S - \bar {S}) \right. \\ \left. + \frac {\frac {r}{K} + \lambda}{\lambda} \frac {\eta_ {1} r}{K \lambda} \sigma S I (I - \bar {I}) \right] d B (t), \\ \end{array}
$$

两边从 0 到 t 积分, 得到

$$
\begin{array}{l} V (t) - V (0) \\ \leqslant - \frac {r}{2 K} \int_ {0} ^ {t} (S (s) - \bar {S}) ^ {2} \mathrm{d} s - \frac {\mu \eta_ {1}}{2} \left(\frac {\frac {r}{K} + \lambda}{\lambda}\right) ^ {2} \int_ {0} ^ {t} (I (s) - \bar {I}) ^ {2} \mathrm{d} s \\ - \int_ {0} ^ {t} \frac {\alpha P (s)}{a + S (s)} (S (s) - \overline {{S}}) d s \\ - \int_ {0} ^ {t} \frac {\frac {r}{K} + \lambda}{\lambda} \beta P (s) (I (s) - \bar {I}) d s \\ + \eta_ {1} \left[ - \int_ {0} ^ {t} \frac {\frac {r}{K} + \lambda}{\lambda} \beta P (s) (S (s) - \bar {S}) (I (s) - \bar {I}) d s \right. \\ - \int_ {0} ^ {t} \frac {\alpha S (s) P (s)}{a + S (s)} (S (s) - \bar {S}) d s - \int_ {0} ^ {t} \frac {\frac {r}{K} + \lambda}{\lambda} \beta \bar {I} P (s) (S (s) - \bar {S}) d s \\ \end{array}
$$

$$
\begin{array}{l} - \int_ {0} ^ {t} \frac {\frac {r}{K} + \lambda}{\lambda} \frac {\alpha S (s)}{a + S (s)} P (s) (I (s) - \bar {I}) d s \\ \left. - \int_ {0} ^ {t} \left(\frac {\frac {r}{K} + \lambda}{\lambda}\right) ^ {2} \beta \bar {I} P (s) (I (s) - \bar {I}) d s \right] \\ + \frac {\sigma^ {2} K ^ {2}}{2} \left(\bar {S} + \frac {\frac {r}{K} + \lambda}{\lambda} \bar {I} + \frac {\eta_ {1} r ^ {2}}{\lambda^ {2}}\right) t \\ + \int_ {0} ^ {t} \sigma \left[ \frac {\frac {r}{K} + \lambda}{\lambda} S (s) (I (s) - \bar {I} (s)) - I (s) (S (s) - \bar {S} (s)) \right. \\ \left. + \frac {\eta_ {1} r}{K \lambda} S (s) I (s) (S (s) - \overline {{{S}}} (s)) + \frac {\frac {r}{K} + \lambda}{\lambda} \frac {\eta_ {1} r}{K \lambda} S (s) I (s) (I (s) - \overline {{{I}}} (s)) \right] \mathrm{d} B (s). \tag {2.21} \\ \end{array}
$$

设

$$
\begin{array}{l} f (t) = - \int_ {0} ^ {t} \frac {\alpha P (s)}{a + S (s)} (S (s) - \bar {S}) \mathrm{d} s - \int_ {0} ^ {t} \frac {\frac {r}{K} + \lambda}{\lambda} \beta P (s) (I (s) - \bar {I}) \mathrm{d} s \\ + \eta_ {1} \left[ - \int_ {0} ^ {t} \frac {\frac {r}{K} + \lambda}{\lambda} \beta P (s) (S (s) - \overline {{S}}) (I (s) - \overline {{I}}) d s - \int_ {0} ^ {t} \frac {\alpha S (s) P (s)}{a + S (s)} (S (s) - \overline {{S}}) d s \right. \\ - \int_ {0} ^ {t} \frac {\frac {r}{K} + \lambda}{\lambda} \beta \bar {I} P (s) (S (s) - \bar {S}) d s - \int_ {0} ^ {t} \frac {\frac {r}{K} + \lambda}{\lambda} \frac {\alpha S (s)}{a + S (s)} P (s) (I (s) - \bar {I}) d s \\ \left. - \int_ {0} ^ {t} \left(\frac {\frac {r}{K} + \lambda}{\lambda}\right) ^ {2} \beta \bar {I} P (s) (I (s) - \bar {I}) \mathrm{d} s \right], \\ \end{array}
$$

由 $S(t), I(t)$ 的有界性和式 (2.20) 可知

$$
\limsup _ {t \to \infty} \frac {f (t)}{t} = 0 \text { a.s. }
$$

设

$$
\begin{array}{l} M _ {2} (t) = \int_ {0} ^ {t} \sigma \left[ \frac {\frac {r}{K} + \lambda}{\lambda} S (s) (I (s) - \bar {I} (s)) - I (s) (S (s) - \bar {S} (s)) + \frac {\eta_ {1} r}{K \lambda} S (s) I (s) (S (s) - \bar {S} (s)) \right. \\ \left. + \frac {\frac {r}{K} + \lambda}{\lambda} \frac {\eta_ {1} r}{K \lambda} S (s) I (s) (I (s) - \bar {I} (s)) \right] d B (s) \\ \end{array}
$$

是一个实值连续局部鞅, $M_{2}(0)=0$ , 且有

$$
\begin{array}{l} \operatorname * {l i m s u p} _ {t \to \infty} \frac {\langle M _ {2} , M _ {2} \rangle_ {t}}{t} \\ = \limsup _ {t \to \infty} \frac {1}{t} \int_ {0} ^ {t} \sigma^ {2} \left[ \frac {\frac {r}{K} + \lambda}{\lambda} S (s) (I (s) - \overline {{I}}) - I (s) (S (s) - \overline {{S}}) + \frac {r \eta_ {1}}{K \lambda} S (s) I (s) (S (s) - \overline {{S}}) \right. \\ \left. + \frac {r \eta_ {1}}{K \lambda} \frac {\frac {r}{K} + \lambda}{\lambda} S (s) I (s) (I (s) - \bar {I}) \right] ^ {2} d s \\ \leqslant 1 6 K ^ {4} \sigma^ {2} \left[ \left(\frac {\frac {r}{K} + \lambda}{\lambda}\right) ^ {2} + 1 + \frac {r ^ {2} \eta_ {1} ^ {2}}{\lambda^ {2}} \left(1 + \left(\frac {\frac {r}{K} + \lambda}{\lambda}\right) ^ {2}\right) \right] <   \infty \text {a.s.}, \\ \end{array}
$$

由强大数定律得

$$
\lim _ {t \rightarrow \infty} \frac {M _ {2} (t)}{t} = 0 \text { a   .   s   . },
$$

再由式 (2.21) 可知

$$
\begin{array}{l} \limsup _ {t \to \infty} \frac {1}{t} \int_ {0} ^ {t} \left[ \frac {r}{2 K} (S (s) - \overline {{S}}) ^ {2} + \frac {\mu \eta_ {1}}{2} \left(\frac {\frac {r}{K} + \lambda}{\lambda}\right) ^ {2} (I (s) - \overline {{I}}) ^ {2} \right] \mathrm{d} s \\ \leqslant \frac {\sigma^ {2} K ^ {2} \left(\bar {S} + \frac {\frac {r}{K} + \lambda}{\lambda} \bar {I} + \frac {\eta_ {1} r ^ {2}}{\lambda^ {2}}\right)}{2} \text { a   .   s   . }. \\ \end{array}
$$

这样得到

$$
\limsup _ {t \to \infty} \frac {1}{t} \int_ {0} ^ {t} [ (S (s) - \overline {{S}}) ^ {2} + (I (s) - \overline {{I}}) ^ {2} ] \mathrm{d} s \leqslant \frac {\sigma^ {2} K ^ {2} \left(\overline {{S}} + \frac {\frac {r}{K} + \lambda}{\lambda} \overline {{I}} + \frac {\eta_ {1} r ^ {2}}{\lambda^ {2}}\right)}{2 m} \quad \text { a.s. },
$$

其中 $m = \min \left\{\frac{r}{2K}, \frac{\mu \eta_1}{2} \left(\frac{\frac{r}{K} + \lambda}{\lambda}\right)^2\right\}$ .

## 2.3.5 平衡点 $E_{3} = (\widetilde{S},0,\widetilde{P})$ 的随机渐近稳定性

因为 $(\widetilde{S},0,\widetilde{P})$ 是系统(2.4)的边界平衡点，所以

$$
r \left(1 - \frac {\widetilde {S}}{K}\right) = \frac {\alpha \widetilde {P}}{a + \widetilde {S}}, \quad \delta = \frac {\theta \alpha \widetilde {S}}{a + \widetilde {S}}.
$$

通过变量代换

$$
u = S - \widetilde {S}, \quad w = P - \widetilde {P},
$$

随机系统 (2.4) 在 $E_{3} = (\widetilde{S}, 0, \widetilde{P})$ 处中心化.

利用泰勒公式, 可以得到如下系统

$$
\left\{ \begin{array}{l} \mathrm{d} u = \left\{\left(r - \frac {2 r}{K} \widetilde {S} - \frac {\alpha a \widetilde {P}}{(a + \widetilde {S}) ^ {2}}\right) u - \left(\frac {r}{K} + \lambda\right) \widetilde {S} I \right. \\ \left. - \frac {\alpha \widetilde {S}}{a + \widetilde {S}} w - \frac {r}{K} u ^ {2} - \left(\frac {r}{K} + \lambda\right) u I \right. \\ \left. - \frac {\alpha}{a + \widetilde {S}} u w - \frac {\alpha}{(a + \widetilde {S}) ^ {2}} [ (a + \widetilde {S}) u w \right. \\ \left. + a \widetilde {P} u + \widetilde {S} (a + \widetilde {S}) w ] \sum_ {n = 1} ^ {\infty} (- 1) ^ {n} \left(\frac {u}{a + \widetilde {S}}\right) ^ {n} \right\} \mathrm{d} t \\ - (\sigma u I + \sigma \widetilde {S} I) \mathrm{d} B (t), \\ \mathrm{d} I = [ (\lambda \widetilde {S} - \beta \widetilde {P} - \mu) I + \lambda u I - \beta I w ] \mathrm{d} t + (\sigma u I + \sigma \widetilde {S} I) \mathrm{d} B (t), \\ \mathrm{d} w = \left\{- \theta \beta I \widetilde {P} + \frac {\theta \alpha a \widetilde {P}}{(a + \widetilde {S}) ^ {2}} u + \frac {\theta \alpha}{a + \widetilde {S}} u w - \theta \beta I w \right. \\ + \frac {\theta \alpha}{(a + \widetilde {S}) ^ {2}} [ (a + \widetilde {S}) u w + a \widetilde {P} u \\ + \widetilde {S} (a + \widetilde {S}) w ] \sum_ {n = 1} ^ {\infty} (- 1) ^ {n} \left(\frac {u}{a + \widetilde {S}}\right) ^ {n} \Bigg \} \mathrm{d} t, \end{array} \right. (2. 2 2)
$$

这里 $|S - \widetilde{S}| < |a + \widetilde{S}|$ .

容易看出系统 (2.4) 平衡点的稳定性等价于系统 (2.22) 零解的稳定性.

下面我们将得到系统 (2.22) 零解的稳定性的充分条件. 首先, 考虑系统 (2.22)

的线性部分

$$
\left\{ \begin{array}{l} \mathrm{d} z _ {1} = \left[ \left(r - \frac {2 r}{K} \widetilde {S} - \frac {\alpha a \widetilde {P}}{(a + \widetilde {S}) ^ {2}}\right) z _ {1} - \left(\frac {r}{K} + \lambda\right) \widetilde {S} z _ {2} - \frac {\alpha \widetilde {S}}{a + \widetilde {S}} z _ {3} \right] \mathrm{d} t \\ - \sigma \widetilde {S} z _ {2} \mathrm{d} B (t), \\ \mathrm{d} z _ {2} = (\lambda \widetilde {S} - \beta \widetilde {P} - \mu) z _ {2} \mathrm{d} t + \sigma \widetilde {S} z _ {2} \mathrm{d} B (t), \\ \mathrm{d} z _ {3} = \left(- \theta \beta \widetilde {P} z _ {2} + \frac {\theta \alpha a \widetilde {P}}{(a + \widetilde {S}) ^ {2}} z _ {1}\right) \mathrm{d} t. \end{array} \right. \tag {2.23}
$$

定理2.12 当

$$
\frac {\delta (a \lambda + \mu)}{\mu \theta} <   \alpha <   \frac {\delta (K + a)}{\theta (K - a)}, \quad \lambda > \frac {\mu}{K}, \quad \sigma^ {2} <   \frac {2 (\beta \widetilde {P} + \mu - \lambda \widetilde {S})}{\widetilde {S} ^ {2}},
$$

系统 $(2.23)$ 的零解是随机渐近稳定的.

证明 定义 Lyapunov 函数 $V(x)$ 如下:

$$
V (x) = \eta_ {2} \frac {1}{2} \left(z _ {1} + z _ {2} + \frac {1}{\theta} z _ {3}\right) ^ {2} + \frac {1}{2} \left(z _ {1} + z _ {2}\right) ^ {2} + \eta_ {1} \frac {1}{2} z _ {3} ^ {2} + \eta_ {3} \frac {1}{2} z _ {2} ^ {2}
$$

$$
:= \eta_ {2} V _ {1} + V _ {2} + \eta_ {1} V _ {4} + \eta_ {3} V _ {3},
$$

其中 $\eta_{1},\eta_{2},\eta_{3}$ 都是由后面定义的正常数. 由 Itô 公式可得

$$
\mathrm{d} V _ {1} = \left(z _ {1} + z _ {2} + \frac {1}{\theta} z _ {3}\right) \left[ \left(r - \frac {2 r}{K} \widetilde {S}\right) z _ {1} - \left(\frac {r}{K} \widetilde {S} + 2 \beta \widetilde {P} + \mu\right) z _ {2} - \frac {\alpha \widetilde {S}}{a + \widetilde {S}} z _ {3} \right] \mathrm{d} t,
$$

其中

$$
\begin{array}{l} L V _ {1} = \left(r - \frac {2 r}{K} \widetilde {S}\right) z _ {1} ^ {2} - \left(\frac {r}{K} \widetilde {S} + 2 \beta \widetilde {P} + \mu\right) z _ {2} ^ {2} - \frac {\delta}{\theta^ {2}} z _ {3} ^ {2} + \left(r - \frac {3 r}{K} \widetilde {S} - 2 \beta \widetilde {P} - \mu\right) z _ {1} z _ {2} \\ + \frac {1}{\theta} \left(r - \frac {2 r}{K} \widetilde {S} - \delta\right) z _ {1} z _ {3} - \frac {1}{\theta} \left(\frac {r}{K} \widetilde {S} + 2 \beta \widetilde {P} + \mu + \delta\right) z _ {2} z _ {3}. \\ \end{array}
$$

由条件 $\alpha < \frac{\delta(K + a)}{\theta(K - a)}$ 可得 $r - \frac{2r}{K}\widetilde{S} < \frac{\alpha a\widetilde{P}}{(a + \widetilde{S})^2}$ . 并且, 由柯西不等式, 可以得到

$$
L V _ {1} \leqslant \frac {\alpha a \widetilde {P}}{(a + \widetilde {S}) ^ {2}} z _ {1} ^ {2} - \left(\frac {r}{K} \widetilde {S} + 2 \beta \widetilde {P} + \mu\right) z _ {2} ^ {2} - \frac {\delta}{\theta^ {2}} z _ {3} ^ {2} + \left(r - \frac {3 r}{K} \widetilde {S} - 2 \beta \widetilde {P} - \mu\right) z _ {1} z _ {2}
$$

$$
+ \frac {1}{\theta} \left(r - \frac {2 r}{K} \widetilde {S} - \delta\right) z _ {1} z _ {3} - \frac {1}{\theta} \left(\frac {r}{K} \widetilde {S} + 2 \beta \widetilde {P} + \mu + \delta\right) z _ {2} z _ {3}
$$

$$
\leqslant \left[ \frac {\alpha a \widetilde {P}}{(a + \widetilde {S}) ^ {2}} + \frac {1}{\delta} \left(r - \frac {2 r}{K} \widetilde {S} - \delta\right) ^ {2} + \frac {1}{4} \left(r - \frac {3 r}{K} \widetilde {S} - 2 \beta \widetilde {P} - \mu\right) ^ {2} \left(\frac {r}{K} \widetilde {S} + 2 \beta \widetilde {P} + \mu\right) ^ {- 1} \right]
$$

$$
z _ {1} ^ {2} + \frac {1}{\delta} \left(\frac {r}{K} \widetilde {S} + 2 \beta \widetilde {P} + \mu + \delta\right) ^ {2} z _ {2} ^ {2} - \frac {\delta}{2 \theta^ {2}} z _ {3} ^ {2}.
$$

$$
\mathrm{d} V _ {2} = \left(z _ {1} + z _ {2}\right) \left[ \left(r - \frac {2 r}{K} \widetilde {S} - \frac {\alpha a \widetilde {P}}{(a + \widetilde {S}) ^ {2}}\right) z _ {1} - \left(\frac {r}{K} \widetilde {S} + \beta \widetilde {P} + \mu\right) z _ {2} - \frac {\alpha \widetilde {S}}{a + \widetilde {S}} w \right] \mathrm{d} t,
$$

其中

$$
L V _ {2} = \left(r - \frac {2 r}{K} \widetilde {S} - \frac {\alpha a \widetilde {P}}{(a + \widetilde {S}) ^ {2}}\right) z _ {1} ^ {2} - \left(\frac {r}{K} \widetilde {S} + \beta \widetilde {P} + \mu\right) z _ {2} ^ {2} - \frac {\delta}{\theta} z _ {1} z _ {3} - \frac {\delta}{\theta} z _ {2} z _ {3}
$$

$$
+ \left(r - \frac {3 r}{K} \widetilde {S} - \frac {\alpha a \widetilde {P}}{(a + \widetilde {S}) ^ {2}} - \beta \widetilde {P} - \mu\right) z _ {1} z _ {2},
$$

$$
\mathrm{d} V _ {3} = z _ {2} \left[ (\lambda \widetilde {S} - \beta \widetilde {P} - \mu) z _ {2} \mathrm{d} t + \sigma \widetilde {S} z _ {2} \mathrm{d} B (t) \right] + \frac {1}{2} (\sigma \widetilde {S} z _ {2}) ^ {2} \mathrm{d} t,
$$

其中

$$
L V _ {3} = \left(\lambda \widetilde {S} - \beta \widetilde {P} - \mu + \frac {1}{2} \sigma^ {2} \widetilde {S} ^ {2}\right) z _ {2} ^ {2}.
$$

由条件 $\sigma^2 < \frac{2(\beta\widetilde{P} + \mu - \lambda\widetilde{S})}{\widetilde{S}^2}$ , 可得 $\lambda\widetilde{S} - \beta\widetilde{P} - \mu + \frac{1}{2}\sigma^2\widetilde{S}^2 < 0$ .

$$
\mathrm{d} V _ {4} = z _ {3} \left[ - \theta \beta \widetilde {P} z _ {2} + \frac {\theta \alpha a \widetilde {P}}{(a + \widetilde {S}) ^ {2}} z _ {1} \right] \mathrm{d} t,
$$

其中

$$
L V _ {4} = z _ {3} \left[ - \theta \beta \widetilde {P} z _ {2} + \frac {\theta \alpha a \widetilde {P}}{(a + \widetilde {S}) ^ {2}} z _ {1} \right].
$$

这样可以得到

$$
\begin{array}{l} L V _ {2} + \eta_ {1} L V _ {4} \\ = \left(r - \frac {2 r}{K} \widetilde {S} - \frac {\alpha a \widetilde {P}}{(a + \widetilde {S}) ^ {2}}\right) z _ {1} ^ {2} - \left(\frac {r}{K} \widetilde {S} + \beta \widetilde {P} + \mu\right) z _ {2} ^ {2} \\ + \left(\eta_ {1} \frac {\theta \alpha a \widetilde {P}}{(a + \widetilde {S}) ^ {2}} - \frac {\delta}{\theta}\right) z _ {1} z _ {3} \\ + \left(r - \frac {3 r}{K} \widetilde {S} - \frac {\alpha a \widetilde {P}}{(a + \widetilde {S}) ^ {2}} - \beta \widetilde {P} - \mu\right) z _ {1} z _ {2} - \left(\frac {\delta}{\theta} + \eta_ {1} \theta \beta \widetilde {P}\right) z _ {2} z _ {3}. \tag {2.24} \\ \end{array}
$$

在式 (2.24) 中我们选择

$$
\eta_ {1} \frac {\theta \alpha a \widetilde {P}}{(a + \widetilde {S}) ^ {2}} - \frac {\delta}{\theta} = 0,
$$

则

$$
\eta_ {1} = \frac {\delta (a + \widetilde {S}) ^ {2}}{\alpha a \widetilde {P} \theta^ {2}}.
$$

并且，由柯西不等式，可以得到

$$
\begin{array}{l} \left(r - \frac {3 r}{K} \widetilde {S} - \frac {\alpha a \widetilde {P}}{(a + \widetilde {S}) ^ {2}} - \beta \widetilde {P} - \mu\right) z _ {1} z _ {2} \\ \leqslant \frac {1}{2} \left(r - \frac {3 r}{K} \widetilde {S} - \frac {\alpha a \widetilde {P}}{(a + \widetilde {S}) ^ {2}} - \beta \widetilde {P} - \mu\right) ^ {2} \left(\frac {2 r}{K} \widetilde {S} + \frac {\alpha a \widetilde {P}}{(a + \widetilde {S}) ^ {2}} - r\right) ^ {- 1} z _ {2} ^ {2} \\ + \frac {1}{2} \left(\frac {2 r}{K} \widetilde {S} + \frac {\alpha a \widetilde {P}}{(a + \widetilde {S}) ^ {2}} - r\right) z _ {1} ^ {2}. \tag {2.25} \\ \end{array}
$$

把式 $(2.25)$ 代入式 $(2.24)$ ，可得

$$
\begin{array}{l} L V _ {2} + \eta_ {1} L V _ {4} \\ \leqslant - \frac {1}{2} \left(\frac {2 r}{K} \widetilde {S} + \frac {\alpha a \widetilde {P}}{(a + \widetilde {S}) ^ {2}} - r\right) z _ {1} ^ {2} - \left(\frac {r}{K} \widetilde {S} + \beta \widetilde {P} + \mu\right) z _ {2} ^ {2} - \left(\frac {\delta}{\theta} + \eta_ {1} \theta \beta \widetilde {P}\right) z _ {2} z _ {3} \\ + \frac {1}{2} \left(r - \frac {3 r}{K} \widetilde {S} - \frac {\alpha a \widetilde {P}}{(a + \widetilde {S}) ^ {2}} - \beta \widetilde {P} - \mu\right) ^ {2} \left(\frac {2 r}{K} \widetilde {S} + \frac {\alpha a \widetilde {P}}{(a + \widetilde {S}) ^ {2}} - r\right) ^ {- 1} z _ {2} ^ {2} \\ \end{array}
$$

和

$$
\begin{array}{l} \eta_ {2} L V _ {1} + L V _ {2} + \eta_ {1} L V _ {4} \\ \leqslant - \left\{- \eta_ {2} \left[ \frac {\alpha a \widetilde {P}}{(a + \widetilde {S}) ^ {2}} + \frac {1}{\delta} \left(r - \frac {2 r}{K} \widetilde {S} - \delta\right) ^ {2} + \frac {1}{4} \left(r - \frac {3 r}{K} \widetilde {S} - 2 \beta \widetilde {P} - \mu\right) ^ {2} \right. \right. \\ \left. \cdot \left(\frac {r}{K} \widetilde {S} + 2 \beta \widetilde {P} + \mu\right) ^ {- 1} \right] + \frac {1}{2} \left(\frac {2 r}{K} \widetilde {S} + \frac {\alpha a \widetilde {P}}{(a + \widetilde {S}) ^ {2}} - r\right) \Bigg \} z _ {1} ^ {2} + \left[ \frac {\eta_ {2}}{\delta} \left(\frac {r}{K} \widetilde {S} + 2 \beta \widetilde {P} + \mu + \delta\right) ^ {2} \right. \\ \left. + \frac {1}{2} \left(r - \frac {3 r}{K} \widetilde {S} - \frac {\alpha a \widetilde {P}}{(a + \widetilde {S}) ^ {2}} - \beta \widetilde {P} - \mu\right) ^ {2} \left(\frac {2 r}{K} \widetilde {S} + \frac {\alpha a \widetilde {P}}{(a + \widetilde {S}) ^ {2}} - r\right) ^ {- 1} \right] z _ {2} ^ {2} - \frac {\eta_ {2} \delta}{2 \theta^ {2}} z _ {3} ^ {2} \\ - \left(\frac {r}{K} \widetilde {S} + \beta \widetilde {P} + \mu\right) z _ {2} ^ {2} - \left(\frac {\delta}{\theta} + \eta_ {1} \theta \beta \widetilde {P}\right) z _ {2} z _ {3}. \\ \end{array}
$$

令

$$
\begin{array}{l} \eta_ {2} = \frac {1}{4} \left(\frac {2 r}{K} \widetilde {S} + \frac {\alpha a \widetilde {P}}{(a + \widetilde {S}) ^ {2}} - r\right) \left[ \frac {\alpha a \widetilde {P}}{(a + \widetilde {S}) ^ {2}} + \frac {1}{\delta} \left(r - \frac {2 r}{K} \widetilde {S} - \delta\right) ^ {2} \right. \\ \left. + \frac {1}{4} \left(r - \frac {3 r}{K} \widetilde {S} - 2 \beta \widetilde {P} - \mu\right) ^ {2} \left(\frac {r}{K} \widetilde {S} + 2 \beta \widetilde {P} + \mu\right) ^ {- 1} \right] ^ {- 1}, \\ \end{array}
$$

使得

$$
\eta_ {2} \left[ \frac {\alpha a \widetilde {P}}{(a + \widetilde {S}) ^ {2}} + \frac {1}{\delta} \left(r - \frac {2 r}{K} \widetilde {S} - \delta\right) ^ {2} + \frac {1}{4} \left(r - \frac {3 r}{K} \widetilde {S} - 2 \beta \widetilde {P} - \mu\right) ^ {2} \left(\frac {r}{K} \widetilde {S} + 2 \beta \widetilde {P} + \mu\right) ^ {- 1} \right]
$$

$$
= \frac {1}{4} \left(\frac {2 r}{K} \widetilde {S} + \frac {\alpha a \widetilde {P}}{(a + \widetilde {S}) ^ {2}} - r\right).
$$

这样得到

$$
\eta_ {2} L V _ {1} + L V _ {2} + \eta_ {1} L V _ {4}
$$

$$
\leqslant - \frac {1}{4} \left(\frac {2 r}{K} \widetilde {S} + \frac {\alpha a \widetilde {P}}{(a + \widetilde {S}) ^ {2}} - r\right) z _ {1} ^ {2} - \left(\frac {r}{K} \widetilde {S} + \beta \widetilde {P} + \mu\right) z _ {2} ^ {2} - \frac {\eta_ {2} \delta}{4 \theta^ {2}} z _ {3} ^ {2} + \left[ \frac {\theta^ {2} \left(\frac {\delta}{\theta} + \eta_ {1} \theta \beta \widetilde {P}\right) ^ {2}}{\delta \eta_ {2}} \right.
$$

$$
+ \frac {1}{2} \left(r - \frac {3 r}{K} \widetilde {S} - \frac {\alpha a \widetilde {P}}{(a + \widetilde {S}) ^ {2}} - \beta \widetilde {P} - \mu\right) ^ {2} \left(\frac {2 r}{K} \widetilde {S} + \frac {\alpha a \widetilde {P}}{(a + \widetilde {S}) ^ {2}} - r\right) ^ {- 1}
$$

$$
\left. + \frac {\eta_ {2}}{\delta} \left(\frac {r}{K} \widetilde {S} + 2 \beta \widetilde {P} + \mu + \delta\right) ^ {2} \right] z _ {2} ^ {2}.
$$

最后, 得到

$$
\begin{array}{l} L V = \eta_ {2} L V _ {1} + L V _ {2} + \eta_ {1} L V _ {4} + \eta_ {3} L V _ {3} \\ \leqslant - \frac {1}{4} \left(\frac {2 r}{K} \widetilde {S} + \frac {\alpha a \widetilde {P}}{(a + \widetilde {S}) ^ {2}} - r\right) z _ {1} ^ {2} - \left(\frac {r}{K} \widetilde {S} + \beta \widetilde {P} + \mu\right) z _ {2} ^ {2} - \frac {\eta_ {2} \delta}{4 \theta^ {2}} z _ {3} ^ {2} \\ + \left[ \frac {\theta^ {2} \left(\frac {\delta}{\theta} + \eta_ {1} \theta \beta \widetilde {P}\right) ^ {2}}{\delta \eta_ {2}} \right. \\ + \frac {1}{2} \left(r - \frac {3 r}{K} \widetilde {S} - \frac {\alpha a \widetilde {P}}{(a + \widetilde {S}) ^ {2}} - \beta \widetilde {P} - \mu\right) ^ {2} \left(\frac {2 r}{K} \widetilde {S} + \frac {\alpha a \widetilde {P}}{(a + \widetilde {S}) ^ {2}} - r\right) ^ {- 1} \\ \left. + \frac {\eta_ {2}}{\delta} \left(\frac {r}{K} \widetilde {S} + 2 \beta \widetilde {P} + \mu + \delta\right) ^ {2} - \eta_ {3} \left(\beta \widetilde {P} + \mu - \lambda \widetilde {S} - \frac {1}{2} \sigma^ {2} \widetilde {S} ^ {2}\right) \right] z _ {2} ^ {2}. \\ \end{array}
$$

令

$$
\begin{array}{l} \eta_ {3} = \left[ \frac {1}{2} \left(r - \frac {3 r}{K} \widetilde {S} - \frac {\alpha a \widetilde {P}}{(a + \widetilde {S}) ^ {2}} - \beta \widetilde {P} - \mu\right) ^ {2} \left(\frac {2 r}{K} \widetilde {S} + \frac {\alpha a \widetilde {P}}{(a + \widetilde {S}) ^ {2}} - r\right) ^ {- 1} \right. \\ + \frac {\theta^ {2} \left(\frac {\delta}{\theta} + \eta_ {1} \theta \beta \widetilde {P}\right) ^ {2}}{\delta \eta_ {2}} \\ \left. + \frac {\eta_ {2}}{\delta} \left(\frac {r}{K} \widetilde {S} + 2 \beta \widetilde {P} + \mu + \delta\right) ^ {2} \right] \left(\beta \widetilde {P} + \mu - \lambda \widetilde {S} - \frac {1}{2} \sigma^ {2} \widetilde {S} ^ {2}\right) ^ {- 1}, \\ \end{array}
$$

使得

$$
\begin{array}{l} \frac {1}{2} \left(r - \frac {3 r}{K} \widetilde {S} - \frac {\alpha a \widetilde {P}}{(a + \widetilde {S}) ^ {2}} - \beta \widetilde {P} - \mu\right) ^ {2} \left(\frac {2 r}{K} \widetilde {S} + \frac {\alpha a \widetilde {P}}{(a + \widetilde {S}) ^ {2}} - r\right) ^ {- 1} + \frac {\theta^ {2} \left(\frac {\delta}{\theta} + \eta_ {1} \theta \beta \widetilde {P}\right) ^ {2}}{\delta \eta_ {2}} \\ + \frac {\eta_ {2}}{\delta} \left(\frac {r}{K} \widetilde {S} + 2 \beta \widetilde {P} + \mu + \delta\right) ^ {2} - \eta_ {3} \left(\beta \widetilde {P} + \mu - \lambda \widetilde {S} - \frac {1}{2} \sigma^ {2} \widetilde {S} ^ {2}\right) = 0, \\ \end{array}
$$

则

$$
\begin{array}{l} L V = \eta_ {2} L V _ {1} + L V _ {2} + \eta_ {1} L V _ {4} + \eta_ {3} L V _ {3} \\ \leqslant - \frac {1}{4} \left(\frac {2 r}{K} \widetilde {S} + \frac {\alpha a \widetilde {P}}{(a + \widetilde {S}) ^ {2}} - r\right) z _ {1} ^ {2} - \left(\frac {r}{K} \widetilde {S} + \beta \widetilde {P} + \mu\right) z _ {2} ^ {2} - \frac {\eta_ {2} \delta}{4 \theta^ {2}} z _ {3} ^ {2}. \\ \end{array}
$$

设 $\overline{\lambda} = \min \left\{\frac{1}{4}\left(\frac{2r}{K}\widetilde{S} +\frac{\alpha a\widetilde{P}}{(a + \widetilde{S})^2} -r\right),\frac{r}{K}\widetilde{S} +\beta \widetilde{P} +\mu ,\frac{\eta_2\delta}{4\theta^2}\right\}$ ，则

$$
L V \leqslant - \overline {{{\lambda}}} | z (t) | ^ {2}.
$$

这样, 对 $t \geqslant 0$ , 在 $z = 0$ 的充分小的邻域里, $LV(x)$ 是非负定的. 由定理1.5(Mao[11]), 我们可以推断式(2.23)的零解是随机渐近稳定的.

定理2.13 设 $(S(t), I(t), P(t))$ 是系统(2.4)初值为 $(S(0), I(0), P(0)) \in \Gamma$ 的解. 当

$$
\frac {\delta (a \lambda + \mu)}{\mu \theta} <   \alpha <   \frac {\delta (K + a)}{\theta (K - a)}, \quad \lambda > \frac {\mu}{K}, \quad \sigma^ {2} <   \frac {2 (\beta \widetilde {P} + \mu - \lambda \widetilde {S})}{\widetilde {S} ^ {2}},
$$

则 $E_{3}=(\widetilde{S},0,\widetilde{P})$ 是随机渐近稳定的.

证明 我们只需证明式 $(2.22)$ 的零解是随机渐近稳定的.

定义 $C^2$ 函数 $V:\mathbb{R}_+^3\to \overline{\mathbb{R}}_+$ 

$$
V (x) = \eta_ {2} \frac {1}{2} \left(u + I + \frac {1}{\theta} w\right) ^ {2} + \frac {1}{2} (u + I) ^ {2} + \eta_ {1} \frac {1}{2} w ^ {2} + \eta_ {3} \frac {1}{2} I ^ {2}
$$

$$
:= \eta_ {2} V _ {1} + V _ {2} + \eta_ {1} V _ {4} + \eta_ {3} V _ {3},
$$

其中 $\eta_1, \eta_2, \eta_3$ 都是在定理2.12所定义的正常数. 由Itô公式, 可知

$$
\begin{array}{l} \mathrm{d} V _ {1} = \left(u + I + \frac {1}{\theta} w\right) \left[ \left(r - \frac {2 r}{K} \widetilde {S}\right) u - \left(\frac {r}{K} \widetilde {S} + 2 \beta \widetilde {P} + \mu\right) I - \frac {\alpha \widetilde {S}}{a + \widetilde {S}} w \right. \\ \left. - \frac {r}{K} u ^ {2} - \frac {r}{K} u I - 2 \beta I w \right] \mathrm{d} t, \\ \end{array}
$$

其中

$$
\begin{array}{l} L V _ {1} = \left(r - \frac {2 r}{K} \widetilde {S}\right) u ^ {2} - \left(\frac {r}{K} \widetilde {S} + 2 \beta \widetilde {P} + \mu\right) I ^ {2} - \frac {\delta}{\theta^ {2}} w ^ {2} + \left(r - \frac {3 r}{K} \widetilde {S} - 2 \beta \widetilde {P} - \mu\right) u I \\ + \frac {1}{\theta} \left(r - \frac {2 r}{K} \widetilde {S} - \delta\right) u w - \frac {1}{\theta} \left(\frac {r}{K} \widetilde {S} + 2 \beta \widetilde {P} + \mu + \delta\right) I w \\ + \left(u + I + \frac {1}{\theta} w\right) \left(- \frac {r}{K} u ^ {2} - \frac {r}{K} u I - 2 \beta I w\right). \\ \end{array}
$$

由条件 $\alpha < \frac{\delta(K + a)}{\theta(K - a)}$ ，可知 $r - \frac{2r}{K}\widetilde{S} < \frac{\alpha a\widetilde{P}}{(a + \widetilde{S})^2}$ . 由柯西不等式, 可得

$$
\begin{array}{l} L V _ {1} \leqslant \left[ \frac {\alpha a \widetilde {P}}{(a + \widetilde {S}) ^ {2}} + \frac {1}{\delta} \left(r - \frac {2 r}{K} \widetilde {S} - \delta\right) ^ {2} + \frac {1}{4} \left(r - \frac {3 r}{K} \widetilde {S} - 2 \beta \widetilde {P} - \mu\right) ^ {2} \left(\frac {r}{K} \widetilde {S} + 2 \beta \widetilde {P} + \mu\right) ^ {- 1} \right] \\ \cdot u ^ {2} + \frac {1}{\delta} \left(\frac {r}{K} \widetilde {S} + 2 \beta \widetilde {P} + \mu + \delta\right) ^ {2} I ^ {2} - \frac {\delta}{2 \theta^ {2}} w ^ {2} \\ + \left(u + I + \frac {1}{\theta} w\right) \left(- \frac {r}{K} u ^ {2} - \frac {r}{K} u I - 2 \beta I w\right). \\ \end{array}
$$

$$
\mathrm{d} V _ {2} = (u + I) \left\{\left(r - \frac {2 r}{K} \widetilde {S} - \frac {\alpha a \widetilde {P}}{(a + \widetilde {S}) ^ {2}}\right) u - \left(\frac {r}{K} \widetilde {S} + \beta \widetilde {P} + \mu\right) I - \frac {\alpha \widetilde {S}}{a + \widetilde {S}} w - \frac {r}{K} u ^ {2} \right.
$$

$$
- \frac {r}{K} u I - \frac {\alpha}{a + \widetilde {S}} u w - \beta I w - \frac {\alpha}{(a + \widetilde {S}) ^ {2}} [ (a + \widetilde {S}) u w + a \widetilde {P} u + \widetilde {S} (a + \widetilde {S}) w ]
$$

$$
\cdot \sum_ {n = 1} ^ {\infty} (- 1) ^ {n} \left(\frac {u}{a + \widetilde {S}}\right) ^ {n} \Bigg \} d t,
$$

其中

$$
\begin{array}{l} L V _ {2} = \left(r - \frac {2 r}{K} \widetilde {S} - \frac {\alpha a \widetilde {P}}{(a + \widetilde {S}) ^ {2}}\right) u ^ {2} - \left(\frac {r}{K} \widetilde {S} + \beta \widetilde {P} + \mu\right) I ^ {2} + \left(r - \frac {3 r}{K} \widetilde {S} - \frac {\alpha a \widetilde {P}}{(a + \widetilde {S}) ^ {2}} - \beta \widetilde {P} \right. \\ - \mu) u I - \frac {\delta}{\theta} u w - \frac {\delta}{\theta} I w + (u + I) \left\{- \frac {r}{K} u ^ {2} - \frac {r}{K} u I - \frac {\alpha}{a + \widetilde {S}} u w - \beta I w \right. \\ \left. - \frac {\alpha}{(a + \widetilde {S}) ^ {2}} [ (a + \widetilde {S}) u w + a \widetilde {P} u + \widetilde {S} (a + \widetilde {S}) w ] \sum_ {n = 1} ^ {\infty} (- 1) ^ {n} \left(\frac {u}{a + \widetilde {S}}\right) ^ {n} \right\}, \\ \end{array}
$$

$$
\mathrm{d} V _ {3} = I \left\{\left[ (\lambda \widetilde {S} - \beta \widetilde {P} - \mu) I + \lambda u I - \beta I w \right] \mathrm{d} t + (\sigma u I + \sigma \widetilde {S} I) \mathrm{d} B (t) \right\} + \frac {1}{2} (\sigma u I + \sigma \widetilde {S} I) ^ {2} \mathrm{d} t,
$$

其中

$$
L V _ {3} = \left(\lambda \widetilde {S} - \beta \widetilde {P} - \mu + \frac {1}{2} \sigma^ {2} \widetilde {S} ^ {2}\right) I ^ {2} + \left(\lambda u - \beta w + \frac {1}{2} \sigma^ {2} u ^ {2} + \sigma^ {2} \widetilde {S} u\right) I ^ {2}.
$$

由条件 $\sigma^2 < \frac{2(\beta\widetilde{P} + \mu - \lambda\widetilde{S})}{\widetilde{S}^2}$ , 可得 $\lambda \widetilde{S} - \beta \widetilde{P} - \mu + \frac{1}{2}\sigma^2\widetilde{S}^2 < 0$ .

$$
\begin{array}{l} \mathrm{d} V _ {4} = w \left\{- \theta \beta I \widetilde {P} + \frac {\theta \alpha a \widetilde {P}}{(a + \widetilde {S}) ^ {2}} u + \frac {\theta \alpha}{a + \widetilde {S}} u w - \theta \beta I w + \frac {\theta \alpha}{(a + \widetilde {S}) ^ {2}} [ (a + \widetilde {S}) u w + a \widetilde {P} u \right. \\ \left. + \widetilde {S} (a + \widetilde {S}) w ] \sum_ {n = 1} ^ {\infty} (- 1) ^ {n} \left(\frac {u}{a + \widetilde {S}}\right) ^ {n} \right\} d t, \\ \end{array}
$$

其中

$$
\begin{array}{l} L V _ {4} = w \left\{- \theta \beta I \widetilde {P} + \frac {\theta \alpha a \widetilde {P}}{(a + \widetilde {S}) ^ {2}} u + \frac {\theta \alpha}{a + \widetilde {S}} u w - \theta \beta I w + \frac {\theta \alpha}{(a + \widetilde {S}) ^ {2}} [ (a + \widetilde {S}) u w + a \widetilde {P} u \right. \\ \left. + \widetilde {S} (a + \widetilde {S}) w ] \sum_ {n = 1} ^ {\infty} (- 1) ^ {n} \left(\frac {u}{a + \widetilde {S}}\right) ^ {n} \right\}. \\ \end{array}
$$

这样得到

$$
\begin{array}{l} L V _ {2} + \eta_ {1} L V _ {4} \\ = \left(r - \frac {2 r}{K} \widetilde {S} - \frac {\alpha a \widetilde {P}}{(a + \widetilde {S}) ^ {2}}\right) u ^ {2} - \left(\frac {r}{K} \widetilde {S} + \beta \widetilde {P} + \mu\right) I ^ {2} - \left(\frac {\delta}{\theta} + \eta_ {1} \theta \beta \widetilde {P}\right) I w \\ + \left(r - \frac {3 r}{K} \widetilde {S} - \frac {\alpha a \widetilde {P}}{(a + \widetilde {S}) ^ {2}} - \beta \widetilde {P} - \mu\right) u I + M _ {1} (u, I, w), \tag {2.26} \\ \end{array}
$$

其中

$$
\begin{array}{l} M _ {1} (u, I, w) \\ = (u + I) \left\{- \frac {r}{K} u ^ {2} - \frac {r}{K} u I - \frac {\alpha}{a + \widetilde {S}} u w - \beta I w - \frac {\alpha}{(a + \widetilde {S}) ^ {2}} [ (a + \widetilde {S}) u w + a \widetilde {P} u \right. \\ + \widetilde {S} (a + \widetilde {S}) w ] \sum_ {n = 1} ^ {\infty} (- 1) ^ {n} \left(\frac {u}{a + \widetilde {S}}\right) ^ {n} \Bigg \} + \eta_ {1} \left\{\frac {\theta \alpha}{a + \widetilde {S}} u w ^ {2} - \theta \beta I w ^ {2} + \frac {\theta \alpha w}{(a + \widetilde {S}) ^ {2}} [ (a + \widetilde {S}) u w \right. \\ \left. + a \widetilde {P} u + \widetilde {S} (a + \widetilde {S}) w \right] \sum_ {n = 1} ^ {\infty} (- 1) ^ {n} \left(\frac {u}{a + \widetilde {S}}\right) ^ {n} \Bigg \}. \\ \end{array}
$$

由柯西不等式, 可得

$$
\begin{array}{l} \left(r - \frac {3 r}{K} \widetilde {S} - \frac {\alpha a \widetilde {P}}{(a + \widetilde {S}) ^ {2}} - \beta \widetilde {P} - \mu\right) u I \\ \leqslant \frac {1}{2} \left(r - \frac {3 r}{K} \widetilde {S} - \frac {\alpha a \widetilde {P}}{(a + \widetilde {S}) ^ {2}} - \beta \widetilde {P} - \mu\right) ^ {2} \left(\frac {2 r}{K} \widetilde {S} + \frac {\alpha a \widetilde {P}}{(a + \widetilde {S}) ^ {2}} - r\right) ^ {- 1} I ^ {2} \\ + \frac {1}{2} \left(\frac {2 r}{K} \widetilde {S} + \frac {\alpha a \widetilde {P}}{(a + \widetilde {S}) ^ {2}} - r\right) u ^ {2}. \tag {2.27} \\ \end{array}
$$

把式 $(2.27)$ 代入式 $(2.26)$ ，得

$$
\begin{array}{l} L V _ {2} + \eta_ {1} L V _ {4} \\ \leqslant \frac {1}{2} \left(r - \frac {3 r}{K} \widetilde {S} - \frac {\alpha a \widetilde {P}}{(a + \widetilde {S}) ^ {2}} - \beta \widetilde {P} - \mu\right) ^ {2} \left(\frac {2 r}{K} \widetilde {S} + \frac {\alpha a \widetilde {P}}{(a + \widetilde {S}) ^ {2}} - r\right) ^ {- 1} I ^ {2} + M _ {1} (u, I, w) \\ - \frac {1}{2} \left(\frac {2 r}{K} \widetilde {S} + \frac {\alpha a \widetilde {P}}{(a + \widetilde {S}) ^ {2}} - r\right) u ^ {2} - \left(\frac {r}{K} \widetilde {S} + \beta \widetilde {P} + \mu\right) I ^ {2} - \left(\frac {\delta}{\theta} + \eta_ {1} \theta \beta \widetilde {P}\right) I w \\ \end{array}
$$

和

$$
\begin{array}{l} \eta_ {2} L V _ {1} + L V _ {2} + \eta_ {1} L V _ {4} \\ \leqslant \eta_ {2} \left\{ \right.\left[ \frac {\alpha a \widetilde {P}}{(a + \widetilde {S}) ^ {2}} + \frac {1}{\delta} \left(r - \frac {2 r}{K} \widetilde {S} - \delta\right) ^ {2} + \frac {1}{4} \left(r - \frac {3 r}{K} \widetilde {S} - 2 \beta \widetilde {P} - \mu\right) ^ {2} \left(\frac {r}{K} \widetilde {S} + 2 \beta \widetilde {P} + \mu\right) ^ {- 1} \right] / u ^ {2} \\ \left. + \frac {1}{\delta} \left(\frac {r}{K} \widetilde {S} + 2 \beta \widetilde {P} + \mu + \delta\right) ^ {2} I ^ {2} - \frac {\delta}{2 \theta^ {2}} w ^ {2} + \left(u + I + \frac {1}{\theta} w\right) \left(- \frac {r}{K} u ^ {2} - \frac {r}{K} u I - 2 \beta I w\right) \right\} \\ + \frac {1}{2} \left(r - \frac {3 r}{K} \widetilde {S} - \frac {\alpha a \widetilde {P}}{(a + \widetilde {S}) ^ {2}} - \beta \widetilde {P} - \mu\right) ^ {2} \left(\frac {2 r}{K} \widetilde {S} + \frac {\alpha a \widetilde {P}}{(a + \widetilde {S}) ^ {2}} - r\right) ^ {- 1} I ^ {2} + M _ {1} (u, I, w) \\ \end{array}
$$

$$
- \frac {1}{2} \left(\frac {2 r}{K} \widetilde {S} + \frac {\alpha a \widetilde {P}}{(a + \widetilde {S}) ^ {2}} - r\right) u ^ {2} - \left(\frac {r}{K} \widetilde {S} + \beta \widetilde {P} + \mu\right) I ^ {2} - \left(\frac {\delta}{\theta} + \eta_ {1} \theta \beta \widetilde {P}\right) I w
$$

$$
\leqslant - \frac {1}{4} \left(\frac {2 r}{K} \widetilde {S} + \frac {\alpha a \widetilde {P}}{(a + \widetilde {S}) ^ {2}} - r\right) u ^ {2} - \left(\frac {r}{K} \widetilde {S} + \beta \widetilde {P} + \mu\right) I ^ {2} - \frac {\eta_ {2} \delta}{4 \theta^ {2}} w ^ {2}
$$

$$
+ \left[ \frac {1}{2} \left(r - \frac {3 r}{K} \widetilde {S} - \frac {\alpha a \widetilde {P}}{(a + \widetilde {S}) ^ {2}} - \beta \widetilde {P} - \mu\right) ^ {2} \left(\frac {2 r}{K} \widetilde {S} + \frac {\alpha a \widetilde {P}}{(a + \widetilde {S}) ^ {2}} - r\right) ^ {- 1} \right.
$$

$$
\left. + \frac {\theta^ {2} \left(\frac {\delta}{\theta} + \eta_ {1} \theta \beta \widetilde {P}\right) ^ {2}}{\delta \eta_ {2}} + \frac {\eta_ {2}}{\delta} \left(\frac {r}{K} \widetilde {S} + 2 \beta \widetilde {P} + \mu + \delta\right) ^ {2} \right] I ^ {2}
$$

$$
+ \eta_ {2} \left(u + I + \frac {1}{\theta} w\right) \left(- \frac {r}{K} u ^ {2} - \frac {r}{K} u I - 2 \beta I w\right)
$$

$$
+ M _ {1} (u, I, w).
$$

最后，得到

$$
L V = \eta_ {2} L V _ {1} + L V _ {2} + \eta_ {1} L V _ {4} + \eta_ {3} L V _ {3}
$$

$$
\leqslant - \frac {1}{4} \left(\frac {2 r}{K} \widetilde {S} + \frac {\alpha a \widetilde {P}}{(a + \widetilde {S}) ^ {2}} - r\right) u ^ {2} - \left(\frac {r}{K} \widetilde {S} + \beta \widetilde {P} + \mu\right) I ^ {2} - \frac {\eta_ {2} \delta}{4 \theta^ {2}} w ^ {2}
$$

$$
+ \left[ \frac {1}{2} \left(r - \frac {3 r}{K} \widetilde {S} - \frac {\alpha a \widetilde {P}}{(a + \widetilde {S}) ^ {2}} - \beta \widetilde {P} - \mu\right) ^ {2} \left(\frac {2 r}{K} \widetilde {S} + \frac {\alpha a \widetilde {P}}{(a + \widetilde {S}) ^ {2}} - r\right) ^ {- 1} \right.
$$

$$
\left. + \frac {\eta_ {2}}{\delta} \left(\frac {r}{K} \widetilde {S} + 2 \beta \widetilde {P} + \mu + \delta\right) ^ {2} + \frac {\theta^ {2} \left(\frac {\delta}{\theta} + \eta_ {1} \theta \beta \widetilde {P}\right) ^ {2}}{\delta \eta_ {2}} - \eta_ {3} \left(\beta \widetilde {P} + \mu - \lambda \widetilde {S} - \frac {1}{2} \sigma^ {2} \widetilde {S} ^ {2}\right) \right] I ^ {2}
$$

$$
+ M _ {1} (u, I, w) + \eta_ {2} \left(u + I + \frac {1}{\theta} w\right) \left(- \frac {r}{K} u ^ {2} - \frac {r}{K} u I - 2 \beta I w\right)
$$

$$
+ \eta_ {3} (\lambda u - \beta w + \frac {1}{2} \sigma^ {2} u ^ {2} + \sigma^ {2} \widetilde {S} u) I ^ {2}
$$

$$
\leqslant - \frac {1}{4} \left(\frac {2 r}{K} \widetilde {S} + \frac {\alpha a \widetilde {P}}{(a + \widetilde {S}) ^ {2}} - r\right) u ^ {2} - \left(\frac {r}{K} \widetilde {S} + \beta \widetilde {P} + \mu\right) I ^ {2} - \frac {\eta_ {2} \delta}{4 \theta^ {2}} w ^ {2} + M _ {1} (u, I, w)
$$

$$
+ \eta_ {2} \left(u + I + \frac {1}{\theta} w\right) \left(- \frac {r}{K} u ^ {2} - \frac {r}{K} u I - 2 \beta I w\right) + \eta_ {3} \left(\lambda u - \beta w + \frac {1}{2} \sigma^ {2} u ^ {2} + \sigma^ {2} \widetilde {S} u\right) I ^ {2}.
$$

设 $\widetilde{\lambda} = \min \left\{\frac{1}{4}\left(\frac{2r}{K}\widetilde{S} +\frac{\alpha a\widetilde{P}}{(a + \widetilde{S})^2} -r\right),\frac{r}{K}\widetilde{S} +\beta \widetilde{P} +\mu ,\frac{\eta_2\delta}{4\theta^2}\right\}$ ，则

$$
L V \leqslant - \widetilde {\lambda} | x (t) | ^ {2} + o (| x (t) | ^ {2}).
$$

这样, 对 $t \geqslant 0$ , 在 $x = 0$ 的充分小的邻域里, $LV(x)$ 是非负定的. 由定理1.5(Mao[11]), 我们可以推断式(2.22)的零解是随机渐近稳定的.

## 2.3.6 数值模拟

下面我们用 Milstein 高阶方法 [33] 进行数值模拟验证所得到的结论. 变量和参数的选取来自 Chattopadhyay 等 (参见文献 [26] 的表 2), 这里

$$
r = 3, \quad K = 4 5, \quad a = 1 5, \quad \beta = 0. 0 5, \quad \mu = 0. 2 4, \quad \theta = 0. 4, \quad \delta = 0. 0 9.
$$

首先, 选取 $\alpha = 0.2$ , $\lambda = 0.003$ , $\sigma = 0.045$ , 即

$$
\alpha = 0. 2 <   \frac {\delta (a + K)}{K \theta} = 0. 3, \quad \sigma^ {2} = 0. 0 0 2 0 2 5 <   \frac {2 (r + K \lambda) (\mu - K \lambda)}{\lambda K ^ {3}} = 0. 0 0 2 4 0 8.
$$

由定理 2.9, 我们可知系统 (2.4) 的平衡点 $E_{1} = (45, 0, 0)$ 是大范围随机渐近稳定的.
图 2.6 中的数值模拟显然地证明了上述结论成立.

注意到

$$
\sigma^ {2} = 0. 0 0 2 0 2 5 > \max \left\{\frac {\lambda}{K} = 0. 0 0 0 0 6 7, \frac {\lambda^ {2}}{2 \mu} = 0. 0 0 0 0 1 8 7 5 \right\} = 0. 0 0 0 0 6 7
$$

和

$$
\alpha = 0. 2 <   \frac {\delta (a + K)}{K \theta} = 0. 3
$$

满足定理 2.10 的条件 (a) 和 (c). 因此, 由定理 2.10, 对初值为 $S(0) = 30$ , $I(0) = 10$ , $P(0) = 15$ 的系统 (2.4) 的解有

$$
\operatorname * {l i m s u p} _ {t \to \infty} \frac {\log I (t)}{t} \leqslant - 0. 2 3 7 8 <   0 \text {   a.s. };
$$

$$
\operatorname * {l i m s u p} _ {t \to \infty} \frac {\log P (t)}{t} \leqslant - 0. 0 3 <   0 a. s.;
$$

$$
\lim _ {t \rightarrow \infty} \frac {1}{t} \int_ {0} ^ {t} S (s) \mathrm{d} s = 4 5 \text {a.s.}
$$

从图 2.6 可以看出感染者和捕食者的灭绝.

接着, 选取 $\alpha = 0.09$ , $\lambda = 0.015$ , 则

$$
\overline {{{S}}} = \frac {\mu}{\lambda} = 1 6, \quad \overline {{{I}}} = \frac {r \left(1 - \frac {\overline {{{S}}}}{K}\right)}{\frac {r}{K} + \lambda} = 2 3. 6 7
$$

和满足条件

$$
\mu = 0. 2 4 <   \lambda K = 0. 6 7 5, \quad \alpha = 0. 0 9 <   \frac {\delta (a + K)}{K \theta} = 0. 3,
$$

因此, 由定理 2.11, $P(t)$ 以概率 1 指数收敛于 0. 系统 (2.4) 的解和 $E_{2} = (16, 23.67, 0)$ 在时间平均意义下的差别只与白噪声的强度有关. 白噪声的强度越弱, 它们之间的差别就越小. 从图 2.7~ 图 2.9 可知, 系统 (2.4) 的解围绕 $E_{2} = (16, 23.67, 0)$ 随机扰动, 扰动的强度随着 $\sigma$ 的增加而增大.

再次, 选取 $\alpha = 0.44$ , $\lambda = 0.015$ , $\sigma = 0.05$ , 则

$$
\widetilde {S} = \frac {a \delta}{\theta \alpha - \delta} = 1 9. 2 8 6, \quad \widetilde {P} = \frac {r}{\alpha} (a + \widetilde {S}) \left(1 - \frac {\widetilde {S}}{K}\right) = 1 4 6. 9 3 9,
$$

$$
\frac {\delta (a \lambda + \mu)}{\mu \theta} = 0. 4 3 5 9 <   \alpha = 0. 4 4 <   \frac {\delta (K + a)}{\theta (K - a)} = 0. 4 5, \quad \frac {\mu}{K} = 0. 0 0 5 3 <   \lambda = 0. 0 1 5,
$$

$$
\sigma^ {2} = 0. 0 0 2 5 <   \frac {2 (\beta \widetilde {P} + \mu - \lambda \widetilde {S})}{\widetilde {S} ^ {2}} = 0. 0 3 9 2 4.
$$
