## 第 3 章 随机生物模型的竞争排斥原理

## 3.1 引言

Volterra 认为两个或两个以上争夺猎物的食肉动物的共存是不可能的, 这后来被称为竞争排斥原理. 1974 年 Koch $^{[34]}$ 再次检验竞争排斥原理, 他通过数值模拟发现在一个常数和统一的环境下当捕食者猎物密度功能性反应是非线性函数时, 两个捕食者争夺一种猎物的共存实际上是可能的, 这种共存发生在 $R^{3}$ 空间正卦限的一个周期轨道而不是平衡点. 文献 [35]~[41] 也有类似的讨论. 文献 [39] 研究了在一个常数和统一的环境下两个食肉动物争夺一个猎物物种的三维 Lotka-Volterra 模型的全局动力学. 假设两个食肉动物物种在没有竞争对手之间的干扰下争夺猎物, 在没有捕食的情况下猎物的增长率是 Logistic 或线性, 捕食者的功能性反应是线性的. 于是模型可以描述为

$$
\left\{ \begin{array}{l} \frac {\mathrm{d} S (t)}{\mathrm{d} t} = S (t) (b _ {1} - a _ {1 1} S (t) - a _ {1 2} X (t) - a _ {1 3} Y (t)), \\ \frac {\mathrm{d} X (t)}{\mathrm{d} t} = X (t) (- b _ {2} + b _ {2 1} S (t)), \\ \frac {\mathrm{d} Y (t)}{\mathrm{d} t} = Y (t) (- b _ {3} + b _ {3 1} S (t)), \end{array} \right. \tag {3.1}
$$

其中 $S(t), X(t), Y(t)$ 分别表示 $t$ 时刻猎物和第 $i (i = 1,2)$ 捕食者的种群密度. $b_{1}$ 表示猎物的内禀增长率, $\frac{1}{a_{11}}$ 表示猎物的承载能力, 它描述了丰富的猎物资源. 若 $\frac{1}{a_{11}}$ 有界, 也就是说, 猎物资源有限, 则在没有捕食的情况下猎物的增长率是 Logistic; $a_{12}, a_{13}$ 表示第 $i$ 捕食者对猎物的影响, $b_{2}, b_{3}$ 表示在没有猎物的情况下第 $i$ 捕食者的自然死亡率, $b_{21}, b_{31}$ 表示在没有猎物的情况下第 $i$ 捕食者的效率和传播率.

若 $\frac{b_2}{b_{21}} = \frac{b_3}{b_{31}}$ 且 $\frac{b_1}{a_{11}} >\frac{b_3}{b_{31}}$ ，则系统(3.1)无限多的正平衡点 $(S^{*},X^{*},Y^{*})$ 填满一段 $L$ ，它吸引系统(3.1)具有正的初始条件的所有解， $L$ 的端点分别地吸引系统(3.1)具有正的初始条件 $(S^{*},X^{*},0)$ 或 $(S^{*},0,Y^{*})$ 的所有解，原点 $(0,0,0)$ 吸引系统(3.1)具有正的初始条件 $(0,X^{*},Y^{*})$ 的所有解．系统(3.1)的竞争排斥原理成立当且仅当下列条件之一成立.

(i) 若 $\frac{b_1}{a_{11}} > \frac{b_3}{b_{31}}$ 且 $\frac{b_2}{b_{21}} > \frac{b_3}{b_{31}}$ , 则捕食者 $X(t)$ 灭绝, 捕食者 $Y(t)$ 幸存.

(ii) 若 $\frac{b_1}{a_{11}} > \frac{b_2}{b_{21}}$ 且 $\frac{b_3}{b_{31}} > \frac{b_2}{b_{21}}$ , 则捕食者 $Y(t)$ 灭绝, 捕食者 $X(t)$ 幸存.

(iii) 若 $\frac{b_1}{a_{11}} \leqslant \frac{b_3}{b_{31}} < \frac{b_2}{b_{21}}$ 或 $\frac{b_1}{a_{11}} \leqslant \frac{b_2}{b_{21}} < \frac{b_3}{b_{31}}$ , 则系统 (3.1) 的竞争排斥原理不成立. 在这种情况下, 这两个相互竞争的捕食者灭绝, 除去 $x_1 x_2$ 平面的轨道外, 系统 (3.1) 的所有轨道渐近于平衡点 $\left(\frac{b_1}{a_{11}}, 0, 0\right)$ .

另外, 由任意数量的竞争对手和一个单一限制的营养组成的恒化器竞争模型的竞争排斥原理也被研究, 见文献 [41]~[45]. 经典的恒化器模型满足如下方程:

$$
\left\{ \begin{array}{l} \frac {\mathrm{d} S (t)}{\mathrm{d} t} = \left(S ^ {0} - S (t)\right) D - \sum_ {i = 1} ^ {n} \frac {a _ {i} S (t)}{1 + b _ {i} S (t)} x _ {i} (t), \\ \frac {\mathrm{d} x _ {i} (t)}{\mathrm{d} t} = - d _ {i} x _ {i} (t) + \frac {a _ {i} S (t)}{1 + b _ {i} S (t)} x _ {i} (t), \quad i = 1, 2, \dots , n, \end{array} \right. \tag {3.2}
$$

其中 $S(t)$ 表示恒化器的培养容器里营养物在 $t$ 时刻的浓度, $S^0$ 表示生长限制营养物在营养瓶中的浓度. $D = F / V$ 表示营养物从营养瓶进入生长室的稀释率. 对每个 $i$ , $x_i(t)$ 表示第 $i$ 种群在 $t$ 时刻的浓度, Holling II 反应函数 $\frac{a_i S(t)}{1 + b_i S(t)}$ 表示第 $i$ 种群具体人均营养吸收率 ( $a_i > 0$ 和 $b_i \geqslant 0$ 都是常数), $d_i$ 表示结合恒化器的稀释率和第 $i$ 种群的特殊死亡率的特殊去除率.

对每个 $i$ ，反应函数 $p_i(S) = \frac{a_iS(t)}{1 + b_iS(t)}: \mathbb{R}^+ \to \mathbb{R}^+$ 是局部 Lipschitz 满足 $p_i(0) = 0$ ，且存在唯一的正数 $0 < \lambda_i\left(= \frac{d_i}{a_i - b_id_i}\right) \leqslant \infty$ 满足

$$
\begin{array}{l} - d _ {i} + p _ {i} (S) <   0, \quad 0 <   S <   \lambda_ {i}, \\ - d _ {i} + p _ {i} (S) > 0, \quad \lambda_ {i} <   S. \\ \end{array}
$$

若对某些 $i (i = 1,2,\dots ,n),\lambda_i\geqslant S^0$ ，则物种 $i$ 被称为一个不足的竞争对手，即使在它死后没有其他物种．若对 $i (i = 1,2,\dots ,n),\lambda_i <   S^0$ ，则在培养容器里的每个物种是一个适当的竞争对手.此外，如果条件 $0 <   \lambda_1 <   \lambda_2\leqslant \dots \leqslant \lambda_n <   S^0$ 成立，系统(3.2)的解满足

$$
\lim _ {t \rightarrow \infty} S (t) = \lambda_ {1}, \quad \lim _ {t \rightarrow \infty} x _ {1} (t) = x _ {1} ^ {*}, \quad \lim _ {t \rightarrow \infty} x _ {i} (t) = 0, \quad i = 2, \dots , n,
$$

其中 $(\lambda_{1}, x_{1}^{*}, 0, \cdots, 0)$ 是系统 (3.2) 的边界平衡点. 类似的结论在文献 [46], [47] 和 [48] 中得到, 他们证明时滞恒化器竞争模型的竞争排斥原理成立.

由于环境白噪声无处不在, 本章分别考虑系统 (3.1) 和 (3.2) 受随机扰动, 研究相应的随机模型的竞争排斥原理.

## 3.2 随机 Lotka-Volterra 模型的竞争排斥原理

本节我们考虑系统（3.1）的随机模型如下：

$$
\left\{ \begin{array}{l} \mathrm{d} S (t) = S (t) \left(b _ {1} - a _ {1 1} S (t) - a _ {1 2} X (t) - a _ {1 3} Y (t)\right) \mathrm{d} t + \sigma_ {1} S (t) \mathrm{d} B _ {1} (t), \\ \mathrm{d} X (t) = X (t) \left(- b _ {2} + b _ {2 1} S (t)\right) \mathrm{d} t + \sigma_ {2} X (t) \mathrm{d} B _ {2} (t), \\ \mathrm{d} Y (t) = Y (t) \left(- b _ {3} + b _ {3 1} S (t)\right) \mathrm{d} t + \sigma_ {3} Y (t) \mathrm{d} B _ {3} (t), \end{array} \right. \tag {3.3}
$$

其中 $B_{1}(t), B_{2}(t), B_{3}(t)$ 是独立的布朗运动, $\sigma_{1}^{2}, \sigma_{2}^{2}, \sigma_{3}^{2}$ 是环境白噪声的强度.

## 3.2.1 系统（3.3）正解的存在唯一性

定理3.1 对任何初值 $(S(0), X(0), Y(0)) \in \mathbb{R}_+^3$ ，系统(3.3)几乎必然存在唯一的正解 $(S(t), X(t), Y(t))$ .

证明 因为方程 (3.3) 的系数满足局部 Lipschitz 条件, 所以对任何初值 $(S(0), X(0), Y(0)) \in \mathbb{R}_{+}^{3}$ , 存在唯一的局部解 $(S(t), X(t), Y(t)), t \in [0, \tau_{e})$ , 其中 $\tau_{e}$ 是爆破时刻.

下面证明此解是全局存在的, 显然只需证明 $\tau_{e} = \infty$ a.s. 令 $k_{0} \geqslant 0$ 充分大, 使得 $S(0), X(0)$ 和 $Y(0)$ 都在区间 $\left[\frac{1}{k_0}, k_0\right]$ 中. 对每个整数 $k \geqslant k_0$ , 定义停时

$$
\tau_ {k} = \inf \left\{t \in [ 0, \tau_ {e}): \min \{S (t), X (t), Y (t) \} \leqslant {\frac {1}{k}}   \text {或}   \max \{S (t), X (t), Y (t) \} \geqslant k \right\},
$$

其中, 约定 $\inf \varnothing = \infty$ . 显然, $\tau_{k}$ 当 $k \to \infty$ 时是单调增加的. 令 $\tau_{\infty} = \lim_{k \to \infty} \tau_{k}$ , 显然 $\tau_{\infty} \leqslant \tau_{e}$ a.s.. 若能证明 $\tau_{\infty} = \infty$ a.s., 则 $\tau_{e} = \infty$ , 即 $(S(0), X(0), Y(0)) \in \mathbb{R}_{+}^{3}(t \geqslant 0)$ a.s. 换言之, 完成定理的证明只需证明 $\tau_{\infty} = \infty$ a.s. 若不然, 则存在常数 $T > 0$ 和 $\varepsilon \in (0,1)$ , 使得

$$
P \{\tau_ {\infty} \leqslant T \} > \varepsilon ,
$$

于是存在整数 $k_{1} \geqslant k_{0}$ 使得

$$
P \{\tau_ {k} \leqslant T \} \geqslant \varepsilon , \quad \text {对所有} k \geqslant k _ {1}.
$$

定义 $C^2$ 函数 $V:\mathbb{R}_+^3\to \overline{\mathbb{R}}_+$ 

$$
V (S, X, Y) = S - a - a \log \frac {S}{a} + \frac {a _ {1 2}}{b _ {2 1}} (X - 1 - \log X) + \frac {a _ {1 3}}{b _ {3 1}} (Y - 1 - \log Y),
$$

其中 $a = \min \left\{\frac{b_2}{b_{21}},\frac{b_3}{b_{31}}\right\}$ 此函数的非负性可以由 $u - 1 - \log u\geqslant 0,\forall u > 0$ 看出.由Itô公式，有

$$
\begin{array}{l} \mathrm{d} V = \left[ (S - a) \left(b _ {1} - a _ {1 1} S - a _ {1 2} X - a _ {1 3} Y\right) + \frac {a \sigma_ {1} ^ {2}}{2} \right] \mathrm{d} t + \sigma_ {1} (S - a) \mathrm{d} B _ {1} (t) \\ + \frac {a _ {1 2}}{b _ {2 1}} \left\{\left[ (X - 1) (- b _ {2} + b _ {2 1} S) + \frac {\sigma_ {2} ^ {2}}{2} \right] \mathrm{d} t + \sigma_ {2} (X - 1) \mathrm{d} B _ {2} (t) \right\} \\ + \frac {a _ {1 3}}{b _ {3 1}} \left\{\left[ (Y - 1) (- b _ {3} + b _ {3 1} S) + \frac {\sigma_ {3} ^ {2}}{2} \right] \mathrm{d} t + \sigma_ {3} (Y - 1) \mathrm{d} B _ {3} (t) \right\} \\ := L V \mathrm{d} t + \sigma_ {1} (S - a) \mathrm{d} B _ {1} (t) + \frac {a _ {1 2}}{b _ {2 1}} \sigma_ {2} (X - 1) \mathrm{d} B _ {2} (t) + \frac {a _ {1 3}}{b _ {3 1}} \sigma_ {3} (Y - 1) \mathrm{d} B _ {3} (t), \\ \end{array}
$$

这里由 a 的定义, 可得

$$
\begin{array}{l} L V = (S - a) \left(b _ {1} - a _ {1 1} S - a _ {1 2} X - a _ {1 3} Y\right) + \frac {a \sigma_ {1} ^ {2}}{2} + \frac {a _ {1 2}}{b _ {2 1}} \left[ (X - 1) (- b _ {2} + b _ {2 1} S) + \frac {\sigma_ {2} ^ {2}}{2} \right] \\ + \frac {a _ {1 3}}{b _ {3 1}} \left[ (Y - 1) (- b _ {3} + b _ {3 1} S) + \frac {\sigma_ {3} ^ {2}}{2} \right] \\ = - a _ {1 1} S ^ {2} + \left(b _ {1} + a a _ {1 1} - a _ {1 2} - a _ {1 3}\right) S + \left(a a _ {1 2} - \frac {a _ {1 2}}{b _ {2 1}} b _ {2}\right) X + \left(a a _ {1 3} - \frac {a _ {1 3}}{b _ {3 1}} b _ {3}\right) Y \\ - a b _ {1} + \frac {a _ {1 2} b _ {2}}{b _ {2 1}} + \frac {a _ {1 3} b _ {3}}{b _ {3 1}} + \frac {a \sigma_ {1} ^ {2}}{2} + \frac {a _ {1 2} \sigma_ {2} ^ {2}}{2 b _ {2 1}} + \frac {a _ {1 3} \sigma_ {3} ^ {2}}{2 b _ {3 1}} \\ \leqslant \max _ {S \in \mathbb {R} ^ {+}} \left\{- a _ {1 1} S ^ {2} + \left(b _ {1} + a a _ {1 1} - a _ {1 2} - a _ {1 3}\right) S \right\} + \frac {a _ {1 2} b _ {2}}{b _ {2 1}} + \frac {a _ {1 3} b _ {3}}{b _ {3 1}} + \frac {a \sigma_ {1} ^ {2}}{2} + \frac {a _ {1 2} \sigma_ {2} ^ {2}}{2 b _ {2 1}} \\ + \frac {a _ {1 3} \sigma_ {3} ^ {2}}{2 b _ {3 1}}. \\ \end{array}
$$

用 A. Gray 在文献 [49] 中的定理 3.1 类似的证明方法, 可以证明结果成立. □

## 3.2.2 系统（3.3）的竞争排斥原理

为方便起见, 定义

$$
\langle x (t) \rangle = \frac {1}{t} \int_ {0} ^ {t} x (r) \mathrm{d} r.
$$

引理3.1 假设 $(b_{2} \wedge b_{3}) > \frac{1}{2} (\sigma_{2}^{2} \vee \sigma_{3}^{2})$ 成立, 则对任何初值 $(S(0), X(0), Y(0)) \in \mathbb{R}_{+}^{3}$ , 系统 (3.3) 的解有如下性质

$$
\limsup _ {t \to \infty} \frac {\log \left[ S (t) + \frac {a _ {1 2}}{b _ {2 1}} X (t) + \frac {a _ {1 3}}{b _ {3 1}} Y (t) \right]}{\log t} \leqslant \frac {1}{\theta} \text {   a.s.,   }
$$

其中 $\theta$ 是一个满足 $2 < \theta < 1 + 2\frac{b_2 \wedge b_3}{\sigma_2^2 \vee \sigma_3^2}$ 的正常数.

证明 令

$$
U (t) = S (t) + \frac {a _ {1 2}}{b _ {2 1}} X (t) + \frac {a _ {1 3}}{b _ {3 1}} Y (t) := S (t) + X _ {1} (t) + Y _ {1} (t).
$$

由 Itô 公式和式 (3.3) 有

$$
\begin{array}{l} \mathrm{d} U (t) = \left[ S (t) \left(b _ {1} - a _ {1 1} S (t)\right) - b _ {2} X _ {1} (t) - b _ {3} Y _ {1} (t) \right] \mathrm{d} t + \sigma_ {1} S (t) \mathrm{d} B _ {1} (t) + \sigma_ {2} X _ {1} (t) \mathrm{d} B _ {2} (t) \\ + \sigma_ {3} Y _ {1} (t) \mathrm{d} B _ {3} (t). \\ \end{array}
$$

定义函数 $W$ 为 $W(U) = (1 + U)^{\theta}$ , 则

$$
\begin{array}{l} \mathrm{d} W (U) = L W (U) \mathrm{d} t + \theta (1 + U (t)) ^ {\theta - 1} \left[ \sigma_ {1} S (t) \mathrm{d} B _ {1} (t) \right. \\ \left. + \sigma_ {2} X _ {1} (t) \mathrm{d} B _ {2} (t) + \sigma_ {3} Y _ {1} (t) \mathrm{d} B _ {3} (t) \right], \tag {3.4} \\ \end{array}
$$

其中

$$
\begin{array}{l} L W (U) = \theta (1 + U) ^ {\theta - 1} \left[ S \left(b _ {1} - a _ {1 1} S\right) - b _ {2} X _ {1} - b _ {3} Y _ {1} \right] \\ + \frac {\theta (\theta - 1)}{2} (1 + U) ^ {\theta - 2} \left(\sigma_ {1} ^ {2} S ^ {2} + \sigma_ {2} ^ {2} X _ {1} ^ {2} + \sigma_ {3} ^ {2} Y _ {1} ^ {2}\right) \\ = \theta (1 + U) ^ {\theta - 2} \left\{(1 + U) \left[ S \left(b _ {1} - a _ {1 1} S\right) - b _ {2} X _ {1} - b _ {3} Y _ {1} \right] \right. \\ \left. + \frac {\theta - 1}{2} \left(\sigma_ {1} ^ {2} S ^ {2} + \sigma_ {2} ^ {2} X _ {1} ^ {2} + \sigma_ {3} ^ {2} Y _ {1} ^ {2}\right) \right\} \\ \leqslant \theta (1 + U) ^ {\theta - 2} \left\{(1 + S + Z) \left[ b _ {1} S - a _ {1 1} S ^ {2} - \left(b _ {2} \wedge b _ {3}\right) Z \right] \right. \\ \left. + \frac {\theta - 1}{2} \left[ \sigma_ {1} ^ {2} S ^ {2} + \left(\sigma_ {2} ^ {2} \vee \sigma_ {3} ^ {2}\right) Z ^ {2} \right] \right\} \\ \leqslant \theta (1 + U) ^ {\theta - 2} \left\{- a _ {1 1} S ^ {3} + \left(b _ {1} + \frac {\theta - 1}{2} \sigma_ {1} ^ {2}\right) S ^ {2} + b _ {1} S Z + b _ {1} S \right. \\ \left. - \left[ \left(b _ {2} \wedge b _ {3}\right) - \frac {\theta - 1}{2} \left(\sigma_ {2} ^ {2} \vee \sigma_ {3} ^ {2}\right) \right] Z ^ {2} \right\}, \\ \end{array}
$$

这里 $Z = X_{1} + Y_{1}$ 选取常数 $\lambda >0$ 充分小使得

$$
(b _ {2} \wedge b _ {3}) - \frac {\theta - 1}{2} (\sigma_ {2} ^ {2} \vee \sigma_ {3} ^ {2}) - \frac {3 \lambda}{\theta} \gg 0.
$$

利用 Itô 公式有

$$
E [ \mathrm{e} ^ {\lambda t} W (U (t)) ] = W (U (0)) + E \int_ {0} ^ {t} L [ \mathrm{e} ^ {\lambda s} W (U (s)) ] \mathrm{d} s,
$$

其中

$$
\begin{array}{l} L \left[ \mathrm{e} ^ {\lambda t} W (U) \right] = \lambda \mathrm{e} ^ {\lambda t} W (U (t)) + \mathrm{e} ^ {\lambda t} L W (U) \\ \leqslant \theta \mathrm{e} ^ {\lambda t} (1 + U) ^ {\theta - 2} \left\{- a _ {1 1} S ^ {3} + \left(b _ {1} + \frac {\theta - 1}{2} \sigma_ {1} ^ {2}\right) S ^ {2} + b _ {1} S Z + b _ {1} S \right. \\ \left. - \left[ \left(b _ {2} \wedge b _ {3}\right) - \frac {\theta - 1}{2} \left(\sigma_ {2} ^ {2} \vee \sigma_ {3} ^ {2}\right) \right] Z ^ {2} + \frac {\lambda}{\theta} (1 + S + Z) ^ {2} \right\} \\ \leqslant \theta \mathrm{e} ^ {\lambda t} (1 + U) ^ {\theta - 2} \left\{- a _ {1 1} S ^ {3} + \left(b _ {1} + \frac {\theta - 1}{2} \sigma_ {1} ^ {2} + \frac {3 \lambda}{\theta}\right) S ^ {2} + b _ {1} S Z + b _ {1} S + \frac {3 \lambda}{\theta} \right. \\ \left. - \left[ \left(b _ {2} \wedge b _ {3}\right) - \frac {\theta - 1}{2} \left(\sigma_ {2} ^ {2} \vee \sigma_ {3} ^ {2}\right) - \frac {3 \lambda}{\theta} \right] Z ^ {2} \right\} \\ \leqslant \theta \mathrm{e} ^ {\lambda t} (1 + U) ^ {\theta - 2} \left\{- a _ {1 1} S ^ {3} + c S ^ {2} + b _ {1} S + \frac {3 \lambda}{\theta} \right. \\ \left. - \frac {1}{2} \left[ \left(b _ {2} \wedge b _ {3}\right) - \frac {\theta - 1}{2} \left(\sigma_ {2} ^ {2} \vee \sigma_ {3} ^ {2}\right) - \frac {3 \lambda}{\theta} \right] Z ^ {2} \right\} \\ \leqslant \theta \mathrm{e} ^ {\lambda t} H, \\ \end{array}
$$

这里 $c := b_{1} + \frac{\theta - 1}{2}\sigma_{1}^{2} + \frac{3\lambda}{\theta} + \frac{1}{2}b_{1}^{2}\left[(b_{2} \wedge b_{3}) - \frac{\theta - 1}{2}(\sigma_{2}^{2} \vee \sigma_{3}^{2}) - \frac{3\lambda}{\theta}\right]^{-1}$ 和 $H :=$ 

$$
\sup _ {(S, Z) \in \mathbb {R} _ {+} ^ {2}} (1 + S + Z) ^ {\theta - 2} \left\{- a _ {1 1} S ^ {3} + c S ^ {2} + b _ {1} S + \frac {3 \lambda}{\theta} - \frac {1}{2} \left[ \left(b _ {2} \wedge b _ {3}\right) - \frac {\theta - 1}{2} \left(\sigma_ {2} ^ {2} \vee \sigma_ {3} ^ {2}\right) \right. \right.
$$

$-\frac{3\lambda}{\theta}\Bigg]Z^{2}\Bigg\}.$ 这意味着

$$
E [ \mathrm{e} ^ {\lambda t} (1 + U) ^ {\theta} ] \leqslant (1 + U (0)) ^ {\theta} + \frac {\theta \mathrm{e} ^ {\lambda t} H}{\lambda}.
$$

于是

$$
\limsup _ {t \to \infty} E [ (1 + U) ^ {\theta} ] \leqslant \frac {\theta H}{\lambda},
$$

存在一个正常数 M 使得

$$
E [ (1 + U) ^ {\theta} ] \leqslant M, \quad t \geqslant 0.
$$

对充分小的 $\delta > 0, k = 1, 2, \cdots$ ，式 (3.4) 蕴涵着

$$
E \left[ \sup _ {(k - 1) \delta \leqslant t \leqslant k \delta} (1 + U (t)) ^ {\theta} \right] \leqslant E [ (1 + U ((k - 1) \delta)) ^ {\theta} ] + I _ {1} + I _ {2} \leqslant M + I _ {1} + I _ {2},
$$

其中

$$
\begin{array}{l} I _ {1} = E \left[ \sup _ {(k - 1) \delta \leqslant t \leqslant k \delta} \int_ {(k - 1) \delta} ^ {t} \theta (1 + U (s)) ^ {\theta - 2} \left\{- a _ {1 1} S ^ {3} (s) + \left(b _ {1} + \frac {\theta - 1}{2} \sigma_ {1} ^ {2}\right) S ^ {2} (s) \right. \right. \\ \left. + b _ {1} S (s) Z (s) + b _ {1} S (s) - \left[ \left(b _ {2} \wedge b _ {3}\right) - \frac {\theta - 1}{2} \left(\sigma_ {2} ^ {2} \vee \sigma_ {3} ^ {2}\right) \right] Z ^ {2} (s) \right\} d s \Bigg ] \\ \leqslant E \left[ \sup _ {(k - 1) \delta \leqslant t \leqslant k \delta} \int_ {(k - 1) \delta} ^ {t} \theta (1 + U (s)) ^ {\theta - 2} \left\{- a _ {1 1} S ^ {3} (s) + \left(b _ {1} + \frac {\theta - 1}{2} \sigma_ {1} ^ {2} + \frac {1}{2} b _ {1} ^ {2} \right. \right. \left[ (b _ {2} \wedge b _ {3}) \right. \right. \\ \left. \left. - \frac {\theta - 1}{2} \left(\sigma_ {2} ^ {2} \vee \sigma_ {3} ^ {2}\right) \right] ^ {- 1}\right) S ^ {2} (s) + b _ {1} S (s) - \frac {1}{2} \left[ \left(b _ {2} \wedge b _ {3}\right) - \frac {\theta - 1}{2} \left(\sigma_ {2} ^ {2} \vee \sigma_ {3} ^ {2}\right) \right] Z ^ {2} (s) \Bigg \} d s ] \\ \leqslant \theta H _ {0} E \left[ \sup _ {(k - 1) \delta \leqslant t \leqslant k \delta} \int_ {(k - 1) \delta} ^ {t} (1 + U (s)) ^ {\theta} \mathrm{d} s \right] \\ \leqslant \theta H _ {0} E \left[ \int_ {(k - 1) \delta} ^ {k \delta} (1 + U (s)) ^ {\theta} \mathrm{d} s \right] \leqslant \theta H _ {0} \delta E [ \sup _ {(k - 1) \delta \leqslant t \leqslant k \delta} (1 + U (t)) ^ {\theta} ], \\ \end{array}
$$

这里 $H_0 := \sup_{(S, Z) \in \mathbb{R}_+^2} \left\{-a_{11} S^3(s) + \left(b_1 + \frac{\theta - 1}{2} \sigma_1^2 + \frac{1}{2} b_1^2 \left[(b_2 \wedge b_3) - \frac{\theta - 1}{2} (\sigma_2^2 \vee \sigma_3^2)\right]^{-1}\right)\right.$ 

$S^2 (s) + b_1S(s) - \frac{1}{2}\left[(b_2\wedge b_3) - \frac{\theta - 1}{2} (\sigma_2^2\vee \sigma_3^2)\right]Z^2 (s)\Bigg\} ,$ 由Burkholder-Davis-Gundy不等式有

$$
\begin{array}{l} I _ {2} = E \left[ \sup _ {(k - 1) \delta \leqslant t \leqslant k \delta} \right| \int_ {(k - 1) \delta} ^ {t} \theta (1 + U (t)) ^ {\theta - 1} \left(\sigma_ {1} S (s) \mathrm{d} B _ {1} (s) + \sigma_ {2} X _ {1} (s) \mathrm{d} B _ {2} (s) \right. \\ \left. + \sigma_ {3} Y _ {1} (s) \mathrm{d} B _ {3} (s)) \right\rvert ] \\ \leqslant \sqrt {3 2} E \left[ \int_ {(k - 1) \delta} ^ {k \delta} \theta^ {2} (1 + U (s)) ^ {2 (\theta - 1)} \left(\sigma_ {1} ^ {2} S ^ {2} (s) + \sigma_ {2} ^ {2} X _ {1} ^ {2} (s) + \sigma_ {3} ^ {2} Y _ {1} ^ {2} (s)\right) d s \right] ^ {\frac {1}{2}} \\ \leqslant \sqrt {3 2} \theta \left(\sigma_ {1} ^ {2} \vee \sigma_ {2} ^ {2} \vee \sigma_ {3} ^ {2}\right) ^ {\frac {1}{2}} \delta^ {\frac {1}{2}} E \left[ \sup _ {(k - 1) \delta \leqslant t \leqslant k \delta} (1 + U (t)) ^ {2 \theta} \right] ^ {\frac {1}{2}} \\ \leqslant \sqrt {3 2} \theta \left(\sigma_ {1} ^ {2} \vee \sigma_ {2} ^ {2} \vee \sigma_ {3} ^ {2}\right) ^ {\frac {1}{2}} \delta^ {\frac {1}{2}} E \left[ \sup _ {(k - 1) \delta \leqslant t \leqslant k \delta} (1 + U (t)) ^ {\theta} \right]. \\ \end{array}
$$

于是

$$
E \left[ \sup _ {(k - 1) \delta \leqslant t \leqslant k \delta} (1 + U (t)) ^ {\theta} \right]
$$

$$
\begin{array}{l} \leqslant E \left[ (1 + U ((k - 1) \delta)) ^ {\theta} \right] \\ + \left[ \theta H _ {0} \delta + \sqrt {3 2} \theta \left(\sigma_ {1} ^ {2} \vee \sigma_ {2} ^ {2} \vee \sigma_ {3} ^ {2}\right) ^ {\frac {1}{2}} \delta^ {\frac {1}{2}} \right] E \left[ \sup _ {(k - 1) \delta \leqslant t \leqslant k \delta} (1 + U (t)) ^ {\theta} \right]. \\ \end{array}
$$

特别地, 选取 $\delta > 0$ 满足 $\theta H_0\delta + \sqrt{32}\theta (\sigma_1^2 \vee \sigma_2^2 \vee \sigma_3^2)^{\frac{1}{2}}\delta^{\frac{1}{2}} \leqslant \frac{1}{2}$ , 则有

$$
E \left[ \sup _ {(k - 1) \delta \leqslant t \leqslant k \delta} (1 + U (t)) ^ {\theta} \right] \leqslant 2 E [ (1 + U ((k - 1) \delta)) ^ {\theta} ] \leqslant 2 M.
$$

设 $\varepsilon > 0$ 是任意的, 由 Chebyshev 不等式有

$$
P \left\{\omega : \sup _ {(k - 1) \delta \leqslant t \leqslant k \delta} (1 + U (t)) ^ {\theta} > (k \delta) ^ {1 + \varepsilon} \right\} \leqslant \frac {2 M}{(k \delta) ^ {1 + \varepsilon}}, \quad k = 1, 2, \dots .
$$

根据 Borel-Cantelli 引理, 存在整数 $k_{0}(\omega) > \frac{1}{\delta} + 2$ , 对几乎所有的满足 $k \geqslant k_{0}$ 的 $\omega \in \Omega$ , 有

$$
\sup _ {(k - 1) \delta \leqslant t \leqslant k \delta} (1 + U (t)) ^ {\theta} \leqslant (k \delta) ^ {1 + \varepsilon}. \tag {3.5}
$$

于是, 对几乎所有的 $\omega \in \Omega$ , 如果 $k \geqslant k_0$ 且 $(k - 1)\delta \leqslant t \leqslant k\delta$ , 有

$$
\frac {\log (1 + U (t)) ^ {\theta}}{\log t} \leqslant \frac {(1 + \varepsilon) \log (k \delta)}{\log ((k - 1) \delta)}.
$$

因此得到

$$
\limsup _ {t \to \infty} \frac {\log (1 + U (t)) ^ {\theta}}{\log t} \leqslant 1 + \varepsilon \text { a.s. },
$$

令 $\varepsilon \to 0$ ，则有

$$
\limsup _ {t \to \infty} \frac {\log (1 + U (t)) ^ {\theta}}{\log t} \leqslant 1 \text {   a.s.   }
$$

和

$$
\limsup _ {t \to \infty} \frac {\log U (t)}{\log t} \leqslant \limsup _ {t \to \infty} \frac {\log (1 + U (t))}{\log t} \leqslant \frac {1}{\theta} \quad \text { a.s. }
$$

定理3.2 设 $(S(t), X(t), Y(t))$ 是系统(3.3)满足初值 $(S(0), X(0), Y(0)) \in \mathbb{R}_+^3$ 的解. 假设 $(b_2 \wedge b_3) > \frac{1}{2} (\sigma_2^2 \vee \sigma_3^2)$ 成立, 若下面的条件有一个成立, 则系统(3.3)的竞争排斥原理成立.

(i) 若 $b_{1} > \frac{1}{2}\sigma_{1}^{2}, \frac{b_{3}}{b_{31}} \geqslant \frac{b_{2}}{b_{21}}, \frac{b_{3} + \frac{1}{2}\sigma_{3}^{2}}{b_{31}} > \frac{b_{2}}{b_{21}} + \left(\frac{K(\sigma)}{a_{11}}\right)^{\frac{1}{2}}$ 且 $\frac{b_1 - \frac{1}{2}\sigma_1^2}{a_{11}} > \frac{b_2 + \frac{1}{2}\sigma_2^2}{b_{21}}$ ，则

$$
\liminf _ {t \to \infty} \langle X (t) \rangle \geqslant \frac {1}{a _ {1 2}} \left[ b _ {1} - \frac {1}{2} \sigma_ {1} ^ {2} - a _ {1 1} \frac {b _ {2} + \frac {1}{2} \sigma_ {2} ^ {2}}{b _ {2 1}} \right] > 0 \mathrm{a.s.},
$$

$$
\limsup _ {t \to \infty} \frac {\log Y (t)}{t} \leqslant - b _ {3} - \frac {1}{2} \sigma_ {3} ^ {2} + \frac {b _ {3 1} b _ {2}}{b _ {2 1}} + b _ {3 1} \left(\frac {K (\sigma)}{a _ {1 1}}\right) ^ {\frac {1}{2}} <   0 \text { a.s.},
$$

其中 $K(\sigma) = \frac{\overline{S}\sigma_1^2}{2} +\frac{a_{12}\overline{X}\sigma_2^2}{2b_{21}},(\overline{S},\overline{X},0)$ 是系统（3.1）的边界平衡点

(ii) 若 $b_{1} > \frac{1}{2}\sigma_{1}^{2}, \frac{b_{2}}{b_{21}} \geqslant \frac{b_{3}}{b_{31}}, \frac{b_{2} + \frac{1}{2}\sigma_{2}^{2}}{b_{21}} > \frac{b_{3}}{b_{31}} + \left(\frac{K_{0}(\sigma)}{a_{11}}\right)^{\frac{1}{2}}$ 且 $\frac{b_1 - \frac{1}{2}\sigma_1^2}{a_{11}} > \frac{b_3 + \frac{1}{2}\sigma_3^2}{b_{31}}$ ，则

$$
\limsup _ {t \to \infty} \frac {\log X (t)}{t} \leqslant - b _ {2} - \frac {1}{2} \sigma_ {2} ^ {2} + \frac {b _ {2 1} b _ {3}}{b _ {3 1}} + b _ {2 1} \left(\frac {K _ {0} (\sigma)}{a _ {1 1}}\right) ^ {\frac {1}{2}} <   0 \text {   a.s. },
$$

$$
\liminf _ {t \to \infty} \langle Y (t) \rangle \geqslant \frac {1}{a _ {1 3}} \left[ b _ {1} - \frac {1}{2} \sigma_ {1} ^ {2} - a _ {1 1} \frac {b _ {3} + \frac {1}{2} \sigma_ {3} ^ {2}}{b _ {3 1}} \right] > 0 \text {   a.s.,   }
$$

其中 $K_{0}(\sigma) = \frac{\widetilde{S}\sigma_{1}^{2}}{2} +\frac{a_{13}\widetilde{Y}\sigma_{3}^{2}}{2b_{31}},(\widetilde{S},0,\widetilde{Y})$ 是系统(3.1）的边界平衡点

证明 (i) 若 $\frac{b_1 - \frac{1}{2}\sigma_1^2}{a_{11}} >\frac{b_2 + \frac{1}{2}\sigma_2^2}{b_{21}}$ ，则系统(3.1)存在边界平衡点 $(\overline{S},\overline{X},0)$ 即

$$
\overline {{{S}}} = \frac {b _ {2}}{b _ {2 1}}, \quad \overline {{{X}}} = \frac {b _ {1} b _ {2 1} - a _ {1 1} b _ {2}}{a _ {1 2} b _ {2 1}}.
$$

定义

$$
V (S, X, Y) = S - \overline {{S}} - \overline {{S}} \log \frac {S}{\overline {{S}}} + \frac {a _ {1 2}}{b _ {2 1}} \left(X - \overline {{X}} - \overline {{X}} \log \frac {X}{\overline {{X}}}\right) + \frac {a _ {1 3}}{b _ {3 1}} Y.
$$

由 Itô 公式和式 (3.3) 有

$$
\mathrm{d} V = \left\{- a _ {1 1} (S - \overline {{{S}}}) ^ {2} - a _ {1 2} (S - \overline {{{S}}}) (X - \overline {{{X}}}) - a _ {1 3} (S - \overline {{{S}}}) Y + \frac {\overline {{{S}}} \sigma_ {1} ^ {2}}{2} \right\} \mathrm{d} t
$$

$$
+ \sigma_ {1} (S - \overline {{S}}) \mathrm{d} B _ {1} (t) + \frac {a _ {1 2}}{b _ {2 1}} \left\{\left[ b _ {2 1} (S - \overline {{S}}) (X - \overline {{X}}) + \frac {\overline {{X}} \sigma_ {2} ^ {2}}{2} \right] \mathrm{d} t + \sigma_ {2} (X - \overline {{X}}) \mathrm{d} B _ {2} (t) \right\}
$$

$$
+ \frac {a _ {1 3}}{b _ {3 1}} \left\{\left[ (- b _ {3} + b _ {3 1} \overline {{{S}}}) Y + b _ {3 1} (S - \overline {{{S}}}) Y \right] \mathrm{d} t + \sigma_ {3} Y \mathrm{d} B _ {3} (t) \right\}
$$

$$
:= L V \mathrm{d} t + \sigma_ {1} (S - \bar {S}) \mathrm{d} B _ {1} (t) + \frac {a _ {1 2}}{b _ {2 1}} \sigma_ {2} (X - \bar {X}) \mathrm{d} B _ {2} (t) + \frac {a _ {1 3}}{b _ {3 1}} \sigma_ {3} Y \mathrm{d} B _ {3} (t),
$$

其中由 $\frac{b_3}{b_{31}}\geqslant \frac{b_2}{b_{21}}$ 可得

$$
L V = - a _ {1 1} (S - \bar {S}) ^ {2} - a _ {1 2} (S - \bar {S}) (X - \bar {X}) - a _ {1 3} (S - \bar {S}) Y + \frac {\bar {S} \sigma_ {1} ^ {2}}{2}
$$

$$
+ \frac {a _ {1 2}}{b _ {2 1}} \left[ b _ {2 1} (S - \overline {{S}}) (X - \overline {{X}}) + \frac {\overline {{X}} \sigma_ {2} ^ {2}}{2} \right]
$$

$$
+ \frac {a _ {1 3}}{b _ {3 1}} [ (- b _ {3} + b _ {3 1} \overline {{{S}}}) Y + b _ {3 1} (S - \overline {{{S}}}) Y ]
$$

$$
= - a _ {1 1} (S - \overline {{{S}}}) ^ {2} + \frac {\overline {{{S}}} \sigma_ {1} ^ {2}}{2} + \frac {a _ {1 2} \overline {{{X}}} \sigma_ {2} ^ {2}}{2 b _ {2 1}} + \frac {a _ {1 3}}{b _ {3 1}} (- b _ {3} + b _ {3 1} \overline {{{S}}}) Y
$$

$$
\leqslant - a _ {1 1} (S - \overline {{{{S}}}}) ^ {2} + K (\sigma).
$$

两端由0到 $t$ 积分得到

$$
V (t) - V (0)
$$

$$
\leqslant - a _ {1 1} \int_ {0} ^ {t} (S (s) - \overline {{{{S}}}}) ^ {2} \mathrm{d} s + K (\sigma) t + \int_ {0} ^ {t} \sigma_ {1} (S (s) - \overline {{{{S}}}}) \mathrm{d} B _ {1} (s)
$$

$$
+ \frac {a _ {1 2}}{b _ {2 1}} \sigma_ {2} (X (s) - \overline {{{X}}}) \mathrm{d} B _ {2} (s) + \frac {a _ {1 3}}{b _ {3 1}} \sigma_ {3} Y (s) \mathrm{d} B _ {3} (s). \tag {3.6}
$$

设

$$
M (t) = \int_ {0} ^ {t} \sigma_ {1} (S (s) - \overline {{{{S}}}}) \mathrm{d} B _ {1} (s) + \frac {a _ {1 2}}{b _ {2 1}} \sigma_ {2} (X (s) - \overline {{{{X}}}}) \mathrm{d} B _ {2} (s) + \frac {a _ {1 3}}{b _ {3 1}} \sigma_ {3} Y (s) \mathrm{d} B _ {3} (s)
$$

是实值连续局部鞅且 $M(0)=0$ .

由引理3.1, 对任意小的 $0 < \varepsilon < \frac{1}{2} - \frac{1}{\theta}$ , 存在常数 $T = T(\omega)$ 和集合 $\Omega_{\varepsilon}$ 使得 $P(\Omega_{\varepsilon}) \geqslant 1 - \varepsilon$ , 对 $t \geqslant T, \omega \in \Omega_{\varepsilon}$ ,

$$
U (t) \leqslant t ^ {\frac {1}{\theta} + \varepsilon},
$$

则有

$$
\begin{array}{l} \int_ {0} ^ {\infty} \frac {\mathrm{d} \langle M , M \rangle_ {t}}{(1 + t) ^ {2}} \leqslant \int_ {0} ^ {\infty} \frac {\sigma_ {1} ^ {2} (S - \overline {{S}}) ^ {2} + \frac {a _ {1 2} ^ {2}}{b _ {2 1} ^ {2}} \sigma_ {2} ^ {2} (X - \overline {{X}}) ^ {2} + \frac {a _ {1 3} ^ {2}}{b _ {3 1} ^ {2}} \sigma_ {3} ^ {2} Y ^ {2}}{(1 + t) ^ {2}} \mathrm{d} t \\ \leqslant 2 \int_ {0} ^ {\infty} \frac {U ^ {2}}{(1 + t) ^ {2}} \mathrm{d} t + 2 \int_ {0} ^ {\infty} \frac {\sigma_ {1} ^ {2} \overline {{{S}}} ^ {2} + \frac {a _ {1 2} ^ {2}}{b _ {2 1} ^ {2}} \sigma_ {2} ^ {2} \overline {{{X}}} ^ {2}}{(1 + t) ^ {2}} \mathrm{d} t <   \infty . \\ \end{array}
$$

令 $\varepsilon \rightarrow 0$ ，应用强大数定律得到

$$
\lim _ {t \to \infty} \frac {M (t)}{t} = 0 \text { a.s. }
$$

由式 (3.6) 得到

$$
\lim _ {t \to \infty} \sup \frac {1}{t} \int_ {0} ^ {t} (S (s) - \overline {{{S}}}) ^ {2} \mathrm{d} s \leqslant \frac {K (\sigma)}{a _ {1 1}} \quad \text { a.s. } \tag {3.7}
$$

由 Itô 公式有

$$
\begin{array}{l} \mathrm{d} \log Y = \left(- b _ {3} + b _ {3 1} S - \frac {1}{2} \sigma_ {3} ^ {2}\right) \mathrm{d} t + \sigma_ {3} \mathrm{d} B _ {3} (t) \\ = \left[ - b _ {3} - \frac {1}{2} \sigma_ {3} ^ {2} + b _ {3 1} \overline {{{S}}} + b _ {3 1} (S - \overline {{{S}}}) \right] \mathrm{d} t + \sigma_ {3} \mathrm{d} B _ {3} (t), \\ \end{array}
$$

且

$$
\begin{array}{l} \frac {\log Y (t)}{t} = \frac {\log Y (0)}{t} - b _ {3} - \frac {1}{2} \sigma_ {3} ^ {2} + b _ {3 1} \overline {{{S}}} + b _ {3 1} \langle S (t) - \overline {{{S}}} \rangle + \frac {\sigma_ {3} B _ {3} (t)}{t} \\ \leqslant \frac {\log Y (0)}{t} - b _ {3} - \frac {1}{2} \sigma_ {3} ^ {2} + b _ {3 1} \overline {{{S}}} + b _ {3 1} \left[ \frac {1}{t} \int_ {0} ^ {t} (S (s) - \overline {{{S}}}) ^ {2} \mathrm{d} s \right] ^ {\frac {1}{2}} + \frac {\sigma_ {3} B _ {3} (t)}{t}, \\ \end{array}
$$

由 $\lim_{t\to \infty}\frac{B_3(t)}{t} = 0$ 和式(3.7)可得

$$
\limsup _ {t \to \infty} \frac {\log Y (t)}{t} \leqslant - b _ {3} - \frac {1}{2} \sigma_ {3} ^ {2} + \frac {b _ {3 1} b _ {2}}{b _ {2 1}} + b _ {3 1} \left(\frac {K (\sigma)}{a _ {1 1}}\right) ^ {\frac {1}{2}} \quad \text { a.s. }
$$

由条件 $\frac{b_3 + \frac{1}{2}\sigma_3^2}{b_{31}} >\frac{b_2}{b_{21}} +\left(\frac{K(\sigma)}{a_{11}}\right)^{\frac{1}{2}}$ ，易知

$$
\limsup _ {t \to \infty} \frac {\log Y (t)}{t} \leqslant - b _ {3} - \frac {1}{2} \sigma_ {3} ^ {2} + \frac {b _ {3 1} b _ {2}}{b _ {2 1}} + b _ {3 1} \left(\frac {K (\sigma)}{a _ {1 1}}\right) ^ {\frac {1}{2}} <   0 \text {   a.s.. }
$$

也就是说, $Y(t)$ 几乎必然以指数收敛于零.

由式 (3.3) 得到

$$
\begin{array}{l} \frac {\log S (t)}{t} = \frac {\log S (0)}{t} + b _ {1} - \frac {1}{2} \sigma_ {1} ^ {2} - a _ {1 1} \langle S (t) \rangle \\ - a _ {1 2} \langle X (t) \rangle - a _ {1 3} \langle Y (t) \rangle + \frac {\sigma_ {1} B _ {1} (t)}{t} \tag {3.8} \\ \end{array}
$$

和

$$
\frac {\log X (t)}{t} = \frac {\log X (0)}{t} - b _ {2} - \frac {1}{2} \sigma_ {2} ^ {2} + b _ {2 1} \langle S (t) \rangle + \frac {\sigma_ {2} B _ {2} (t)}{t}. \tag {3.9}
$$

由引理3.1, 可知

$$
\limsup _ {t \to \infty} \frac {\log U (t)}{t} \leqslant 0 \text { a.s. }
$$

这意味着

$$
\limsup _ {t \to \infty} \frac {\log X (t)}{t} \leqslant \limsup _ {t \to \infty} \frac {\log U (t)}{t} \leqslant 0 \text {   a.s. }, \tag {3.10}
$$

$$
\limsup _ {t \rightarrow \infty} \frac {\log S (t)}{t} \leqslant \limsup _ {t \rightarrow \infty} \frac {\log U (t)}{t} \leqslant 0 \quad \text { a   .   s   . }. \tag {3.11}
$$

把式 (3.10) 代入式 (3.9) 得到

$$
\lim _ {t \to \infty} \sup \langle S (t) \rangle \leqslant \frac {b _ {2} + \frac {1}{2} \sigma_ {2} ^ {2}}{b _ {2 1}} \quad \text { a.s. } \tag {3.12}
$$

由式(3.8)，(3.11)和(3.12)，并利用 $\lim_{t\to \infty}\langle Y(t)\rangle = 0$ 和条件 $\frac{b_1 - \frac{1}{2}\sigma_1^2}{a_{11}} >\frac{b_2 + \frac{1}{2}\sigma_2^2}{b_{21}}$ 可得

$$
\liminf _ {t \to \infty} \langle X (t) \rangle \geqslant \frac {1}{a _ {1 2}} \left[ b _ {1} - \frac {1}{2} \sigma_ {1} ^ {2} - a _ {1 1} \frac {b _ {2} + \frac {1}{2} \sigma_ {2} ^ {2}}{b _ {2 1}} \right] > 0 \text {   a.s.. }
$$

(ii) 证明类似于 (i) 的证明.

定理3.3 设 $(S(t), X(t), Y(t))$ 是系统(3.3)满足初值 $(S(0), X(0), Y(0)) \in \mathbb{R}_+^3$ 的解. 若 $b_1 > \frac{1}{2}\sigma_1^2$ , $\frac{b_1 - \frac{1}{2}\sigma_1^2}{a_{11}} < \frac{b_2 + \frac{1}{2}\sigma_2^2}{b_{21}}$ 且 $\frac{b_1 - \frac{1}{2}\sigma_1^2}{a_{11}} < \frac{b_3 + \frac{1}{2}\sigma_3^2}{b_{31}}$ , 则系统(3.3)的竞争排斥原理不成立. 在这种情况下,

$$
\begin{array}{l} \lim _ {t \rightarrow \infty} \langle S (t) \rangle = \frac {b _ {1} - \frac {1}{2} \sigma_ {1} ^ {2}}{a _ {1 1}} \text { a   .   s   . }, \\ \operatorname * {l i m s u p} _ {t \to \infty} \frac {\log X (t)}{t} \leqslant - b _ {2} - \frac {1}{2} \sigma_ {2} ^ {2} + b _ {2 1} \frac {b _ {1} - \frac {1}{2} \sigma_ {1} ^ {2}}{a _ {1 1}} <   0 \text {   a.s., } \\ \limsup _ {t \to \infty} \frac {\log Y (t)}{t} \leqslant - b _ {3} - \frac {1}{2} \sigma_ {3} ^ {2} + b _ {3 1} \frac {b _ {1} - \frac {1}{2} \sigma_ {1} ^ {2}}{a _ {1 1}} <   0 \text { a.s. } \\ \end{array}
$$

此外，若 $b_{1} < \frac{1}{2}\sigma_{1}^{2}$ ，则

$$
\lim _ {t \to \infty} S (t) = 0, \quad \lim _ {t \to \infty} X (t) = 0, \quad \lim _ {t \to \infty} Y (t) = 0.
$$

证明 因为系统 (3.3) 的解是正的, 所以由随机微分方程的比较定理可以得出 $S(t) \leqslant \phi(t)$ a.s., 其中 $\phi(t)$ 是随机 Logistic 方程

$$
\mathrm{d} \phi (t) = \phi (t) \left(b _ {1} - a _ {1 1} \phi (t)\right) \mathrm{d} t + \sigma_ {1} \phi (t) \mathrm{d} B _ {1} (t)
$$

的解.

由文献 [57] 的结论可知, 若 $b_{1} > \frac{\sigma_{1}^{2}}{2}$ , 则

$$
\lim _ {t \to \infty} \langle \phi (t) \rangle = \frac {b _ {1} - \frac {\sigma_ {1} ^ {2}}{2}}{a _ {1 1}}, \quad \lim _ {t \to \infty} \frac {\log \phi (t)}{t} = 0 \text {   a.s..   } \tag {3.13}
$$

由 Itô 公式有

$$
\mathrm{d} \log X = \left(- b _ {2} + b _ {2 1} S - \frac {\sigma_ {2} ^ {2}}{2}\right) \mathrm{d} t + \sigma_ {2} \mathrm{d} B _ {2} (t) \leqslant \left(- b _ {2} + b _ {2 1} \phi - \frac {\sigma_ {2} ^ {2}}{2}\right) \mathrm{d} t + \sigma_ {2} \mathrm{d} B _ {2} (t)
$$

和

$$
\frac {\log X (t)}{t} \leqslant \frac {\log X (0)}{t} - b _ {2} - \frac {\sigma_ {2} ^ {2}}{2} + b _ {2 1} \langle \phi (t) \rangle + \sigma_ {2} \frac {B _ {2} (t)}{t},
$$

由式(3.13)和条件 $\frac{b_1 - \frac{1}{2}\sigma_1^2}{a_{11}} < \frac{b_2 + \frac{1}{2}\sigma_2^2}{b_{21}}$ 易知

$$
\lim _ {t \to \infty} \sup \frac {\log X (t)}{t} \leqslant - b _ {2} - \frac {\sigma_ {2} ^ {2}}{2} + b _ {2 1} \frac {b _ {1} - \frac {1}{2} \sigma_ {1} ^ {2}}{a _ {1 1}} <   0 \quad \text { a.s. } \tag {3.14}
$$

也就是说， $X(t)$ 几乎必然以指数收敛于零.

由 Itô 公式有

$$
\mathrm{d} \log Y = \left(- b _ {3} + b _ {3 1} S - \frac {\sigma_ {3} ^ {2}}{2}\right) \mathrm{d} t + \sigma_ {3} \mathrm{d} B _ {3} (t) \leqslant \left(- b _ {3} + b _ {3 1} \phi - \frac {\sigma_ {3} ^ {2}}{2}\right) \mathrm{d} t + \sigma_ {3} \mathrm{d} B _ {3} (t),
$$

于是

$$
\frac {\log Y (t)}{t} \leqslant \frac {\log Y (0)}{t} - b _ {3} - \frac {\sigma_ {3} ^ {2}}{2} + b _ {3 1} \langle \phi (t) \rangle + \sigma_ {3} \frac {B _ {3} (t)}{t}.
$$

由式(3.13)和条件 $\frac{b_1 - \frac{1}{2}\sigma_1^2}{a_{11}} < \frac{b_3 + \frac{1}{2}\sigma_3^2}{b_{31}}$ 可得

$$
\lim _ {t \to \infty} \sup \frac {\log Y (t)}{t} \leqslant - b _ {3} - \frac {\sigma_ {3} ^ {2}}{2} + b _ {3 1} \frac {b _ {1} - \frac {1}{2} \sigma_ {1} ^ {2}}{a _ {1 1}} <   0 \text {   a.s.,   } \tag {3.15}
$$

也就是说, $Y(t)$ 几乎必然以指数收敛于零.

由式 (3.8) 可得

$$
a _ {1 1} \langle S (t) \rangle = b _ {1} - \frac {\sigma_ {1} ^ {2}}{2} - \frac {\log S (t) - \log S (0)}{t} - a _ {1 2} \langle X (t) \rangle - a _ {1 3} \langle Y (t) \rangle + \sigma_ {1} \frac {B _ {1} (t)}{t}
$$

$$
\geqslant b _ {1} - \frac {\sigma_ {1} ^ {2}}{2} - \frac {\log \phi (t) - \log S (0)}{t} - a _ {1 2} \langle X (t) \rangle - a _ {1 3} \langle Y (t) \rangle + \sigma_ {1} \frac {B _ {1} (t)}{t}.
$$

再由式 $(3.13)\sim(3.15)$ 可得

$$
\liminf _ {t \to \infty} \langle S (t) \rangle \geqslant \frac {b _ {1} - \frac {\sigma_ {1} ^ {2}}{2}}{a _ {1 1}}.
$$

由

$$
\frac {b _ {1} - \frac {\sigma_ {1} ^ {2}}{2}}{a _ {1 1}} \leqslant \liminf _ {t \to \infty} \langle S (t) \rangle \leqslant \limsup _ {t \to \infty} \langle S (t) \rangle \leqslant \limsup _ {t \to \infty} \langle \phi (t) \rangle = \frac {b _ {1} - \frac {\sigma_ {1} ^ {2}}{2}}{a _ {1 1}},
$$

可得

$$
\lim _ {t \to \infty} \langle S (t) \rangle = \frac {b _ {1} - \frac {\sigma_ {1} ^ {2}}{2}}{a _ {1 1}} \quad \text { a.s. }
$$

若 $b_{1} < \frac{1}{2}\sigma_{1}^{2}$ , 由式 (3.13)~(3.15), 易知

$$
\lim _ {t \rightarrow \infty} S (t) = 0, \quad \lim _ {t \rightarrow \infty} X (t) = 0, \quad \lim _ {t \rightarrow \infty} Y (t) = 0.
$$

## 3.2.3 系统（3.3）的数值模拟

下面我们用 Milstein 高阶方法 [33] 进行数值模拟验证所得到的结论.

第一组数值试验使用以下参数:

$$
b _ {1} = 1, b _ {2} = 1. 2, b _ {3} = 1. 5, a _ {1 1} = 0. 3, a _ {1 2} = 0. 2, a _ {1 3} = 0. 3, b _ {2 1} = 0. 4, b _ {3 1} = 0. 3.
$$

这意味着 $\frac{b_2}{b_{21}} < \frac{b_1}{a_{11}} < \frac{b_3}{b_{31}}$ 。对确定性系统 (3.1)，捕食者 $X(t)$ 幸存，捕食者 $Y(t)$ 灭绝。

因为 $(\overline{S},\overline{X},0)$ 是系统(3.1)的平衡点，所以

$$
\overline {{{{S}}}} = \frac {b _ {2}}{b _ {2 1}} = 3, \quad \overline {{{{X}}}} = \frac {b _ {1} b _ {2 1} - a _ {1 1} b _ {2}}{a _ {1 2} b _ {2 1}} = 0. 5.
$$

接下来, 我们将观察在环境扰动下的随机系统 (3.3) 的竞争角色.

(i) 选取 $\sigma_{1} = 0.1, \sigma_{2} = 0.1, \sigma_{3} = 0.1$ ，则

$$
1 = b _ {1} > \frac {1}{2} \sigma_ {1} ^ {2} = 0. 0 0 5, \quad 5 = \frac {b _ {3}}{b _ {3 1}} \geqslant \frac {b _ {2}}{b _ {2 1}} = 3, \quad 5. 0 1 7 = \frac {b _ {3} + \frac {1}{2} \sigma_ {3} ^ {2}}{b _ {3 1}} > \frac {b _ {2}}{b _ {2 1}} + \left(\frac {K (\sigma)}{a _ {1 1}}\right) ^ {\frac {1}{2}} = 3. 2 3
$$

和

$$
3. 3 1 7 = \frac {b _ {1} - \frac {1}{2} \sigma_ {1} ^ {2}}{a _ {1 1}} > \frac {b _ {2} + \frac {1}{2} \sigma_ {2} ^ {2}}{b _ {2 1}} = 3. 0 1 2 5.
$$

由定理3.2, 对初值 $S(0) = 1, X(0) = 1, Y(0) = 1$ , 系统 (3.3) 的解有

$$
\liminf _ {t \to \infty} \langle X (t) \rangle \geqslant \frac {1}{a _ {1 2}} \left[ b _ {1} - \frac {1}{2} \sigma_ {1} ^ {2} - a _ {1 1} \frac {b _ {2} + \frac {1}{2} \sigma_ {2} ^ {2}}{b _ {2 1}} \right] = 0. 4 5 6 2 5 > 0 \mathrm{a.s.,}
$$

$$
\limsup _ {t \to \infty} \frac {\log Y (t)}{t} \leqslant - b _ {3} - \frac {1}{2} \sigma_ {3} ^ {2} + \frac {b _ {3 1} b _ {2}}{b _ {2 1}} + b _ {3 1} \left(\frac {K (\sigma)}{a _ {1 1}}\right) ^ {\frac {1}{2}} = - 0. 5 3 6 <   0 \text { a.s.. }
$$

从图 3.1 的数值模拟可见捕食者 $X(t)$ 幸存和捕食者 $Y(t)$ 灭绝.

此外, 选取与图 3.1 相同的参数, 但是改变白噪声的强度 ( $\sigma_1^2 = \sigma_2^2 = \sigma_3^2 = 0.1^2$ 和 $\sigma_1^2 = \sigma_2^2 = \sigma_3^2 = 0.06^2$ ), 这些都满足定理 3.2 的条件. 正如预期的那样, 图 3.2 和图 3.3 显示解 (波动较大的曲线) 在一个小区域震动. 通过比较图 3.2 和图 3.3, 可以看到随着白噪声强度的减小, 解震动的区域也随之变小. 从它们的密度函数 (图 3.2 和图 3.3(b), (d)) 可以看出 $(S(t), X(t))$ 存在平稳分布.

(ii) 选取 $\sigma_{1} = 0.1, \sigma_{2} = 0.6, \sigma_{3} = 0.3$ . 定理3.3的条件

$$
1 = b _ {1} > \frac {1}{2} \sigma_ {1} ^ {2} = 0. 0 0 5, \quad 3. 3 1 7 = \frac {b _ {1} - \frac {1}{2} \sigma_ {1} ^ {2}}{a _ {1 1}} <   \frac {b _ {2} + \frac {1}{2} \sigma_ {2} ^ {2}}{b _ {2 1}} = 3. 4 5
$$

和

$$
3. 3 1 7 = \frac {b _ {1} - \frac {1}{2} \sigma_ {1} ^ {2}}{a _ {1 1}} <   \frac {b _ {3} + \frac {1}{2} \sigma_ {3} ^ {2}}{b _ {3 1}} = 5. 1 5
$$

满足，则

$$
\begin{array}{l} \lim _ {t \rightarrow \infty} \langle S (t) \rangle = \frac {b _ {1} - \frac {1}{2} \sigma_ {1} ^ {2}}{a _ {1 1}} = 3. 3 1 7 \text { a   .   s   . }, \\ \limsup _ {t \to \infty} \frac {\log X (t)}{t} \leqslant - b _ {2} - \frac {1}{2} \sigma_ {2} ^ {2} + b _ {2 1} \frac {b _ {1} - \frac {1}{2} \sigma_ {1} ^ {2}}{a _ {1 1}} = - 0. 0 5 3 3 <   0 \text {   a.s.,   } \\ \limsup _ {t \to \infty} \frac {\log Y (t)}{t} \leqslant - b _ {3} - \frac {1}{2} \sigma_ {3} ^ {2} + b _ {3 1} \frac {b _ {1} - \frac {1}{2} \sigma_ {1} ^ {2}}{a _ {1 1}} = - 0. 5 4 9 9 <   0 \text {   a.s.   } \\ \end{array}
$$

从图 3.4 的数值模拟可见竞争的捕食者 $X(t)$ 和 $Y(t)$ 都灭绝.

(iii) 选取 $\sigma_{1} = 1.5, \sigma_{2} = 0.8, \sigma_{3} = 1.0$ . 在这种情况下, $1 = b_{1} < \frac{1}{2}\sigma_{1}^{2} = 1.125$ , 则

$$
\lim _ {t \to \infty} S (t) = 0, \quad \lim _ {t \to \infty} X (t) = 0, \quad \lim _ {t \to \infty} Y (t) = 0.
$$

图3.5 系统(3.3)和对应的确定性系统(3.1)在初值 $S(0) = 1, X(0) = 1, Y(0) = 1$ 及 $\sigma_{1} = 1.5, \sigma_{2} = 0.8, \sigma_{3} = 1.0$ 下解的数值模拟


第二组数值试验使用以下参数:

$$
b _ {1} = 1. 5, b _ {2} = 0. 9, b _ {3} = 1. 2, a _ {1 1} = 0. 3, a _ {1 2} = 0. 2, a _ {1 3} = 0. 3, b _ {2 1} = 0. 3, b _ {3 1} = 0. 4.
$$

这意味着 $\frac{b_2}{b_{21}} = \frac{b_3}{b_{31}} < \frac{b_1}{a_{11}}$ 对确定性系统(3.1)，两个相互竞争的捕食者共存于一个正的平衡点，则

$$
S ^ {*} = \frac {b _ {2}}{b _ {2 1}} = 3, \quad 2 X ^ {*} + 3 Y ^ {*} = 6.
$$

然而, 选取 $\sigma_{1} = 0.1$ , $\sigma_{2} = 0.1$ , $\sigma_{3} = 0.6$ . 定理 3.2 的条件

$$
\begin{array}{l} 1. 5 = b _ {1} > \frac {1}{2} \sigma_ {1} ^ {2} = 0. 0 0 5, \quad 3 = \frac {b _ {3}}{b _ {3 1}} \geqslant \frac {b _ {2}}{b _ {2 1}} = 3, \\ 3. 4 5 = \frac {b _ {3} + \frac {1}{2} \sigma_ {3} ^ {2}}{b _ {3 1}} > \max \left\{\frac {b _ {2}}{b _ {2 1}} + \left(\frac {K (\sigma)}{a _ {1 1}}\right) ^ {\frac {1}{2}} \right\} = 3. 2 8 \\ \end{array}
$$

和

$$
4. 9 8 3 = \frac {b _ {1} - \frac {1}{2} \sigma_ {1} ^ {2}}{a _ {1 1}} > \frac {b _ {2} + \frac {1}{2} \sigma_ {2} ^ {2}}{b _ {2 1}} = 3. 0 1 7
$$

满足. 对随机系统 (3.3), 从图 3.6 的数值模拟可见捕食者 $X(t)$ 幸存, 捕食者 $Y(t)$ 灭绝.

(a)

图3.6 系统(3.3)和对应的确定性系统(3.1)在初值 $S(0) = 1, X(0) = 1, Y(0) = 1$ 及 $\sigma_{1} = 0.1, \sigma_{2} = 0.1, \sigma_{3} = 0.6$ 下解的数值模拟


## 3.3 具有 Holling II 功能性反应的随机恒化器模型的竞争排斥原理

本节我们考虑系统 (3.2) 的随机模型如下:

$$
\left\{ \begin{array}{l} \mathrm{d} S (t) = \left[ (S ^ {0} - S (t)) D - \sum_ {i = 1} ^ {n} \frac {a _ {i} S (t)}{1 + b _ {i} S (t)} x _ {i} (t) \right] \mathrm{d} t + \sigma_ {1} S (t) \mathrm{d} B _ {1} (t), \\ \mathrm{d} x _ {i} (t) = \left(- d _ {i} x _ {i} (t) + \frac {a _ {i} S (t)}{1 + b _ {i} S (t)} x _ {i} (t)\right) \mathrm{d} t \\ \quad + \sigma_ {i + 1} x _ {i} (t) \mathrm{d} B _ {i + 1} (t), \quad i = 1, 2, \dots , n, \end{array} \right. \tag {3.16}
$$

其中 $B_{i}(t)$ ( $i=1,2,\cdots,n+1$ ) 是独立的布朗运动, $\sigma_{i}^{2}$ ( $i=1,2,\cdots,n+1$ ) 是环境白噪声的强度.

## 3.3.1 系统 (3.16) 正解的存在唯一性

定理 3.4 对任何初值 $(S(0), x_{1}(0), \cdots, x_{n}(0)) \in \mathbb{R}_{+}^{n+1}$ ，系统 (3.16) 几乎必然存在唯一的正解 $(S(t), x_{1}(t), \cdots, x_{n}(t))$ .

证明 考虑初值为 $(u(0), v_{1}(0), \cdots, v_{n}(0)) = (\log S(0), \log x_{1}(0), \cdots, \log x_{n}(0))$ 

的方程

$$
\left\{ \begin{array}{l} \mathrm{d} u (t) = \left[ \frac {\left(S ^ {0} - \mathrm{e} ^ {u (t)}\right) D}{\mathrm{e} ^ {u (t)}} - \sum_ {i = 1} ^ {n} \frac {a _ {i} \mathrm{e} ^ {v _ {i} (t)}}{1 + b _ {i} \mathrm{e} ^ {u (t)}} - \frac {\sigma_ {1} ^ {2}}{2} \right] \mathrm{d} t + \sigma_ {1} \mathrm{d} B _ {1} (t), \\ \mathrm{d} v _ {i} (t) = \left(- d _ {i} + \frac {a _ {i} \mathrm{e} ^ {u (t)}}{1 + b _ {i} \mathrm{e} ^ {u (t)}} - \frac {\sigma_ {i + 1} ^ {2}}{2}\right) \mathrm{d} t + \sigma_ {i + 1} \mathrm{d} B _ {i + 1} (t), \quad i = 1, 2, \dots , n, \end{array} \right. \tag {3.17}
$$

显然, 系统 (3.17) 的系数是局部 Lipschitz 连续的, 则系统存在唯一的局部解 $(u(t), v_1(t), \cdots, v_n(t))$ , 其中 $\tau_e$ 是爆破时间. 于是, 由 Itô 公式, 显然可得 $(S(t) = \mathrm{e}^{u(t)}, I(t), P(t))$ 是系统 (3.16) 初值为 $(S(0), x_1(0), \cdots, x_n(0)) \in \mathbb{R}_+^{n+1}$ 的唯一局部正解.

下面证明此解是全局存在的, 显然只需证明 $\tau_{e} = \infty$ a.s. 令 $k_{0} \geqslant 0$ 充分大使得 $S(0), x_{1}(0), \cdots, x_{n}(0)$ 都在区间 $\left[\frac{1}{k_{0}}, k_{0}\right]$ 中. 对每个整数 $k \geqslant k_{0}$ , 定义停时

$$
\tau_ {k} = \inf \left\{t \in [ 0, \tau_ {e}): \min \{S (t), x _ {1} (t), \dots , x _ {n} (t) \} \leqslant \frac {1}{k} \right.
$$

或 $\max\{S(t), x_{1}(t), \cdots, x_{n}(t)\} \geqslant k$ 

其中, 约定 $\inf \varnothing = \infty$ . 显然, $\tau_{k}$ 当 $k \to \infty$ 时是单调增加的. 令 $\tau_{\infty} = \lim_{k \to \infty} \tau_{k}$ , 显然 $\tau_{\infty} \leqslant \tau_{e}$ a.s. 若能证明 $\tau_{\infty} = \infty$ a.s., 则 $\tau_{e} = \infty$ , 即 $(S(0), x_{1}(0), \cdots, x_{n}(0)) \in \mathbb{R}_{+}^{n+1}$ a.s. 换言之, 完成定理的证明只需证明 $\tau_{\infty} = \infty$ a.s.. 若不然, 则存在常数 $T > 0$ 和 $\varepsilon \in (0,1)$ , 使得

$$
P \{\tau_ {\infty} \leqslant T \} > \varepsilon ,
$$

于是存在整数 $k_{1} \geqslant k_{0}$ 使得

$$
P \{\tau_ {k} \leqslant T \} \geqslant \varepsilon , \quad \text {对所有} \quad k \geqslant k _ {1}.
$$

定义 $C^2$ 函数 $V:\mathbb{R}_+^{n + 1}\to \overline{\mathbb{R}}_+$ 

$$
V (S, x _ {1}, \dots , x _ {n}) = S - a - a \log \frac {S}{a} + \sum_ {i = 1} ^ {n} (x _ {i} - 1 - \log x _ {i}),
$$

其中 $a = \min\left\{\frac{d_{1}}{a_{1}}, \frac{d_{2}}{a_{2}}, \cdots, \frac{d_{n}}{a_{n}}\right\}$ . 此函数的非负性可以由 $u - 1 - \log u \geqslant 0, \forall u > 0$ 看出. 由 Itô 公式, 有

$$
\begin{array}{l} \mathrm{d} V = \left\{\left(1 - \frac {a}{S}\right) \left[ (S ^ {0} - S) D - \sum_ {i = 1} ^ {n} \frac {a _ {i} S x _ {i}}{1 + b _ {i} S} \right] + \frac {a \sigma_ {1} ^ {2}}{2} \right\} \mathrm{d} t + \sigma_ {1} (S - a) \mathrm{d} B _ {1} (t) \\ + \sum_ {i = 1} ^ {n} \left\{\left[ (x _ {i} - 1) \left(- d _ {i} + \frac {a _ {i} S}{1 + b _ {i} S}\right) + \frac {\sigma_ {i + 1} ^ {2}}{2} \right] \mathrm{d} t + \sigma_ {i + 1} (x _ {i} - 1) \mathrm{d} B _ {i + 1} (t) \right\} \\ := L V \mathrm{d} t + \sigma_ {1} (S - a) \mathrm{d} B _ {1} (t) + \sum_ {i = 1} ^ {n} \sigma_ {i + 1} \left(x _ {i} - 1\right) \mathrm{d} B _ {i + 1} (t), \\ \end{array}
$$

这里由 $a$ 的定义可得

$$
\begin{array}{l} L V = \left(1 - \frac {a}{S}\right) \left[ (S ^ {0} - S) D - \sum_ {i = 1} ^ {n} \frac {a _ {i} S x _ {i}}{1 + b _ {i} S} \right] + \frac {a \sigma_ {1} ^ {2}}{2} \\ + \sum_ {i = 1} ^ {n} \left[ \left(x _ {i} - 1\right) \left(- d _ {i} + \frac {a _ {i} S}{1 + b _ {i} S}\right) + \frac {\sigma_ {i + 1} ^ {2}}{2} \right] \\ = (S ^ {0} + a) D + \sum_ {i = 1} ^ {n} d _ {i} + \frac {a \sigma_ {1} ^ {2}}{2} + \sum_ {i = 1} ^ {n} \frac {\sigma_ {i + 1} ^ {2}}{2} + \sum_ {i = 1} ^ {n} \frac {a a _ {i} x _ {i}}{1 + b _ {i} S} - \sum_ {i = 1} ^ {n} d _ {i} x _ {i} - S D - \frac {a S ^ {0} D}{S} \\ - \sum_ {i = 1} ^ {n} \frac {a _ {i} S x _ {i}}{1 + b _ {i} S} \\ \leqslant (S ^ {0} + a) D + \sum_ {i = 1} ^ {n} d _ {i} + \frac {a \sigma_ {1} ^ {2}}{2} + \sum_ {i = 1} ^ {n} \frac {\sigma_ {i + 1} ^ {2}}{2} + \sum_ {i = 1} ^ {n} (a a _ {i} - d _ {i}) x _ {i} \\ \leqslant (S ^ {0} + a) D + \sum_ {i = 1} ^ {n} d _ {i} + \frac {a \sigma_ {1} ^ {2}}{2} + \sum_ {i = 1} ^ {n} \frac {\sigma_ {i + 1} ^ {2}}{2}. \\ \end{array}
$$

用 A. Gray 在文献 [49] 中的定理 3.1 类似的证明方法, 可以证明结果成立. □

## 3.3.2 系统 (3.16) 的竞争排斥原理

为方便起见, 定义

$$
\langle x (t) \rangle = \frac {1}{t} \int_ {0} ^ {t} x (r) \mathrm{d} r.
$$

类似于文献 [50] 的引理 2.1 和引理 2.2 以及文献 [51] 的定理 2.4 的证明, 我们可以证明以下结论.

引理 3.2 设 $(S(t), x_{1}(t), \cdots, x_{n}(t))$ 是系统 (3.16) 满足初值 $(S(0), x_{1}(0), \cdots, x_{n}(0)) \in \mathbb{R}_{+}^{n+1}$ 的解，则

$$
\limsup _ {t \to \infty} \frac {\log \left[ S (t) + \sum_ {i = 1} ^ {n} x _ {i} (t) \right]}{\log t} \leqslant \frac {1}{\theta} \text {   a.s.,   }
$$

其中 $\theta$ 是一个满足 $1 < \theta < 1 + \frac{2\min\{D, d_{1}, \cdots, d_{n}\}}{\sigma_{1}^{2} \vee \sigma_{2}^{2} \vee \cdots \vee \sigma_{n+1}^{2}}$ 的正常数.

证明 令 $u(t) = S(t) + \sum_{i=1}^{n} x_i(t)$ . 定义

$$
W (u) = (1 + u) ^ {\theta},
$$

其中 $\theta$ 是一个待定的正常数，则

$$
\mathrm{d} W (u) = L W (u) \mathrm{d} t + \theta (1 + u (t)) ^ {\theta - 1} \left[ \sigma_ {1} S (t) \mathrm{d} B _ {1} (t) + \sum_ {i = 1} ^ {n} \sigma_ {i + 1} x _ {i} (t) \mathrm{d} B _ {i + 1} (t) \right],
$$

其中

$$
L W (u) = \theta (1 + u) ^ {\theta - 1} \left(S ^ {0} D - D S - \sum_ {i = 1} ^ {n} d _ {i} x _ {i}\right)
$$

$$
+ \frac {\theta (\theta - 1)}{2} (1 + u) ^ {\theta - 2} \left(\sigma_ {1} ^ {2} S ^ {2} + \sum_ {i = 1} ^ {n} \sigma_ {i + 1} ^ {2} x _ {i} ^ {2}\right)
$$

$$
= \theta (1 + u) ^ {\theta - 2} \left\{(1 + u) \left(S ^ {0} D - D S - \sum_ {i = 1} ^ {n} d _ {i} x _ {i}\right) + \frac {\theta - 1}{2} \left(\sigma_ {1} ^ {2} S ^ {2} + \sum_ {i = 1} ^ {n} \sigma_ {i + 1} ^ {2} x _ {i} ^ {2}\right) \right\}
$$

$$
\leqslant \theta (1 + u) ^ {\theta - 2} \left\{(1 + u) \left(S ^ {0} D - \min \{D, d _ {1}, \dots , d _ {n} \} u\right) \right.
$$

$$
\left. + \left(\frac {\theta - 1}{2} \vee 0\right) \left(\sigma_ {1} ^ {2} \vee \sigma_ {2} ^ {2} \vee \dots \vee \sigma_ {n + 1} ^ {2}\right) u ^ {2} \right\}
$$

$$
= \theta (1 + u) ^ {\theta - 2} \left\{- \left[ \min \{D, d _ {1}, \dots , d _ {n} \} - \left(\frac {\theta - 1}{2} \vee 0\right) \right] u ^ {2} \right.
$$

$$
\left. + \left(S ^ {0} D - \min \{D, d _ {1}, \dots , d _ {n} \}\right) u + S ^ {0} D \right\}.
$$

其他的证明类似于 Zhao 和 Jiang 文献 [50] 中的引理 2.1.

引理 3.3 假设 $\min\{D,d_{1},\cdots,d_{n}\}>\frac{1}{2}(\sigma_{1}^{2}\vee\cdots\vee\sigma_{n+1}^{2})$ 成立. 设 $(S(t),x_{1}(t),\cdots,x_{n}(t))$ 是系统 (3.16) 满足初值 $(S(0),x_{1}(0),\cdots,x_{n}(0))\in\mathbb{R}_{+}^{n+1}$ 的解, 则

$$
\lim _ {t \to \infty} \frac {\int_ {0} ^ {t} (S (s) - \lambda_ {1}) \mathrm{d} B _ {1} (s)}{t} = 0 \text {a.s.},
$$

$$
\begin{array}{l} \lim _ {t \rightarrow \infty} \frac {\int_ {0} ^ {t} \left(x _ {1} (s) - x _ {1} ^ {*}\right) \mathrm{d} B _ {2} (s)}{t} = 0 \text {a.s.}, \\ \lim _ {t \rightarrow \infty} \frac {\int_ {0} ^ {t} x _ {i} (s) \mathrm{d} B _ {i + 1} (s)}{t} = 0 \quad (i = 2, \dots , n) \text {a.s.} \\ \end{array}
$$

引理3.4 设 $\phi (t)$ 是随机方程

$$
\mathrm{d} \phi (t) = \left(S ^ {0} - \phi (t)\right) D \mathrm{d} t + \sigma_ {1} \phi (t) \mathrm{d} B _ {1} (t) \tag {3.18}
$$

的解, 则 $\phi(t)$ 弱收敛于分布 $\nu$ 且 $\nu$ 是一个在 $\mathbb{R}_{+}^{1}$ 上满足 $\int_{0}^{\infty} x\nu(\mathrm{d}x) = S^{0}$ 的概率测度. 特别地, $\nu$ 有密度 $(A\sigma_{1}^{2}x^{2}p(x))^{-1}$ , 其中 $A$ 是一个常数,

$$
p (x) = \exp \left(- \frac {2 S ^ {0} D}{\sigma_ {1} ^ {2}}\right) x ^ {\frac {2 D}{\sigma_ {1} ^ {2}}} \exp \left(\frac {2 S ^ {0} D}{\sigma_ {1} ^ {2} x}\right), \quad x > 0.
$$

应用这些引理, 我们得到如下结论.

定理 3.5 设 $(S(t), x_{1}(t), \cdots, x_{n}(t))$ 是系统 (3.16) 满足初值 $(S(0), x_{1}(0), \cdots, x_{n}(0)) \in \mathbb{R}_{+}^{n+1}$ 的解. 假设 $\min\{D, d_{1}, \cdots, d_{n}\} > \frac{1}{2} (\sigma_{1}^{2} \vee \cdots \vee \sigma_{n+1}^{2})$ 成立. 若下面的条件成立, 则系统 (3.16) 的竞争排斥原理成立. 若 $0 < \lambda_{1} < \lambda_{2} \leqslant \cdots \leqslant \lambda_{n} < S^{0}$ , $a_{1} \int_{0}^{\infty} \frac{x \nu(\mathrm{d}x)}{1 + b_{1}x} > d_{1} + \frac{\sigma_{2}^{2}}{2}$ 且 $\frac{a_{i} \lambda_{1}}{1 + b_{i} \lambda_{1}} + \frac{a_{i}}{2 \sqrt{b_{i}} (1 + b_{i} \lambda_{1})} \left( \frac{K(\sigma)}{D} \right)^{\frac{1}{2}} < d_{i} + \frac{\sigma_{i+1}^{2}}{2} (i = 2, \cdots, n)$ , 则

$$
\liminf _ {t \to \infty} \langle x _ {1} (t) \rangle \geqslant \frac {D S ^ {0} b _ {1} ^ {2}}{a _ {1} ^ {2}} \left[ - d _ {1} - \frac {\sigma_ {2} ^ {2}}{2} + a _ {1} \int_ {0} ^ {\infty} \frac {x \nu (\mathrm{d} x)}{1 + b _ {1} x} \right] > 0 \text {   a.s. },
$$

$$
\limsup _ {t \to \infty} \frac {\log x _ {i} (t)}{t} \leqslant \frac {a _ {i} \lambda_ {1}}{1 + b _ {i} \lambda_ {1}} - d _ {i} - \frac {\sigma_ {i + 1} ^ {2}}{2} + \frac {a _ {i}}{2 \sqrt {b _ {i}} (1 + b _ {i} \lambda_ {1})} \left(\frac {K (\sigma)}{D}\right) ^ {\frac {1}{2}} <   0 \quad a. s.
$$

$$
(i = 2, \dots , n),
$$

其中 $K(\sigma)=\frac{\lambda_{1}\sigma_{1}^{2}}{2}+(1+b_{1}\lambda_{1})\frac{x_{1}^{*}\sigma_{2}^{2}}{2},(\lambda_{1},x_{1}^{*},0,\cdots,0)$ 是系统 (3.2) 的边界平衡点， $\nu$ 如引理 3.4 所定义.

证明 若 $\lambda_{1}<S^{0}$ ，系统 (3.2) 存在边界平衡点 $(\lambda_{1},x_{1}^{*},0,\cdots,0)$ ，即

$$
d _ {1} = \frac {a _ {1} \lambda_ {1}}{1 + b _ {1} \lambda_ {1}}, (S ^ {0} - \lambda_ {1}) D = \frac {a _ {1} \lambda_ {1}}{1 + b _ {1} \lambda_ {1}} x _ {1} ^ {*}.
$$

定义 $C^2$ 函数 $V:\mathbb{R}_+^{n + 1}\to \overline{\mathbb{R}}_+$ 

$$
\begin{array}{l} V (S, x _ {1}, \dots , x _ {n}) = S - \lambda_ {1} - \lambda_ {1} \log \frac {S}{\lambda_ {1}} + \left(1 + b _ {1} \lambda_ {1}\right) \left(x _ {1} - x _ {1} ^ {*} - x _ {1} ^ {*} \log \frac {x _ {1}}{x _ {1} ^ {*}}\right) \\ + \sum_ {i = 2} ^ {n} (1 + b _ {i} \lambda_ {1}) x _ {i}. \\ \end{array}
$$

由 Itô 公式和式 (3.16), 可以得到

$$
\mathrm{d} V = \left\{- \frac {D (S - \lambda_ {1}) ^ {2}}{S} - \frac {a _ {1} x _ {1} ^ {*} (S - \lambda_ {1}) ^ {2}}{(1 + b _ {1} \lambda_ {1}) (1 + b _ {1} S) S} - \frac {a _ {1} \left(x _ {1} - x _ {1} ^ {*}\right) (S - \lambda_ {1})}{1 + b _ {1} S} \right.
$$

$$
\left. - \sum_ {i = 2} ^ {n} \frac {a _ {i} x _ {i} (S - \lambda_ {1})}{1 + b _ {i} S} + \frac {\lambda_ {1} \sigma_ {1} ^ {2}}{2} \right\} d t + \sigma_ {1} (S - \lambda_ {1}) d B _ {1} (t)
$$

$$
+ \left(1 + b _ {1} \lambda_ {1}\right) \left\{\left[ \frac {a _ {1} \left(x _ {1} - x _ {1} ^ {*}\right) (S - \lambda_ {1})}{\left(1 + b _ {1} \lambda_ {1}\right) \left(1 + b _ {1} S\right)} + \frac {x _ {1} ^ {*} \sigma_ {2} ^ {2}}{2} \right] d t + \sigma_ {2} \left(x _ {1} - x _ {1} ^ {*}\right) d B _ {2} (t) \right\}
$$

$$
+ \sum_ {i = 2} ^ {n} \left(1 + b _ {i} \lambda_ {1}\right) \left\{\left[ \frac {a _ {i} x _ {i} (S - \lambda_ {1})}{\left(1 + b _ {i} \lambda_ {1}\right) \left(1 + b _ {i} S\right)} + x _ {i} \left(\frac {a _ {i} \lambda_ {1}}{1 + b _ {i} \lambda_ {1}} - d _ {i}\right) \right] d t \right.
$$

$$
\left. + \sigma_ {i + 1} x _ {i} (t) \mathrm{d} B _ {i + 1} (t) \right\}
$$

$$
:= L V \mathrm{d} t + \sigma_ {1} (S - \lambda_ {1}) \mathrm{d} B _ {1} (t) + \left(1 + b _ {1} \lambda_ {1}\right) \sigma_ {2} \left(x _ {1} - x _ {1} ^ {*}\right) \mathrm{d} B _ {2} (t)
$$

$$
+ \sum_ {i = 2} ^ {n} (1 + b _ {i} \lambda_ {1}) \sigma_ {i + 1} x _ {i} (t) \mathrm{d} B _ {i + 1} (t), \tag {3.19}
$$

其中

$$
\frac {a _ {i} \lambda_ {1}}{1 + b _ {i} \lambda_ {1}} - d _ {i} = \frac {a _ {i} \lambda_ {1}}{1 + b _ {i} \lambda_ {1}} - \frac {a _ {i} \lambda_ {i}}{1 + b _ {i} \lambda_ {i}} = \frac {a _ {i} (\lambda_ {1} - \lambda_ {i})}{(1 + b _ {i} \lambda_ {1}) (1 + b _ {i} \lambda_ {i})} <   0, \quad i = 2, \dots , n,
$$

可得

$$
\begin{array}{l} L V = - \frac {D (S - \lambda_ {1}) ^ {2}}{S} - \frac {a _ {1} x _ {1} ^ {*} (S - \lambda_ {1}) ^ {2}}{(1 + b _ {1} \lambda_ {1}) (1 + b _ {1} S) S} - \frac {a _ {1} \left(x _ {1} - x _ {1} ^ {*}\right) (S - \lambda_ {1})}{1 + b _ {1} S} \\ - \sum_ {i = 2} ^ {n} \frac {a _ {i} x _ {i} (S - \lambda_ {1})}{1 + b _ {i} S} + \frac {\lambda_ {1} \sigma_ {1} ^ {2}}{2} + (1 + b _ {1} \lambda_ {1}) \left[ \frac {a _ {1} \left(x _ {1} - x _ {1} ^ {*}\right) (S - \lambda_ {1})}{(1 + b _ {1} \lambda_ {1}) (1 + b _ {1} S)} + \frac {x _ {1} ^ {*} \sigma_ {2} ^ {2}}{2} \right] \\ + \sum_ {i = 2} ^ {n} \left(1 + b _ {i} \lambda_ {1}\right) \left[ \frac {a _ {i} x _ {i} (S - \lambda_ {1})}{\left(1 + b _ {i} \lambda_ {1}\right) \left(1 + b _ {i} S\right)} + x _ {i} \left(\frac {a _ {i} \lambda_ {1}}{1 + b _ {i} \lambda_ {1}} - d _ {i}\right) \right] \\ \leqslant - \frac {D (S - \lambda_ {1}) ^ {2}}{S} + \frac {\lambda_ {1} \sigma_ {1} ^ {2}}{2} + (1 + b _ {1} \lambda_ {1}) \frac {x _ {1} ^ {*} \sigma_ {2} ^ {2}}{2} + \sum_ {i = 2} ^ {n} (1 + b _ {i} \lambda_ {1}) x _ {i} \left(\frac {a _ {i} \lambda_ {1}}{1 + b _ {i} \lambda_ {1}} - d _ {i}\right) \\ \leqslant - \frac {D (S - \lambda_ {1}) ^ {2}}{S} + K (\sigma). \\ \end{array}
$$

两端由0到 $t$ 积分式(3.19)得到

$$
\begin{array}{l} \frac {V (t) - V (0)}{t} \\ \leqslant - \frac {D}{t} \int_ {0} ^ {t} \frac {(S (s) - \lambda_ {1}) ^ {2}}{S (s)} \mathrm{d} s + K (\sigma) + \sigma_ {1} \frac {\int_ {0} ^ {t} (S (s) - \lambda_ {1}) \mathrm{d} B _ {1} (s)}{t} \\ + (1 + b _ {1} \lambda_ {1}) \sigma_ {2} \frac {\int_ {0} ^ {t} \left(x _ {1} (s) - x _ {1} ^ {*}\right) \mathrm{d} B _ {2} (s)}{t} + \sum_ {i = 2} ^ {n} \left(1 + b _ {i} \lambda_ {1}\right) \sigma_ {i + 1} \frac {\int_ {0} ^ {t} x _ {i} (s) \mathrm{d} B _ {i + 1} (s)}{t}. \\ \end{array}
$$

由引理 3.3, 可以看出

$$
\limsup _ {t \to \infty} \frac {1}{t} \int_ {0} ^ {t} \frac {(S (s) - \overline {{S}}) ^ {2}}{S (s)} \mathrm{d} s \leqslant \frac {K (\sigma)}{D} \quad \text { a.s. } \tag {3.20}
$$

对 $i=2,\cdots,n,$ 由 Itô 公式有

$$
\begin{array}{l} \mathrm{d} \log x _ {i} (t) = \left(- d _ {i} + \frac {a _ {i} S (t)}{1 + b _ {i} S (t)} - \frac {\sigma_ {i + 1} ^ {2}}{2}\right) \mathrm{d} t + \sigma_ {i + 1} \mathrm{d} B _ {i + 1} (t) \\ = \left[ \frac {a _ {i} (S (t) - \lambda_ {1})}{(1 + b _ {i} \lambda_ {1}) (1 + b _ {i} S (t))} + \frac {a _ {i} \lambda_ {1}}{1 + b _ {i} \lambda_ {1}} - d _ {i} - \frac {\sigma_ {i + 1} ^ {2}}{2} \right] \mathrm{d} t + \sigma_ {i + 1} \mathrm{d} B _ {i + 1} (t) \\ \end{array}
$$

和

$$
\begin{array}{l} \frac {\log x _ {i} (t)}{t} = \frac {\log x _ {i} (0)}{t} + \frac {a _ {i} \lambda_ {1}}{1 + b _ {i} \lambda_ {1}} - d _ {i} - \frac {\sigma_ {i + 1} ^ {2}}{2} + \frac {a _ {i}}{1 + b _ {i} \lambda_ {1}} \left\langle \frac {S (t) - \lambda_ {1}}{1 + b _ {i} S (t)} \right\rangle + \frac {\sigma_ {i + 1} B _ {i + 1} (t)}{t} \\ \leqslant \frac {\log x _ {i} (0)}{t} + \frac {a _ {i} \lambda_ {1}}{1 + b _ {i} \lambda_ {1}} - d _ {i} - \frac {\sigma_ {i + 1} ^ {2}}{2} + \frac {a _ {i}}{2 \sqrt {b _ {i}} \left(1 + b _ {i} \lambda_ {1}\right)} \left\langle \frac {| S (t) - \lambda_ {1} |}{\sqrt {S (t)}} \right\rangle \\ + \frac {\sigma_ {i + 1} B _ {i + 1} (t)}{t} \\ \leqslant \frac {\log x _ {i} (0)}{t} + \frac {a _ {i} \lambda_ {1}}{1 + b _ {i} \lambda_ {1}} - d _ {i} - \frac {\sigma_ {i + 1} ^ {2}}{2} + \frac {a _ {i}}{2 \sqrt {b _ {i}} (1 + b _ {i} \lambda_ {1})} \left[ \frac {1}{t} \int_ {0} ^ {t} \frac {(S (s) - \lambda_ {1}) ^ {2}}{S (s)} d s \right] ^ {\frac {1}{2}} \\ + \frac {\sigma_ {i + 1} B _ {i + 1} (t)}{t}, \\ \end{array}
$$

再由 $\lim_{t\to \infty}\frac{B_{i + 1}(t)}{t} = 0$ 和式(3.20)可知

$$
\limsup _ {t \to \infty} \frac {\log x _ {i} (t)}{t} \leqslant \frac {a _ {i} \lambda_ {1}}{1 + b _ {i} \lambda_ {1}} - d _ {i} - \frac {\sigma_ {i + 1} ^ {2}}{2} + \frac {a _ {i}}{2 \sqrt {b _ {i}} (1 + b _ {i} \lambda_ {1})} \left(\frac {K (\sigma)}{D}\right) ^ {\frac {1}{2}} \quad \text { a.s. } \quad (i = 2, \dots , n).
$$

若条件 $\frac{a_{i}\lambda_{1}}{1+b_{i}\lambda_{1}}+\frac{a_{i}}{2\sqrt{b_{i}}(1+b_{i}\lambda_{1})}\left(\frac{K(\sigma)}{D}\right)^{\frac{1}{2}}<d_{i}+\frac{\sigma_{i+1}^{2}}{2}(i=2,\cdots,n)$ 成立, 可以推断

$$
\begin{array}{l} \limsup _ {t \to \infty} \frac {\log x _ {i} (t)}{t} \leqslant \frac {a _ {i} \lambda_ {1}}{1 + b _ {i} \lambda_ {1}} - d _ {i} - \frac {\sigma_ {i + 1} ^ {2}}{2} + \frac {a _ {i}}{2 \sqrt {b _ {i}} (1 + b _ {i} \lambda_ {1})} \left(\frac {K (\sigma)}{D}\right) ^ {\frac {1}{2}} \\ <   0 \quad \text {a.s.} \quad (i = 2, \dots , n). \\ \end{array}
$$

也就是说, $x_{i}(t)(i=2,\cdots,n)$ 几乎必然以指数收敛于零.

因为系统 (3.16) 的解是正的, 所以由随机微分方程的比较定理可以得出 $S(t) \leqslant \phi(t)$ a.s., 其中 $\phi(t)$ 是随机 Logistic 方程 (3.18) 满足初值 $\phi(0) = S(0)$ 的解.

从式 (3.16) 和式 (3.18) 可以推出

$$
\begin{array}{l} \frac {\log S (t)}{t} = \frac {\log S (0)}{t} + D S ^ {0} \left\langle \frac {1}{S} \right\rangle - a _ {1} \left\langle \frac {x _ {1}}{1 + b _ {1} S} \right\rangle \\ - \sum_ {i = 2} ^ {n} a _ {i} \left\langle \frac {x _ {i}}{1 + b _ {i} S} \right\rangle - D - \frac {\sigma_ {1} ^ {2}}{2} + \frac {\sigma_ {1} B _ {1} (t)}{t} \\ \end{array}
$$

和

$$
\frac {\log \phi (t)}{t} = \frac {\log S (0)}{t} + D S ^ {0} \left\langle \frac {1}{\phi} \right\rangle - D - \frac {\sigma_ {1} ^ {2}}{2} + \frac {\sigma_ {1} B _ {1} (t)}{t}.
$$

因此

$$
\begin{array}{l} 0 \geqslant \frac {\log S (t) - \log \phi (t)}{t} = D S ^ {0} \left\langle \frac {1}{S} - \frac {1}{\phi} \right\rangle - a _ {1} \left\langle \frac {x _ {1}}{1 + b _ {1} S} \right\rangle - \sum_ {i = 2} ^ {n} a _ {i} \left\langle \frac {x _ {i}}{1 + b _ {i} S} \right\rangle \\ \geqslant D S ^ {0} \left\langle \frac {1}{S} - \frac {1}{\phi} \right\rangle - a _ {1} \langle x _ {1} \rangle - \sum_ {i = 2} ^ {n} a _ {i} \langle x _ {i} \rangle , \\ \end{array}
$$

也就是说，

$$
\left\langle \frac {1}{S} - \frac {1}{\phi} \right\rangle \leqslant \frac {a _ {1}}{D S ^ {0}} \left\langle x _ {1} \right\rangle + \sum_ {i = 2} ^ {n} \frac {a _ {i}}{D S ^ {0}} \left\langle x _ {i} \right\rangle . \tag {3.21}
$$

由 Itô 公式有

$$
\mathrm{d} \log x _ {1} (t) = \left(- d _ {1} + \frac {a _ {1} S (t)}{1 + b _ {1} S (t)} - \frac {\sigma_ {2} ^ {2}}{2}\right) \mathrm{d} t + \sigma_ {2} \mathrm{d} B _ {2} (t).
$$

这样, 由式 (3.21) 可得

$$
\begin{array}{l} \frac {\log x _ {1} (t)}{t} = \frac {\log x _ {1} (0)}{t} - d _ {1} - \frac {\sigma_ {2} ^ {2}}{2} + a _ {1} \left\langle \frac {S}{1 + b _ {1} S} \right\rangle + \frac {\sigma_ {2} B _ {2} (t)}{t} \\ = \frac {\log x _ {1} (0)}{t} - d _ {1} - \frac {\sigma_ {2} ^ {2}}{2} + a _ {1} \left\langle \frac {\phi}{1 + b _ {1} \phi} \right\rangle - a _ {1} \left\langle \frac {\phi - S}{(1 + b _ {1} \phi) (1 + b _ {1} S)} \right\rangle \\ + \frac {\sigma_ {2} B _ {2} (t)}{t} \\ \geqslant \frac {\log x _ {1} (0)}{t} - d _ {1} - \frac {\sigma_ {2} ^ {2}}{2} + a _ {1} \left\langle \frac {\phi}{1 + b _ {1} \phi} \right\rangle - \frac {a _ {1}}{b _ {1} ^ {2}} \left\langle \frac {1}{S} - \frac {1}{\phi} \right\rangle + \frac {\sigma_ {2} B _ {2} (t)}{t} \\ \geqslant \frac {\log x _ {1} (0)}{t} - d _ {1} - \frac {\sigma_ {2} ^ {2}}{2} + a _ {1} \left\langle \frac {\phi}{1 + b _ {1} \phi} \right\rangle - \frac {a _ {1} ^ {2}}{D S ^ {0} b _ {1} ^ {2}} \langle x _ {1} \rangle \\ - \frac {a _ {1}}{D S ^ {0} b _ {1} ^ {2}} \sum_ {i = 2} ^ {n} a _ {i} \left\langle x _ {i} \right\rangle + \frac {\sigma_ {2} B _ {2} (t)}{t}. \tag {3.22} \\ \end{array}
$$

由引理3.2可知

$$
\limsup _ {t \to \infty} \frac {\log x _ {1} (t)}{t} \leqslant 0 \quad \text { a.s. } \tag {3.23}
$$

由式 (3.22), (3.23) 和 $\lim_{t\to\infty}\langle x_{i}(t)\rangle=0(i=2,\cdots,n)$ ，并利用条件 $a_{1}\int_{0}^{\infty}\frac{x\nu(\mathrm{d}x)}{1+b_{1}x}>d_{1}+\frac{\sigma_{2}^{2}}{2}$ 和 $\lim_{t\to\infty}\frac{B_{2}(t)}{t}=0$ ，可得

$$
\liminf _ {t \to \infty} \langle x _ {1} (t) \rangle \geqslant \frac {D S ^ {0} b _ {1} ^ {2}}{a _ {1} ^ {2}} \left[ - d _ {1} - \frac {\sigma_ {2} ^ {2}}{2} + a _ {1} \int_ {0} ^ {\infty} \frac {x \nu (\mathrm{d} x)}{1 + b _ {1} x} \right] > 0 \text {   a.s.. }
$$

定理 3.6 设 $(S(t), x_{1}(t), \cdots, x_{n}(t))$ 是系统 (3.16) 满足初值 $(S(0), x_{1}(0), \cdots, x_{n}(0)) \in \mathbb{R}_{+}^{n+1}$ 的解. 若 $a_{i} \int_{0}^{\infty} \frac{x \nu(\mathrm{d}x)}{1 + b_{i} x} < d_{i} + \frac{\sigma_{i+1}^{2}}{2} (i = 1, \cdots, n)$ ，则系统 (3.16) 的竞争排斥原理不成立. 在这种情况下，

$$
\lim _ {t \rightarrow \infty} \langle S (t) \rangle = S ^ {0} \text {a.s.} S (t) \xrightarrow {\omega} \nu \text {a.s.} t \rightarrow \infty ,
$$

$$
\operatorname * {l i m s u p} _ {t \to \infty} \frac {\log x _ {i} (t)}{t} \leqslant - d _ {i} - \frac {\sigma_ {i + 1} ^ {2}}{2} + a _ {i} \int_ {0} ^ {\infty} \frac {x \nu (\mathrm{d} x)}{1 + b _ {i} x} <   0 \text {   a.s.   } (i = 1, \dots , n),
$$

其中 $\stackrel{\omega}{\rightarrow}$ 意味着依分布收敛且 $\nu$ 是一个在 $\mathbb{R}_+^1$ 满足 $\int_0^\infty x\nu (\mathrm{d}x) = S^0$ 的概率测度. 特别地, $\nu$ 有密度 $(A\sigma_1^2 x^2 p(x))^{-1}$ , 其中 $A$ 是一个常数,

$$
p (x) = \exp \left(- \frac {2 S ^ {0} D}{\sigma_ {1} ^ {2}}\right) x ^ {\frac {2 D}{\sigma_ {1} ^ {2}}} \exp \left(\frac {2 S ^ {0} D}{\sigma_ {1} ^ {2} x}\right), \quad x > 0.
$$

证明 因为系统 (3.16) 的解是正的, 所以由随机微分方程的比较定理可以得出 $S(t) \leqslant \phi(t)$ a.s., 其中 $\phi(t)$ 是随机 Logistic 方程 (3.18) 满足初值 $\phi(0) = S(0)$ 的解.

对 $i=1,\cdots,n,$ 由 Itô 公式有

$$
\begin{array}{l} \mathrm{d} \log x _ {i} (t) = \left(- d _ {i} - \frac {\sigma_ {i + 1} ^ {2}}{2} + \frac {a _ {i} S (t)}{1 + b _ {i} S (t)}\right) \mathrm{d} t + \sigma_ {i + 1} \mathrm{d} B _ {i + 1} (t) \\ \leqslant \left(- d _ {i} - \frac {\sigma_ {i + 1} ^ {2}}{2} + \frac {a _ {i} \phi}{1 + b _ {i} \phi}\right) \mathrm{d} t + \sigma_ {i + 1} \mathrm{d} B _ {i + 1} (t) \\ \end{array}
$$

和

$$
\frac {\log x _ {i} (t)}{t} \leqslant \frac {\log x _ {i} (0)}{t} - d _ {i} - \frac {\sigma_ {i + 1} ^ {2}}{2} + a _ {i} \left\langle \frac {\phi}{1 + b _ {i} \phi} \right\rangle + \sigma_ {i + 1} \frac {B _ {i + 1} (t)}{t}.
$$

从 $\lim_{t\to \infty}\frac{B_{i + 1}(t)}{t} = 0$ 和条件 $a_{i}\int_{0}^{\infty}\frac{x\nu(\mathrm{d}x)}{1 + b_{i}x} < d_{i} + \frac{\sigma_{i + 1}^{2}}{2},$ 易知

$$
\lim _ {t \to \infty} \sup \frac {\log x _ {i} (t)}{t} \leqslant - d _ {i} - \frac {\sigma_ {i + 1} ^ {2}}{2} + a _ {i} \int_ {0} ^ {\infty} \frac {x \nu (\mathrm{d} x)}{1 + b _ {i} x} <   0 \quad \text { a.s. }, \tag {3.24}
$$

也就是说， $x_{i}(i=1,\cdots,n)$ 几乎必然以指数收敛于零.

关于 $S(t)$ 的证明类似于Yang等在文献[51]中的定理2.4证明，这样可以得到

$$
\lim _ {t \to \infty} \langle S (t) \rangle = S ^ {0} \text {a.s.} S (t) \stackrel {\omega} {\to} \nu , \text {a.s.} t \to \infty .
$$

## 3.3.3 系统 (3.16) 的数值模拟

下面我们用 Milstein 高阶方法 [33] 进行数值模拟验证所得到的结论.

第一组数值试验使用以下参数:

$$
S ^ {0} = 1. 2, \quad D = 0. 3, \quad d _ {1} = 0. 4, \quad d _ {2} = 0. 3, \quad a _ {1} = 2, \quad b _ {1} = 3, \quad a _ {2} = 2, \quad b _ {2} = 5,
$$

我们得到 $\lambda_1 = 0.5, \lambda_2 = 0.6, x_1^* = 0.525$ . 这意味着 $\lambda_1 < \lambda_2 < S^0$ . 对确定性系统 (3.2),

$$
\lim _ {t \rightarrow \infty} S (t) = 0. 5, \quad \lim _ {t \rightarrow \infty} x _ {1} (t) = 0. 5 2 5, \quad \lim _ {t \rightarrow \infty} x _ {2} (t) = 0.
$$

对随机系统 (3.16), 我们选取 $\sigma_{1} = \sigma_{2} = 0.1, \sigma_{3} = 0.2$ . 从图 3.7 我们可以看出随机系统有与确定性系统相似的性质. 图 3.8 显示解 (波动较大的曲线) 在一个小区域震动. 从它们的密度函数 (图 (b) 和 (d)) 可以看出 $(S(t), x_{1}(t))$ 存在平稳分布.

S ^ {0} = 1. 2, \quad D = 0. 3, \quad d _ {1} = 0. 4, \quad d _ {2} = 0. 3, \quad a _ {1} = 1, \quad b _ {1} = 2, \quad a _ {2} = 1, \quad b _ {2} = 2. 5,
