# 第 4 章 污染环境下的随机非自治 Lotka-Volterra 模型

## 4.1 引言

近几十年来, 学者们建立数学模型来研究污染环境中生物的生长所受到的影响, 见文献 [52] ~ [54]. 经典的污染环境下的 Lotka-Volterra 模型可以表示为

$$
\left\{ \begin{array}{l} \mathrm{d} x _ {1} (t) = x _ {1} (t) \left[ b _ {1} - r _ {1 1} C _ {1} (t) + a _ {1 1} x _ {1} (t) + a _ {1 2} x _ {2} (t) \right] \mathrm{d} t, \\ \mathrm{d} x _ {2} (t) = x _ {2} (t) \left[ b _ {2} - r _ {2 1} C _ {2} (t) + a _ {2 1} x _ {1} (t) + a _ {2 2} x _ {2} (t) \right] \mathrm{d} t, \\ \mathrm{d} C _ {1} (t) = \left[ k _ {1} C _ {E} (t) - \left(g _ {1} + m _ {1}\right) C _ {1} (t) \right] \mathrm{d} t, \\ \mathrm{d} C _ {2} (t) = \left[ k _ {2} C _ {E} (t) - \left(g _ {2} + m _ {2}\right) C _ {2} (t) \right] \mathrm{d} t, \\ \mathrm{d} C _ {E} (t) = \left[ - h C _ {E} (t) + u (t) \right] \mathrm{d} t, \end{array} \right. \tag {4.1}
$$

其中 $x_{i}(t)$ 表示 $t$ 时刻物种 $i$ 的种群密度, $C_i(t)$ 表示 $t$ 时刻物种 $i$ 体内毒物的浓度, $C_E(t)$ 表示 $t$ 时刻环境中的毒物浓度, $b_i$ 表示物种 $i$ 的内禀增长率, $r_{i1}$ 表示物种 $i$ 对体内毒物浓度的剂量反应参数, $k_i$ 表示每单位质量的物种 $i$ 对毒素的吸收率, $g_i$ 和 $m_i$ 分别表示物种 $i$ 对毒物的分解率和排泄率. $-hC_E(t)$ 表示毒物的损失率, $-k_1C_E(t)x_1(t)$ 和 $-k_2C_E(t)x_2(t)$ 表示生物对毒物的吸收, $g_1C_1(t)x_1(t) + g_2C_2(t)x_2(t)$ 表示毒物的增长率, $u(t)$ 表示 $t$ 时刻对环境的毒素排放率. 设 $u(t)$ 是有界的且满足 $0 \leqslant u(t) \leqslant u_1 < +\infty$ .

上述的讨论是基于假设所涉及模型的环境参数不受环境波动的影响. 然而, 一个真实的系统不会保存在这样一个稳定状态, 因为它是受制于环境白噪声, 白噪声通过直接地作用于密度或者间接地影响参数值扰动这个稳定状态, 见文献 [55] ~ [59]. 文献 [55] 考虑污染环境下的随机时滞两种群捕食-食饵模型, 并得到在一定条件下, 系统存在时间均值意义下持久的唯一非负解. 文献 [56] 研究污染环境下的随机单物种模型的动力学行为, 得到种群局部灭绝和随机弱平均持久的准确阈值. 文献 [59] 用状态变换扩散建立模型来研究受外部环境随机变化的 $n$ 种群的动力学行为, 证明种群人口的增长率都是有界的. 他们从几个角度来验证解的某些长期平均的极限, 并获得部分随机竞争排斥原理.

因为对环境的毒素排放率的周期性总是存在的，所以研究具有周期系数的Lotka-Volterra系统是合理的，参见文献[7],[8],[60]~[63].文献[60]利用重合度理论和Lyapunov函数方法研究带有几个偏差参数的周期 $n$ 种群Lotka-Volterra竞争系统的正周期解的存在性和全局性.文献[62]考虑具有周期系数的随机两种群模型，并得到一些渐近稳定周期解存在的充分条件.文献[63]研究随机非自治Lotka-Volterra捕食-食饵模型．他们证明在一些简单合理的条件下系统至少存在一个正周期解，并得到随机非自治系统平均持久和灭绝的充分条件.

据我们所知, 关于污染环境下的随机非自治 Lotka-Volterra 模型的正周期解的存在性在理论上很少见. 因此, 本章主要针对污染环境下的随机非自治竞争系统、随机非自治互惠系统和随机非自治捕食-食饵系统来讨论正周期解的存在性和全局性.

## 4.2 污染环境下的随机非自治竞争模型

本节我们考虑污染环境下的随机非自治竞争模型.

$$
\left\{ \begin{array}{l} \mathrm{d} x _ {1} (t) = x _ {1} (t) \left[ b _ {1} - r _ {1 1} C _ {1} (t) - a _ {1 1} x _ {1} (t) - a _ {1 2} x _ {2} (t) \right] \mathrm{d} t + \sigma_ {1} (t) x _ {1} (t) \mathrm{d} B _ {1} (t), \\ \mathrm{d} x _ {2} (t) = x _ {2} (t) \left[ b _ {2} - r _ {2 1} C _ {2} (t) - a _ {2 1} x _ {1} (t) - a _ {2 2} x _ {2} (t) \right] \mathrm{d} t + \sigma_ {2} (t) x _ {2} (t) \mathrm{d} B _ {2} (t), \\ \mathrm{d} C _ {1} (t) = \left[ k _ {1} C _ {E} (t) - \left(g _ {1} + m _ {1}\right) C _ {1} (t) \right] \mathrm{d} t, \\ \mathrm{d} C _ {2} (t) = \left[ k _ {2} C _ {E} (t) - \left(g _ {2} + m _ {2}\right) C _ {2} (t) \right] \mathrm{d} t, \\ \mathrm{d} C _ {E} (t) = [ - h C _ {E} (t) + u (t) ] \mathrm{d} t, \end{array} \right. \tag {4.2}
$$

其中参数函数 $u(t)$ 和 $\sigma_{i}(t)(i = 1,2)$ 为正的、非常值、具有 $\theta$ -周期的连续函数， $B_{i}(t), i = 1,2$ 是独立的标准的布朗运动.

本节, 我们始终使用如下记法. 如果 $f(t)$ 是定义在 $[0, \infty)$ 上的有界函数, 记

$$
f ^ {u} = \sup _ {t \in [ 0, \infty)} f (t), \quad f ^ {l} = \inf _ {t \in [ 0, \infty)} f (t).
$$

如果 $f(t)$ 为定义在 $[0, \infty)$ 上的一个可积函数，记

$$
\langle f \rangle_ {\theta} = \frac {1}{\theta} \int_ {0} ^ {\theta} f (s) \mathrm{d} s, \quad t > 0.
$$

因为 $C_1(t), C_2(t)$ 和 $C_E(t)$ 是毒物的浓度, 所以不等式 $0 \leqslant C_i(t) < 1 (i = 1,2)$ 和 $0 \leqslant C_E(t) < 1$ 成立. 本节, 我们总假设条件 $0 < k_i \leqslant g_i + m_i (i = 1,2), u(t) \leqslant h$ 成立, 这意味着 $0 \leqslant C_i(t) < 1 (i = 1,2), 0 \leqslant C_E(t) < 1$ (见文献 [56]).

因为 $u(t)$ 是一个正的 $\theta$ -周期连续函数, 所以易证 $C_E^*(t)$ 是一个正的 $\theta$ -周期连续函数, 这里

$$
C _ {E} ^ {*} (t) = \frac {\int_ {t} ^ {t + \theta} \mathrm{e} ^ {h (s - t)} u (s) \mathrm{d} s}{\mathrm{e} ^ {h \theta} - 1},
$$

且满足 $\mathrm{d}C_E^* (t) = [-hC_E^* (t) + u(t)]\mathrm{d}t.$ 

从式 (4.2) 可以推出

$$
C _ {E} (t) - C _ {E} ^ {*} (t) = \left(C _ {E} (0) - C _ {E} ^ {*} (0)\right) \mathrm{e} ^ {- h t},
$$

且满足 $\mathrm{d}(C_E(t) - C_E^* (t)) = -h(C_E(t) - C_E^* (t))\mathrm{d}t.$ 

同理, 我们可以得出 $C_i^*(t)(i = 1,2)$ 也是一个正的 $\theta$ -周期连续函数, 这里

$$
C _ {i} ^ {*} (t) = \frac {\int_ {t} ^ {t + \theta} \mathrm{e} ^ {(g _ {i} + m _ {i}) (s - t)} k _ {i} C _ {E} ^ {*} (s) \mathrm{d} s}{\mathrm{e} ^ {(g _ {i} + m _ {i}) \theta} - 1}, \tag {4.3}
$$

且满足 $\mathrm{d}C_i^* (t) = [k_iC_E^* (t) - (g_i + m_i)C_i^* (t)]\mathrm{d}t.$ 

利用常数变易法可以推出

$$
\begin{array}{l} C _ {i} (t) - C _ {i} ^ {*} (t) = \mathrm{e} ^ {- (g _ {i} + m _ {i}) t} \left[ C _ {i} (0) - C _ {i} ^ {*} (0) + k _ {i} \int_ {0} ^ {t} (C _ {E} (s) - C _ {E} ^ {*} (s)) \mathrm{e} ^ {(g _ {i} + m _ {i}) s} \mathrm{d} s \right] \\ = \left[ C _ {i} (0) - C _ {i} ^ {*} (0) - \frac {k _ {i} (C _ {E} (0) - C _ {E} ^ {*} (0))}{g _ {i} + m _ {i} - h} \right] \mathrm{e} ^ {- (g _ {i} + m _ {i}) t} \\ + \frac {k _ {i} (C _ {E} (0) - C _ {E} ^ {*} (0))}{g _ {i} + m _ {i} - h} \mathrm{e} ^ {- h t}, \\ \end{array}
$$

且满足 $\mathrm{d}(C_i(t) - C_i^* (t)) = [k_i(C_E(t) - C_E^* (t)) - (g_i + m_i)(C_i(t) - C_i^* (t))] \mathrm{d}t.$ 

这意味着

$$
\begin{array}{l} \int_ {0} ^ {t} \left| C _ {i} (s) - C _ {i} ^ {*} (s) \right| \mathrm{d} s \\ = \frac {1}{g _ {i} + m _ {i}} \left| C _ {i} (0) - C _ {i} ^ {*} (0) - \frac {k _ {i} \left(C _ {E} (0) - C _ {E} ^ {*} (0)\right)}{g _ {i} + m _ {i} - h} \right| \left(1 - \mathrm{e} ^ {- \left(g _ {i} + m _ {i}\right) t}\right) \\ + \left| \frac {k _ {i} (C _ {E} (0) - C _ {E} ^ {*} (0))}{h (g _ {i} + m _ {i} - h)} \right| (1 - \mathrm{e} ^ {- h t}) <   \infty . \\ \end{array}
$$

综上所述, 我们得到如下结论.

引理 4.1 假设 $C_{i}(t)$ , $C_{i}^{*}(t)$ (i=1,2) 如上所定义, 则

$$
\int_ {0} ^ {\infty} | C _ {i} (s) - C _ {i} ^ {*} (s) | \mathrm{d} s <   \infty . \tag {4.4}
$$

与系统 (4.2) 一起, 我们将考虑系统

$$
\left\{ \begin{array}{l} \mathrm{d} x _ {1} (t) = x _ {1} (t) \left[ b _ {1} - r _ {1 1} C _ {1} ^ {*} (t) - a _ {1 1} x _ {1} (t) - a _ {1 2} x _ {2} (t) \right] \mathrm{d} t + \sigma_ {1} (t) x _ {1} (t) \mathrm{d} B _ {1} (t), \\ \mathrm{d} x _ {2} (t) = x _ {2} (t) \left[ b _ {2} - r _ {2 1} C _ {2} ^ {*} (t) - a _ {2 1} x _ {1} (t) - a _ {2 2} x _ {2} (t) \right] \mathrm{d} t + \sigma_ {2} (t) x _ {2} (t) \mathrm{d} B _ {2} (t). \end{array} \right. \tag {4.5}
$$

## 4.2.1 边界周期解的存在性和全局吸引性

类似于文献 [64] 中定理 4.2 的证明, 我们能证明如下结论.

引理4.2 设 $X_{i}(t)$ 是随机方程

$$
\mathrm{d} X _ {i} (t) = X _ {i} (t) [ b _ {i} - r _ {i 1} C _ {i} ^ {*} (t) - a _ {i i} X _ {i} (t) ] \mathrm{d} t + \sigma_ {i} (t) X _ {i} (t) \mathrm{d} B _ {i} (t), \quad i = 1, 2 \tag {4.6}
$$

的解, 其中 $C_i^*(t)(i=1,2)$ 如 (4.3) 所定义, $\sigma_i(t)(i=1,2)$ 是正的, 非常值 $\theta$ -周期连续函数. 若 $b_i - r_{i1} C_i^*(t) > 0$ 且 $\left\langle b_i - r_{i1} C_i^* - \frac{1}{2} \sigma_i^2 \right\rangle_\theta > 0$ , 则系统 (4.6) 有唯一的 $\theta$ -周期解 $X_i^*(t)$ .

由随机微分方程比较定理和引理 4.2, 我们能证明如下结论.

定理 4.1 设 $(x_{1}(t), x_{2}(t))$ 是系统 (4.5) 满足初值 $(x_{1}(0), x_{2}(0)) \in \mathbb{R}_{+}^{2}$ 的解. 那么,

(i) 若 $b_{1} - r_{11}C_{1}^{*}(t) > 0, \left\langle b_{1} - r_{11}C_{1}^{*} - \frac{1}{2}\sigma_{1}^{2}\right\rangle_{\theta} > 0$ 且 $\left\langle b_{2} - r_{21}C_{2}^{*} - \frac{1}{2}\sigma_{2}^{2}\right\rangle_{\theta} < 0,$ 则系统(4.5)存在一个边界周期解 $(X_1^*(t),0)$ ;

(ii) 若 $b_{2} - r_{21}C_{2}^{*}(t) > 0, \left\langle b_{2} - r_{21}C_{2}^{*} - \frac{1}{2}\sigma_{2}^{2}\right\rangle_{\theta} > 0$ 且 $\left\langle b_{1} - r_{11}C_{1}^{*} - \frac{1}{2}\sigma_{1}^{2}\right\rangle_{\theta} < 0,$ 则系统 (4.5) 存在一个边界周期解 $(0,X_2^*(t))$ 

定义 4.1 设 $x^{*}(t)$ 是系统 (4.2) 的正周期解, 且 $x(t)$ 是系统 (4.2) 满足初值 $x(0) > 0$ 的一个任意解, 若

$$
\lim _ {t \to \infty} | x (t) - x ^ {*} (t) | = 0, \quad {\text {对几乎所有的}} \omega \in \Omega ,
$$

则系统(4.2)的正周期解 $x^{*}(t)$ 是全局吸引的.

类似于文献 [34] 中引理 3.1 的证明, 我们能证明如下结论.

引理4.3 对任意初值 $x(0) \in \mathbb{R}_+^2$ ，系统(4.2)的解有性质

$$
\lim _ {t \to \infty} \sup E (| x _ {i} (t) | ^ {p}) \leqslant K _ {i} (p), \quad \text {对任意} p > 0,
$$

其中 $K_{i}(p)$ 是正常数.

定理 4.2 设 $(x_{1}(t), x_{2}(t))$ 是系统 (4.2) 满足初值 $(x_{1}(0), x_{2}(0)) \in \mathbb{R}_{+}^{2}$ 的正解. 那么,

(i) 若 $b_{1} - r_{11}C_{1}^{*}(t) > 0, \left\langle b_{1} - r_{11}C_{1}^{*} - \frac{1}{2}\sigma_{1}^{2}\right\rangle_{\theta} > 0$ 且 $\left\langle b_{2} - r_{21}C_{2}^{*} - \frac{1}{2}\sigma_{2}^{2}\right\rangle_{\theta} < 0,$ 则系统(4.2）的边界周期解 $(X_1^*(t),0)$ 是全局吸引的；

(ii) 若 $b_{2} - r_{21}C_{2}^{*}(t) > 0, \left\langle b_{2} - r_{21}C_{2}^{*} - \frac{1}{2}\sigma_{2}^{2}\right\rangle_{\theta} > 0$ 且 $\left\langle b_{1} - r_{11}C_{1}^{*} - \frac{1}{2}\sigma_{1}^{2}\right\rangle_{\theta} < 0,$ 则系统(4.2）的边界周期解 $(0,X_2^* (t))$ 是全局吸引的.

证明 我们只证明定理的前半部分.

由定理 4.1, 系统 (4.2) 存在一个边界周期解 $(X_{1}^{*}(t),0)$ . 设 $x(t)$ 是系统 (4.2) 满足初值 $x(0) \in \mathbb{R}_{+}^{2}$ 的一个任意解.

由 Itô 公式和式 (4.2), 可以得到

$$
\begin{array}{l} \mathrm{d} \ln x _ {2} (t) = \left[ b _ {2} - r _ {2 1} C _ {2} (t) - \frac {1}{2} \sigma_ {2} ^ {2} (t) - a _ {2 1} x _ {1} (t) - a _ {2 2} x _ {2} (t) \right] \mathrm{d} t + \sigma_ {2} (t) \mathrm{d} B _ {2} (t) \\ \leqslant \left[ b _ {2} - r _ {2 1} C _ {2} (t) - \frac {1}{2} \sigma_ {2} ^ {2} (t) \right] \mathrm{d} t + \sigma_ {2} (t) \mathrm{d} B _ {2} (t), \\ \end{array}
$$

这意味着

$$
\ln x _ {2} (t) \leqslant \ln x _ {2} (0) + \int_ {0} ^ {t} \left[ b _ {2} - r _ {2 1} C _ {2} (s) - \frac {1}{2} \sigma_ {2} ^ {2} (s) \right] \mathrm{d} s + \int_ {0} ^ {t} \sigma_ {2} (s) \mathrm{d} B _ {2} (s).
$$

利用强大数定律, 函数 $C_{2}^{*}(t)$ 和 $\sigma_{2}(t)$ 的周期性, 可以得到

$$
\limsup _ {t \to \infty} \frac {\ln x _ {2} (t)}{t} \leqslant \left\langle b _ {2} - r _ {2 1} C _ {2} ^ {*} - \frac {1}{2} \sigma_ {2} ^ {2} \right\rangle_ {\theta}.
$$

若条件 $\left\langle b_2 - r_{21}C_2^* -\frac{1}{2}\sigma_2^2\right\rangle_\theta < 0$ 成立, 则

$$
\limsup _ {t \to \infty} \frac {\ln x _ {2} (t)}{t} \leqslant \left\langle b _ {2} - r _ {2 1} C _ {2} ^ {*} - \frac {1}{2} \sigma_ {2} ^ {2} \right\rangle_ {\theta} <   0,
$$

也就是说, $x_{2}(t)$ 几乎必然以指数收敛于0.

由 Itô 公式得

$$
\mathrm{d} \log x _ {1} (t) = \left[ b _ {1} - \frac {\sigma_ {1} ^ {2} (t)}{2} - r _ {1 1} C _ {1} (t) - \sum_ {j = 1} ^ {2} a _ {1 j} x _ {j} (t) \right] \mathrm{d} t + \sigma_ {1} (t) \mathrm{d} B _ {i} (t),
$$

$$
\mathrm{d} \log X _ {1} ^ {*} (t) = \left[ b _ {1} - \frac {\sigma_ {1} ^ {2} (t)}{2} - r _ {1 1} C _ {1} ^ {*} (t) - a _ {1 1} X _ {1} ^ {*} (t) \right] \mathrm{d} t + \sigma_ {1} (t) \mathrm{d} B _ {i} (t),
$$

则

$$
\mathrm{d} (\log x _ {1} (t) - \log X _ {1} ^ {*} (t)) \leqslant [ - r _ {1 1} (C _ {1} (t) - C _ {1} ^ {*} (t)) - a _ {1 1} (x _ {1} (t) - X _ {1} ^ {*} (t)) ] \mathrm{d} t. \tag {4.7}
$$

定义函数 V:

$$
V (t) = | \log x _ {1} (t) - \log X _ {1} ^ {*} (t) |, \quad t \geqslant 0.
$$

由式(4.7)直接计算 $V(t)$ 的右微分 $\mathrm{d}^{+}V(t)$ 得

$$
\begin{array}{l} \mathrm{d} ^ {+} V (t) = \operatorname{sgn} \left(x _ {1} (t) - X _ {1} ^ {*} (t)\right) \mathrm{d} \left(\log x _ {1} (t) - \log X _ {1} ^ {*} (t)\right) \\ \leqslant - \operatorname{sgn} \left(x _ {1} (t) - X _ {1} ^ {*} (t)\right) \left[ r _ {1 1} \left(C _ {1} (t) - C _ {1} ^ {*} (t)\right) + a _ {1 1} \left(x _ {1} (t) - X _ {1} ^ {*} (t)\right) \right] \mathrm{d} t \\ \leqslant r _ {1 1} \left| C _ {1} (t) - C _ {1} ^ {*} (t) \right| \mathrm{d} t - a _ {1 1} \left| x _ {1} (t) - X _ {1} ^ {*} (t) \right| \mathrm{d} t. \tag {4.8} \\ \end{array}
$$

由引理 4.1, 对式 (4.8) 的两端积分得

$$
V (t) + a _ {1 1} \int_ {0} ^ {t} | x _ {1} (s) - X _ {1} ^ {*} (s) | \mathrm{d} s \leqslant V (0) + \int_ {0} ^ {t} r _ {1 1} | C _ {1} (t) - C _ {1} ^ {*} (t) | \mathrm{d} t <   \infty ,
$$

这导致

$$
\left| x (t) - x ^ {*} (t) \right| \in L ^ {1} [ 0, + \infty). \tag {4.9}
$$

从式 (4.9) 可以推出

$$
\liminf _ {t \to \infty} | x _ {1} (t) - X _ {1} ^ {*} (t) | = 0.
$$

下面, 我们将证明

$$
\lim _ {t \rightarrow \infty} | x _ {1} (t) - X _ {1} ^ {*} (t) | = 0. \tag {4.10}
$$

由引理 4.3 我们得到系统 (4.2) 解的 p-阶矩是有界的. 类似于 Li 和 Mao 在文献 [34] 中的定理 6.2 的证明, 可以得到结论. □

## 4.2.2 非平凡周期正解的存在性

本节, 我们将得到系统 (4.5) 非平凡周期正解存在的充分条件.

首先, 我们列出一些用在证明主要结论的假设.

$(H_{1})\ m_{i}>0,\ i=1,2,$ 其中

$$
m _ {i} = \frac {1}{\theta} \int_ {0} ^ {\theta} \left(b _ {i} - \frac {1}{2} \sigma_ {i} ^ {2} (s) - r _ {i 1} C _ {i} ^ {*} (s)\right) \mathrm{d} s.
$$

$$
\left(\mathrm{H} _ {2}\right) \lambda_ {1} > 0, \left(\frac {\lambda_ {1}}{a _ {2 1}}\right) ^ {2} > \left[ \left(b _ {1} - r _ {1 1} C _ {1} ^ {*}\right) ^ {u} + \left(b _ {1} - r _ {1 1} C _ {1} ^ {*}\right) ^ {l} \right] ^ {2} - 4 \left(b _ {1} - r _ {1 1} C _ {1} ^ {*}\right) ^ {l} m _ {1},
$$

其中

$$
\lambda_ {1} = 2 a _ {1 1} m _ {2} - a _ {2 1} \left[ \left(b _ {1} - r _ {1 1} C _ {1} ^ {*}\right) ^ {u} + \left(b _ {1} - r _ {1 1} C _ {1} ^ {*}\right) ^ {l} \right].
$$

$\left(\mathrm{H}_{3}\right)\lambda_{2}>0,\frac{\left(b_{1}-r_{11}C_{1}^{*}\right)^{l}}{a_{11}}\lambda_{2}>\frac{\lambda_{1}}{a_{21}^{2}}a_{22}\left[\left(b_{2}-r_{21}C_{2}^{*}\right)^{u}-m_{2}\right],$ 其中

$$
\lambda_ {2} = a _ {2 2} m _ {1} - a _ {1 2} (b _ {2} - r _ {2 1} C _ {2} ^ {*}) ^ {u}.
$$

$$
\left(\mathrm{H} _ {4}\right) a _ {i i} - \sum_ {j = 1, j \neq i} ^ {2} a _ {j i} > 0, i = 1, 2.
$$

定理 4.3 假设 $(\mathrm{H}_{1}), (\mathrm{H}_{2})$ 和 $(\mathrm{H}_{3})$ 成立，则系统 (4.5) 存在正的 $\theta$ -周期解.

证明 根据引理 1.2 和注 1.2, 为证明定理 4.3, 要找到一个 $C^{2}$ 函数 $V(t, x)$ 和一个闭集 $U \in R_{+}^{2}$ 使得 (1.3) 和 (1.4) 成立.

定义非负 $C^2$ 函数

$$
\begin{array}{l} V (t, x _ {1}, x _ {2}) = x _ {1} - \frac {(b _ {1} - r _ {1 1} C _ {1} ^ {*}) ^ {l}}{a _ {1 1}} \log x _ {1} + \frac {(b _ {1} - r _ {1 1} C _ {1} ^ {*}) ^ {l}}{a _ {1 1}} w _ {1} (t) - \frac {\lambda_ {1}}{a _ {2 1} ^ {2}} \log x _ {2} \\ + \frac {\lambda_ {1}}{a _ {2 1} ^ {2}} w _ {2} (t) + p x _ {2}, \\ \end{array}
$$

其中 $p = \frac{a_{22}\left[\frac{(b_1 - r_{11}C_1^*)^l}{a_{11}}m_1 + \frac{\lambda_1}{a_{21}^2}m_2\right]}{[(b_2 - r_{21}C_2^*)^u]^2}, w_i(t)$ 是定义在 $[0,\infty)$ 上的满足 $w_i'(t) = b_i - \frac{1}{2}\sigma_i^2 (t) - r_{i1}C_i^* (t) - m_i$ 和 $w_{i}(0) = 0$ 的函数. 显然, $w_{i}(t)$ 是一个定义在 $[0,\infty)$ 上的 $\theta$ -周期函数. 事实上, 从 $t$ 到 $t + \theta$ 积分 $w_i'(t)$ , 可得

$$
\begin{array}{l} w _ {i} (t + \theta) - w _ {i} (t) = \int_ {t} ^ {t + \theta} w _ {i} ^ {\prime} (s) \mathrm{d} s \\ = \int_ {t} ^ {t + \theta} \left(b _ {i} - \frac {1}{2} \sigma_ {i} ^ {2} (s) - r _ {i 1} C _ {i} ^ {*} (s)\right) d s \\ - \int_ {0} ^ {\theta} \left(b _ {i} - \frac {1}{2} \sigma_ {i} ^ {2} (s) - r _ {i 1} C _ {i} ^ {*} (s)\right) d s = 0. \\ \end{array}
$$

易知

$$
\liminf _ {k \to \infty , (x _ {1}, x _ {2}) \in \mathbb {R} _ {+} ^ {2} \setminus U _ {k}} V (t, x _ {1} ^ {*}, x _ {2}) = \infty ,
$$

其中 $U_{k} = \left\{(x_{1},x_{2}):(x_{1},x_{2})\in \left(\frac{1}{k},k\right)\times \left(\frac{1}{k},k\right)\right\}$ 因此 $V(t,x_1,x_2)$ 是 $\theta$ -周期且满足(1.2).

下面将找到一个闭集 $U \in \mathbb{R}_+^2$ 满足 $LV(t, x_1, x_2) \leqslant -1$ , $(x_1, x_2) \in \mathbb{R}_+^2 \setminus U$ . 定义 $V_1 = x_1 - \frac{(b_1 - r_{11}C_1^*)^l}{a_{11}} \log x_1 + \frac{(b_1 - r_{11}C_1^*)^l}{a_{11}} w_1(t)$ , $V_2 = -\frac{\lambda_1}{a_{21}^2} \log x_2 + \frac{\lambda_1}{a_{21}^2} w_2(t)$ , $V_3 = px_2$ . 则 $LV = LV_1 + LV_2 + LV_3$ . 应用 Itô 公式, 可得

$$
L V _ {1} = x _ {1} \left[ b _ {1} - r _ {1 1} C _ {1} ^ {*} (t) - a _ {1 1} x _ {1} - a _ {1 2} x _ {2} \right] - \frac {\left(b _ {1} - r _ {1 1} C _ {1} ^ {*}\right) ^ {l}}{a _ {1 1}}
$$

$$
\cdot \left[ b _ {1} - r _ {1 1} C _ {1} ^ {*} (t) - a _ {1 1} x _ {1} - a _ {1 2} x _ {2} - \frac {\sigma_ {1} ^ {2} (t)}{2} \right] + \frac {(b _ {1} - r _ {1 1} C _ {1} ^ {*}) ^ {l}}{a _ {1 1}} w _ {1} ^ {\prime} (t)
$$

$$
\leqslant - a _ {1 1} x _ {1} ^ {2} + \left[ \left(b _ {1} - r _ {1 1} C _ {1} ^ {*}\right) ^ {u} + \left(b _ {1} - r _ {1 1} C _ {1} ^ {*}\right) ^ {l} \right] x _ {1}
$$

$$
+ \frac {a _ {1 2}}{a _ {1 1}} \left(b _ {1} - r _ {1 1} C _ {1} ^ {*}\right) ^ {l} x _ {2} - \frac {\left(b _ {1} - r _ {1 1} C _ {1} ^ {*}\right) ^ {l}}{a _ {1 1}} m _ {1},
$$

$$
L V _ {2} = - \frac {\lambda_ {1}}{a _ {2 1} ^ {2}} \left[ b _ {2} - \frac {\sigma_ {2} ^ {2} (t)}{2} - r _ {2 1} C _ {2} ^ {*} (t) \right] + \frac {\lambda_ {1}}{a _ {2 1} ^ {2}} \left[ a _ {2 1} x _ {1} + a _ {2 2} x _ {2} \right] + \frac {\lambda_ {1}}{a _ {2 1} ^ {2}} w _ {2} ^ {\prime} (t)
$$

$$
= - \frac {\lambda_ {1}}{a _ {2 1} ^ {2}} m _ {2} + \frac {\lambda_ {1}}{a _ {2 1}} x _ {1} + \frac {\lambda_ {1} a _ {2 2}}{a _ {2 1} ^ {2}} x _ {2},
$$

且

$$
L V _ {3} = p x _ {2} \left[ b _ {2} - r _ {2 1} C _ {2} ^ {*} (t) - a _ {2 1} x _ {1} - a _ {2 2} x _ {2} \right] \leqslant p \left(b _ {2} - r _ {2 1} C _ {2} ^ {*}\right) ^ {u} x _ {2} - p a _ {2 2} x _ {2} ^ {2}.
$$

因此

$$
L V \leqslant - a _ {1 1} x _ {1} ^ {2} + \left[ \left(b _ {1} - r _ {1 1} C _ {1} ^ {*}\right) ^ {u} + \left(b _ {1} - r _ {1 1} C _ {1} ^ {*}\right) ^ {l} + \frac {\lambda_ {1}}{a _ {2 1}} \right] x _ {1}
$$

$$
- \frac {\left(b _ {1} - r _ {1 1} C _ {1} ^ {*}\right) ^ {l}}{a _ {1 1}} m _ {1} - p a _ {2 2} x _ {2} ^ {2}
$$

$$
+ \left[ \frac {a _ {1 2}}{a _ {1 1}} (b _ {1} - r _ {1 1} C _ {1} ^ {*}) ^ {l} + \frac {\lambda_ {1} a _ {2 2}}{a _ {2 1} ^ {2}} + p (b _ {2} - r _ {2 1} C _ {2} ^ {*}) ^ {u} \right] x _ {2} - \frac {\lambda_ {1}}{a _ {2 1} ^ {2}} m _ {2}. \tag {4.11}
$$

定义闭集

$$
U _ {\varepsilon} = \left\{\left(x _ {1}, x _ {2}\right) \in \mathbb {R} _ {+} ^ {2}: \varepsilon \leqslant x _ {1} \leqslant \frac {1}{\varepsilon}, \varepsilon \leqslant x _ {2} \leqslant \frac {1}{\varepsilon} \right\},
$$

其中 $0 < \varepsilon < 1$ 是一个充分小的数, 满足

$$
\left[ \left(b _ {1} - r _ {1 1} C _ {1} ^ {*}\right) ^ {u} + \left(b _ {1} - r _ {1 1} C _ {1} ^ {*}\right) ^ {l} + \frac {\lambda_ {1}}{a _ {2 1}} \right] \varepsilon
$$

$$
<   \frac {1}{2} \left\{\frac {\left(b _ {1} - r _ {1 1} C _ {1} ^ {*}\right) ^ {l}}{a _ {1 1}} m _ {1} + \frac {\lambda_ {1}}{a _ {2 1} ^ {2}} m _ {2} \right.
$$

$$
\left. - \frac {\left[ \frac {a _ {1 2}}{a _ {1 1}} \left(b _ {1} - r _ {1 1} C _ {1} ^ {*}\right) ^ {l} + \frac {\lambda_ {1}}{a _ {2 1} ^ {2}} a _ {2 2} + p \left(b _ {2} - r _ {2 1} C _ {2} ^ {*}\right) ^ {u} \right] ^ {2}}{4 p a _ {2 2}} \right\}, \tag {4.12}
$$

$$
\left[ \frac {a _ {1 2}}{a _ {1 1}} \left(b _ {1} - r _ {1 1} C _ {1} ^ {*}\right) ^ {l} + \frac {\lambda_ {1}}{a _ {2 1} ^ {2}} a _ {2 2} + p \left(b _ {2} - r _ {2 1} C _ {2} ^ {*}\right) ^ {u} \right] \varepsilon
$$

$$
<   - \frac {1}{8 a _ {1 1}} \left\{\left[ \left(b _ {1} - r _ {1 1} C _ {1} ^ {*}\right) ^ {u} + \left(b _ {1} - r _ {1 1} C _ {1} ^ {*}\right) ^ {l} \right] ^ {2} - 4 \left(b _ {1} - r _ {1 1} C _ {1} ^ {*}\right) ^ {l} m _ {1} - \left(\frac {\lambda_ {1}}{a _ {2 1}}\right) ^ {2} \right\}, \tag {4.13}
$$

$$
- \frac {a _ {1 1}}{2 \varepsilon^ {2}} + K _ {3} \leqslant - 1, \tag {4.14}
$$

且

$$
- \frac {p a _ {2 2}}{2 \varepsilon^ {2}} + K _ {4} \leqslant - 1, \tag {4.15}
$$

其中 $K_{3}, K_{4}$ 是在后面的证明中定义的正常数.

定义

$$
D _ {\varepsilon} ^ {1} = \left\{\left(x _ {1}, x _ {2}\right) \in \mathbb {R} _ {+} ^ {2}: 0 <   x _ {1} <   \varepsilon \right\}, \quad D _ {\varepsilon} ^ {2} = \left\{\left(x _ {1}, x _ {2}\right) \in \mathbb {R} _ {+} ^ {2}: 0 <   x _ {2} <   \varepsilon \right\},
$$

$$
D _ {\varepsilon} ^ {3} = \left\{\left(x _ {1}, x _ {2}\right) \in \mathbb {R} _ {+} ^ {2}: x _ {1} > \frac {1}{\varepsilon} \right\}, \quad D _ {\varepsilon} ^ {4} = \left\{\left(x _ {1}, x _ {2}\right) \in \mathbb {R} _ {+} ^ {2}: x _ {2} > \frac {1}{\varepsilon} \right\}.
$$

则 $\mathbb{R}_+^2\backslash U_\varepsilon = D_\varepsilon^1\cup D_\varepsilon^2\cup D_\varepsilon^3\cup D_\varepsilon^4.$ 因此我们考虑四种情况.

情况 1 在 $D_{\varepsilon}^{1}$ ,

$$
L V \leqslant - p a _ {2 2} \left(x _ {2} - \frac {\frac {a _ {1 2}}{a _ {1 1}} \left(b _ {1} - r _ {1 1} C _ {1} ^ {*}\right) ^ {l} + \frac {\lambda_ {1} a _ {2 2}}{a _ {2 1} ^ {2}} + p \left(b _ {2} - r _ {2 1} C _ {2} ^ {*}\right) ^ {u}}{2 p a _ {2 2}}\right) ^ {2}
$$

$$
+ \frac {\left[ \frac {a _ {1 2}}{a _ {1 1}} \left(b _ {1} - r _ {1 1} C _ {1} ^ {*}\right) ^ {l} + \frac {\lambda_ {1} a _ {2 2}}{a _ {2 1} ^ {2}} + p \left(b _ {2} - r _ {2 1} C _ {2} ^ {*}\right) ^ {u} \right] ^ {2}}{4 p a _ {2 2}}
$$

$$
- \frac {\left(b _ {1} - r _ {1 1} C _ {1} ^ {*}\right) ^ {l}}{a _ {1 1}} m _ {1} - \frac {\lambda_ {1}}{a _ {2 1} ^ {2}} m _ {2} + \left[ \left(b _ {1} - r _ {1 1} C _ {1} ^ {*}\right) ^ {u} + \left(b _ {1} - r _ {1 1} C _ {1} ^ {*}\right) ^ {l} + \frac {\lambda_ {1}}{a _ {2 1}} \right] \varepsilon
$$

$$
\leqslant \frac {\left[ \frac {a _ {1 2}}{a _ {1 1}} \left(b _ {1} - r _ {1 1} C _ {1} ^ {*}\right) ^ {l} + \frac {\lambda_ {1} a _ {2 2}}{a _ {2 1} ^ {2}} + p \left(b _ {2} - r _ {2 1} C _ {2} ^ {*}\right) ^ {u} \right] ^ {2}}{4 p a _ {2 2}} - \frac {\left(b _ {1} - r _ {1 1} C _ {1} ^ {*}\right) ^ {l}}{a _ {1 1}} m _ {1} - \frac {\lambda_ {1}}{a _ {2 1} ^ {2}} m _ {2}
$$

$$
+ \left[ \left(b _ {1} - r _ {1 1} C _ {1} ^ {*}\right) ^ {u} + \left(b _ {1} - r _ {1 1} C _ {1} ^ {*}\right) ^ {l} + \frac {\lambda_ {1}}{a _ {2 1}} \right] \varepsilon
$$

$$
\leqslant K _ {1},
$$

其中 $K_{1} = \frac{1}{2}\left\{\frac{\left[\frac{a_{12}}{a_{11}}(b_{1} - r_{11}C_{1}^{*})^{l} + \frac{\lambda_{1}a_{22}}{a_{21}^{2}} + p(b_{2} - r_{21}C_{2}^{*})^{u}\right]^{2}}{4pa_{22}} -\frac{(b_{1} - r_{11}C_{1}^{*})^{l}}{a_{11}} m_{1}- \right.$ $\frac{\lambda_1}{a_{21}^2} m_2\Bigg\} < 0.$ 事实上，

$$
\begin{array}{l} \frac {a _ {1 2}}{a _ {1 1}} \left(b _ {1} - r _ {1 1} C _ {1} ^ {*}\right) ^ {l} + \frac {\lambda_ {1}}{a _ {2 1} ^ {2}} a _ {2 2} + p \left(b _ {2} - r _ {2 1} C _ {2} ^ {*}\right) ^ {u} \\ - 2 \sqrt {p} \sqrt {a _ {2 2}} \sqrt {\frac {(b _ {1} - r _ {1 1} C _ {1} ^ {*}) ^ {l}}{a _ {1 1}} m _ {1} + \frac {\lambda_ {1}}{a _ {2 1} ^ {2}} m _ {2}} \\ = \frac {\frac {\lambda_ {1}}{a _ {2 1} ^ {2}} a _ {2 2} \left[ \left(b _ {2} - r _ {2 1} C _ {2} ^ {*}\right) ^ {u} - m _ {2} \right] - \frac {\left(b _ {1} - r _ {1 1} C _ {1} ^ {*}\right) ^ {l}}{a _ {1 1}} \lambda_ {2}}{\left(b _ {2} - r _ {2 1} C _ {2} ^ {*}\right) ^ {u}} <   0, \\ \end{array}
$$

也就是说，

$$
\frac {\left[ \frac {a _ {1 2}}{a _ {1 1}} (b _ {1} - r _ {1 1} C _ {1} ^ {*}) ^ {l} + \frac {\lambda_ {1}}{a _ {2 1} ^ {2}} a _ {2 2} + p (b _ {2} - r _ {2 1} C _ {2} ^ {*}) ^ {u} \right] ^ {2}}{4 p a _ {2 2}} - \frac {(b _ {1} - r _ {1 1} C _ {1} ^ {*}) ^ {l}}{a _ {1 1}} m _ {1} - \frac {\lambda_ {1}}{a _ {2 1} ^ {2}} m _ {2} <   0.
$$

情况 2 在 $D_{\varepsilon}^{2}$ ,

$$
\begin{array}{l} L V \leqslant - a _ {1 1} \left(x _ {1} - \frac {\left(b _ {1} - r _ {1 1} C _ {1} ^ {*}\right) ^ {u} + \left(b _ {1} - r _ {1 1} C _ {1} ^ {*}\right) ^ {l} + \frac {\lambda_ {1}}{a _ {2 1}}}{2 a _ {1 1}}\right) ^ {2} \\ + \frac {1}{4 a _ {1 1}} \left\{\left[ \left(b _ {1} - r _ {1 1} C _ {1} ^ {*}\right) ^ {u} + \left(b _ {1} - r _ {1 1} C _ {1} ^ {*}\right) ^ {l} \right] ^ {2} - 4 \left(b _ {1} - r _ {1 1} C _ {1} ^ {*}\right) ^ {l} m _ {1} - \left(\frac {\lambda_ {1}}{a _ {2 1}}\right) ^ {2} \right\} \\ + \left[ \frac {a _ {1 2}}{a _ {1 1}} \left(b _ {1} - r _ {1 1} C _ {1} ^ {*}\right) ^ {l} + \frac {\lambda_ {1}}{a _ {2 1} ^ {2}} a _ {2 2} + p \left(b _ {2} - r _ {2 1} C _ {2} ^ {*}\right) ^ {u} \right] \varepsilon \\ \leqslant \frac {1}{4 a _ {1 1}} \left\{\left[ \left(b _ {1} - r _ {1 1} C _ {1} ^ {*}\right) ^ {u} + \left(b _ {1} - r _ {1 1} C _ {1} ^ {*}\right) ^ {l} \right] ^ {2} - 4 \left(b _ {1} - r _ {1 1} C _ {1} ^ {*}\right) ^ {l} m _ {1} - \left(\frac {\lambda_ {1}}{a _ {2 1}}\right) ^ {2} \right\} \\ + \left[ \frac {a _ {1 2}}{a _ {1 1}} (b _ {1} - r _ {1 1} C _ {1} ^ {*}) ^ {l} + \frac {\lambda_ {1}}{a _ {2 1} ^ {2}} a _ {2 2} + p (b _ {2} - r _ {2 1} C _ {2} ^ {*}) ^ {u} \right] \varepsilon \\ \leqslant K _ {2}, \\ \end{array}
$$

其中

$$
K _ {2} = \frac {1}{8 a _ {1 1}} \left\{\left[ (b _ {1} - r _ {1 1} C _ {1} ^ {*}) ^ {u} + (b _ {1} - r _ {1 1} C _ {1} ^ {*}) ^ {l} \right] ^ {2} - 4 (b _ {1} - r _ {1 1} C _ {1} ^ {*}) ^ {l} m _ {1} - \left(\frac {\lambda_ {1}}{a _ {2 1}}\right) ^ {2} \right\} <   0.
$$

情况 3 在 $D_{\varepsilon}^{3}$ ,

$$
\begin{array}{l} L V \leqslant - \frac {a _ {1 1}}{2} x _ {1} ^ {2} - \frac {a _ {1 1}}{2} x _ {1} ^ {2} + \left[ \left(b _ {1} - r _ {1 1} C _ {1} ^ {*}\right) ^ {u} + \left(b _ {1} - r _ {1 1} C _ {1} ^ {*}\right) ^ {l} + \frac {\lambda_ {1}}{a _ {2 1}} \right] x _ {1} \\ - \frac {\left(b _ {1} - r _ {1 1} C _ {1} ^ {*}\right) ^ {l}}{a _ {1 1}} m _ {1} - p a _ {2 2} x _ {2} ^ {2} \\ + \left[ \frac {a _ {1 2}}{a _ {1 1}} \left(b _ {1} - r _ {1 1} C _ {1} ^ {*}\right) ^ {l} + \frac {\lambda_ {1}}{a _ {2 1} ^ {2}} a _ {2 2} + p \left(b _ {2} - r _ {2 1} C _ {2} ^ {*}\right) ^ {u} \right] x _ {2} - \frac {\lambda_ {1}}{a _ {2 1} ^ {2}} m _ {2} \\ \leqslant - \frac {a _ {1 1}}{2 \varepsilon^ {2}} + K _ {3}, \\ \end{array}
$$

其中 $K_{3} = \sup_{(x_{1},x_{2})\in \mathbb{R}_{+}^{2}}\left\{-\frac{a_{11}}{2} x_{1}^{2} + \left[(b_{1} - r_{11}C_{1}^{*})^{u} + (b_{1} - r_{11}C_{1}^{*})^{l} + \frac{\lambda_{1}}{a_{21}}\right]x_{1}- \right.$ 

$$
\frac {\left(b _ {1} - r _ {1 1} C _ {1} ^ {*}\right) ^ {l}}{a _ {1 1}} m _ {1} - p a _ {2 2} x _ {2} ^ {2} + \left[ \frac {a _ {1 2}}{a _ {1 1}} \left(b _ {1} - r _ {1 1} C _ {1} ^ {*}\right) ^ {l} + \frac {\lambda_ {1}}{a _ {2 1} ^ {2}} a _ {2 2} + p \left(b _ {2} - r _ {2 1} C _ {2} ^ {*}\right) ^ {u} \right] x _ {2} -
$$

$\left.\frac{\lambda_{1}}{a_{21}^{2}}m_{2}\right\}.$ 由式 (4.14) 可得

$$
L V \leqslant - 1.
$$

情况 4 在 $D_{\varepsilon}^{4}$ ,

$$
\begin{array}{l} L V \leqslant - \frac {p a _ {2 2}}{2} x _ {2} ^ {2} - a _ {1 1} x _ {1} ^ {2} \\ + \left[ \left(b _ {1} - r _ {1 1} C _ {1} ^ {*}\right) ^ {u} + \left(b _ {1} - r _ {1 1} C _ {1} ^ {*}\right) ^ {l} + \frac {\lambda_ {1}}{a _ {2 1}} \right] x _ {1} - \frac {\left(b _ {1} - r _ {1 1} C _ {1} ^ {*}\right) ^ {l}}{a _ {1 1}} m _ {1} \\ - \frac {p a _ {2 2}}{2} x _ {2} ^ {2} + \left[ \frac {a _ {1 2}}{a _ {1 1}} \left(b _ {1} - r _ {1 1} C _ {1} ^ {*}\right) ^ {l} + \frac {\lambda_ {1}}{a _ {2 1} ^ {2}} a _ {2 2} + p \left(b _ {2} - r _ {2 1} C _ {2} ^ {*}\right) ^ {u} \right] x _ {2} - \frac {\lambda_ {1}}{a _ {2 1} ^ {2}} m _ {2} \\ \leqslant - \frac {p a _ {2 2}}{2 \varepsilon^ {2}} + K _ {4}, \\ \end{array}
$$

其中

$$
\begin{array}{l} K _ {4} = \sup _ {(x _ {1}, x _ {2}) \in \mathbb {R} _ {+} ^ {2}} \left\{- a _ {1 1} x _ {1} ^ {2} + \left[ (b _ {1} - r _ {1 1} C _ {1} ^ {*}) ^ {u} + (b _ {1} - r _ {1 1} C _ {1} ^ {*}) ^ {l} + \frac {\lambda_ {1}}{a _ {2 1}} \right] x _ {1} \right. \\ - \frac {\left(b _ {1} - r _ {1 1} C _ {1} ^ {*}\right) ^ {l}}{a _ {1 1}} m _ {1} - \frac {p a _ {2 2}}{2} x _ {2} ^ {2} + \left[ \frac {a _ {1 2}}{a _ {1 1}} \left(b _ {1} - r _ {1 1} C _ {1} ^ {*}\right) ^ {l} + \frac {\lambda_ {1}}{a _ {2 1} ^ {2}} a _ {2 2} \right. \\ \left. + p \left(b _ {2} - r _ {2 1} C _ {2} ^ {*}\right) ^ {u} \right] x _ {2} - \frac {\lambda_ {1}}{a _ {2 1} ^ {2}} m _ {2} \Bigg \}. \\ \end{array}
$$

由式 (4.15) 可得

$$
L V \leqslant - 1.
$$

综上所述, 我们得到

$$
L V \leqslant \min \left\{K _ {1}, K _ {2}, - 1 \right\}, \quad \left(x _ {1}, x _ {2}\right) \in \mathbb {R} _ {+} ^ {2} \backslash U _ {\varepsilon}.
$$

## 4.2.3 非平凡周期正解的全局吸引性

本节, 我们将建立系统 (4.2) 非平凡周期正解的全局吸引性的充分准则.

定理 4.4 假设 $\left(\mathrm{H}_{1}\right)\sim\left(\mathrm{H}_{4}\right)$ 成立，则系统 (4.2) 的周期解是全局吸引的.

证明 由定理4.3, 系统(4.2)存在正周期解 $x^{*}(t)$ . 设 $x(t)$ 是系统(4.2)满足初值 $x(0) \in \mathbb{R}_{+}^{2}$ 的任意解. 由 Itô 公式, 可得

$$
\mathrm{d} \log x _ {i} (t) = \left[ b _ {i} - \frac {\sigma_ {i} ^ {2} (t)}{2} - r _ {i 1} C _ {i} (t) - \sum_ {j = 1} ^ {2} a _ {i j} x _ {j} (t) \right] \mathrm{d} t + \sigma_ {i} (t) \mathrm{d} B _ {i} (t),
$$

$$
\mathrm{d} \log x _ {i} ^ {*} (t) = \left[ b _ {i} - \frac {\sigma_ {i} ^ {2} (t)}{2} - r _ {i 1} C _ {i} ^ {*} (t) - \sum_ {j = 1} ^ {2} a _ {i j} x _ {j} ^ {*} (t) \right] \mathrm{d} t + \sigma_ {i} (t) \mathrm{d} B _ {i} (t),
$$

则

$$
\mathrm{d} (\log x _ {i} (t) - \log x _ {i} ^ {*} (t)) = \left[ - r _ {i 1} (C _ {i} (t) - C _ {i} ^ {*} (t)) - \sum_ {j = 1} ^ {2} a _ {i j} (x _ {j} (t) - x _ {j} ^ {*} (t)) \right] \mathrm{d} t. \tag {4.16}
$$

定义函数 V:

$$
V (t) = \sum_ {i = 1} ^ {2} | \log x _ {i} (t) - \log x _ {i} ^ {*} (t) |, \quad t \geqslant 0.
$$

由式 (4.16) 直接计算 $V(t)$ 的右微分 $\mathrm{d}^{+}V(t)$ 得

$$
\begin{array}{l} \mathrm{d} ^ {+} V (t) \\ = \sum_ {i = 1} ^ {2} \operatorname{sgn} (x _ {i} (t) - x _ {i} ^ {*} (t)) \mathrm{d} (\log x _ {i} (t) - \log x _ {i} ^ {*} (t)) \\ = - \sum_ {i = 1} ^ {2} \operatorname{sgn} (x _ {i} (t) - x _ {i} ^ {*} (t)) \left[ r _ {i 1} (C _ {i} (t) - C _ {i} ^ {*} (t)) + \sum_ {j = 1} ^ {2} a _ {i j} (x _ {j} (t) - x _ {j} ^ {*} (t)) \right] \mathrm{d} t \\ \leqslant \sum_ {i = 1} ^ {2} r _ {i 1} | C _ {i} (t) - C _ {i} ^ {*} (t) | \mathrm{d} t - \sum_ {i = 1} ^ {2} a _ {i i} | x _ {i} (t) - x _ {i} ^ {*} (t) | \mathrm{d} t + \sum_ {i = 1} ^ {2} \sum_ {j = 1, j \neq i} ^ {2} a _ {i j} | x _ {j} (t) - x _ {j} ^ {*} (t) | \mathrm{d} t \\ = \sum_ {i = 1} ^ {2} r _ {i 1} | C _ {i} (t) - C _ {i} ^ {*} (t) | \mathrm{d} t - \sum_ {i = 1} ^ {2} a _ {i i} | x _ {i} (t) - x _ {i} ^ {*} (t) | \mathrm{d} t + \sum_ {j = 1} ^ {2} \sum_ {i = 1, i \neq j} ^ {2} a _ {j i} | x _ {i} (t) - x _ {i} ^ {*} (t) | \mathrm{d} t \\ = \sum_ {i = 1} ^ {2} r _ {i 1} | C _ {i} (t) - C _ {i} ^ {*} (t) | \mathrm{d} t - \sum_ {i = 1} ^ {2} a _ {i i} | x _ {i} (t) - x _ {i} ^ {*} (t) | \mathrm{d} t + \sum_ {i = 1} ^ {2} \sum_ {j = 1, j \neq i} ^ {2} a _ {j i} | x _ {i} (t) - x _ {i} ^ {*} (t) | \mathrm{d} t \\ \end{array}
$$

$$
\begin{array}{l} = \sum_ {i = 1} ^ {2} r _ {i 1} \left| C _ {i} (t) - C _ {i} ^ {*} (t) \right| \mathrm{d} t - \sum_ {i = 1} ^ {2} \left(a _ {i i} - \sum_ {j = 1, j \neq i} ^ {2} a _ {j i}\right) \left| x _ {i} (t) - x _ {i} ^ {*} (t) \right| \mathrm{d} t \\ \leqslant \sum_ {i = 1} ^ {2} r _ {i 1} \left| C _ {i} (t) - C _ {i} ^ {*} (t) \right| \mathrm{d} t - \varphi \sum_ {i = 1} ^ {2} \left| x _ {i} (t) - x _ {i} ^ {*} (t) \right| \mathrm{d} t, \tag {4.17} \\ \end{array}
$$

其中 $\varphi = \min_{1\leqslant i\leqslant 2}\left(a_{ii} - \sum_{j = 1,j\neq i}^{2}a_{ji}\right) > 0.$ 由引理4.1,对式(4.17)的两端积分得

$$
V (t) + \varphi \int_ {0} ^ {t} \sum_ {i = 1} ^ {2} | x _ {i} (s) - x _ {i} ^ {*} (s) | \mathrm{d} s \leqslant V (0) + \int_ {0} ^ {t} \sum_ {i = 1} ^ {2} r _ {i 1} | C _ {i} (s) - C _ {i} ^ {*} (s) | \mathrm{d} s <   \infty .
$$

因此，

$$
| x (t) - x ^ {*} (t) | \leqslant \sum_ {i = 1} ^ {n} | x _ {i} (t) - x _ {i} ^ {*} (t) | \in L ^ {1} [ 0, + \infty). \tag {4.18}
$$

从式 (4.18) 可以推出

$$
\liminf _ {t \to \infty} | x (t) - x ^ {*} (t) | = 0.
$$

下面, 我们将证明

$$
\lim _ {t \rightarrow \infty} | x (t) - x ^ {*} (t) | = 0. \tag {4.19}
$$

由引理 4.3 我们得到系统 (4.2) 解的 p-阶矩是有界的. 类似于 Li 和 Mao 文献 [34] 中的定理 6.2 的证明, 可以得到结论. □

## 4.2.4 数值模拟

本节我们将用数值的方法进行结论的模拟. 利用 Milstein 高阶方法 [33], 我们考虑离散方程

$$
\left\{ \begin{array}{l} x _ {1, k + 1} = x _ {1, k} + x _ {1, k} \left[ \left(b _ {1} - r _ {1 1} C _ {1} (k \Delta t) - a _ {1 1} x _ {1, k} - a _ {1 2} x _ {2, k}\right) \Delta t \right. \\ \quad \left. + \sigma_ {1} (k \Delta t) \varepsilon_ {1, k} \sqrt {\Delta t} + \frac {\sigma_ {1} ^ {2} (k \Delta t)}{2} \left(\varepsilon_ {1, k} ^ {2} \Delta t - \Delta t\right) \right], \\ x _ {2, k + 1} = x _ {2, k} + x _ {2, k} \left[ \left(b _ {2} - r _ {2 1} C _ {2} (k \Delta t) - a _ {2 1} x _ {1, k} - a _ {2 2} x _ {2, k}\right) \Delta t \right. \\ \quad \left. + \sigma_ {2} (k \Delta t) \varepsilon_ {2, k} \sqrt {\Delta t} + \frac {\sigma_ {2} ^ {2} (k \Delta t)}{2} \left(\varepsilon_ {2, k} ^ {2} \Delta t - \Delta t\right) \right]. \end{array} \right. \tag {4.20}
$$

例 4.1 选取参数 $b_{1}=0.8, b_{2}=0.6, r_{11}=r_{21}=0.5, C_{1}^{*}(t)=C_{2}^{*}(t)=0.4-0.2\sin t, a_{11}=a_{12}=a_{21}=a_{22}=0.2, \sigma_{1}(t)=0.1+0.05\sin t, \sigma_{2}(t)=0.9+0.1\sin t, x_{1}(0)=1.8, x_{2}(0)=0.8.$ 

记 $b_{1} - r_{11}C_{1}^{*}(t) = 0.6 + 0.1\sin t > 0,\left\langle b_{1} - r_{11}C_{1}^{*} - \frac{1}{2}\sigma_{1}^{2}\right\rangle_{2\pi} = 0.595 > 0$ 和 $\left\langle b_2 - r_{21}C_2^* -\frac{1}{2}\sigma_2^2\right\rangle_{2\pi} = -0.0075 <   0.$ 定理4.1的条件满足，则系统(4.5）存在边界周期解 $(X_1^* (t),0)$ 图4.1和图4.2的数值模拟可以验证这些结论.

例4.2 选取参数 $b_{1} = 0.8, b_{2} = 0.55, r_{11} = 0.5, r_{21} = 0.5, C_{1}^{*}(t) = 0.4 + 0.2\sin t, C_{2}^{*}(t) = 0.3 + 0.2\sin t, a_{11} = 0.6, a_{12} = 0.2, a_{21} = 0.2, a_{22} = 0.8, \sigma_{1}(t) = 0.02 + 0.001\sin t, \sigma_{2}(t) = 0.01 + 0.002\sin t, x_{1}(0) = 0.9, x_{2}(0) = 0.3.$ 

记

$$
m _ {1} = \frac {1}{2 \pi} \int_ {0} ^ {2} \pi \left(b _ {1} - \frac {1}{2} \sigma_ {1} ^ {2} (s) - r _ {1 1} C _ {1} ^ {*} (s)\right) d s \doteq 0. 7 9 9 8 > 0,
$$

$$
m _ {2} = \frac {1}{2 \pi} \int_ {0} ^ {2} \pi \left(b _ {2} - \frac {1}{2} \sigma_ {2} ^ {2} (s) - r _ {2 1} C _ {2} ^ {*} (s)\right) d s \doteq 0. 3 9 9 9 5 > 0,
$$

$$
\lambda_ {1} = 2 a _ {1 1} m _ {2} - a _ {2 1} \left[ \left(b _ {1} - r _ {1 1} C _ {1} ^ {*} (t)\right) ^ {u} + \left(b _ {1} - r _ {1 1} C _ {1} ^ {*} (t)\right) ^ {l} \right] = 0. 2 3 9 9 3 > 0,
$$

$$
\lambda_ {2} = a _ {2 2} m _ {1} - a _ {1 2} \left(b _ {2} - r _ {2 1} C _ {2} ^ {*} (t)\right) ^ {u} = 0. 5 3 9 8 4 > 0,
$$

$$
\left(\frac {\lambda_ {1}}{a _ {2 1}}\right) ^ {2} - \left[ \left(b _ {1} - r _ {1 1} C _ {1} ^ {*} (t)\right) ^ {u} + \left(b _ {1} - r _ {1 1} C _ {1} ^ {*} (t)\right) ^ {l} \right] ^ {2} + 4 \left(b _ {1} - r _ {1 1} C _ {1} ^ {*} (t)\right) ^ {l} m _ {1} = 1. 1 1 8 4 > 0
$$

和

$$
\frac {\left(b _ {1} - r _ {1 1} C _ {1} ^ {*} (t)\right) ^ {l}}{a _ {1 1}} \lambda_ {2} - \frac {\lambda_ {1}}{a _ {2 1} ^ {2}} a _ {2 2} \left[ \left(b _ {2} - r _ {2 1} C _ {2} ^ {*} (t)\right) ^ {u} - m _ {2} \right] = 0. 3 0 9 7 7 > 0.
$$

定理 4.3 的条件满足, 则系统 (4.5) 存在一个 $2\pi$ 的周期解. 从图 4.3 和图 4.4, 我们看到对任意初值, 确定性系统的解经过一个周期的时间后进入周期轨道; 当噪声强度很小时, 随机系统的解在周期轨道的小邻域内震动.

## 4.3 污染环境下的随机非自治互惠模型

本节我们考虑污染环境下的随机非自治互惠模型

$$
\left\{ \begin{array}{l} \mathrm{d} x _ {1} (t) = x _ {1} (t) [ b _ {1} - r _ {1 1} C _ {1} (t) - a _ {1 1} x _ {1} (t) + a _ {1 2} x _ {2} (t) ] \mathrm{d} t + \sigma_ {1} (t) x _ {1} (t) \mathrm{d} B _ {1} (t), \\ \mathrm{d} x _ {2} (t) = x _ {2} (t) [ b _ {2} - r _ {2 1} C _ {2} (t) + a _ {2 1} x _ {1} (t) - a _ {2 2} x _ {2} (t) ] \mathrm{d} t + \sigma_ {2} (t) x _ {2} (t) \mathrm{d} B _ {2} (t), \\ \mathrm{d} C _ {1} (t) = [ k _ {1} C _ {E} (t) - (g _ {1} + m _ {1}) C _ {1} (t) ] \mathrm{d} t, \\ \mathrm{d} C _ {2} (t) = [ k _ {2} C _ {E} (t) - (g _ {2} + m _ {2}) C _ {2} (t) ] \mathrm{d} t, \\ \mathrm{d} C _ {E} (t) = [ - h C _ {E} (t) + u (t) ] \mathrm{d} t, \end{array} \right. \tag {4.21}
$$

其中参数函数 $u(t)$ 和 $\sigma_{i}(t)(i=1,2)$ 为正的、非常值、具有 $\theta$ -周期的连续函数， $B_{i}(t), i=1,2$ 是独立的标准的布朗运动.

本节, 我们始终使用如下记法. 如果 $f(t)$ 是定义在 $[0, \infty)$ 上的有界函数, 记

$$
f ^ {u} = \sup _ {t \in [ 0, \infty)} f (t), \quad f ^ {l} = \inf _ {t \in [ 0, \infty)} f (t).
$$

定义

$$
\bar {\boldsymbol {A}} = \left( \begin{array}{c c} a _ {1 1} & - a _ {1 2} \\ - a _ {2 1} & a _ {2 2} \end{array} \right),
$$

其中 $a_{ij}(i,j=1,2)$ 是系统 (4.21) 的系数.

因为 $C_1(t), C_2(t)$ 和 $C_E(t)$ 是毒物的浓度, 所以不等式 $0 \leqslant C_i(t) < 1 (i = 1,2)$ 和 $0 \leqslant C_E(t) < 1$ 成立. 本节, 我们总假设条件 $0 < k_i \leqslant g_i + m_i (i = 1,2), u(t) \leqslant h$ 成立, 这意味着 $0 \leqslant C_i(t) < 1 (i = 1,2), 0 \leqslant C_E(t) < 1$ (见文献 [56]).

因为 $u(t)$ 是一个正的 $\theta$ -周期连续函数, 所以易证 $C_E^*(t)$ 是一个正的 $\theta$ -周期连续函数, 这里

$$
C _ {E} ^ {*} (t) = \frac {\int_ {t} ^ {t + \theta} \mathrm{e} ^ {h (s - t)} u (s) \mathrm{d} s}{\mathrm{e} ^ {h \theta} - 1},
$$

且满足 $\mathrm{d}C_E^* (t) = [-hC_E^* (t) + u(t)]\mathrm{d}t.$ 

同理, 我们可以得出 $C_i^*(t)(i = 1,2)$ 也是一个正的 $\theta$ -周期连续函数, 这里

$$
C _ {i} ^ {*} (t) = \frac {\int_ {t} ^ {t + \theta} \mathrm{e} ^ {(g _ {i} + m _ {i}) (s - t)} k _ {i} C _ {E} ^ {*} (s) \mathrm{d} s}{\mathrm{e} ^ {(g _ {i} + m _ {i}) \theta} - 1}, \tag {4.22}
$$

且满足 $\mathrm{d}C_i^* (t) = [k_iC_E^* (t) - (g_i + m_i)C_i^* (t)]\mathrm{d}t.$ 

引理 4.4 假设 $C_{i}(t)$ , $C_{i}^{*}(t)$ (i=1,2) 如上所定义. 则

$$
\int_ {0} ^ {\infty} | C _ {i} (s) - C _ {i} ^ {*} (s) | \mathrm{d} s <   \infty . \tag {4.23}
$$

证明 从式 (4.21) 可以推出

$$
C _ {E} (t) - C _ {E} ^ {*} (t) = \left(C _ {E} (0) - C _ {E} ^ {*} (0)\right) \mathrm{e} ^ {- h t},
$$

且满足 $\mathrm{d}(C_E(t) - C_E^* (t)) = -h(C_E(t) - C_E^* (t))\mathrm{d}t.$ 

利用常数变易法可以推出

$$
\begin{array}{l} C _ {i} (t) - C _ {i} ^ {*} (t) \\ = \mathrm{e} ^ {- (g _ {i} + m _ {i}) t} \left[ C _ {i} (0) - C _ {i} ^ {*} (0) + k _ {i} \int_ {0} ^ {t} \left(C _ {E} (s) - C _ {E} ^ {*} (s)\right) \mathrm{e} ^ {(g _ {i} + m _ {i}) s} \mathrm{d} s \right] \\ = \left[ C _ {i} (0) - C _ {i} ^ {*} (0) - \frac {k _ {i} \left(C _ {E} (0) - C _ {E} ^ {*} (0)\right)}{g _ {i} + m _ {i} - h} \right] \mathrm{e} ^ {- \left(g _ {i} + m _ {i}\right) t} + \frac {k _ {i} \left(C _ {E} (0) - C _ {E} ^ {*} (0)\right)}{g _ {i} + m _ {i} - h} \mathrm{e} ^ {- h t}, \\ \end{array}
$$

且满足 $\mathrm{d}(C_i(t) - C_i^*(t)) = [k_i(C_E(t) - C_E^*(t)) - (g_i + m_i)(C_i(t) - C_i^*(t))] \mathrm{d}t.$ 

这意味着

$$
\begin{array}{l} \int_ {0} ^ {t} \left| C _ {i} (s) - C _ {i} ^ {*} (s) \right| \mathrm{d} s \\ = \frac {1}{g _ {i} + m _ {i}} \left| C _ {i} (0) - C _ {i} ^ {*} (0) - \frac {k _ {i} \left(C _ {E} (0) - C _ {E} ^ {*} (0)\right)}{g _ {i} + m _ {i} - h} \right| \left(1 - \mathrm{e} ^ {- \left(g _ {i} + m _ {i}\right) t}\right) \\ + \left| \frac {k _ {i} \left(C _ {E} (0) - C _ {E} ^ {*} (0)\right)}{h \left(g _ {i} + m _ {i} - h\right)} \right| \left(1 - \mathrm{e} ^ {- h t}\right) <   \infty . \\ \end{array}
$$

□ 

下面我们将考虑系统

$$
\left\{ \begin{array}{l} \mathrm{d} x _ {1} (t) = x _ {1} (t) \left[ b _ {1} - r _ {1 1} C _ {1} ^ {*} (t) - a _ {1 1} x _ {1} (t) + a _ {1 2} x _ {2} (t) \right] \mathrm{d} t + \sigma_ {1} (t) x _ {1} (t) \mathrm{d} B _ {1} (t), \\ \mathrm{d} x _ {2} (t) = x _ {2} (t) \left[ b _ {2} - r _ {2 1} C _ {2} ^ {*} (t) + a _ {2 1} x _ {1} (t) - a _ {2 2} x _ {2} (t) \right] \mathrm{d} t + \sigma_ {2} (t) x _ {2} (t) \mathrm{d} B _ {2} (t). \end{array} \right. \tag {4.24}
$$

## 4.3.1 非平凡周期解的存在性和全局吸引性

本节, 我们将得到系统 (4.24) 非平凡周期正解的存在和全局吸引的充分条件.
首先, 我们列出一些用在证明主要结论的假设和引理.

$(H_{1}) a_{ii} > 0 (i = 1, 2);$ 

$(H_{2}) \bar{A}$ 是非奇异 M-矩阵;

$(H_{3})\int_{0}^{\theta}b_{i}-r_{i1}C_{i}^{*}(s)-\frac{\sigma_{i}^{2}(s)}{2}\mathrm{d}s>0(i=1,2).$ 

引理 4.5 $^{[65]}$ 若 $A \in Z^{n \times n}$ , 其中 $Z^{n \times n} = \{A = (a_{ij})_{n \times n} : a_{ij} \leqslant 0, i \neq j\}$ , 则下列表述都是等价的:

(1) A 是非奇异 M-矩阵.

(2) A 的所有顺序主子式都是正的.

(3) 对 $R^{n}$ 中的任意 $y \gg 0$ ，线性方程 Ax = y 存在唯一解 $x \gg 0$ .

引理 4.6 $^{[66]}$ 若 A 是非奇异 M-矩阵，则存在一列正数 $d_{1}, d_{2}, \cdots, d_{n}$ 使得矩阵 $B = \frac{1}{2}(DA + A^{\mathrm{T}}D)$ 是正定的，其中 $D = \mathrm{diag}(d_{1}, d_{2}, \cdots, d_{n})$ .

利用与文献 [7] 相同的方法可以证明.

定理 4.5 假设 $(\mathrm{H}_{1}), (\mathrm{H}_{2})$ 和 $(\mathrm{H}_{3})$ 成立，则系统 (4.24) 存在正的 $\theta$ -周期解.

下面我们来建立系统 (4.21) 非平凡周期正解的全局吸引性的充分准则.

定义 4.2 设 $x^{*}(t)$ 是系统 (4.21) 的正周期解, 且 $x(t)$ 是系统 (4.21) 满足初值 $x(0) > 0$ 的一个任意解. 若

$$
\lim _ {t \to \infty} | x (t) - x ^ {*} (t) | = 0, \quad {\text {对几乎所有的}} \omega \in \Omega ,
$$

则系统(4.21)的正周期解 $x^{*}(t)$ 是全局吸引的.

类似于文献 [34] 中引理 3.1 的证明, 我们能证明如下引理.

引理 4.7 假设 $(\mathrm{H}_{1})$ 和 $(\mathrm{H}_{2})$ 成立，则对任意初值 $x(0) \in \mathbb{R}_{+}^{2}$ ，系统 (4.21) 的解有性质

$$
\lim _ {t \to \infty} \sup E (| x _ {i} (t) | ^ {p}) \leqslant K _ {i} (p), \quad \text {对任意} \quad p > 0,
$$

其中 $K_{i}(p)$ 是正常数.

定理 4.6 假设 $(\mathrm{H}_{1}), (\mathrm{H}_{2})$ 和 $(\mathrm{H}_{3})$ 成立，则系统 (4.21) 的周期解是全局吸引的.

证明 由定理4.5可知, 系统(4.21)存在一个正周期解. 令 $x(t)$ 为系统(4.21)的任意一个解. 定义 $V$ 函数如下:

$$
V (t) = \sum_ {i = 1} ^ {2} k _ {i} | \log x _ {i} (t) - \log x _ {i} ^ {*} (t) |,
$$

其中 $k_{i}$ 为正常数满足

$$
\bar {\boldsymbol {A}} ^ {\mathrm{T}} (k _ {1}, k _ {2}) ^ {\mathrm{T}} = (1, 1) ^ {\mathrm{T}},
$$

则

$$
k _ {i} a _ {i i} - \sum_ {j \neq i} k _ {j} a _ {j i} = 1, \quad 1 \leqslant i \leqslant n. \tag {4.25}
$$

我们知道如果 $\bar{A}$ 是非奇异 $M$ -矩阵, 那么 $\bar{A}^{\mathrm{T}}$ 也是非奇异 $M$ -矩阵. 则 $k_{i}$ 的存在性可由引理4.5得证. 由Itô公式可得

$$
\begin{array}{l} \mathrm{d} (\log x _ {i} (t) - \log x _ {i} ^ {*} (t)) = - \left[ r _ {i 1} \left(C _ {i} (t) - C _ {i} ^ {*} (t)\right) + a _ {i i} \left(x _ {i} (t) - x _ {i} ^ {*} (t)\right) \right. \\ \left. - \sum_ {j \neq i} a _ {i j} (x _ {j} (t) - x _ {j} ^ {*} (t)) \right] \mathrm{d} t. \tag {4.26} \\ \end{array}
$$

沿着方程 (4.26) 计算右导数可得

$$
\begin{array}{l} \mathrm{d} ^ {+} V (t) = \sum_ {i = 1} ^ {2} k _ {i} \mathrm{sgn} (x _ {i} (t) - x _ {i} ^ {*} (t)) \mathrm{d} (\log x _ {i} (t) - \log x _ {i} ^ {*} (t)) \\ = - \sum_ {i = 1} ^ {2} k _ {i} \mathrm{sgn} (x _ {i} (t) - x _ {i} ^ {*} (t)) \left[ r _ {i 1} (C _ {i} (t) - C _ {i} ^ {*} (t)) + a _ {i i} (x _ {i} (t) - x _ {i} ^ {*} (t)) \right. \\ \left. - \sum_ {j \neq i} a _ {i j} (x _ {j} (t) - x _ {j} ^ {*} (t)) \right] \mathrm{d} t \\ \leqslant \sum_ {i = 1} ^ {2} k _ {i} r _ {i 1} | C _ {i} (t) - C _ {i} ^ {*} (t) | \mathrm{d} t - \sum_ {i = 1} ^ {2} k _ {i} a _ {i i} | x _ {i} (t) - x _ {i} ^ {*} (t) | \mathrm{d} t \\ + \sum_ {i = 1} ^ {2} \sum_ {j \neq i} k _ {i} a _ {i j} | x _ {j} (t) - x _ {j} ^ {*} (t) | \mathrm{d} t \\ = \sum_ {i = 1} ^ {2} k _ {i} r _ {i 1} | C _ {i} (t) - C _ {i} ^ {*} (t) | \mathrm{d} t - \sum_ {i = 1} ^ {2} k _ {i} a _ {i i} | x _ {i} (t) - x _ {i} ^ {*} (t) | \mathrm{d} t \\ + \sum_ {j = 1} ^ {2} \sum_ {i \neq j} k _ {j} a _ {j i} | x _ {i} (t) - x _ {i} ^ {*} (t) | \mathrm{d} t \\ = \sum_ {i = 1} ^ {2} k _ {i} r _ {i 1} | C _ {i} (t) - C _ {i} ^ {*} (t) | \mathrm{d} t - \sum_ {i = 1} ^ {2} k _ {i} a _ {i i} | x _ {i} (t) - x _ {i} ^ {*} (t) | \mathrm{d} t \\ \end{array}
$$

$$
+ \sum_ {i = 1} ^ {2} \sum_ {j \neq i} k _ {j} a _ {j i} | x _ {i} (t) - x _ {i} ^ {*} (t) | \mathrm{d} t
$$

$$
= \sum_ {i = 1} ^ {2} k _ {i} r _ {i 1} \left| C _ {i} (t) - C _ {i} ^ {*} (t) \right| \mathrm{d} t - \sum_ {i = 1} ^ {2} \left(k _ {i} a _ {i i} (t) - \sum_ {j \neq i} k _ {j} a _ {j i} (t)\right) \left| x _ {i} (t) - x _ {i} ^ {*} (t) \right| \mathrm{d} t,
$$

由式 (4.25) 有

$$
\mathrm{d} ^ {+} V (t) \leqslant \sum_ {i = 1} ^ {2} k _ {i} r _ {i 1} \left| C _ {i} (t) - C _ {i} ^ {*} (t) \right| \mathrm{d} t - \sum_ {i = 1} ^ {2} \left| x _ {i} (t) - x _ {i} ^ {*} (t) \right| \mathrm{d} t. \tag {4.27}
$$

由引理 4.4, 将 (4.27) 两端同时积分有

$$
V (t) + \int_ {0} ^ {t} \sum_ {i = 1} ^ {2} | x _ {i} (s) - x _ {i} ^ {*} (s) | \mathrm{d} s \leqslant V (0) + \int_ {0} ^ {t} \sum_ {i = 1} ^ {2} k _ {i} r _ {i 1} | C _ {i} (s) - C _ {i} ^ {*} (s) | \mathrm{d} s <   \infty ,
$$

则

$$
\left| \left(x (t) - x ^ {*} (t)\right) \right| \leqslant \sum_ {i = 1} ^ {n} \left| x _ {i} (t) - x _ {i} ^ {*} (t) \right| \in L ^ {1} [ 0, + \infty). \tag {4.28}
$$

由式 $(4.28)$ 可直接得出

$$
\liminf _ {t \to \infty} | x (t) - x ^ {*} (t) | = 0.
$$

接着我们将证明

$$
\lim _ {t \to \infty} | x (t) - x ^ {*} (t) | = 0. \tag {4.29}
$$

由引理 4.7, 我们得到系统 (4.21) 解的 p 阶矩是有界的, 用文献 [34] 中定理 6.2 类似的方法则可完成余下的证明. □

## 4.3.2 数值模拟

本节我们将用数值的方法进行结论的模拟. 利用 Milstein 高阶方法 [33], 我们考虑离散方程

$$
\left\{ \begin{array}{l} x _ {1, k + 1} = x _ {1, k} + x _ {1, k} \left[ \left(b _ {1} - r _ {1 1} C _ {1} (k \Delta t) - a _ {1 1} x _ {1, k} + a _ {1 2} x _ {2, k}\right) \Delta t \right. \\ \quad \left. + \sigma_ {1} (k \Delta t) \varepsilon_ {1, k} \sqrt {\Delta t} + \frac {\sigma_ {1} ^ {2} (k \Delta t)}{2} \left(\varepsilon_ {1, k} ^ {2} \Delta t - \Delta t\right) \right], \\ x _ {2, k + 1} = x _ {2, k} + x _ {2, k} \left[ \left(b _ {2} - r _ {2 1} C _ {2} (k \Delta t) + a _ {2 1} x _ {1, k} - a _ {2 2} x _ {2, k}\right) \Delta t \right. \\ \quad \left. + \sigma_ {2} (k \Delta t) \varepsilon_ {2, k} \sqrt {\Delta t} + \frac {\sigma_ {2} ^ {2} (k \Delta t)}{2} \left(\varepsilon_ {2, k} ^ {2} \Delta t - \Delta t\right) \right], \end{array} \right. \tag {4.30}
$$

选取参数 $b_{1} = 0.8, b_{2} = 0.6, r_{11} = r_{21} = 0.5, C_{1}^{*}(t) = C_{2}^{*}(t) = 0.4 - 0.2\sin t, a_{11} = 0.4, a_{12} = a_{21} = 0.3, a_{22} = 0.6, x_{1}(0) = 2, x_{2}(0) = 1.5.$ 可以看出 $\bar{A}$ 是非奇异 $M$ -矩阵，其中 $\bar{A} = \left( \begin{array}{cc}0.4 & -0.3\\ -0.3 & 0.6 \end{array} \right).$ 

图 4.5 和图 4.6 中, 我们选择 $\sigma_{1}(t)=0.03+0.005\sin t,\sigma_{2}(t)=0.01+0.002\sin t,$ 则条件 $\int_{0}^{2\pi}\left(b_{1}-r_{11}C_{1}(s)-\frac{\sigma_{1}^{2}(s)}{2}\right)\mathrm{d}s>0,\int_{0}^{2\pi}\left(b_{2}-r_{21}C_{2}(s)-\frac{\sigma_{2}^{2}(s)}{2}\right)\mathrm{d}s>0$ 满足. 从图中我们可以看出, 确定性系统的解经过一个周期的时间后进入周期轨道; 当噪声强度很小时, 随机系统的解在周期轨道的小邻域内震动.

## 4.4 污染环境下的随机非自治捕食-食饵模型

本节我们考虑污染环境下的随机非自治捕食-食饵模型

$$
\left\{ \begin{array}{l} \mathrm{d} x _ {1} (t) = x _ {1} (t) [ b _ {1} - r _ {1 1} C _ {1} (t) - a _ {1 1} x _ {1} (t) - a _ {1 2} x _ {2} (t) ] \mathrm{d} t + \sigma_ {1} (t) x _ {1} (t) \mathrm{d} B _ {1} (t), \\ \mathrm{d} x _ {2} (t) = x _ {2} (t) [ - b _ {2} - r _ {2 1} C _ {2} (t) + a _ {2 1} x _ {1} (t) - a _ {2 2} x _ {2} (t) ] \mathrm{d} t + \sigma_ {2} (t) x _ {2} (t) \mathrm{d} B _ {2} (t), \\ \mathrm{d} C _ {1} (t) = [ k _ {1} C _ {E} (t) - (g _ {1} + m _ {1}) C _ {1} (t) ] \mathrm{d} t, \\ \mathrm{d} C _ {2} (t) = [ k _ {2} C _ {E} (t) - (g _ {2} + m _ {2}) C _ {2} (t) ] \mathrm{d} t, \\ \mathrm{d} C _ {E} (t) = [ - h C _ {E} (t) + u (t) ] \mathrm{d} t, \end{array} \right. \tag {4.31}
$$

其中参数函数 $u(t)$ 和 $\sigma_{i}(t)(i = 1,2)$ 为正的、非常值、具有 $\theta$ -周期的连续函数， $B_{i}(t), i = 1,2$ 是独立的标准的布朗运动.

本节, 我们始终使用如下记法. 如果 $f(t)$ 是定义在 $[0, \infty)$ 上的有界函数, 记

$$
f ^ {u} = \sup _ {t \in [ 0, \infty)} f (t), \quad f ^ {l} = \inf _ {t \in [ 0, \infty)} f (t).
$$

因为 $C_1(t), C_2(t)$ 和 $C_E(t)$ 是毒物的浓度, 所以不等式 $0 \leqslant C_i(t) < 1 (i = 1,2)$ 和 $0 \leqslant C_E(t) < 1$ 成立. 本节, 我们总假设条件 $0 < k_i \leqslant g_i + m_i (i = 1,2), u(t) \leqslant h$ 成立, 这意味着 $0 \leqslant C_i(t) < 1 (i = 1,2), 0 \leqslant C_E(t) < 1$ (见文献 [56]).

因为 $u(t)$ 是一个正的 $\theta$ -周期连续函数, 所以易证 $C_E^*(t)$ 是一个正的 $\theta$ -周期连续函数, 这里

$$
C _ {E} ^ {*} (t) = \frac {\int_ {t} ^ {t + \theta} \mathrm{e} ^ {h (s - t)} u (s) \mathrm{d} s}{\mathrm{e} ^ {h \theta} - 1},
$$

且满足 $\mathrm{d}C_E^* (t) = [-hC_E^* (t) + u(t)]\mathrm{d}t.$ 

同理, 我们可以得出 $C_i^*(t)(i = 1,2)$ 也是一个正的 $\theta$ -周期连续函数, 这里

$$
C _ {i} ^ {*} (t) = \frac {\int_ {t} ^ {t + \theta} \mathrm{e} ^ {(g _ {i} + m _ {i}) (s - t)} k _ {i} C _ {E} ^ {*} (s) \mathrm{d} s}{\mathrm{e} ^ {(g _ {i} + m _ {i}) \theta} - 1}, \tag {4.32}
$$

且满足 $\mathrm{d}C_i^* (t) = [k_iC_E^* (t) - (g_i + m_i)C_i^* (t)]\mathrm{d}t.$ 

引理4.8 假设 $C_i(t), C_i^*(t) (i = 1,2)$ 如上所定义. 则

$$
\int_ {0} ^ {\infty} \left| C _ {i} (s) - C _ {i} ^ {*} (s) \right| \mathrm{d} s <   \infty . \tag {4.33}
$$

证明 从式 (4.31) 可以推出

$$
C _ {E} (t) - C _ {E} ^ {*} (t) = \left(C _ {E} (0) - C _ {E} ^ {*} (0)\right) \mathrm{e} ^ {- h t},
$$

且满足 $\mathrm{d}(C_E(t) - C_E^* (t)) = -h(C_E(t) - C_E^* (t))\mathrm{d}t.$ 

利用常数变易法可以推出

$$
\begin{array}{l} C _ {i} (t) - C _ {i} ^ {*} (t) \\ = \mathrm{e} ^ {- (g _ {i} + m _ {i}) t} \left[ C _ {i} (0) - C _ {i} ^ {*} (0) + k _ {i} \int_ {0} ^ {t} \left(C _ {E} (s) - C _ {E} ^ {*} (s)\right) \mathrm{e} ^ {(g _ {i} + m _ {i}) s} \mathrm{d} s \right] \\ = \left[ C _ {i} (0) - C _ {i} ^ {*} (0) - \frac {k _ {i} \left(C _ {E} (0) - C _ {E} ^ {*} (0)\right)}{g _ {i} + m _ {i} - h} \right] \mathrm{e} ^ {- \left(g _ {i} + m _ {i}\right) t} + \frac {k _ {i} \left(C _ {E} (0) - C _ {E} ^ {*} (0)\right)}{g _ {i} + m _ {i} - h} \mathrm{e} ^ {- h t}, \\ \end{array}
$$

且满足 $\mathrm{d}(C_i(t) - C_i^* (t)) = [k_i(C_E(t) - C_E^* (t)) - (g_i + m_i)(C_i(t) - C_i^* (t))] \mathrm{d}t.$ 

这意味着

$$
\begin{array}{l} \int_ {0} ^ {t} \left| C _ {i} (s) - C _ {i} ^ {*} (s) \right| \mathrm{d} s \\ = \frac {1}{g _ {i} + m _ {i}} \left| C _ {i} (0) - C _ {i} ^ {*} (0) - \frac {k _ {i} \left(C _ {E} (0) - C _ {E} ^ {*} (0)\right)}{g _ {i} + m _ {i} - h} \right| \left(1 - \mathrm{e} ^ {- \left(g _ {i} + m _ {i}\right) t}\right) \\ + \left| \frac {k _ {i} \left(C _ {E} (0) - C _ {E} ^ {*} (0)\right)}{h \left(g _ {i} + m _ {i} - h\right)} \right| \left(1 - \mathrm{e} ^ {- h t}\right) <   \infty . \\ \end{array}
$$

下面我们将考虑系统

$$
\left\{ \begin{array}{l} \mathrm{d} x _ {1} (t) = x _ {1} (t) \left[ b _ {1} - r _ {1 1} C _ {1} ^ {*} (t) - a _ {1 1} x _ {1} (t) - a _ {1 2} x _ {2} (t) \right] \mathrm{d} t + \sigma_ {1} (t) x _ {1} (t) \mathrm{d} B _ {1} (t), \\ \mathrm{d} x _ {2} (t) = x _ {2} (t) \left[ - b _ {2} - r _ {2 1} C _ {2} ^ {*} (t) + a _ {2 1} x _ {1} (t) - a _ {2 2} x _ {2} (t) \right] \mathrm{d} t + \sigma_ {2} (t) x _ {2} (t) \mathrm{d} B _ {2} (t). \end{array} \right. \tag {4.34}
$$

## 4.4.1 非平凡周期解的存在性和全局吸引性

本节, 我们将得到系统 (4.34) 非平凡周期正解的存在和全局吸引的充分条件. 令

$$
\lambda = \frac {1}{\theta} \int_ {0} ^ {\theta} a _ {2 1} \left[ b _ {1} - r _ {1 1} C _ {1} ^ {*} (t) - \frac {1}{2} \sigma_ {1} ^ {2} (t) \right] - a _ {1 1} \left[ b _ {2} + r _ {2 1} C _ {2} ^ {*} (t) + \frac {1}{2} \sigma_ {2} ^ {2} (t) \right] \mathrm{d} t.
$$

利用与文献 [63] 相同的方法可以证明如下定理.

定理 4.7 若 $\lambda > 0$ ，则系统 (4.34) 存在正的 $\theta$ -周期解.

下面我们来建立系统 (4.31) 非平凡周期正解的全局吸引性的充分准则.

定义 4.3 设 $x^{*}(t)$ 是系统 (4.31) 的正周期解, 且 $x(t)$ 是系统 (4.31) 满足初值 $x(0) > 0$ 的一个任意解. 若

$$
\lim _ {t \to \infty} | x (t) - x ^ {*} (t) | = 0, \quad \text {对几乎所有的} \omega \in \Omega ,
$$

则系统(4.31)的正周期解 $x^{*}(t)$ 是全局吸引的.

类似于文献 [67] 中引理 4 的证明, 我们能证明如下引理.

引理 4.9 假设 $a_{22} > a_{21}$ 成立，则对任意初值 $x(0) \in \mathbb{R}_{+}^{2}$ ，系统 (4.31) 的解有性质

$$
\lim _ {t \to \infty} \sup E (x _ {i} ^ {p} (t)) \leqslant K _ {i} (p), \quad \text {对任意} p > 1,
$$

其中 $K_{i}(p)$ 是正常数.

定理 4.8 假设 $\lambda > 0, a_{22} > a_{21}, a_{11} > a_{21}$ 和 $a_{22} > a_{12}$ 成立，则系统 (4.31) 的周期解是全局吸引的.

证明 由定理4.7可知, 系统(4.31)存在一个正周期解. 令 $x(t)$ 为系统(4.31)的任意一个解. 定义函数 $V$ ,

$$
V (t) = \sum_ {i = 1} ^ {2} | \log x _ {i} (t) - \log x _ {i} ^ {*} (t) |, \quad t \geqslant 0.
$$

直接计算 $V(t)$ 的右微分 $\mathrm{d}^{+}V(t)$ 得

$$
\begin{array}{l} \mathrm{d} ^ {+} V (t) = \sum_ {i = 1} ^ {2} \operatorname{sgn} (x _ {i} (t) - x _ {i} ^ {*} (t)) \mathrm{d} (\log x _ {i} (t) - \log x _ {i} ^ {*} (t)) \\ = - \sum_ {i = 1} ^ {2} \operatorname{sgn} (x _ {i} (t) - x _ {i} ^ {*} (t)) r _ {i 1} (C _ {i} (t) - C _ {i} ^ {*} (t)) \mathrm{d} t \\ + \operatorname{sgn} \left(x _ {1} (t) - x _ {1} ^ {*} (t)\right) \left[ - a _ {1 1} \left(x _ {1} (t) - x _ {1} ^ {*} (t)\right) - a _ {1 2} \left(x _ {2} (t) - x _ {2} ^ {*} (t)\right) \right] \mathrm{d} t \\ + \operatorname{sgn} \left(x _ {2} (t) - x _ {2} ^ {*} (t)\right) \left[ a _ {2 1} \left(x _ {1} (t) - x _ {1} ^ {*} (t)\right) - a _ {2 2} \left(x _ {2} (t) - x _ {2} ^ {*} (t)\right) \right] \mathrm{d} t \\ \leqslant \sum_ {i = 1} ^ {2} r _ {i 1} | C _ {i} (t) - C _ {i} ^ {*} (t) | \mathrm{d} t - [ (a _ {1 1} - a _ {2 1}) | x _ {1} (t) - x _ {1} ^ {*} (t) | \\ + (a _ {2 2} - a _ {1 2}) | x _ {2} (t) - x _ {2} ^ {*} (t) | ] \mathrm{d} t. \tag {4.35} \\ \end{array}
$$

由引理 4.8, 对式 (4.35) 的两端积分得

$$
V (t) + \int_ {0} ^ {t} [ (a _ {1 1} - a _ {2 1}) | x _ {1} (s) - x _ {1} ^ {*} (s) | + (a _ {2 2} - a _ {1 2}) | x _ {2} (s) - x _ {2} ^ {*} (s) | ] \mathrm{d} s
$$

$$
\leqslant V (0) + \int_ {0} ^ {t} \sum_ {i = 1} ^ {2} r _ {i 1} | C _ {i} (s) - C _ {i} ^ {*} (s) | \mathrm{d} s <   \infty .
$$

由条件 $a_{11} > a_{21}$ 和 $a_{22} > a_{12}$ 可得

$$
\left| x (t) - x ^ {*} (t) \right| \leqslant \sum_ {i = 1} ^ {n} \left| x _ {i} (t) - x _ {i} ^ {*} (t) \right| \in L ^ {1} [ 0, + \infty). \tag {4.36}
$$

从式 (4.36) 可以推出

$$
\liminf _ {t \to \infty} | x (t) - x ^ {*} (t) | = 0.
$$

接着我们将证明

$$
\lim _ {t \rightarrow \infty} | x (t) - x ^ {*} (t) | = 0. \tag {4.37}
$$

由引理 4.9, 我们得到系统 (4.31) 解的 p 阶矩是有界的, 用文献 [34] 中定理 6.2 类似的方法则可完成余下的证明. □

## 4.4.2 数值模拟

本节我们将用数值的方法进行结论的模拟. 利用 Milstein 高阶方法 [33], 我们考虑离散方程

$$
\left\{ \begin{array}{l} x _ {1, k + 1} = x _ {1, k} + x _ {1, k} \left[ \left(b _ {1} - r _ {1 1} C _ {1} (k \Delta t) - a _ {1 1} x _ {1, k} - a _ {1 2} x _ {2, k}\right) \Delta t \right. \\ \quad \left. + \sigma_ {1} (k \Delta t) \varepsilon_ {1, k} \sqrt {\Delta t} + \frac {\sigma_ {1} ^ {2} (k \Delta t)}{2} \left(\varepsilon_ {1, k} ^ {2} \Delta t - \Delta t\right) \right], \\ x _ {2, k + 1} = x _ {2, k} + x _ {2, k} \left[ \left(- b _ {2} - r _ {2 1} C _ {2} (k \Delta t) + a _ {2 1} x _ {1, k} - a _ {2 2} x _ {2, k}\right) \Delta t \right. \\ \quad \left. + \sigma_ {2} (k \Delta t) \varepsilon_ {2, k} \sqrt {\Delta t} + \frac {\sigma_ {2} ^ {2} (k \Delta t)}{2} \left(\varepsilon_ {2, k} ^ {2} \Delta t - \Delta t\right) \right], \end{array} \right. \tag {4.38}
$$

选取参数 $b_{1} = 1, b_{2} = 0.05, r_{11} = r_{21} = 0.5, C_{1}^{*}(t) = 0.4 - 0.2\sin t, C_{2}^{*}(t) = 0.1 + 0.06\sin t, a_{11} = a_{22} = 5, a_{12} = a_{21} = 2, x_{1}(0) = 0.3, x_{2}(0) = 0.2.$ 

图 4.7 和图 4.8 中, 我们选择 $\sigma_{1}(t)=\sigma_{2}(t)=0.1+0.01\sin t$ , 则条件

$$
\lambda = \frac {1}{\theta} \int_ {0} ^ {\theta} a _ {2 1} \left[ b _ {1} - r _ {1 1} C _ {1} ^ {*} (t) - \frac {1}{2} \sigma_ {1} ^ {2} (t) \right] - a _ {1 1} \left[ b _ {2} + r _ {2 1} C _ {2} ^ {*} (t) + \frac {1}{2} \sigma_ {2} ^ {2} (t) \right] \mathrm{d} t > 0
$$

满足. 我们可以看出, 确定性系统的解经过一个周期的时间后进入周期轨道; 当噪声强度很小时, 随机系统的解在周期轨道的小邻域内震动.

# 第5章 随机 Lotka-Volterra 捕食-食饵模型及竞争系统模型

# 5.1 具有周期系数的随机非自治 Lotka-Volterra 捕食-食饵模型

## 5.1.1 引言

下面给出了一个经典的非自治 Lotka-Volterra 捕食-食饵模型

$$
\left\{ \begin{array}{l} \dot {x} (t) = x (t) \left[ a _ {1} (t) - b _ {1} (t) y (t) - c _ {1} (t) x (t) \right], \\ \dot {y} (t) = y (t) \left[ - a _ {2} (t) + b _ {2} (t) x (t) - c _ {2} (t) y (t) \right], \end{array} \right. \tag {5.1}
$$

其中 $x(t)$ 和 $y(t)$ 分别是食饵和捕食者在时刻 $t$ 的种群密度. 这里 $a_1(t)$ 和 $a_2(t)$ 表示食饵和捕食者的内禀增长率, $b_1(t)$ 表示捕食者的捕获率, $b_2(t)$ 表示捕食者将食饵的转化率, $c_1(t)$ 和 $c_2(t)$ 分别表示食饵和捕食者的密度依赖系数. 最近, 很多学者研究了系统 (5.1) 并得到了系统灭绝、持久性及渐近稳定性 (参见文献 [68]~[70] 及相关的参考文献).

然而，生物种群往往受到自然环境噪声的影响（参见文献[11],[31],[71]~[73]).我们考虑了白噪声扰动系统(5.1)的内禀增长率，即 $a_1(t)\rightarrow a_1(t) + \alpha (t)\dot{B}_1(t),$ $-a_{2}(t)\rightarrow -a_{2}(t) + \beta (t)\dot{B}_{2}(t)$ ，于是得到下面的随机微分方程

$$
\left\{ \begin{array}{l} \mathrm{d} x (t) = x (t) \left[ a _ {1} (t) - b _ {1} (t) y (t) - c _ {1} (t) x (t) \right] \mathrm{d} t + \alpha (t) x (t) \mathrm{d} B _ {1} (t), \\ \mathrm{d} y (t) = y (t) \left[ - a _ {2} (t) + b _ {2} (t) x (t) - c _ {2} (t) y (t) \right] \mathrm{d} t + \beta (t) y (t) \mathrm{d} B _ {2} (t), \end{array} \right. \tag {5.2}
$$

其中 $B_{i}(t), i = 1,2$ 是互相独立的标准布朗运动. 随机系统 (5.2) 由于其重要的理论和现实意义被视为经典模型 (参见文献 [67], [74]~[79]). 应用 Fokker-Planck 方程, 文献 [75], [76] 的作者研究了系统 (5.2) 自治情况并证明了分布密度将收敛于一个不变密度或弱收敛于一个 $L^{1}$ 中的奇异测度. Liu 等在文献 [67] 得到随机系统 (5.2) 灭绝、均值意义下的非持久和弱持久并得到系统 (5.2) 持久和灭绝的阈值. Wu 等在文献 [77] 中研究了脉冲对系统的影响.

生物种群多少会表现出周期性, 主要也是由于例如四季变化、繁殖、捕获等. 对周期解的研究参见文献 [80] ~ [83]. 然而, 至今没有随机非自治周期系统的相关研究结果. 本章我们将研究具有周期系数的系统 (5.2) 并给出周期解的存在性和其他相关性质. 我们假设 $B_{i}(t)$ 是互相独立的 Wiener 过程, 系数 $a_{1}(t), a_{2}(t), b_{1}(t), b_{2}(t), c_{1}(t), c_{2}(t), \alpha(t)$ 和 $\beta(t)$ 是正的 $\theta$ -周期连续函数并有界; $\theta$ 是一个正的常数.

考虑一个 $l$ -维的随机微分方程

$$
\mathrm{d} z (t) = f (z (t), t) \mathrm{d} t + g (z (t), t) \mathrm{d} B (t), \quad t \geqslant t _ {0} \tag {5.3}
$$

满足初始条件 $z(0) = z_0 \in \mathbb{R}^l$ 。定义微分算子 $\mathcal{L}$ 并作用于方程 (5.3) 得

$$
\mathcal {L} = \frac {\partial}{\partial t} + \sum_ {k = 1} ^ {l} f _ {k} (z, t) \frac {\partial}{\partial z _ {k}} + \frac {1}{2} \sum_ {k, j = 1} ^ {l} [ g ^ {\mathrm{T}} (z, t) g (z, t) ] _ {k j} \frac {\partial^ {2}}{\partial z _ {k} \partial z _ {j}}.
$$

## 5.1.2 周期系统 (5.2) 周期正解的存在性

不失一般性, 假设 $\{\mathcal{F}_t\}_{t \geqslant 0}$ 是完备概率空间 $(\Omega, \mathcal{F}, \{\mathcal{F}_t\}_{t \geqslant 0}, \mathbb{P})$ 中的域流并满足一般性的条件 (i.e. 右连续且 $\mathcal{F}_0$ 包含所有的 $P$ -测集). 考虑到生物学模型的实际意义, 我们在空间 $\mathbb{R}_+^2 = \{(x, y) \in \mathbb{R}^2 : x > 0, y > 0\}$ 中研究系统 (5.2) 满足 $x(0) > 0, y(0) > 0$ 的解. 为了方便起见, 表示 $f^u = \max_{t \in [0, \theta]} f(t), f^l = \min_{t \in [0, \theta]} f(t)$ , 其中 $f(t)$ 是连续 $\theta$ -周期函数. 如果 $C$ 是一个矩阵, 它的转置矩阵表示为 $C^{\mathrm{T}}$ .

定义 5.1 称方程 (5.2) 的平凡解是几乎必然指数稳定的, 如果对所有的 $x_{0} \in R^{d}$ 都满足下式

$$
\limsup _ {t \to \infty} \frac {1}{t} \log \| x (t; t _ {0}, x _ {0}) \| <   0 \quad \text { a.s. }
$$

定义 5.2 $^{[73]}$ 一个随机过程 $\xi(t)=\xi(t,\omega)(-\infty<t<+\infty)$ 称为是 $\theta-$ 周期的，如果对每一个有限序列 $t_{1},\cdots,t_{n},\xi(t_{1}+h),\cdots,\xi(t_{n}+h)$ 的联合分布是独立于 h 的，其中 $h=k\theta(k=\pm1,\pm2,\cdots)$ .

Khasminskii $^{[73]}$ 证明了 Markov 过程 $z(t)$ 是 $\theta$ -周期的, 当且仅当它的转移概率函数是 $\theta$ -周期的且函数 $P_{0}(t,A)=P\{X(t)\in A\}$ 满足下面的方程:

$$
P _ {0} (s, A) = \int_ {\mathbb {R} ^ {l}} P _ {0} (s, \mathrm{d} z) P (s, z, s + \theta , A) \equiv P _ {0} (s + \theta , A),
$$

其中 $A \in \mathcal{B}, \mathcal{B}$ 是 $\sigma$ -代数.

考虑方程

$$
X (t) = X \left(t _ {0}\right) + \int_ {t _ {0}} ^ {t} \boldsymbol {b} (s, X (s)) \mathrm{d} s + \sum_ {r = 1} ^ {k} \int_ {t _ {0}} ^ {t} \sigma_ {r} (s, X (s)) \mathrm{d} \xi_ {r} (s), \tag {5.4}
$$

其中向量 $b(s,z),\sigma_{1}(s,z),\cdots,\sigma_{k}(s,z)(s\in[t_{0},T],z\in\mathbb{R}^{l})$ 是 $(s,z)$ 的连续函数，且满足下面的条件（这里 B 是一个常数）

$$
| \boldsymbol {b} (s, z) - \boldsymbol {b} (s, \bar {z}) | + \sum_ {r = 1} ^ {k} | \sigma_ {r} (s, z) - \sigma_ {r} (s, \bar {z}) | \leqslant B | z - \bar {z} |,
$$

$$
| \boldsymbol {b} (s, z) | + \sum_ {r = 1} ^ {k} | \sigma_ {r} (s, z) | \leqslant B (1 + | z |). \tag {5.5}
$$

令 U 是一个给定的开集且 $E = I \times R^{l}$ . 令 $C^{2}$ 是 E 中的函数, 并且关于 $z_{1}, \cdots, z_{l}$ 是二阶连续可微且关于 t 可微.

引理 5.1 $^{[73]}$ 假设式 (5.4) 的系数是关于 t 的 $\theta$ -周期函数且在每个 $I \times U$ 上满足条件式 (5.5)，进一步假设存在一个 E 上的函数 $V(t, z) \in C^{2}$ 关于 t 是 $\theta$ -周期的且满足下面的条件：

(i) $\inf_{|z|>R} V(t,z) \to \infty$ a.s. $R \to \infty;$ 

(ii) $\mathcal{L}V(t,z)\leqslant-1$ 在某个紧集外部成立,

则系统(5.4)存在一个解并且这个解是 $\theta$ -周期的Markov过程.

定理 5.1 对每个给定的初始值 $(x(0), y(0)) \in \mathbb{R}_{+}^{2}$ ，系统 (5.2) 在 $t \geqslant 0$ 上存在唯一正解 $(x(t), y(t))$ ，这个解将以概率 1 存在于 $R_{+}^{2}$ 中.

本节将应用引理 5.1 构造 Lyapunov 函数, 我们将证明系统 (5.2) 正周期解的存在性. 令 $\lambda = \frac{1}{\theta} \int_{0}^{\theta} \left( b_{2}^{l} \left[ a_{1}(t) - \frac{\alpha^{2}(t)}{2} \right] - c_{1}^{u} \left[ a_{2}(t) + \frac{\beta^{2}(t)}{2} \right] \right) dt.$ 

定理 5.2 如果 $\lambda > 0$ 成立，则系统 (5.2) 至少有一个正 $\theta$ -周期解.

证明 由于随机系统 (5.2) 的系数是连续有界的正周期函数, 于是式 (5.5) 成立. 现在证明引理 5.1 的条件 (i) 和 (ii) 成立.

定义一个 $C^2$ 函数 $V:[0, + \infty)\times \mathbb{R}_+^2\to \mathbb{R}$ 如下

$$
\begin{array}{l} V (t, x, y) = M \left(- b _ {2} ^ {l} \log x - c _ {1} ^ {u} \log y + \frac {b _ {1} ^ {u} b _ {2} ^ {l} + c _ {1} ^ {u} c _ {2} ^ {u}}{a _ {2} ^ {l}} y\right) + \frac {(x + p y) ^ {2}}{2} + M \varpi (t) \\ = V _ {1} (x, y) + V _ {2} (x, y) + V _ {3} (t), \tag {5.6} \\ \end{array}
$$

其中 $p = \frac{b_1^l}{b_2^u}$ 和 $M = (\frac{2}{\lambda})\max \left\{2,\sup_{(x,y)\in \mathbb{R}_+^2}\left\{-\frac{c_1^l}{2} x^3 -\frac{p^2c_2^l}{2} y^3 +\left(a_1^u +\frac{(\alpha^2)^u}{2}\right)x^2 -\right.\right.$ $p^2\left(a_2^l -\frac{(\beta^2)^u}{2}\right)y^2 +pa_1^uxy\Bigg\} \Bigg\} ,$ 这里 $V_{3}(t) = M\varpi (t)$ 且 $V_{2}(x,y) = \frac{(x + py)^{2}}{2} .$ 显然， $\frac{M\lambda}{4}\geqslant 1.$ 令

$$
\begin{array}{l} \dot {\varpi} (t) = - \frac {1}{\theta} \int_ {0} ^ {\theta} \left(b _ {2} ^ {l} \left[ a _ {1} (t) - \frac {\alpha^ {2} (t)}{2} \right] - c _ {1} ^ {u} \left[ a _ {2} (t) + \frac {\beta^ {2} (t)}{2} \right]\right) d t \\ \left. + \left(b _ {2} ^ {l} \left[ a _ {1} (t) - \frac {\alpha^ {2} (t)}{2} \right] - c _ {1} ^ {u} \left[ a _ {2} (t) + \frac {\beta^ {2} (t)}{2} \right]\right)\right) \\ = - \lambda + \left(b _ {2} ^ {l} \left[ a _ {1} (t) - \frac {\alpha^ {2} (t)}{2} \right] - c _ {1} ^ {u} \left[ a _ {2} (t) + \frac {\beta^ {2} (t)}{2} \right]\right). \tag {5.7} \\ \end{array}
$$

容易看出 $\varpi(t)$ 是一个 $\theta$ -周期函数. 事实上, 对式 (5.7) 从 $t$ 到 $t + \theta$ 积分, 得

$$
\begin{array}{l} \varpi (t + \theta) - \varpi (t) = \int_ {t} ^ {t + \theta} \dot {\varpi} (s) d s \\ = - \int_ {0} ^ {\theta} \left(b _ {2} ^ {l} \left[ a _ {1} (s) - \frac {\alpha^ {2} (s)}{2} \right] - c _ {1} ^ {u} \left[ a _ {2} (s) + \frac {\beta^ {2} (s)}{2} \right]\right) d s \\ + \int_ {t} ^ {t + \theta} \left(b _ {2} ^ {l} \left[ a _ {1} (s) - \frac {\alpha^ {2} (s)}{2} \right] - c _ {1} ^ {u} \left[ a _ {2} (s) + \frac {\beta^ {2} (s)}{2} \right]\right) d s \\ = 0. \tag {5.8} \\ \end{array}
$$

于是引理5.1的条件(i)成立.我们只要证明

$$
\inf _ {(x, y) \in \mathbb {R} _ {+} ^ {2} \backslash U _ {k}} V (t, x, y) \rightarrow \infty , \tag {5.9}
$$

其中 $U_{k} = \left(\frac{1}{k}, k\right) \times \left(\frac{1}{k}, k\right)$ ，由于 $V(t, x, y)$ 系数的平方项是正的，所以式 (5.9) 显然成立。现在证明引理 5.1 的条件 (ii) 成立。应用 Itô 公式得

$$
\mathcal {L} V _ {1} (x, y) \leqslant M \left(- b _ {2} ^ {l} \left[ a _ {1} (t) - \frac {\alpha^ {2} (t)}{2} \right] + c _ {1} ^ {u} \left[ a _ {2} (t) + \frac {\beta^ {2} (t)}{2} \right] + \frac {\left(b _ {1} ^ {u} b _ {2} ^ {l} + c _ {1} ^ {u} c _ {2} ^ {u}\right) b _ {2} ^ {u}}{a _ {2} ^ {l}} x y\right). \tag {5.10}
$$

结合式 $(5.7)$ 和式 $(5.10)$ ，可得

$$
\mathcal {L} \left(V _ {1} + V _ {3}\right) \leqslant M \left[ - \lambda + \frac {\left(b _ {1} ^ {u} b _ {2} ^ {l} + c _ {1} ^ {u} c _ {2} ^ {u}\right) b _ {2} ^ {u}}{a _ {2} ^ {l}} x y \right]. \tag {5.11}
$$

类似地，有

$$
\begin{array}{l} \mathcal {L} V _ {2} (x, y) \\ = (x + p y) \left[ a _ {1} (t) x - b _ {1} (t) x y - c _ {1} (t) x ^ {2} - p a _ {2} (t) y + p b _ {2} (t) x y - p c _ {2} (t) y ^ {2} \right] \\ + \frac {\alpha^ {2} (t)}{2} x ^ {2} + p ^ {2} \frac {\beta^ {2} (t)}{2} y ^ {2} \\ \leqslant - c _ {1} ^ {l} x ^ {3} - p ^ {2} c _ {2} ^ {l} y ^ {3} + \left(a _ {1} ^ {u} + \frac {\left(\alpha^ {u}\right) ^ {2}}{2}\right) x ^ {2} - p ^ {2} \left(a _ {2} ^ {l} - \frac {\left(\beta^ {u}\right) ^ {2}}{2}\right) y ^ {2} + p a _ {1} ^ {u} x y \\ \end{array}
$$

$$
\begin{array}{l} + p b _ {2} ^ {u} x ^ {2} y + p ^ {2} b _ {2} ^ {u} x y ^ {2} - b _ {1} ^ {l} x ^ {2} y - p b _ {1} ^ {l} x y ^ {2} \\ = - c _ {1} ^ {l} x ^ {3} - p ^ {2} c _ {2} ^ {l} y ^ {3} + \left(a _ {1} ^ {u} + \frac {\left(\alpha^ {u}\right) ^ {2}}{2}\right) x ^ {2} - p ^ {2} \left(a _ {2} ^ {l} - \frac {\left(\beta^ {u}\right) ^ {2}}{2}\right) y ^ {2} + p a _ {1} ^ {u} x y. \\ \end{array}
$$

上面不等式结合式 (5.11), 可得

$$
\begin{array}{l} \mathcal {L} V (t, x, y) \leqslant - M \lambda + M \frac {\left(b _ {1} ^ {u} b _ {2} ^ {l} + c _ {1} ^ {u} c _ {2} ^ {u}\right) b _ {2} ^ {u}}{a _ {2} ^ {l}} x y - c _ {1} ^ {l} x ^ {3} - p ^ {2} c _ {2} ^ {l} y ^ {3} \\ + \left(a _ {1} ^ {u} + \frac {\left(\alpha^ {u}\right) ^ {2}}{2}\right) x ^ {2} - p ^ {2} \left(a _ {2} ^ {l} - \frac {\left(\beta^ {u}\right) ^ {2}}{2}\right) y ^ {2} + p a _ {1} ^ {u} x y. \\ \end{array}
$$

定义一个有界闭集

$$
\mathcal {D} = \left\{(x, y) \in \mathbb {R} _ {+} ^ {2}: \varepsilon \leqslant x \leqslant \frac {1}{\varepsilon}, \varepsilon \leqslant y \leqslant \frac {1}{\varepsilon} \right\},
$$

$0 < \varepsilon < 1$ . 选取充分小的正数 $\varepsilon$ 使得

$$
0 <   \varepsilon <   \frac {a _ {2} ^ {l} \lambda}{4 b _ {2} ^ {u} \left(b _ {1} ^ {u} b _ {2} ^ {l} + c _ {1} ^ {u} c _ {2} ^ {u}\right)}, \tag {5.12}
$$

$$
0 <   \varepsilon <   \frac {p ^ {2} a _ {2} ^ {l} c _ {2} ^ {l}}{2 M b _ {2} ^ {u} \left(b _ {1} ^ {u} b _ {2} ^ {l} + c _ {1} ^ {u} c _ {2} ^ {u}\right)}, \tag {5.13}
$$

$$
0 <   \varepsilon <   \frac {a _ {2} ^ {l} c _ {1} ^ {l}}{2 M b _ {2} ^ {u} \left(b _ {1} ^ {u} b _ {2} ^ {l} + c _ {1} ^ {u} c _ {2} ^ {u}\right)}, \tag {5.14}
$$

$$
- M \lambda - \frac {c _ {1} ^ {l}}{2 \varepsilon^ {3}} + K _ {1} \leqslant - 1, \tag {5.15}
$$

$$
- M \lambda - \frac {p ^ {2} c _ {2} ^ {l}}{2 \varepsilon^ {3}} + K _ {2} \leqslant - 1, \tag {5.16}
$$

其中 $K_{1}$ 和 $K_{2}$ 是待定的正常数. 令

$$
\mathcal {D} _ {\varepsilon} ^ {1} = \left\{(x, y) \in \mathbb {R} _ {+} ^ {2}: 0 <   x <   \varepsilon \right\}, \quad \mathcal {D} _ {\varepsilon} ^ {2} = \left\{(x, y) \in \mathbb {R} _ {+} ^ {2}: 0 <   y <   \varepsilon \right\},
$$

$$
\mathcal {D} _ {\varepsilon} ^ {3} = \left\{(x, y) \in \mathbb {R} _ {+} ^ {2}: x > \frac {1}{\varepsilon} \right\}, \quad \mathcal {D} _ {\varepsilon} ^ {4} = \left\{(x, y) \in \mathbb {R} _ {+} ^ {2}: y > \frac {1}{\varepsilon} \right\}.
$$

注意到 $\mathcal{D}^{\mathcal{C}} = \mathcal{D}_{\varepsilon}^{1}\cup \mathcal{D}_{\varepsilon}^{2}\cup \mathcal{D}_{\varepsilon}^{3}\cup \mathcal{D}_{\varepsilon}^{4}$ .现证明 $\mathcal{LV}(t,x,y)\leqslant -1$ 在 $[0, + \infty)\times \mathcal{D}^{\mathcal{C}}$ 上成立.

情况1 当 $(t,x,y)\in [0, + \infty)\times \mathcal{D}_{\varepsilon}^{1}$ ，由于 $xy\leqslant \varepsilon y\leqslant \varepsilon (1 + y^3)$ ，有

$$
\begin{array}{l} \mathcal {L} V (t, x, y) \leqslant - \frac {M \lambda}{4} + \left(- \frac {M \lambda}{4} + \frac {M b _ {2} ^ {u} \left(b _ {1} ^ {u} b _ {2} ^ {l} + c _ {1} ^ {u} c _ {2} ^ {u}\right) \varepsilon}{a _ {2} ^ {l}}\right) - \frac {c _ {1} ^ {l}}{2} x ^ {3} \\ + \left(- \frac {p ^ {2} c _ {2} ^ {l}}{2} + \frac {M b _ {2} ^ {u} \left(b _ {1} ^ {u} b _ {2} ^ {l} + c _ {1} ^ {u} c _ {2} ^ {u}\right) \varepsilon}{a _ {2} ^ {l}}\right) y ^ {3} \\ + \left[ - \frac {M \lambda}{2} - \frac {c _ {1} ^ {l}}{2} x ^ {3} - \frac {p ^ {2} c _ {2} ^ {l}}{2} y ^ {3} + \left(a _ {1} ^ {u} + \frac {\left(\alpha^ {u}\right) ^ {2}}{2}\right) x ^ {2} \right. \\ \left. - p ^ {2} \left(a _ {2} ^ {l} - \frac {\left(\beta^ {u}\right) ^ {2}}{2}\right) y ^ {2} + p a _ {1} ^ {u} x y \right] \\ \leqslant - \frac {M \lambda}{4} + \left(- \frac {M \lambda}{4} + \frac {M b _ {2} ^ {u} \left(b _ {1} ^ {u} b _ {2} ^ {l} + c _ {1} ^ {u} c _ {2} ^ {u}\right) \varepsilon}{a _ {2} ^ {l}}\right) - \frac {c _ {1} ^ {l}}{2} x ^ {3} + \left(- \frac {p ^ {2} c _ {2} ^ {l}}{2} \right. \\ \left. + \frac {M b _ {2} ^ {u} \left(b _ {1} ^ {u} b _ {2} ^ {l} + c _ {1} ^ {u} c _ {2} ^ {u}\right) \varepsilon}{a _ {2} ^ {l}}\right) y ^ {3} \\ + \left[ - \frac {M \lambda}{2} + \sup _ {(x, y) \in \mathbb {R} _ {+} ^ {2}} \left\{- \frac {c _ {1} ^ {l}}{2} x ^ {3} - \frac {p ^ {2} c _ {2} ^ {l}}{2} y ^ {3} + \left(a _ {1} ^ {u} + \frac {\left(\alpha^ {u}\right) ^ {2}}{2}\right) x ^ {2} \right. \right. \\ \left. \left. - p ^ {2} \left(a _ {2} ^ {l} - \frac {\left(\beta^ {u}\right) ^ {2}}{2}\right) y ^ {2} + p a _ {1} ^ {u} x y \right\} \right], \\ \end{array}
$$

结合式 (5.12), (5.13) 以及 $M$ 的定义, 可得

$$
\mathcal {L} V (t, x, y) \leqslant - \frac {M \lambda}{4} - \frac {c _ {1} ^ {l}}{2} x ^ {3} \leqslant - \frac {M \lambda}{4} \leqslant - 1,
$$

因此 $\mathcal{L}V\leqslant -1$ ，在 $[0, + \infty)\times \mathcal{D}_{\varepsilon}^{1}$ 上成立.

情况 2 对任何 $(t, x, y) \in [0, +\infty) \times \mathcal{D}_{\varepsilon}^{2}$ ，由于 $xy \leqslant \varepsilon x \leqslant \varepsilon (1 + x^{3})$ ，可得

$$
\begin{array}{l} \mathcal {L} V (t, x, y) \leqslant - \frac {M \lambda}{4} + \left(- \frac {M \lambda}{4} + \frac {M b _ {2} ^ {u} \left(b _ {1} ^ {u} b _ {2} ^ {l} + c _ {1} ^ {u} c _ {2} ^ {u}\right) \varepsilon}{a _ {2} ^ {l}}\right) \\ + \left(- \frac {c _ {1} ^ {l}}{2} + \frac {M b _ {2} ^ {u} \left(b _ {1} ^ {u} b _ {2} ^ {l} + c _ {1} ^ {u} c _ {2} ^ {u}\right) \varepsilon}{a _ {2} ^ {l}}\right) x ^ {3} - \frac {p ^ {2} c _ {2} ^ {l}}{2} y ^ {3} \\ + \left[ - \frac {M \lambda}{2} + \sup _ {(x, y) \in \mathbb {R} _ {+} ^ {2}} \left\{- \frac {c _ {1} ^ {l}}{2} x ^ {3} - \frac {p ^ {2} c _ {2} ^ {l}}{2} y ^ {3} + \left(a _ {1} ^ {u} + \frac {\left(\alpha^ {u}\right) ^ {2}}{2}\right) x ^ {2} \right. \right. \\ \left. \left. - p ^ {2} \left(a _ {2} ^ {l} - \frac {\left(\beta^ {u}\right) ^ {2}}{2}\right) y ^ {2} + p a _ {1} ^ {u} x y \right\} \right]. \\ \end{array}
$$

结合式 $(5.12)$ 和式 $(5.14)$ ，有

$$
\mathcal {L} V (x, y, k) \leqslant - \frac {M \lambda}{4} - \frac {p ^ {2} c _ {2} ^ {l}}{2} y ^ {3} \leqslant - \frac {M \lambda}{4} \leqslant - 1.
$$

因此, $\mathcal{L}V \leqslant -1$ , $(t,x,y) \in [0, +\infty) \times \mathcal{D}_{\varepsilon}^{2}$ .

情况 3 在 $[0, +\infty) \times D_{\varepsilon}^{3}$ ,

$$
\begin{array}{l} \mathcal {L} V (t, x, y) \leqslant - M \lambda - \frac {c _ {1} ^ {l}}{2} x ^ {3} + \left[ - \frac {c _ {1} ^ {l}}{2} x ^ {3} - p ^ {2} c _ {2} ^ {l} y ^ {3} + \left(a _ {1} ^ {u} + \frac {\left(\alpha^ {u}\right) ^ {2}}{2}\right) x ^ {2} \right. \\ - p ^ {2} \left(a _ {2} ^ {l} - \frac {\left(\beta^ {u}\right) ^ {2}}{2}\right) y ^ {2} + \left(M \frac {\left(b _ {1} ^ {u} b _ {2} ^ {l} + c _ {1} ^ {u} c _ {2} ^ {u}\right) b _ {2} ^ {u}}{a _ {2} ^ {l}} + p a _ {1} ^ {u}\right) x y ] \\ \leqslant - M \lambda - \frac {c _ {1} ^ {l}}{2 \varepsilon^ {3}} + K _ {1}, \tag {5.17} \\ \end{array}
$$

其中 $K_{1} = \sup_{(x,y)\in \mathbb{R}_{+}^{2}}\left\{-\frac{c_{1}^{l}}{2} x^{3} - p^{2}c_{2}^{l}y^{3} + \left(a_{1}^{u} + \frac{(\alpha^{u})^{2}}{2}\right)x^{2} - p^{2}\left(a_{2}^{l} - \frac{(\beta^{u})^{2}}{2}\right)y^{2}+\right.$ $\left(M\frac{(b_1^ub_l + c_1^uc_2^u)b_2^u}{a_2^l} +pa_1^u\right)xy\Bigg\},$ 由式(5.15)可得 $\mathcal{L}V\leqslant -1.$ 

情况 4 当 $(t, x, y) \in [0, +\infty) \times \mathcal{D}_{\varepsilon}^{4}$ ,

$$
\begin{array}{l} \mathcal {L} V (t, x, y) \leqslant - M \lambda - \frac {p ^ {2} c _ {2} ^ {l}}{2} y ^ {3} + \left[ - c _ {1} ^ {l} x ^ {3} - \frac {p ^ {2} c _ {2} ^ {l}}{2} y ^ {3} + \left(a _ {1} ^ {u} + \frac {\left(\alpha^ {u}\right) ^ {2}}{2}\right) x ^ {2} \right. \\ \left. - p ^ {2} \left(a _ {2} ^ {l} - \frac {\left(\beta^ {u}\right) ^ {2}}{2}\right) y ^ {2} + \left(M \frac {\left(b _ {1} ^ {u} b _ {2} ^ {l} + c _ {1} ^ {u} c _ {2} ^ {u}\right) b _ {2} ^ {u}}{a _ {2} ^ {l}} + p a _ {1} ^ {u}\right) x y \right] \\ \leqslant - M \lambda - \frac {p ^ {2} c _ {2} ^ {l}}{2 \varepsilon^ {3}} + K _ {2}, \tag {5.18} \\ \end{array}
$$

其中 $K_{2} = \sup_{(x,y)\in \mathbb{R}_{+}^{2}}\left\{-c_{1}^{l}x^{3} - \frac{p^{2}c_{2}^{l}}{2} y^{3} + \left(a_{1}^{u} + \frac{(\alpha^{u})^{2}}{2}\right)x^{2} - p^{2}\left(a_{2}^{l} - \frac{(\beta^{u})^{2}}{2}\right)y^{2}+\right.$ $\left(M\frac{(b_1^ub_l + c_1^uc_2^u)b_2^u}{a_2^l} +pa_1^u\right)xy\Bigg\}$ 由式(5.16)，可知 $\mathcal{L}V\leqslant -1$ 在 $[0, + \infty)\times \mathcal{D}_{\varepsilon}^{4}$ 上成立.

因此

$$
\mathcal {L} V (t, x, y) \leqslant - 1, \text {对所有的} (t, x, y) \in [ 0, + \infty) \times \mathcal {D} ^ {\mathcal {C}}. \tag {5.19}
$$

于是引理 5.1 的条件 (ii) 成立. 由此可知系统 (5.2) 有一个 $\theta$ -周期解. 此外, 定理 5.5 说明对任何给定的正的初始值, (5.2) 有一个唯一正解, 因此 (5.2) 至少有一个正的 $\theta$ -周期解. □

## 5.1.3 周期系统（5.2）的随机持久性及灭绝性

定理5.3 令 $\int_0^\theta \left[a_1(t) - \frac{\alpha^2(t)}{2}\right]\mathrm{d}t > 0$ 成立. 如果 $\tilde{\lambda} = \frac{1}{\theta}\int_0^\theta \left(b_2^u [a_1(t) - \right.$ 

$\frac{\alpha^2(t)}{2}\left[-c_1^l\left[a_2(t) + \frac{\beta^2(t)}{2}\right]\right)\mathrm{d}t < 0$ ，则对任意给定的初始值 $(x(0),y(0))\in \mathbb{R}_+^2$ ，捕食者将灭绝a.s.

定理5.4 假设 $\int_0^\theta \left[a_1(t) - \frac{\alpha^2(t)}{2}\right]\mathrm{d}t > 0$ 成立. 如果 $\lambda = \frac{1}{\theta}\int_0^\theta \left(b_2^l [a_1(t) - \frac{\alpha^2(t)}{2} ] - c_1^u [a_2(t) + \frac{\beta^2(t)}{2} ]\right)\mathrm{d}t > 0,$ 则对任何给定的初始值 $(x(0),y(0))\in \mathbb{R}_+^2$ ，系统(5.2）是均值意义下持久的.

注5.1 如果系统(5.2)是一个自治系统, 则 $\tilde{\lambda} = \lambda$ 是系统(5.2)均值意义下持久和灭绝的阈值.

## 5.1.4 数值模拟

本节将利用文献 [33] 的数值模拟方法, 通过图像验证上述理论, 包括均值意义下的持久性和灭绝性等. 先给出系统 (5.2) 的离散化方程

$$
\left\{ \begin{array}{l} x _ {k + 1} = x _ {k} + x _ {k} \left([ a _ {1} (k \Delta t) - b _ {1} (k \Delta t) y _ {k} - c _ {1} (k \Delta t) x _ {k} ] \Delta t \right. \\ \left. + \alpha (k \Delta t) \varepsilon_ {1, k} \sqrt {\Delta t} + \frac {\alpha^ {2} (k \Delta t)}{2} (\varepsilon_ {1, k} ^ {2} \Delta t - \Delta t)\right), \\ y _ {k + 1} = y _ {k} + y _ {k} \left([ - a _ {2} (k \Delta t) + b _ {2} (k \Delta t) x _ {k} - c _ {2} (k \Delta t) y _ {k} ] \Delta t \right. \\ \left. + \beta (k \Delta t) \varepsilon_ {2, k} \sqrt {\Delta t} + \frac {\beta^ {2} (k \Delta t)}{2} (\varepsilon_ {2, k} ^ {2} \Delta t - \Delta t)\right). \end{array} \right. \tag {5.20}
$$

例5.1 选取系数 $a_1(t) = 0.8 + 0.1\sin t, a_2(t) = 0.5 + 0.2\sin t, b_1(t) = 0.6 + 0.1\sin t, b_2(t) = 0.5 + 0.2\sin t, c_1(t) = 0.35 + 0.1\sin t, c_2(t) = 0.3 + 0.1\sin t,$ 以及 $x(0) = 0.9, y(0) = 0.6.$ 我们将根据白噪声的大小分两种情况讨论.

情况1 令 $\alpha(t) = 0.1 + 0.1\sin t, \beta(t) = 0.12 + 0.1\sin t.$ 

注意到 $\int_0^{2\pi}\left[a_1(t) - \frac{\alpha^2(t)}{2}\right]\mathrm{d}t\div 4.8356 > 0$ 且 $\lambda = \frac{1}{2\pi}\int_0^{2\pi}\left(b_2^l\left[a_1(t) - \frac{\alpha^2(t)}{2}\right] - c_1^u\left[a_2(t) + \frac{\beta^2(t)}{2}\right]\right)\mathrm{d}t = 0.0005 > 0.$ 显然定理5.2和定理5.4的条件成立，于是系统(5.2）至少有一个正周期解且均值意义下持久.在图5.1中，波动较大的曲线表示随机系统的解随着时间变化而波动较小的曲线表示对应确定性系统的变化曲线.可以看出当白噪声小时曲线的波动不大.放大曲线可得图5.1(b)和(d).图5.2是 $x(t)$ 和 $y(t)$ 的柱状图.图5.2和图5.1(c)，(d)表明，对任何正的初始值，确定性系统的解将进入到周期轨道而随机系统的解将在类似于周期轨道的周围波动
