## 5.3 具有 Markov 链的随机 Lotka-Volterra 竞争系统模型

## 5.3.1 引言

Golpalsamy 研究了下面的 Lotka-Volterra 竞争系统模型

$$
\left\{ \begin{array}{l} \mathrm{d} x _ {1} = x _ {1} (t) [ b _ {1} - a _ {1 1} x _ {1} (t) - a _ {1 2} x _ {2} (t) ] \mathrm{d} t, \\ \mathrm{d} x _ {2} = x _ {2} (t) [ b _ {2} - a _ {2 1} x _ {1} (t) - a _ {2 2} x _ {2} (t) ] \mathrm{d} t. \end{array} \right. \tag {5.52}
$$

并在假设下列条件成立的情况下, 得到了正平衡点的全局渐近稳定性.

(i) $b_{i} > 0, a_{ij} \geqslant 0 (i, j = 1, 2);$ 

(ii) $a_{11} > a_{21}, a_{22} > a_{12};$ 

$$
\text {(iii)} b _ {1} > \frac {a _ {1 2}}{a _ {2 2}} b _ {2}, b _ {2} > \frac {a _ {2 1}}{a _ {1 1}} b _ {1}.
$$

本节将在上面确定性模型的基础上研究环境白噪声的影响, 假设

$$
\begin{array}{l} b _ {1} \rightarrow b _ {1} + \sigma_ {1} \dot {B} _ {1} (t), \\ b _ {2} \rightarrow b _ {2} + \sigma_ {2} \dot {B} _ {2} (t), \\ \end{array}
$$

其中 $B_{i}(t)(i = 1,2)$ 是互相独立的布朗运动， $B_{i}(0) = 0$ 且 $\sigma_{i}$ 是一个正常数， $\sigma_{i}^{2}$ 是白噪声的强度。于是得到下面的随机微分方程

$$
\left\{ \begin{array}{l} \mathrm{d} x _ {1} = x _ {1} (t) \left[ \left(b _ {1} - a _ {1 1} x _ {1} (t) - a _ {1 2} x _ {2} (t)\right) \mathrm{d} t + \sigma_ {1} \mathrm{d} B _ {1} (t) \right], \\ \mathrm{d} x _ {2} = x _ {2} (t) \left[ \left(b _ {2} - a _ {2 1} x _ {1} (t) - a _ {2 2} x _ {2} (t)\right) \mathrm{d} t + \sigma_ {2} \mathrm{d} B _ {2} (t) \right]. \end{array} \right. \tag {5.53}
$$

同时我们假设系统受到彩色噪声的影响, 假设有 N 个状态并且系统在连续 Markov 链 $r(t)$ , $t \geqslant 0$ 的影响下在这 N 个状态间转换, 概率空间取值于有限状态 $S = \{1, 2, \cdots, N\}$ , 且生成元为 $\Gamma = (\gamma_{uv})_{n \times n}$ 可由下面方程计算

$$
P \{r (t + \Delta t) = v | r (t) = u \} = \left\{ \begin{array}{l l} \gamma_ {u v} \Delta t + o (\Delta t), & u \neq v, \\ 1 + \gamma_ {u v} \Delta t + o (\Delta t), & u = v, \end{array} \right.
$$

其中 $\Delta t > 0$ 而 $\gamma_{uv}$ 是从 $u$ 到 $v$ 的转移概率，并且 $\gamma_{uv} \geqslant 0$ 如果 $u \neq v$ ，且

$$
\gamma_ {u u} = - \sum_ {u \neq v} \gamma_ {u v}.
$$

注意到 $\Gamma$ 总有特征值 0. 于是系统 (5.53) 在彩色噪声的影响下变为

$$
\left\{ \begin{array}{l} \mathrm{d} x _ {1} = x _ {1} (t) \left[ \left(b _ {1} (r (t)) - a _ {1 1} (r (t)) x _ {1} (t) - a _ {1 2} (r (t)) x _ {2} (t)\right) \mathrm{d} t + \sigma_ {1} (r (t)) \mathrm{d} B _ {1} (t) \right], \\ \mathrm{d} x _ {2} = x _ {2} (t) \left[ \left(b _ {2} (r (t)) - a _ {2 1} (r (t)) x _ {1} (t) - a _ {2 2} (r (t)) x _ {2} (t)\right) \mathrm{d} t + \sigma_ {2} (r (t)) \mathrm{d} B _ {2} (t) \right]. \end{array} \right. \tag {5.54}
$$

这里假设 Markov 链 $r(\cdot)$ 与布朗运动 $B_{1}$ 和 $B_{2}$ 独立.

本节假设 $(\Omega, \mathcal{F}, \{\mathcal{F}_t\}_{t \geqslant 0}, \mathbb{P})$ 是具有域流 $\{\mathcal{F}_t\}_{t \geqslant 0}$ 的完备概率空间，且满足一般性的条件 (i.e. 右连续且 $\mathcal{F}_0$ 包含所有的 $\mathbb{P}$ 集合). 令 $B_i(t)(i=1,2)$ 是一个标准的布朗运动. 令 $\mathbb{R}_+^2$ 表示 $\mathbb{R}^2$ 中的正锥，即 $\mathbb{R}_+^2 = \{(x_1, x_2) \in \mathbb{R}^2 : x_i > 0, i = 1, 2\}$ . 为了方便起见，定义

$$
\hat {b} _ {i} = \min _ {k \in \mathbb {S}} \{b _ {i} (k) \}, \quad \check {a} _ {i j} = \max _ {k \in \mathbb {S}} \{a _ {i j} (k) \}, \quad \text {且} \quad \check {a} _ {i i} = \max _ {k \in \mathbb {S}} \{a _ {i i} (k) \} \quad (i = 1, 2).
$$

本节将假设下面条件成立

$$
\hat {b} _ {i} > 0, \quad \hat {a} _ {i i} > 0 \quad \text {且} \quad \hat {a} _ {i j} > 0 \quad (j \in J _ {i}, i = 1, 2, i \neq j). \tag {5.55}
$$

并给定.

假设1 对每个 $k \in S$ ，令 $\bar{r}_i(k) = b_i(k) - \frac{\sigma_i^2(k)}{2} > 0$ .

假设 2 $\hat{\bar{r}}_{i} > \frac{\check{a}_{ij}\check{\bar{r}}_{j}}{\hat{a}_{jj}}.$ 

假设 3 $\hat{a}_{11}\hat{a}_{22} > \check{a}_{12}\check{a}_{21}.$ 

## 5.3.2 随机竞争系统解的估计

Li 和 Jiang 在文献 [90] 中研究了下面具有白噪声和彩色噪声的 Logistic 方程

$$
\mathrm{d} N (t) = N (t) \left[ (a (r (t)) - b (r (t)) N (t)) \mathrm{d} t + \alpha (r (t)) \mathrm{d} B (t) \right], \tag {5.56}
$$

其中 $B(t)$ 是一维的布朗运动, $N(0) = N_0$ , $N_0$ 是一个正的随机变量, 且与 $B(t)$ 相互独立, 对每个 $k \in S$ , $a(k)$ , $b(k)$ 和 $\alpha(k)$ 均是非负常数.

引理 5.4 $^{[90]}$ 对任意给定的初始值 $N(0)=N_{0}>0$ , SDE(5.56) 存在唯一一个连续正解 $N(t)$ ，这个解是全局的且满足

$$
N (t) = \frac {\mathrm{e} ^ {\int_ {0} ^ {t} \left[ a (r (s)) - \frac {\alpha^ {2} (r (s))}{2} \right] \mathrm{d} s + \alpha (r (s)) \mathrm{d} B (s)}}{\frac {1}{N _ {0}} + \int_ {0} ^ {t} b (r (s)) \mathrm{e} ^ {\int_ {0} ^ {s} \left[ a (r (u)) - \frac {\alpha^ {2} (r (u))}{2} \right] \mathrm{d} u + \alpha (r (u)) \mathrm{d} B (u)} \mathrm{d} s}, \quad t \geqslant 0.
$$

定理 5.11 令假设式 (5.55) 成立, 则对任意给定的初始值 $x(0) \in \mathbb{R}_{+}^{2}$ , 系统 (5.54) 的解 $x(t)$ 具有下面的性质

$$
I (t) \leqslant x (t) \leqslant \phi (t),
$$

且

$$
I _ {i} (t) \leqslant x _ {i} (t) \leqslant \phi_ {i} (t), \quad i = 1, 2,
$$

其中

$$
\phi (t) = \left(\phi_ {1} (t), \phi_ {2} (t)\right) ^ {\mathrm{T}}, \quad I (t) = \left(I _ {1} (t), I _ {2} (t)\right) ^ {\mathrm{T}},
$$

$\phi_1(t)$ 和 $I_{i}(t)(i = 1,2)$ 是下面方程的解

$$
\mathrm{d} \phi_ {i} (t) = \phi_ {i} (t) \left[ \left(b _ {i} (r (t)) - a _ {i i} (r (t)) \phi_ {i} (t)\right) \mathrm{d} t + \sigma_ {i} (r (t)) \mathrm{d} B _ {i} (t) \right], \quad \phi_ {i} (0) = x _ {i} (0); \tag {5.57}
$$

$$
\begin{array}{l} \mathrm{d} I _ {i} (t) = I _ {i} (t) \left[ \left(b _ {i} (r (t)) - a _ {i j} (r (t)) \phi_ {j} (t) - a _ {i i} (r (t)) I _ {i} (t)\right) \mathrm{d} t \right. \\ + \sigma_ {i} (r (t)) \mathrm{d} B _ {i} (t) ], \quad I _ {i} (0) = x _ {i} (0). \tag {5.58} \\ \end{array}
$$

证明 由引理5.4，我们知道

$$
\phi_ {i} (t) = \frac {\mathrm{e} ^ {\int_ {0} ^ {t} \left[ b _ {i} (r (s)) - \frac {\sigma_ {i} ^ {2} (r (s))}{2} \right] \mathrm{d} s + \sigma_ {i} (r (s)) \mathrm{d} B _ {i} (s)}}{\frac {1}{\phi_ {i}} + \int_ {0} ^ {t} a _ {i i} (r (s)) \mathrm{e} ^ {\int_ {0} ^ {s} \left[ b _ {i} (r (\tau)) - \frac {\sigma_ {i} ^ {2} (r (\tau))}{2} \right] \mathrm{d} \tau + \sigma_ {i} (r (\tau)) \mathrm{d} B _ {i} (\tau)} \mathrm{d} s}.
$$

对方程 (5.58), 应用 Itô 公式得

$$
\mathrm{d} \left(\frac {1}{I _ {i} (t)}\right) = - \frac {1}{I _ {i} (t)} \left([ \bar {r} _ {i} (r (t)) - a _ {i j} (r (t)) \phi_ {j} (t) ] \mathrm{d} t + \sigma_ {i} (r (t)) \mathrm{d} B _ {i} (t)\right) + a _ {i i} (r (t)) \mathrm{d} t.
$$

显然, 它是 $\frac{1}{I_i(t)}$ 上的一阶线性微分方程, 于是有

$$
\begin{array}{l} \frac {1}{I _ {i} (t)} = \frac {1}{I _ {i} (0)} \mathrm{e} ^ {- \int_ {0} ^ {t} [ \bar {r} _ {i} (r (s)) - a _ {i j} (r (s)) \phi_ {j} (s) ] \mathrm{d} s + \sigma_ {i} (r (s)) \mathrm{d} B _ {i} (s)} \\ + \int_ {0} ^ {t} a _ {i i} (r (s)) \mathrm{e} ^ {- \int_ {s} ^ {t} [ \bar {r} _ {i} (r (\tau)) - a _ {i j} (r (\tau)) \phi_ {j} (\tau) ] \mathrm{d} \tau + \sigma_ {i} (r (\tau)) \mathrm{d} B _ {i} (\tau)} \mathrm{d} s, \tag {5.59} \\ \end{array}
$$

则

$$
\begin{array}{l} I _ {i} (t) = \frac {1}{I _ {i} (0)} \mathrm{e} ^ {\int_ {0} ^ {t} \left[ b _ {i} (r (s)) - \frac {\sigma_ {i} ^ {2} (r (s))}{2} - a _ {i j} (r (s)) \phi_ {j} (s) \right] \mathrm{d} s + \sigma_ {i} (r (s)) \mathrm{d} B _ {i} (s)} \\ + \int_ {0} ^ {t} a _ {i i} (r (s)) \mathrm{e} ^ {\int_ {0} ^ {s} \left[ b _ {i} (r (\tau)) - \frac {\sigma_ {i} ^ {2} (r (\tau))}{2} a _ {i j} (r (\tau)) \phi_ {j} (\tau) \right] \mathrm{d} \tau + \sigma_ {i} (r (\tau)) \mathrm{d} B _ {i} (\tau)} \mathrm{d} s. \\ \end{array}
$$

由于

$$
\begin{array}{l} \frac {1}{\phi_ {i} (t)} = \frac {1}{\phi_ {i} (0)} \mathrm{e} ^ {- \int_ {0} ^ {t} \bar {r} _ {i} (r (s)) \mathrm{d} s + \sigma_ {i} (r (s)) \mathrm{d} B _ {i} (s)} \\ + \int_ {0} ^ {t} a _ {i i} (r (s)) \mathrm{e} ^ {- \int_ {s} ^ {t} \bar {r} _ {i} (r (\tau)) \mathrm{d} \tau + \sigma_ {i} (r (\tau)) \mathrm{d} B _ {i} (\tau)} \mathrm{d} s, \tag {5.60} \\ \end{array}
$$

可以得到

$$
\begin{array}{l} \frac {1}{x _ {1} (t)} = \frac {1}{x _ {1} (0)} \mathrm{e} ^ {- \int_ {0} ^ {t} [ \bar {r} _ {1} (r (s)) - a _ {1 2} (r (s)) x _ {2} (s) ] \mathrm{d} s + \sigma_ {1} (r (s)) \mathrm{d} B _ {1} (s)} \\ + \int_ {0} ^ {t} a _ {1 1} (r (s)) \mathrm{e} ^ {- \int_ {s} ^ {t} [ \bar {r} _ {1} (r (\tau)) - a _ {1 2} (r (\tau)) x _ {2} (\tau) ] \mathrm{d} \tau + \sigma_ {1} (r (\tau)) \mathrm{d} B _ {1} (\tau)} \mathrm{d} s \\ \geqslant \frac {1}{\phi_ {1} (0)} \mathrm{e} ^ {- \int_ {0} ^ {t} \bar {r} _ {1} (r (s)) \mathrm{d} s + \sigma_ {1} (r (s)) \mathrm{d} B _ {1} (s)} \\ + \int_ {0} ^ {t} a _ {1 1} (r (s)) \mathrm{e} ^ {- \int_ {s} ^ {t} \bar {r} _ {1} (r (\tau)) \mathrm{d} s + \sigma_ {1} (r (\tau)) \mathrm{d} B _ {1} (\tau)} \mathrm{d} s \\ = \frac {1}{\phi_ {1} (t)}, \\ \end{array}
$$

类似地, 由式 (5.59) 可得

$$
\begin{array}{l} \frac {1}{x _ {1} (t)} = \frac {1}{x _ {1} (0)} \mathrm{e} ^ {- \int_ {0} ^ {t} [ \bar {r} _ {1} (r (s)) - a _ {1 2} (r (s)) x _ {2} (s) ] \mathrm{d} s + \sigma_ {1} (r (s)) \mathrm{d} B _ {1} (s)} \\ + \int_ {0} ^ {t} a _ {1 1} (r (s)) \mathrm{e} ^ {- \int_ {s} ^ {t} [ \bar {r} _ {1} (r (\tau)) - a _ {1 2} (r (\tau)) x _ {2} (\tau) ] \mathrm{d} \tau + \sigma_ {1} (r (\tau)) \mathrm{d} B _ {1} (\tau)} \mathrm{d} s \\ \leqslant \frac {1}{I _ {1} (0)} \mathrm{e} ^ {- \int_ {0} ^ {t} [ \bar {r} _ {1} (r (s)) - a _ {1 2} (r (s)) \phi_ {2} (r (s)) ] \mathrm{d} s + \sigma_ {1} (r (s)) \mathrm{d} B _ {1} (s)} \\ + \int_ {0} ^ {t} a _ {1 1} (r (s)) \mathrm{e} ^ {- \int_ {s} ^ {t} [ \bar {r} _ {1} (r (s)) - a _ {1 2} (r (\tau)) \phi_ {2} (r (\tau)) ] \mathrm{d} \tau + \sigma_ {1} (r (\tau)) \mathrm{d} B _ {1} (\tau)} \mathrm{d} s \\ = \frac {1}{I _ {1} (t)}. \\ \end{array}
$$

因此有

$$
I (t) \leqslant x (t) \leqslant \phi (t).
$$

注5.3 由引理5.4可知系统(5.57)和(5.58)分别存在唯一正解.因此，可以由经典的比较定理推断出系统(5.54)有唯一正解

## 5.3.3 主要结论

本小节中我们将证明系统 (5.54) 具有唯一解 $x(t)$ 并且是均值意义下持久的. 首先给出下面的定义.

定义 5.4 SDE(5.54) 被称为是均值意义下持久的, 如果存在正常数 $\tilde{x}_{i}^{*}, x_{i}^{*}(i = 1, 2)$ 使得 SDE(5.54) 的解 $x(t)$ 具有下面的性质:

$$
\lim _ {t \to \infty} \sup \frac {1}{t} \int_ {0} ^ {t} x _ {i} (s) \mathrm{d} s \leqslant x _ {i} ^ {*} \quad \text { a.s. } \quad i = 1, 2 \tag {5.61}
$$

和

$$
\liminf _ {t \to \infty} \frac {1}{t} \int_ {0} ^ {t} x _ {i} (s) \mathrm{d} s \geqslant \tilde {x} _ {i} ^ {*} \quad \text { a.s. } \quad i = 1, 2. \tag {5.62}
$$

引理5.5 令假设1成立，则对任意给定的初始值 $\phi(0) \in \mathbb{R}_+^2$ ，系统(5.57）的解 $\phi(t)$ 具有下面的性质

$$
\begin{array}{l} z _ {i} (t) \mathrm{e} ^ {- \left[ \max _ {0 \leqslant s \leqslant t} \int_ {0} ^ {s} \sigma_ {i} (r (\tau)) \mathrm{d} B _ {i} (\tau) - \int_ {0} ^ {t} \sigma_ {i} (r (\tau)) \mathrm{d} B _ {i} (\tau) \right]} \\ \leqslant \phi_ {i} (t) \leqslant z _ {i} (t) \mathrm{e} ^ {- \left[ \min _ {0 \leqslant s \leqslant t} \int_ {0} ^ {s} \sigma_ {i} (r (\tau)) \mathrm{d} B _ {i} (\tau) - \int_ {0} ^ {t} \sigma_ {i} (r (\tau)) \mathrm{d} B _ {i} (\tau) \right]}, \\ \end{array}
$$

其中 $z_{i}(t)(i = 1,2)$ 是下面方程的解

$$
\mathrm{d} z _ {i} (t) = z _ {i} (t) \left[ b _ {i} (r (s)) - \frac {\sigma_ {i} ^ {2} (r (s))}{2} - \check {a} _ {i i} z _ {i} (t) \right] \mathrm{d} t, \quad z _ {i} (0) = \phi_ {i} (0) \quad (i = 1, 2). \tag {5.63}
$$

证明 由式 (5.59) 可得

$$
\begin{array}{l} \frac {1}{\phi_ {i} (t)} = \frac {1}{\phi_ {i} (0)} \mathrm{e} ^ {- \int_ {0} ^ {t} \bar {r} _ {i} (r (\tau)) \mathrm{d} \tau + \sigma_ {i} (r (\tau)) \mathrm{d} B _ {i} (\tau)} \\ + \int_ {0} ^ {t} a _ {i i} (r (s)) \mathrm{e} ^ {- \int_ {0} ^ {t} \bar {r} _ {i} (r (\tau)) \mathrm{d} \tau + \sigma_ {i} (r (\tau)) \mathrm{d} B _ {i} (\tau) + \int_ {0} ^ {s} \bar {r} _ {i} (r (\tau)) \mathrm{d} \tau + \sigma_ {i} (r (\tau)) \mathrm{d} B _ {i} (\tau)} \mathrm{d} s \\ = \mathrm{e} ^ {- \int_ {0} ^ {t} \sigma_ {i} (r (\tau)) \mathrm{d} B _ {i} (\tau)} \left[ \frac {1}{\phi_ {i} (0)} \mathrm{e} ^ {- \int_ {0} ^ {t} \bar {r} _ {i} (r (\tau)) \mathrm{d} \tau} \right. \\ \left. + \int_ {0} ^ {t} a _ {i i} (r (s)) \mathrm{e} ^ {- \int_ {s} ^ {t} \bar {r} _ {i} (r (\tau)) \mathrm{d} \tau}. \mathrm{e} ^ {\int_ {0} ^ {s} \sigma_ {i} (r (\tau)) \mathrm{d} B _ {i} (\tau)} \mathrm{d} s \right] \\ \leqslant \mathrm{e} ^ {- \int_ {0} ^ {t} \sigma_ {i} (r (\tau)) \mathrm{d} B _ {i} (\tau)} \left[ \frac {1}{\phi_ {i} (0)} \mathrm{e} ^ {- \int_ {0} ^ {t} \bar {r} _ {i} (r (\tau)) \mathrm{d} \tau} \right. \\ + \check {a} _ {i i} \mathrm{e} ^ {\max _ {0 \leqslant s \leqslant t} \left\{\int_ {0} ^ {s} \sigma_ {i} (r (\tau)) \mathrm{d} B _ {i} (\tau) \right\}} \int_ {0} ^ {t} \mathrm{e} ^ {- \int_ {s} ^ {t} \bar {r} _ {i} (r (\tau)) \mathrm{d} \tau} \mathrm{d} s ] \\ \leqslant \mathrm{e} ^ {\max _ {0 \leqslant s \leqslant t} \left\{\int_ {0} ^ {s} \sigma_ {i} (r (\tau)) \mathrm{d} B _ {i} (\tau) \right\} - \int_ {0} ^ {t} \sigma_ {i} (r (\tau)) \mathrm{d} B _ {i} (\tau)} \cdot \frac {1}{z _ {i} (t)}. \\ \end{array}
$$

$$
\begin{array}{l} \leqslant \mathrm{e} ^ {- \int_ {0} ^ {t} \sigma_ {i} (r (\tau)) \mathrm{d} B _ {i} (\tau)} \left[ \frac {1}{\phi_ {i} (0)} \mathrm{e} ^ {- \int_ {0} ^ {t} \bar {r} _ {i} (r (\tau)) \mathrm{d} \tau} \right. \\ + \check {a} _ {i i} \mathrm{e} ^ {\max _ {0 \leqslant s \leqslant t} \left\{\int_ {0} ^ {s} \sigma_ {i} (r (\tau)) \mathrm{d} B _ {i} (\tau) \right\}} \int_ {0} ^ {t} \mathrm{e} ^ {- \int_ {s} ^ {t} \bar {r} _ {i} (r (\tau)) \mathrm{d} \tau} \mathrm{d} s ] \\ \leqslant \mathrm{e} ^ {\max _ {0 \leqslant s \leqslant t} \left\{\int_ {0} ^ {s} \sigma_ {i} (r (\tau)) \mathrm{d} B _ {i} (\tau) \right\} - \int_ {0} ^ {t} \sigma_ {i} (r (\tau)) \mathrm{d} B _ {i} (\tau)} \cdot \frac {1}{z _ {i} (t)}. \\ \end{array}
$$

类似地有

$$
\begin{array}{l} \frac {1}{\phi_ {i} (t)} \geqslant \mathrm{e} ^ {- \int_ {0} ^ {t} \sigma_ {i} (r (\tau)) \mathrm{d} B _ {i} (\tau)} \left[ \frac {1}{\phi_ {i} (0)} \mathrm{e} ^ {- \int_ {0} ^ {t} \bar {r} _ {i} (r (\tau)) \mathrm{d} \tau} \right. \\ \left. + \check {a} _ {i i} \mathrm{e} ^ {\min _ {0 \leqslant s \leqslant t} \left(\int_ {0} ^ {s} \sigma_ {i} (r (\tau)) \mathrm{d} B _ {i} (\tau)\right)} \int_ {0} ^ {t} \mathrm{e} ^ {- \int_ {s} ^ {t} \bar {r} _ {i} (r (\tau)) \mathrm{d} \tau} \mathrm{d} s \right] \\ \geqslant \mathrm{e} ^ {\min _ {0 \leqslant s \leqslant t} \left[ \int_ {0} ^ {s} \sigma_ {i} (r (\tau)) \mathrm{d} B _ {i} (\tau) \right] - \int_ {0} ^ {t} \sigma_ {i} (r (\tau)) \mathrm{d} B _ {i} (\tau)} \cdot \frac {1}{z _ {i} (t)}. \\ \end{array}
$$

引理 5.6 令假设 1 成立, 对任意给定的初始值 $z(0) \in \mathbb{R}_{+}^{2}$ , 方程 (5.63) 的解 $z_{i}(t)$ 具有下面的性质

$$
p _ {i} (t) \leqslant z _ {i} (t) \leqslant q _ {i} (t)
$$

和

$$
\lim _ {t \rightarrow \infty} \frac {\log p _ {i} (t)}{t} = \lim _ {t \rightarrow \infty} \frac {\log z _ {i} (t)}{t} = \lim _ {t \rightarrow \infty} \frac {\log q _ {i} (t)}{t} = 0,
$$

其中 $p_i(t), q_i(t) (i = 1,2)$ 分别是下面两个方程的解

$$
\mathrm{d} p _ {i} (t) = p _ {i} (t) \left[ \hat {\bar {r}} _ {i} - \check {a} _ {i i} p _ {i} (t) \right] \mathrm{d} t, \quad p _ {i} (0) = z _ {i} (0),
$$

$$
\mathrm{d} q _ {i} (t) = q _ {i} (t) \left[ \check {\tilde {r}} _ {i} - \hat {a} _ {i i} q _ {i} (t) \right] \mathrm{d} t, \quad q _ {i} (0) = z _ {i} (0).
$$

证明 由引理5.4可以看出

$$
p _ {i} (t) = \frac {\mathrm{e} ^ {\hat {\tilde {r}} _ {i} t}}{\frac {1}{\tilde {z} _ {i} (0)} + \frac {\check {a} _ {i i}}{\hat {\tilde {r}} _ {i}} (\mathrm{e} ^ {\hat {\tilde {r}} _ {i} t} - 1)}, \quad q _ {i} (t) = \frac {\mathrm{e} ^ {\check {\tilde {r}} _ {i} t}}{\frac {1}{\hat {z} _ {i} (0)} + \frac {\hat {a} _ {i i}}{\check {\tilde {r}} _ {i}} (\mathrm{e} ^ {\check {\tilde {r}} _ {i} t} - 1)},
$$

于是有

$$
\lim _ {t \rightarrow \infty} \frac {\log p _ {i} (t)}{t} = \lim _ {t \rightarrow \infty} \frac {\log q _ {i} (t)}{t} = 0,
$$

由常微分方程经典的比较原理可得

$$
p _ {i} (t) \leqslant z _ {i} (t) \leqslant q _ {i} (t).
$$

因此

$$
\lim _ {t \rightarrow \infty} \frac {\log z _ {i} (t)}{t} = 0.
$$

于是结论得证.

引理 5.7 令假设 1 成立, 对任意给定的初始值 $\phi(0) \in \mathbb{R}_{+}^{2}$ , 系统 (5.57) 的解 $\phi(t)$ 具有下面的性质

$$
\lim _ {t \rightarrow \infty} \frac {\log \phi_ {i} (t)}{t} = 0 \quad \text { a   .   s   . }
$$

证明 由引理5.5可得

$$
\begin{array}{l} z _ {i} (t) \mathrm{e} ^ {- \left[ \max _ {0 \leqslant s \leqslant t} \int_ {0} ^ {s} \sigma_ {i} (r (\tau)) \mathrm{d} B _ {i} (\tau) - \int_ {0} ^ {t} \sigma_ {i} (r (\tau)) \mathrm{d} B _ {i} (\tau) \right]} \\ \leqslant \phi_ {i} (t) \leqslant z _ {i} (t) \mathrm{e} ^ {- \left[ \min _ {0 \leqslant s \leqslant t} \int_ {0} ^ {s} \sigma_ {i} (r (\tau)) \mathrm{d} B _ {i} (\tau) - \int_ {0} ^ {t} \sigma_ {i} (r (\tau)) \mathrm{d} B _ {i} (\tau) \right]}, \\ \end{array}
$$

因此有

$$
\log \phi_ {i} (t) - \log z _ {i} (t) \geqslant \int_ {0} ^ {t} \sigma_ {i} (r (\tau)) \mathrm{d} B _ {i} (\tau) - \max _ {0 \leqslant s \leqslant t} \int_ {0} ^ {s} \sigma_ {i} (r (\tau)) \mathrm{d} B _ {i} (\tau), \tag {5.64}
$$

$$
\log \phi_ {i} (t) - \log z _ {i} (t) \leqslant \int_ {0} ^ {t} \sigma_ {i} (r (\tau)) \mathrm{d} B _ {i} (\tau) - \min _ {0 \leqslant s \leqslant t} \int_ {0} ^ {s} \sigma_ {i} (r (\tau)) \mathrm{d} B _ {i} (\tau). \tag {5.65}
$$

令 $M_{i}(t) = \int_{0}^{t}\sigma_{i}(r(\tau))\mathrm{d}B_{i}(\tau)$ ，则

$$
\langle M _ {i}, M _ {i} \rangle_ {t} = \int_ {0} ^ {t} \sigma_ {i} ^ {2} (r (\tau)) \mathrm{d} \tau \leqslant \max _ {k \in S} \{\sigma_ {1} ^ {2} (k), \sigma_ {2} ^ {2} (k) \} t.
$$

由于 $\sigma_{i}(k)$ 在 $[0, +\infty)$ 上有界，于是有

$$
\lim _ {t \to \infty} \frac {\langle M _ {i} , M _ {i} \rangle_ {t}}{t} = \lim _ {t \to \infty} \frac {1}{t} \int_ {0} ^ {t} \sigma_ {i} ^ {2} (r (\tau)) \mathrm{d} \tau \leqslant \max _ {k \in S} \{\sigma_ {1} ^ {2} (k), \sigma_ {2} ^ {2} (k) \} <   \infty \quad \text { a.s. }
$$

由强大数定律可得

$$
\lim _ {t \rightarrow \infty} \frac {M _ {i} (t)}{t} = \lim _ {t \rightarrow \infty} \frac {\int_ {0} ^ {t} \sigma_ {i} (r (\tau)) \mathrm{d} B _ {i} (\tau)}{t} = 0 \quad \text {a.s..} \tag {5.66}
$$

于是 $\forall \varepsilon > 0, \varepsilon T(\omega)$ , 当 $t > T(\omega)$ 时, 有

$$
\left| \frac {M _ {i} (t)}{t} \right| <   \varepsilon \quad \text { a.s. }.
$$

由此可以得到

$$
\begin{array}{l} \max _ {0 \leqslant s \leqslant t} \left| \frac {M _ {i} (s)}{t} \right| \leqslant \max _ {0 \leqslant s \leqslant T} \left| \frac {M _ {i} (s)}{t} \right| + \max _ {T \leqslant s \leqslant t} \left| \frac {M _ {i} (s)}{t} \right|, \\ \leqslant \frac {M}{t} + \frac {\varepsilon s}{t} \quad \text { a   .   s   . }. \\ \end{array}
$$

其中 $M$ 是 $M_{i}(s)$ 在 $[0,T]$ 上的界. 因此

$$
\lim _ {t \to \infty} \min _ {0 \leqslant s \leqslant t} \left| \frac {M _ {i} (s)}{t} \right| = \lim _ {t \to \infty} \max _ {0 \leqslant s \leqslant t} \left| \frac {M _ {i} (s)}{t} \right| = 0 \quad \text { a.s. }.
$$

于是由式 (5.64) 和式 (5.65) 可以得到

$$
\lim _ {t \to \infty} \frac {\log \phi_ {i} (t)}{t} = 0 \quad \text { a.s. }
$$

引理5.8 令假设1和假设2成立，系统(5.54)的解 $x(t)$ 对任意给定的初始值 $x(0) \in \mathbb{R}_+^2$ 具有下面的性质

$$
\lim _ {t \to \infty} \frac {\log x _ {i} (t)}{t} = 0 \quad \text { a.s. } \quad (i = 1, 2).
$$

证明 首先证明

$$
\limsup _ {t \to \infty} \frac {\log x _ {i} (t)}{t} \leqslant 0 \quad \text { a.s. } \quad (i = 1, 2). \tag {5.67}
$$

事实上, 定理 5.11 表明 $x_{i}(t) \leqslant \phi_{i}(t)$ a.s. $(i = 1,2)$ , 结合引理 5.7 可得式 (5.65) 是正确的. 下面我们将证明

$$
\liminf _ {t \to \infty} \frac {\log x _ {i} (t)}{t} \geqslant 0 \quad \text { a.s. } \quad (i = 1, 2). \tag {5.68}
$$

由 Itô 公式, 可得

$$
\mathrm{d} \log \phi_ {i} (t) = \left[ b _ {i} (r (t)) - \frac {1}{2} \sigma_ {i} ^ {2} (r (t)) - a _ {i i} (r (t)) \phi_ {i} (t) \right] \mathrm{d} t + \sigma_ {i} (r (t)) \mathrm{d} B _ {i} (t). \tag {5.69}
$$

从0到 $t$ 积分得

$$
\begin{array}{l} \log \phi_ {i} (t) = \log \phi_ {i} (0) + \int_ {0} ^ {t} \left[ b _ {i} (r (s)) - \frac {1}{2} \sigma_ {i} ^ {2} (r (s)) \right] \mathrm{d} s \\ - \int_ {0} ^ {t} a _ {i i} (r (s)) \phi_ {i} (s) \mathrm{d} s + \int_ {0} ^ {t} \sigma_ {i} (r (s)) \mathrm{d} B _ {i} (s). \\ \end{array}
$$

因为 $\phi_i(t) > 0$ ，所以有

$$
\log \phi_ {i} (t) \geqslant \log \phi_ {i} (0) + \int_ {0} ^ {t} \left[ b _ {i} (r (s)) - \frac {1}{2} \sigma_ {i} ^ {2} (r (s)) \right] \mathrm{d} s - \check {a} _ {i i} \int_ {0} ^ {t} \phi_ {i} (s) \mathrm{d} s + \int_ {0} ^ {t} \sigma_ {i} (r (s)) \mathrm{d} B _ {i} (s)
$$

和

$$
\log \phi_ {i} (t) \leqslant \log \phi_ {i} (0) + \int_ {0} ^ {t} \left[ b _ {i} (r (s)) - \frac {1}{2} \sigma_ {i} ^ {2} (r (s)) \right] d s - \hat {a} _ {i i} \int_ {0} ^ {t} \phi_ {i} (s) d s + \int_ {0} ^ {t} \sigma_ {i} (r (s)) d B _ {i} (s).
$$

同时得到

$$
\begin{array}{l} \int_ {0} ^ {t} \phi_ {i} (s) \mathrm{d} s \geqslant \frac {\int_ {0} ^ {t} \sigma_ {i} (r (s)) \mathrm{d} B _ {i} (s)}{\check {a} _ {i i}} + \frac {\log \phi_ {i} (0) - \log \phi_ {i} (t)}{\check {a} _ {i i}} \\ + \frac {\int_ {0} ^ {t} \left[ b _ {i} (r (s)) - \frac {1}{2} \sigma_ {i} ^ {2} (r (s)) \right] \mathrm{d} s}{\check {a} _ {i i}}, \tag {5.70} \\ \end{array}
$$

且有

$$
\begin{array}{l} \int_ {0} ^ {t} \phi_ {i} (s) \mathrm{d} s \leqslant \frac {\int_ {0} ^ {t} \sigma_ {i} (r (s)) \mathrm{d} B _ {i} (s)}{\hat {a} _ {i i}} + \frac {\log \phi_ {i} (0) - \log \phi_ {i} (t)}{\hat {a} _ {i i}} \\ + \frac {\int_ {0} ^ {t} \left[ b _ {i} (r (s)) - \frac {1}{2} \sigma_ {i} ^ {2} (r (s)) \right] \mathrm{d} s}{\hat {a} _ {i i}}. \tag {5.71} \\ \end{array}
$$

将式 (5.71) 代入到式 (5.60) 中得

$$
\begin{array}{l} \frac {1}{I _ {i} (t)} \leqslant \frac {1}{I _ {i} (0)} \mathrm{e} ^ {- \int_ {0} ^ {t} \bar {r} _ {i} (r (s)) \mathrm{d} s + \sigma_ {i} (r (s)) \mathrm{d} B _ {i} (s)} \\ \cdot \mathrm{e} ^ {\check {a} _ {i j} \left[ \frac {\int_ {0} ^ {t} \sigma_ {j} (r (s)) \mathrm{d} B _ {j} (s)}{\hat {a} _ {j j}} + \frac {\log \phi_ {j} (0) - \log \phi_ {j} (t)}{\hat {a} _ {j j}} + \frac {\int_ {0} ^ {t} \bar {r} _ {j} (r (s)) \mathrm{d} s}{\hat {a} _ {j j}} \right]} \\ + \int_ {0} ^ {t} a _ {i i} (r (s)) \mathrm{e} ^ {- \int_ {s} ^ {t} \bar {r} _ {i} (r (\tau)) \mathrm{d} \tau + \sigma_ {i} (r (\tau)) \mathrm{d} B _ {i} (\tau)} \\ \cdot \mathrm{e} ^ {\bar {a} _ {i j} \left[ \frac {\int_ {s} ^ {t} \sigma_ {j} (r (\tau)) \mathrm{d} B _ {j} (\tau)}{\hat {a} _ {j j}} + \frac {\log \phi_ {j} (s) - \log \phi_ {j} (t)}{\hat {a} _ {j j}} + \frac {\int_ {s} ^ {t} \bar {r} _ {j} (r (\tau)) \mathrm{d} \tau}{\hat {a} _ {j j}} \right]} \mathrm{d} s. \tag {5.72} \\ \end{array}
$$

注意到

$$
\begin{array}{l} \int_ {0} ^ {t} a _ {i i} (r (s)) \mathrm{e} ^ {- \int_ {s} ^ {t} \bar {r} _ {i} (r (\tau)) \mathrm{d} \tau + \sigma_ {i} (r (\tau)) \mathrm{d} B _ {i} (\tau)} \\ \cdot \mathrm{e} ^ {\tilde {a} _ {i j} \left[ \frac {\int_ {s} ^ {t} \sigma_ {j} (r (\tau)) \mathrm{d} B _ {j} (\tau)}{\hat {a} _ {j j}} + \frac {\log \phi_ {j} (s) - \log \phi_ {j} (t)}{\hat {a} _ {j j}} + \frac {\int_ {s} ^ {t} \bar {r} _ {j} (r (\tau)) \mathrm{d} \tau}{\hat {a} _ {j j}} \right]} \mathrm{d} s \\ \leqslant \check {a} _ {i i} \int_ {0} ^ {t} \mathrm{e} ^ {- \hat {\bar {r}} _ {i} (t - s) + \check {\sigma} _ {i} (B _ {i} (s) - B _ {i} (t))} \\ \cdot \mathrm{e} \left[ \frac {\tilde {a} _ {i j} \tilde {\sigma} _ {j} (B _ {j} (t) - B _ {j} (s))}{\hat {a} _ {j j}} + \frac {\tilde {a} _ {i j} (\log \phi_ {j} (s) - \log \phi_ {j} (t))}{\hat {a} _ {j j}} + \frac {\tilde {a} _ {i j} \tilde {\tau} _ {j} (t - s)}{\hat {a} _ {j j}} \right] \mathrm{d} s \\ \leqslant \check {a} _ {i i} \int_ {0} ^ {t} \mathrm{e} ^ {- \hat {\bar {r}} _ {i} (t - s) + \check {\sigma} _ {i} (\max _ {0 \leqslant s \leqslant t} B _ {i} (s) - B _ {i} (t))} \\ \cdot \mathrm{e} \left[ \frac {\tilde {a} _ {i j} \tilde {\sigma} _ {j} (B _ {j} (t) - \min _ {0 \leqslant s \leqslant t} B _ {j} (s))}{\hat {a} _ {j j}} + \frac {\tilde {a} _ {i j} (\max _ {0 \leqslant s \leqslant t} \log \phi_ {j} (s) - \log \phi_ {j} (t))}{\hat {a} _ {j j}} + \frac {\tilde {a} _ {i j} \tilde {\bar {\tau}} _ {j} (t - s)}{\hat {a} _ {j j}} \right] \mathrm{d} s \\ \leqslant \mathrm{e} ^ {\check {\sigma} _ {i} \left(\max _ {0 \leqslant s \leqslant t} B _ {i} (s) - B _ {i} (t)\right) + \left[ \frac {\check {a} _ {i j} \check {\sigma} _ {j} \left(B _ {j} (t) - \min _ {0 \leqslant s \leqslant t} B _ {j} (s)\right)}{\hat {a} _ {j j}} + \frac {\check {a} _ {i j} \left(\max _ {0 \leqslant s \leqslant t} \log \phi_ {j} (s) - \log \phi_ {j} (t)\right)}{\hat {a} _ {j j}} \right]} \\ \cdot \check {a} _ {i i} \int_ {0} ^ {t} \mathrm{e} ^ {- \hat {\bar {r}} _ {i} (t - s) + \frac {\check {a} _ {i j} \tilde {\bar {r}} _ {j} (t - s)}{\check {a} _ {j j}}} \mathrm{d} s. \\ \end{array}
$$

于是结合式 (5.72), 有

$$
\begin{array}{l} \frac {1}{I _ {i} (t)} \leqslant \mathrm{e} ^ {\check {\sigma} _ {i} (\max _ {0 \leqslant s \leqslant t} B _ {i} (s) - B _ {i} (t))} \\ \cdot \mathrm{e} ^ {\left[ \frac {\bar {a} _ {i j} \bar {\sigma} _ {j} (B _ {j} (t) - \min _ {0 \leqslant s \leqslant t} B _ {j} (s))}{\hat {a} _ {j j}} + \frac {\bar {a} _ {i j} (\max _ {0 \leqslant s \leqslant t} \log \phi_ {j} (s) - \log \phi_ {j} (t))}{\hat {a} _ {j j}} \right]} \\ \times \left[ \frac {1}{I _ {i} (0)} \mathrm{e} ^ {- \hat {\bar {r}} _ {i} t + \frac {\check {a} _ {i j} \tilde {r} _ {i}}{\check {a} _ {j j}} t} + \check {a} _ {i i} \int_ {0} ^ {t} \mathrm{e} ^ {- \hat {\bar {r}} _ {i} (t - s) + \frac {\check {a} _ {i j} \tilde {r} _ {j} (t - s)}{\check {a} _ {j j}}} \mathrm{d} s \right]. \tag {5.73} \\ \end{array}
$$

类似地, 有

$$
\begin{array}{l} \frac {1}{I _ {i} (t)} \geqslant \mathrm{e} ^ {\hat {\sigma} _ {i} \left(\min _ {0 \leqslant s \leqslant t} B _ {i} (s) - B _ {i} (t)\right) + \left[ \frac {\hat {a} _ {i j} \hat {\sigma} _ {j} \left(B _ {j} (t) - \max _ {0 \leqslant s \leqslant t} B _ {j} (s)\right)}{\hat {a} _ {j j}} + \frac {\hat {a} _ {i j} \left(\min _ {0 \leqslant s \leqslant t} \log \phi_ {j} (s) - \log \phi_ {j} (t)\right)}{\hat {a} _ {j j}} \right]} \\ \times \left[ \frac {1}{I _ {i} (0)} \mathrm{e} ^ {- \bar {\tilde {r}} _ {i} t + \frac {\hat {a} _ {i j} \hat {\tilde {r}} _ {i}}{\hat {a} _ {j j}} t} + \hat {a} _ {i i} \int_ {0} ^ {t} \mathrm{e} ^ {- \bar {\tilde {r}} _ {i} (t - s) + \frac {\hat {a} _ {i j} \hat {\tilde {r}} _ {j} (t - s)}{\hat {a} _ {j j}}} \mathrm{d} s \right]. \tag {5.74} \\ \end{array}
$$

于是由式 (5.73) 和式 (5.74) 可以推得

$$
\begin{array}{l} \log I _ {i} (t) \geqslant \log z _ {i} ^ {\star} (t) - \check {\sigma} _ {i} (\max _ {0 \leqslant s \leqslant t} B _ {i} (s) - B _ {i} (t)) \\ - \frac {\check {a} _ {i j} \check {\sigma} _ {j} (B _ {j} (t) - \min _ {0 \leqslant s \leqslant t} B _ {j} (s))}{\hat {a} _ {j j}} - \frac {\check {a} _ {i j} (\max _ {0 \leqslant s \leqslant t} \log \phi_ {j} (s) - \log \phi_ {j} (t))}{\hat {a} _ {j j}}, \\ \end{array}
$$

且

$$
\begin{array}{l} \log I _ {i} (t) \leqslant \log \tilde {z} _ {i} (t) - \hat {\sigma} _ {i} (\min _ {0 \leqslant s \leqslant t} B _ {i} (s) - B _ {i} (t)) \\ - \frac {\hat {a} _ {i j} \hat {\sigma} _ {j} (B _ {j} (t) - \max _ {0 \leqslant s \leqslant t} B _ {j} (s))}{\check {a} _ {j j}} - \frac {\hat {a} _ {i j} (\min _ {0 \leqslant s \leqslant t} \log \phi_ {j} (s) - \log \phi_ {j} (t))}{\check {a} _ {j j}}, \\ \end{array}
$$

其中 $\tilde{z}_i(t), z_i^*(t) (i = 1,2)$ 分别是下面两个方程的解

$$
\mathrm{d} \tilde {z} _ {i} (t) = \tilde {z} _ {i} (t) \left[ \check {\bar {r}} _ {i} - \frac {\hat {a} _ {i j} \hat {\bar {r}} _ {j}}{\check {a} _ {j j}} - \hat {a} _ {i i} \tilde {z} _ {i} (t) \right] \mathrm{d} t, \quad \tilde {z} _ {i} (0) = I _ {i} (0) = x _ {i} (0),
$$

$$
\mathrm{d} z _ {i} ^ {*} (t) = z _ {i} ^ {*} (t) \left[ \hat {\bar {r}} _ {i} - \frac {\check {a} _ {i j} \check {\bar {r}} _ {j}}{\hat {a} _ {j j}} - \check {a} _ {i i} z _ {i} ^ {*} (t) \right] \mathrm{d} t, \quad z _ {i} ^ {*} (t) = I _ {i} (0) = x _ {i} (0).
$$

由引理 5.4, 有

$$
\tilde {z} _ {i} (t) = \frac {\mathrm{e} ^ {\left(\check {\bar {r}} _ {i} - \frac {\hat {a} _ {i j} \hat {\bar {r}} _ {j}}{\check {a} _ {j j}}\right) t}}{1 / \tilde {z} _ {i} (0) + \frac {\hat {a} _ {i i}}{\left(\check {\bar {r}} _ {i} - \frac {\hat {a} _ {i j} \hat {\bar {r}} _ {j}}{\check {a} _ {j j}}\right)} \left[ \mathrm{e} ^ {\left(\check {\bar {r}} _ {i} - \frac {\hat {a} _ {i j} \hat {\bar {r}} _ {j}}{\check {a} _ {j j}}\right) t} - 1 \right]}
$$

和

$$
z _ {i} ^ {*} (t) = \frac {\mathrm{e} ^ {\left(\hat {\bar {r}} _ {i} - \frac {\check {a} _ {i j} \check {r} _ {j}}{\hat {a} _ {j j}}\right) t}}{1 / z _ {i} ^ {*} (0) + \frac {\check {a} _ {i i}}{\left(\hat {\bar {r}} _ {i} - \frac {\check {a} _ {i j} \check {r} _ {j}}{\hat {a} _ {j j}}\right)} \left[ \mathrm{e} ^ {\left(\hat {\bar {r}} _ {i} - \frac {\check {a} _ {i j} \check {r} _ {j}}{\hat {a} _ {j j}}\right) t} - 1 \right]}.
$$

此外，下面的结论成立

$$
\begin{array}{l} \frac {\log I _ {i} (t)}{t} \geqslant \frac {\log z _ {i} ^ {*} (t)}{t} - \frac {\check {\sigma} _ {i} (\max _ {0 \leqslant s \leqslant t} B _ {i} (s) - B _ {i} (t))}{t} \\ - \frac {\check {a} _ {i j}}{\hat {a} _ {j j}} \check {\sigma} _ {j} \frac {B _ {j} (t) - \min _ {0 \leqslant s \leqslant t} B _ {j} (s)}{t} \\ - \frac {\check {a} _ {i j}}{\hat {a} _ {j j}} \frac {\max _ {0 \leqslant s \leqslant t} \log \phi_ {j} (s) - \log \phi_ {j} (t)}{t}, \tag {5.75} \\ \end{array}
$$

且有

$$
\begin{array}{l} \frac {\log I _ {i} (t)}{t} \leqslant \frac {\log \tilde {z} _ {i} (t)}{t} - \frac {\hat {\sigma} _ {i} \left(\min _ {0 \leqslant s \leqslant t} B _ {i} (s) - B _ {i} (t)\right)}{t} \\ - \frac {\hat {a} _ {i j}}{\check {a} _ {j j}} \hat {\sigma} _ {j} \frac {B _ {j} (t) - \max _ {0 \leqslant s \leqslant t} B _ {j} (s)}{t} \\ - \frac {\hat {a} _ {i j}}{\check {a} _ {j j}} \frac {\min _ {0 \leqslant s \leqslant t} \log \phi_ {j} (s) - \log \phi_ {j} (t)}{t}. \tag {5.76} \\ \end{array}
$$

显然, 如果 $\hat{r}_{i}-\frac{\check{a}_{ij}\check{r}_{j}}{\hat{a}_{jj}}>0$ , 则有

$$
\lim _ {t \rightarrow \infty} \frac {\log \tilde {z} _ {i} (t)}{t} = 0, \quad \lim _ {t \rightarrow \infty} \frac {\log z _ {i} ^ {*} (t)}{t} = 0 \quad \text { a   .   s   . }
$$

由布朗运动的性质结合引理 5.7, 在式 (5.75) 和式 (5.76) 中令 $t \rightarrow \infty$ , 可得

$$
\liminf _ {t \to \infty} \frac {\log I _ {i} (t)}{t} \geqslant 0, \quad \limsup _ {t \to \infty} \frac {\log I _ {i} (t)}{t} \leqslant 0. \quad \text { a.s.. }
$$

于是有

$$
\lim _ {t \to \infty} \frac {\log I _ {i} (t)}{t} = 0. \quad \mathrm{a.s.}
$$

结合定理 5.11, 结论得证.

定理5.12 令假设2和假设3成立，则系统(5.54)满足初始条件 $x(0)\in \mathbb{R}_+^2$ 的解 $x(t)$ 具有下面的性质

$$
\tilde {x} _ {i} ^ {*} \leqslant \liminf _ {t \to \infty} \frac {1}{t} \int_ {0} ^ {t} x _ {i} (s) \mathrm{d} s \leqslant \limsup _ {t \to \infty} \frac {1}{t} \int_ {0} ^ {t} x _ {i} (s) \mathrm{d} s \leqslant x _ {i} ^ {*} \quad \text { a.s. },
$$

其中

$$
\tilde {x} _ {1} ^ {*} = \frac {\hat {a} _ {2 2} m _ {1} - \check {a} _ {1 2} m _ {2}}{\check {a} _ {1 1} \hat {a} _ {2 2} - \check {a} _ {1 2} \hat {a} _ {2 1}}, \quad \tilde {x} _ {2} ^ {*} = \frac {\hat {a} _ {1 1} m _ {2} - \check {a} _ {2 1} m _ {1}}{\hat {a} _ {1 1} \check {a} _ {2 2} - \hat {a} _ {1 2} \check {a} _ {2 1}},
$$

$$
x _ {1} ^ {*} = \frac {\check {a} _ {2 2} m _ {1} - \hat {a} _ {1 2} m _ {2}}{\hat {a} _ {1 1} \check {a} _ {2 2} - \hat {a} _ {1 2} \check {a} _ {2 1}}, \quad x _ {2} ^ {*} = \frac {\check {a} _ {1 1} m _ {2} - \hat {a} _ {2 1} m _ {1}}{\check {a} _ {1 1} \hat {a} _ {2 2} - \check {a} _ {1 2} \hat {a} _ {2 1}},
$$

且 $m_{i} = \sum_{k = 1}^{N}\pi_{k}\left[b_{i}(k) - \frac{\sigma_{i}^{2}(k)}{2}\right].$ 

证明 首先证明

$$
\lim _ {t \to \infty} \sup \frac {1}{t} \int_ {0} ^ {t} x _ {i} (s) \mathrm{d} s \leqslant x _ {i} ^ {*} \quad (i = 1, 2) \quad \text { a.s. } \tag {5.77}
$$

由 Itô 公式可得

$$
\begin{array}{l} \mathrm{d} \log x _ {1} (t) = \left[ b _ {1} (r (t)) - \frac {1}{2} \sigma_ {1} ^ {2} (r (t)) - a _ {1 1} (r (t)) x _ {1} (t) - a _ {1 2} (r (t)) x _ {2} (t) \right] \mathrm{d} t \\ + \sigma_ {1} (r (t)) \mathrm{d} B _ {1} (t) \\ \end{array}
$$

和

$$
\begin{array}{l} \mathrm{d} \log x _ {2} (t) = \left[ b _ {2} (r (t)) - \frac {1}{2} \sigma_ {2} ^ {2} (r (t)) - a _ {2 1} (r (t)) x _ {1} (t) - a _ {2 2} (r (t)) x _ {2} (t) \right] \mathrm{d} t \\ + \sigma_ {2} (r (t)) \mathrm{d} B _ {2} (t). \\ \end{array}
$$

从0到 $t$ 积分得

$$
\begin{array}{l} \log x _ {1} (t) = \log x _ {1} (0) + \int_ {0} ^ {t} \bar {r} _ {1} (r (s)) \mathrm{d} s - \int_ {0} ^ {t} a _ {1 1} (r (s)) x _ {1} (s) \mathrm{d} s \\ - \int_ {0} ^ {t} a _ {1 2} (r (s)) x _ {2} (s) \mathrm{d} s + \int_ {0} ^ {t} \sigma_ {1} (r (s)) \mathrm{d} B _ {1} (s) \\ \end{array}
$$

和

$$
\begin{array}{l} \log x _ {2} (t) = \log x _ {2} (0) + \int_ {0} ^ {t} \bar {r} _ {2} (r (s)) \mathrm{d} s - \int_ {0} ^ {t} a _ {2 1} (r (s)) x _ {1} (s) \mathrm{d} s \\ - \int_ {0} ^ {t} a _ {2 2} (r (s)) x _ {2} (s) \mathrm{d} s + \int_ {0} ^ {t} \sigma_ {2} (r (s)) \mathrm{d} B _ {2} (s). \\ \end{array}
$$

因为 $x_{i}(t) > 0, i = 1,2,$ 所以有

$$
\begin{array}{l} \log x _ {1} (t) \leqslant \log x _ {1} (0) + \int_ {0} ^ {t} \bar {r} _ {1} (r (s)) d s - \hat {a} _ {1 1} \int_ {0} ^ {t} x _ {1} (s) d s \\ - \hat {a} _ {1 2} \int_ {0} ^ {t} x _ {2} (s) \mathrm{d} s + \int_ {0} ^ {t} \sigma_ {1} (r (s)) \mathrm{d} B _ {1} (s) \tag {5.78} \\ \end{array}
$$

和

$$
\begin{array}{l} \log x _ {2} (t) \leqslant \log x _ {2} (0) + \int_ {0} ^ {t} \bar {r} _ {2} (r (s)) d s - \hat {a} _ {2 1} \int_ {0} ^ {t} x _ {1} (s) d s \\ - \hat {a} _ {2 2} \int_ {0} ^ {t} x _ {2} (s) \mathrm{d} s + \int_ {0} ^ {t} \sigma_ {2} (r (s)) \mathrm{d} B _ {2} (s). \tag {5.79} \\ \end{array}
$$

类似地有

$$
\begin{array}{l} \log x _ {1} (t) \geqslant \log x _ {1} (0) + \int_ {0} ^ {t} \bar {r} _ {1} (r (s)) d s - \check {a} _ {1 1} \int_ {0} ^ {t} x _ {1} (s) d s \\ - \check {a} _ {1 2} \int_ {0} ^ {t} x _ {2} (s) \mathrm{d} s + \int_ {0} ^ {t} \sigma_ {1} (r (s)) \mathrm{d} B _ {1} (s) \tag {5.80} \\ \end{array}
$$

和

$$
\begin{array}{l} \log x _ {2} (t) \geqslant \log x _ {2} (0) + \int_ {0} ^ {t} \bar {r} _ {2} (r (s)) \mathrm{d} s - \check {a} _ {2 1} \int_ {0} ^ {t} x _ {1} (s) \mathrm{d} s \\ - \check {a} _ {2 2} \int_ {0} ^ {t} x _ {2} (s) \mathrm{d} s + \int_ {0} ^ {t} \sigma_ {2} (r (s)) \mathrm{d} B _ {2} (s). \tag {5.81} \\ \end{array}
$$

由 (5.78) 可得

$$
\begin{array}{l} \check {a} _ {2 2} \log x _ {1} (t) \leqslant \check {a} _ {2 2} \left[ \log x _ {1} (0) + \int_ {0} ^ {t} \sigma_ {1} (r (s)) \mathrm{d} B _ {1} (s) \right] + \check {a} _ {2 2} \int_ {0} ^ {t} \bar {r} _ {1} (r (s)) \mathrm{d} s \\ - \check {a} _ {2 2} \hat {a} _ {1 1} \int_ {0} ^ {t} x _ {1} (s) \mathrm{d} s - \check {a} _ {2 2} \hat {a} _ {1 2} \int_ {0} ^ {t} x _ {2} (s) \mathrm{d} s. \\ \end{array}
$$

类似地由式 (5.81) 有

$$
\begin{array}{l} - \hat {a} _ {1 2} \log x _ {2} (t) \leqslant - \hat {a} _ {1 2} \left[ \log x _ {2} (0) + \int_ {0} ^ {t} \sigma_ {2} (r (s)) \mathrm{d} B _ {2} (s) \right] - \hat {a} _ {1 2} \int_ {0} ^ {t} \bar {r} _ {2} (r (s)) \mathrm{d} s \\ + \hat {a} _ {1 2} \check {a} _ {2 2} \int_ {0} ^ {t} x _ {2} (s) d s + \hat {a} _ {1 2} \check {a} _ {2 1} \int_ {0} ^ {t} x _ {1} (s) d s. \\ \end{array}
$$

因此可得

$$
\begin{array}{l} \check {a} _ {2 2} \log x _ {1} (t) - \hat {a} _ {1 2} \log x _ {2} (t) \\ \leqslant \check {a} _ {2 2} \left[ \log x _ {1} (0) + \int_ {0} ^ {t} \sigma_ {1} (r (s)) \mathrm{d} B _ {1} (s) \right] + \check {a} _ {2 2} \int_ {0} ^ {t} \bar {r} _ {1} (r (s)) \mathrm{d} s \\ + \hat {a} _ {1 2} \left[ - \log x _ {2} (0) - \int_ {0} ^ {t} \sigma_ {2} (r (s)) \mathrm{d} B _ {2} (s) \right] - \hat {a} _ {1 2} \int_ {0} ^ {t} \bar {r} _ {2} (r (s)) \mathrm{d} s \\ - \left(\hat {a} _ {1 1} \check {a} _ {2 2} - \check {a} _ {2 1} \hat {a} _ {1 2}\right) \int_ {0} ^ {t} x _ {1} (s) \mathrm{d} s. \tag {5.82} \\ \end{array}
$$

同时由引理 5.8, 我们知道

$$
\lim _ {t \to \infty} \frac {\log x _ {i} (t)}{t} = 0 \quad \text { a.s. }
$$

由引理5.7可得

$$
\lim _ {t \to \infty} \frac {M _ {i} (t)}{t} = 0 \quad \text { a.s. }
$$

因此有

$$
\operatorname * {l i m s u p} _ {t \to \infty} \frac {1}{t} \int_ {0} ^ {t} x _ {1} (s) \mathrm{d} s \leqslant \frac {\check {a} _ {2 2} m _ {1} - \hat {a} _ {1 2} m _ {2}}{\hat {a} _ {1 1} \check {a} _ {2 2} - \hat {a} _ {1 2} \check {a} _ {2 1}} \quad \text { a.s. }
$$

类似地有

$$
\limsup _ {t \to \infty} \frac {1}{t} \int_ {0} ^ {t} x _ {2} (s) \mathrm{d} s \leqslant \frac {\check {a} _ {1 1} m _ {2} - \hat {a} _ {2 1} m _ {1}}{\check {a} _ {1 1} \hat {a} _ {2 2} - \check {a} _ {1 2} \hat {a} _ {2 1}} \quad \text { a.s. }
$$

接下来证明

$$
\liminf _ {t \to \infty} \frac {1}{t} \int_ {0} ^ {t} x _ {i} (s) \mathrm{d} s \geqslant \tilde {x} _ {i} ^ {*} \quad (i = 1, 2) \quad \text { a.s. } \tag {5.83}
$$

由式 (5.79), 可以得到

$$
\begin{array}{l} \hat {a} _ {2 2} \log x _ {1} (t) \geqslant \hat {a} _ {2 2} \left[ \log x _ {1} (0) + \int_ {0} ^ {t} \sigma_ {1} (r (s)) \mathrm{d} B _ {1} (s) \right] + \hat {a} _ {2 2} \int_ {0} ^ {t} \bar {r} _ {1} (r (s)) \mathrm{d} s \\ - \check {a} _ {1 1} \hat {a} _ {2 2} \int_ {0} ^ {t} x _ {1} (s) \mathrm{d} s - \check {a} _ {1 2} \hat {a} _ {2 2} \int_ {0} ^ {t} x _ {2} (s) \mathrm{d} s. \\ \end{array}
$$

类似地由式 (5.80), 我们知道

$$
- \check {a} _ {1 2} \log x _ {2} (t) \geqslant - \check {a} _ {1 2} \left[ \log x _ {2} (0) + \int_ {0} ^ {t} \sigma_ {2} (r (s)) \mathrm{d} B _ {2} (s) \right] - \check {a} _ {1 2} \int_ {0} ^ {t} \bar {r} _ {2} (r (s)) \mathrm{d} s
$$

$$
+ \check {a} _ {1 2} \hat {a} _ {2 1} \int_ {0} ^ {t} x _ {1} (s) \mathrm{d} s + \check {a} _ {1 2} \hat {a} _ {2 2} \int_ {0} ^ {t} x _ {2} (s) \mathrm{d} s,
$$

因此

$$
\begin{array}{l} \hat {a} _ {2 2} \log x _ {1} (t) - \check {a} _ {1 2} \log x _ {2} (t) \\ \geqslant \hat {a} _ {2 2} \left[ \log x _ {1} (0) + \int_ {0} ^ {t} \sigma_ {1} (r (s)) \mathrm{d} B _ {1} (s) \right] + \hat {a} _ {2 2} \int_ {0} ^ {t} \bar {r} _ {1} (r (s)) \mathrm{d} s \\ + \check {a} _ {1 2} \left[ - \log x _ {2} (0) - \int_ {0} ^ {t} \sigma_ {2} (r (s)) \mathrm{d} B _ {2} (s) \right] - \check {a} _ {1 2} \int_ {0} ^ {t} \bar {r} _ {2} (r (s)) \mathrm{d} s \\ - \left(\check {a} _ {1 1} \hat {a} _ {2 2} - \check {a} _ {1 2} \hat {a} _ {2 1}\right) \int_ {0} ^ {t} x _ {1} (s) \mathrm{d} s. \tag {5.84} \\ \end{array}
$$

类似地得

$$
\liminf _ {t \to \infty} \frac {1}{t} \int_ {0} ^ {t} x _ {1} (s) \mathrm{d} s \geqslant \frac {\hat {a} _ {2 2} m _ {1} - \check {a} _ {1 2} m _ {2}}{\check {a} _ {1 1} \hat {a} _ {2 2} - \check {a} _ {1 2} \hat {a} _ {2 1}}
$$

和

$$
\liminf _ {t \to \infty} \frac {1}{t} \int_ {0} ^ {t} x _ {2} (s) \mathrm{d} s \geqslant \frac {\hat {a} _ {1 1} m _ {2} - \check {a} _ {2 1} m _ {1}}{\hat {a} _ {1 1} \check {a} _ {2 2} - \hat {a} _ {1 2} \check {a} _ {2 1}} \quad \text { a.s. }.
$$

于是定理得证.

引理5.9 取相同形式的增长率 $a_{ij}(r(t))$ ，也就是说， $a_{ij}(r(t)) = a_{ij}(i,j = 1,2)$ 则系统(5.54）可以被表示为

$$
\left\{ \begin{array}{l} \mathrm{d} x _ {1} (t) = x _ {1} (t) \left[ \left(b _ {1} (r (t)) - a _ {1 1} x _ {1} (t) - a _ {1 2} x _ {2} (t)\right) \mathrm{d} t + \sigma_ {1} (r (t)) \mathrm{d} B _ {1} (t) \right], \\ \mathrm{d} x _ {2} (t) = x _ {2} (t) \left[ \left(b _ {2} (r (t)) - a _ {2 1} x _ {1} (t) - a _ {2 2} x _ {2} (t)\right) \mathrm{d} t + \sigma_ {2} (r (t)) \mathrm{d} B _ {2} (t) \right]. \end{array} \right. \tag {5.85}
$$

结论5.1 令假设2和假设3成立，则系统SDE(5.85)对任何满足初始条件的解 $x(t)$ 具有下面的性质

$$
\lim _ {t \rightarrow \infty} \frac {1}{t} \int_ {0} ^ {t} x _ {1} (s) \mathrm{d} s = \frac {a _ {1 1} m _ {1} - a _ {2 1} m _ {2}}{a _ {1 1} a _ {2 2} - a _ {1 2} a _ {2 1}} \quad \text { a   .   s   . }
$$

和

$$
\lim _ {t \rightarrow \infty} \frac {1}{t} \int_ {0} ^ {t} x _ {2} (s) \mathrm{d} s = \frac {a _ {1 1} m _ {2} - a _ {2 1} m _ {1}}{a _ {1 1} a _ {2 2} - a _ {1 2} a _ {2 1}} \quad \text { a   .   s   . }.
$$

## 5.3.4 系统 (5.54) 的灭绝性

本小节将证明当白噪声的强度足够大时, SDE(5.54) 将以概率 1 灭绝.

定理 5.13 令假设 1 成立, 令

$$
\check {b} (k) = \max \{b _ {1} (k), b _ {2} (k) \}, \quad \frac {\hat {\sigma} ^ {2} (k)}{2} = \frac {1}{2 \left(\frac {1}{\sigma_ {1} ^ {2} (k)} + \frac {1}{\sigma_ {2} ^ {2} (k)}\right)},
$$

对任意给定的初始值 $x(0) \in \mathbb{R}_+^2$ , SDE(5.54) 的解具有下面的性质.

$$
\limsup _ {t \to \infty} \frac {\log x _ {i} (t)}{t} \leqslant \sum_ {k = 1} ^ {N} \pi_ {k} \left[ \check {b} (k) - \frac {\hat {\sigma} ^ {2} (k)}{2} \right] \quad \text { a.s. }.
$$

特别地, 如果 $\sum_{k=1}^{N} \pi_k \left[ \check{b}(k) - \frac{\hat{\sigma}^2(k)}{2} \right] < 0$ , 则 $\lim_{t \to \infty} x_i(t) = 0$ a.s..

证明 利用 Itô 公式得

$$
\mathrm{d} \log x _ {1} (t) \leqslant \left[ b _ {1} (r (t)) - \frac {1}{2} \sigma_ {1} ^ {2} (r (t)) \right] \mathrm{d} t + \sigma_ {1} (r (t)) \mathrm{d} B _ {1} (t),
$$

$$
\mathrm{d} \log x _ {2} (t) \leqslant \left[ b _ {2} (r (t)) - \frac {1}{2} \sigma_ {2} ^ {2} (r (t)) \right] \mathrm{d} t + \sigma_ {2} (r (t)) \mathrm{d} B _ {2} (t).
$$

从 0 到 t 积分式 (5.86) 得

$$
\log x _ {1} (t) \leqslant \log x _ {1} (0) + \int_ {0} ^ {t} \left[ b _ {1} (r (s)) - \frac {\sigma_ {1} ^ {2} (r (s))}{2} \right] \mathrm{d} s + M _ {1} (t) \tag {5.87}
$$

和

$$
\log x _ {2} (t) \leqslant \log x _ {2} (0) + \int_ {0} ^ {t} \left[ b _ {2} (r (s)) - \frac {\sigma_ {2} ^ {2} (r (s))}{2} \right] \mathrm{d} s + M _ {2} (t). \tag {5.88}
$$

其中 $M(t)$ 是引理 5.7 定义的鞅, 由鞅的强大数定律并在式 (5.87) 和式 (5.88) 的两侧分别除以 t, 并令 $t \longrightarrow \infty$ , 可得

$$
\limsup _ {t \to \infty} \frac {\log x _ {1} (t)}{t} \leqslant \sum_ {k = 1} ^ {N} \pi_ {k} \left[ \check {b} (k) - \frac {\hat {\sigma} ^ {2} (k)}{2} \right] \quad \text { a.s. },
$$

$$
\limsup _ {t \to \infty} \frac {\log x _ {2} (t)}{t} \leqslant \sum_ {k = 1} ^ {N} \pi_ {k} \left[ \tilde {b} (k) - \frac {\hat {\sigma} ^ {2} (k)}{2} \right] \quad \text { a.s. }
$$

## 5.3.5 系统 (5.54) 的全局吸引性

本小节将给出系统 $(5.54)$ 具有全局吸引的充分条件.

定义 5.5 $^{[97]}$ 令 $x(t), y(t)$ 是系统 (5.54) 的任意两个解，且满足初始条件 $x(0), y(0) \in \mathbb{R}_{+}^{2}$ ，如果

$$
\lim _ {t \rightarrow \infty} | x (t) - y (t) | = 0 \quad \text { a   .   s   . },
$$

则称系统(5.54)是全局吸引的.

定理 5.14 如果假设 3 成立, 则系统 (5.54) 是全局吸引的.

证明 令 $x(t), y(t)$ 是系统 (5.54) 满足初始条件 $x(0), y(0) \in \mathbb{R}_+^2$ 的任意两个解. 由 Itô 公式可得

$$
\begin{array}{l} \mathrm{d} \log x _ {i} (t) = \left[ b _ {i} (r (t)) - \frac {1}{2} \sigma_ {i} ^ {2} (r (t)) - a _ {i i} (r (t)) x _ {i} (t) - a _ {i j} (r (t)) x _ {j} (t) \right] \mathrm{d} t \\ + \sigma_ {i} (r (t)) \mathrm{d} B _ {i} (t), \quad i = 1, 2, \\ \end{array}
$$

$$
\mathrm{d} \log y _ {i} (t) = \left[ b _ {i} (r (t)) - \frac {1}{2} \sigma_ {i} ^ {2} (r (t)) - a _ {i i} (r (t)) y _ {i} (t) - a _ {i j} (r (t)) y _ {j} (t) \right] \mathrm{d} t
$$

$$
+ \sigma_ {i} (r (t)) \mathrm{d} B _ {i} (t), \quad i = 1, 2.
$$

于是有

$$
\begin{array}{l} \mathrm{d} (\log x _ {i} (t) - \log y _ {i} (t)) = \{- a _ {i i} (r (t)) [ x _ {i} (t) - y _ {i} (t) ] \\ - a _ {i j} (r (t)) [ x _ {j} (t) - y _ {j} (t) ] \} \mathrm{d} t, \quad i = 1, 2. \tag {5.89} \\ \end{array}
$$

因为 $\hat{a}_{11}\hat{a}_{22} > \check{a}_{12}\check{a}_{21}$ ，所以存在两个正常数 $c_{1}, c_{2}$ 满足

$$
\frac {\hat {a} _ {2 1}}{\hat {a} _ {1 1}} <   \frac {c _ {1}}{c _ {2}} <   \frac {\hat {a} _ {2 2}}{\hat {a} _ {1 2}},
$$

因此 $c_{1}\hat{a}_{11}-c_{2}\hat{a}_{21}>0$ 且 $c_{2}\hat{a}_{22}-c_{1}\hat{a}_{12}>0$ .

定义 Lyapunov 函数 $V(t)$ 如下

$$
V (t) = c _ {1} | \log x _ {1} (t) - \log y _ {1} (t) | + c _ {2} | \log x _ {2} (t) - \log y _ {2} (t) |.
$$

在方程 (5.89) 中直接计算 $V(t)$ 的右导数 $\mathrm{d}^{+}V(t)$ 可得

$$
\begin{array}{l} \mathrm{d} ^ {+} V (t) \\ = c _ {1} \operatorname{sgn} \left(x _ {1} (t) - y _ {1} (t)\right) \mathrm{d} \left[ \log x _ {1} (t) - \log y _ {1} (t) \right] \\ + c _ {2} \operatorname{sgn} (x _ {2} (t) - y _ {2} (t)) \mathrm{d} [ \log x _ {2} (t) - \log y _ {2} (t) ] \\ = c _ {1} \operatorname{sgn} (x _ {1} (t) - y _ {1} (t)) [ - a _ {1 1} (r (t)) (x _ {1} (t) - y _ {1} (t)) \mathrm{d} t - a _ {1 2} (r (t)) (x _ {2} (t) - y _ {2} (t)) \mathrm{d} t ] \\ + c _ {2} \operatorname{sgn} (x _ {2} (t) - y _ {2} (t)) [ - a _ {2 1} (r (t)) (x _ {1} (t) - y _ {1} (t)) \mathrm{d} t - a _ {2 2} (r (t)) (x _ {2} (t) - y _ {2} (t)) \mathrm{d} t ] \\ \end{array}
$$

$$
\begin{array}{l} \leqslant - c _ {1} \hat {a} _ {1 1} | x _ {1} (t) - y _ {1} (t) | \mathrm{d} t - c _ {1} \operatorname{sgn} \left(x _ {1} (t) - y _ {1} (t)\right) a _ {1 2} (r (t)) \left(x _ {2} (t) - y _ {2} (t)\right) \mathrm{d} t \\ - c _ {2} \hat {a} _ {2 2} | x _ {2} (t) - y _ {2} (t) | \mathrm{d} t - c _ {2} \operatorname{sgn} (x _ {2} (t) - y _ {2} (t)) a _ {2 1} (r (t)) (x _ {1} (t) - y _ {1} (t)) \mathrm{d} t \\ \leqslant - c _ {1} \hat {a} _ {1 1} | x _ {1} (t) - y _ {1} (t) | \mathrm{d} t + c _ {1} \hat {a} _ {1 2} | x _ {2} (t) - y _ {2} (t) | \mathrm{d} t \\ - c _ {2} \hat {a} _ {2 2} | x _ {2} (t) - y _ {2} (t) | \mathrm{d} t + c _ {2} \hat {a} _ {2 1} | x _ {1} (t) - y _ {1} (t) | \mathrm{d} t \\ = - \left(c _ {1} \hat {a} _ {1 1} - c _ {2} \check {a} _ {2 1}\right) \left| x _ {1} (t) - y _ {1} (t) \right| \mathrm{d} t - \left(c _ {2} \hat {a} _ {2 2} - c _ {1} \hat {a} _ {1 2}\right) \left| x _ {2} (t) - y _ {2} (t) \right| \mathrm{d} t, \\ \end{array}
$$

其中 $\gamma = \min \{c_1\hat{a}_{11} - c_2\hat{a}_{21}, c_2\hat{a}_{22} - c_1\hat{a}_{12}\}$ , 于是有

$$
\mathrm{d} ^ {+} V (t) \leqslant - \gamma \left(\left| x _ {1} (t) - y _ {1} (t) \right| \mathrm{d} t + \left| x _ {2} (t) - y _ {2} (t) \right| \mathrm{d} t\right). \tag {5.90}
$$

在式 (5.90) 中从 0 到 $t$ 积分可得

$$
V (t) - V (0) \leqslant - \gamma \int_ {0} ^ {t} [ | x _ {1} (s) - y _ {1} (s) | + | x _ {2} (s) - y _ {2} (s) | ] \mathrm{d} s,
$$

于是有

$$
V (t) + \gamma \int_ {0} ^ {t} [ | x _ {1} (s) - y _ {1} (s) | + | x _ {2} (s) - y _ {2} (s) | ] \mathrm{d} s \leqslant V (0) <   \infty .
$$

令 $t\to \infty$ ，可得

$$
\begin{array}{l} \int_ {0} ^ {\infty} | x (s) - y (s) | \mathrm{d} s \leqslant \int_ {0} ^ {\infty} [ | x _ {1} (s) - y _ {1} (s) | + | x _ {2} (s) - y _ {2} (s) | ] \mathrm{d} s \\ \leqslant \frac {V (0)}{\gamma} <   \infty \quad \text { a   .   s   . }, \tag {5.91} \\ \end{array}
$$

且

$$
E \int_ {0} ^ {\infty} | x (s) - y (s) | \mathrm{d} s <   \infty .
$$

注意到 $z(t) = x(t) - y(t)$ . 显然 $z(t) \in C[\mathbb{R}_{+}, \mathbb{R}^{2}]$ a.s. 由式 (5.91) 有

$$
\liminf _ {t \to \infty} | z (t) | = 0 \quad \text { a.s. }
$$

关于

$$
\lim _ {t \to \infty} | z (t) | = 0 \quad \text { a.s. }
$$

的证明参见文献 [71], 因此这里省略.

## 5.3.6 数值模拟

本小节我们将利用实例和图形验证我们的结论. 令状态空间为 $S = \{1,2\}$ . 利用文献 [33] 的数值计算方法, 对 $k \in S$ , 我们考虑下面的离散化方程

$$
\left\{ \begin{array}{l} x _ {1, i + 1} = x _ {1, i} + \left[ x _ {1, i} \left(b _ {1} (k) - a _ {1 1} (k) x _ {1, i}\right) - a _ {1 2} (k) x _ {2, i} ^ {2} \right] h \\ \quad + \sigma_ {1} (k) x _ {1, i} \sqrt {h} \xi_ {1, i} + \frac {1}{2} \sigma_ {1} ^ {2} (k) x _ {1, i} \left(h \xi_ {1, i} ^ {2} - h\right), \\ x _ {2, i + 1} = x _ {2, i} + \left[ x _ {2, i} \left(b _ {2} (k) - a _ {2 1} (k) x _ {2, i}\right) - a _ {2 2} (k) x _ {1, i} ^ {2} \right] h \\ \quad + \sigma_ {2} (k) x _ {2, i} \sqrt {h} \xi_ {2, i} + \frac {1}{2} \sigma_ {2} ^ {2} (k) x _ {2, i} \left(h \xi_ {2, i} ^ {2} - h\right). \end{array} \right. \tag {5.92}
$$

令 $(x_{1}(0), x_{2}(0))=(0.8, 0.7)$ ，时间步长为 h=0.01。

例 5.3 假设 SDE(5.92) 的 Markov 链 $r(t)$ 取值于有限状态空间 $S = \{1, 2\}$ ，生成元为

$$
\Gamma = \left( \begin{array}{c c} {- 2} & {2} \\ {1} & {- 1} \end{array} \right),
$$

易得 Markov 链 $r(t)$ 平稳分布为 $\pi = (\pi_1, \pi_2) = \left(\frac{1}{3}, \frac{2}{3}\right)$ ，其中 $\pi$ 是下面线性方程的解

$$
\pi \Gamma = 0, \quad \pi_ {1} + \pi_ {2} = 1.
$$

情况 1 假设

$$
b _ {1} (1) = 5, \quad a _ {1 1} (1) = 4, \quad a _ {1 2} (1) = 2, \quad \sigma_ {1} (1) = 0. 0 8;
$$

$$
b _ {2} (1) = 5, \quad a _ {2 1} (1) = 3, \quad a _ {2 2} (1) = 6, \quad \sigma_ {2} (1) = 0. 0 5;
$$

$$
b _ {1} (2) = 4, \quad a _ {1 1} (2) = 4, \quad a _ {1 2} (2) = 2, \quad \sigma_ {1} (2) = 0. 1;
$$

$$
b _ {2} (2) = 4, \quad a _ {2 1} (2) = 1, \quad a _ {2 2} (2) = 3, \quad \sigma_ {2} (2) = 0. 2.
$$

显然 $\hat{a}_{11}\hat{a}_{22} = 12 > \check{a}_{12}\check{a}_{21} = 6, b_i(1) - \frac{1}{2}\sigma_i^2 (1) > 0, b_i(2) - \frac{1}{2}\sigma_i^2 (2) > 0, i = 1,2,$ 

且 $\hat{b}_1(k) - \frac{\check{\sigma}_1^2(k)}{2} = 3.995 > \frac{\check{a}_{12}\left[\hat{b}_2(k) - \frac{\check{\sigma}_2^2(k)}{2}\right]}{\hat{a}_{22}} = 3.3325, \hat{b}_2(k) - \frac{\check{\sigma}_2^2(k)}{2} = 3.98>$ 

$\frac{\check{a}_{21}\left[\hat{b}_1(k) - \frac{\check{\sigma}_1^2(k)}{2}\right]}{\hat{a}_{11}} = 3.7476$ ，则假设 $1\sim$ 假设3成立.利用定理5.11和定理5.12，我们知道SDE(5.92)对任何给定的初始值具有唯一正解 $x(t)$ ，并且这个解是均值意义下持久的.

我们计算

$$
\tilde {x} _ {1} ^ {*} = \frac {\hat {a} _ {2 2} m _ {1} - \check {a} _ {1 2} m _ {2}}{\check {a} _ {1 1} \hat {a} _ {2 2} - \check {a} _ {1 2} \hat {a} _ {2 1}} \doteq 0. 0 4 7 5 2, \quad \hat {x} _ {1} ^ {*} = \frac {\check {a} _ {2 2} m _ {1} - \hat {a} _ {1 2} m _ {2}}{\hat {a} _ {1 1} \check {a} _ {2 2} - \hat {a} _ {1 2} \check {a} _ {2 1}} \doteq 0. 5 9 2 7 1 4 8 1,
$$

$$
\tilde {x} _ {2} ^ {*} = \frac {\hat {a} _ {1 1} m _ {2} - \check {a} _ {2 1} m _ {1}}{\hat {a} _ {1 1} \check {a} _ {2 2} - \hat {a} _ {1 2} \check {a} _ {2 1}} \doteq 0. 4 8 0 1 7, \quad \hat {x} _ {2} ^ {*} = \frac {\check {a} _ {1 1} m _ {2} - \hat {a} _ {2 1} m _ {1}}{\check {a} _ {1 1} \hat {a} _ {2 2} - \check {a} _ {1 2} \hat {a} _ {2 1}} \doteq 1. 5 2 7 5 4 6 6 8.
$$

于是由定理 5.12 知道 SDE(5.92) 是均值意义下持久的.

我们用图 5.8~图 5.10 来说明情况 1. 图 5.8 中图 (a), (d) 说明小强度的白噪声引起的波动平缓. 图 (c), (f) 是 $x_{1}(t)$ 和 $x_{2}(t)$ 的 quantile-quantile 图, 它们都接近直线. 这表明分布接近于标准正态分布 (见中间的柱状图). 从图 5.10 可以看出, $\square$ 表示 $x_{1}(t)$ 和 $x_{2}(t)$ 处于状态 $k = 1$ 下. 类似地, $\circ$ 表示 $x_{1}(t)$ 和 $x_{2}(t)$ 处于状态 $k = 2$ 下, 而 + 表示 $x_{1}(t)$ 和 $x_{2}(t)$ 在 $r(t)$ 的影响下到两种状态下转换时的种群数量. + 区域处于 $\square$ 和 $\circ$ 区域中间. 从图 5.10 可以看出白噪声和彩色噪声对系统的影响, 从初始值 (0.8, 0.7) 出发, $x(t)$ 是正常返的并处于区域 $\{(x_{1}, x_{2}) : 0.05 < x_{1} < 0.6, 0.5 < x_{2} < 1.5\}$ 中, 这便验证了情况 1 的结论.

情况 2 假设

$$
b _ {1} (1) = 1. 4, \quad a _ {1 1} (1) = 3, \quad a _ {1 2} (1) = 1, \quad \sigma_ {1} (1) = 1. 8;
$$

$$
b _ {2} (1) = 1. 4, \quad a _ {2 1} (1) = 2, \quad a _ {2 2} (1) = 4, \quad \sigma_ {2} (1) = 1. 7;
$$

$$
b _ {1} (2) = 1. 2, \quad a _ {1 1} (2) = 4, \quad a _ {1 2} (2) = 1. 5, \quad \sigma_ {1} (2) = 1. 7;
$$

$$
b _ {2} (2) = 1. 1, \quad a _ {2 1} (2) = 1, \quad a _ {2 2} (2) = 2, \quad \sigma_ {2} (2) = 1. 6.
$$

条件 $\hat{a}_{11}\hat{a}_{22} = 6 > \check{a}_{12}\check{a}_{21} = 3$ 成立，但是， $\sum_{k=1}^{2}\pi_k\left[\check{b}(k) - \frac{\hat{\sigma}^2(k)}{2}\right] \doteq -0.05666667 < 0,$ 于是定理5.12不满足，但是灭绝性定理5.13成立，则SDE(5.92)将灭绝.

## 第6章 总结与展望

本书研究在噪声影响下的随机生物模型动力学行为, 主要的工具为 Lyapunov 分析方法和 Khasminskii 的周期解存在定理, 以及 Markov 理论. 本书的研究内容共四部分, 第一部分主要给出具有 Holling II 功能性反应的随机生态流行病模型的动力学行为, 第二部分主要研究了随机生物模型的竞争排斥原理, 第三部分主要探讨了污染环境下的随机非自治 Lotka-Volterra 模型. 第四部分主要研究了随机 Lotka-Volterra 捕食-食饵模型及竞争系统模型的动力学性质.

本书的注意力集中在随机模型解的渐近行为、竞争排斥原理及周期解的存在性和全局吸引性上, 主要贡献如下: ①给出具有 Holling II 功能性反应的随机生态流行病模型的解的渐近行为; ②给出某些随机生物模型的竞争排斥原理成立的充分条件; ③给出污染环境下的随机非自治 Lotka-Volterra 模型的非平凡周期解的存在性和全局吸引性结论; ④给出具有周期系数的非自治 Lotka-Volterra 捕食-食饵模型周期解的存在性, 并给出具有 Markov 链的随机 Lotka-Volterra 捕食-食饵模型和竞争系统模型的平稳分布及全局吸引性等.

本书所涉及的模型有限, 还有许多代表性的模型还没有研究, 或者更一般的模型没有研究. 本书考虑的扰动都是低阶扰动, 若扰动变成高阶扰动, 其模型的动力学行为有何变化, 是否与低阶扰动相似或有本质性区别, 将是我们以后研究的内容. 本书考虑的模型中的参数都是常系数或者周期系数, 若参数是带有 Markov 转换的, 其模型的平稳分布和遍历性的存在性是一个值得探索的课题. 本书考虑的布朗运动驱动的随机微分方程仍属经典范畴, 对于 Poisson 过程或 Levi 过程驱动的随机生物模型是否具有类似的动力学行为也是今后的研究工作之一. 希望本书所运用的方法及得到的结论对其他类型的随机模型研究具有一定的指导意义.
